.SYNOPSIS
    Р”Р°РјРї СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРіРѕ РїР°РїРєРё РІ РѕРґРёРЅ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Р№ С„Р°Р№Р» (СЃ РґРµСЂРµРІРѕРј Рё СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјС‹Рј).

                     РІСЂРѕРґРµ jgraph/drawio, РіРґРµ С‚РѕРЅРµС‚ РІ bundled-libs Рё media.
      -Mode Exclude  вЂ” СЃС‚Р°СЂР°СЏ Р»РѕРіРёРєР°: Р±РµСЂС‘Рј РІСЃС‘, РєСЂРѕРјРµ Exclude.

.PARAMETER IncludePath
    Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРµРїСѓСЃС‚Рѕ вЂ” РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»С‹, С‡РµР№ РїСѓС‚СЊ (РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ $Path)

    [string]$Mode = "Include",

        "\.mjs\.map$",
        "\.map\.json$",
        # TypeScript-РґРµРєР»Р°СЂР°С†РёРё (РіРµРЅРµСЂРёСЂСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ)
        "\.d\.ts$",
        "\.d\.mts$",
        # lock-С„Р°Р№Р»С‹ РїР°РєРµС‚РЅС‹С… РјРµРЅРµРґР¶РµСЂРѕРІ
        "package-lock\.json$",
        "yarn\.lock$",
        "pnpm-lock\.yaml$",
        "npm-shrinkwrap\.json$",
        "composer\.lock$",
        "Gemfile\.lock$",
        "\.lock$",
        # drawio-specific: РґРёР°РіСЂР°РјРјС‹ вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ, РЅРµ РєРѕРґ
        "\.drawio\.xml$",
        "\.drawio$",
        # drawio-specific: Р»РѕРєР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёРё вЂ” РґРµСЃСЏС‚РєРё РјРµРіР°Р±Р°Р№С‚ СЃР»РѕРІР°СЂРµР№,
        # РЅРµ РєРѕРґ. Р›РµР¶Р°С‚ РІ js/diagramly/*.json, РЅРѕ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂСѓРµРј РїРѕ РёРјРµРЅРё.
        "^[A-Za-z]{2}(-[A-Za-z]{2,4})?\.json$"
    ),

    # Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРµРїСѓСЃС‚Рѕ вЂ” РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»С‹, С‡РµР№ rel-РїСѓС‚СЊ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚
    # РѕРґРЅСѓ РёР· СЌС‚РёС… РїРѕРґСЃС‚СЂРѕРє. РќР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ: 'js\diagramly\', 'src\main\java\'.
    [string[]]$IncludePath = @(),

    [long]$MaxFileSize = 1MB
)

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { Write-Error "Folder not found: $Path"; exit 1 }

$root    = (Resolve-Path $Path).Path
$outFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Output)

if (Test-Path $outFull) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $outFull -Force }

# ===============================================================
#  Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂС‹
# ===============================================================

function Test-PathExcluded {
    param([string]$RelPath)

    # РћР±РѕСЂР°С‡РёРІР°РµРј РІ Р±СЌРєСЃР»РµС€Рё, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РїР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅС‹ РІРёРґР° "\.git\" РјР°С‚С‡РёР»РёСЃСЊ
    # Рё РІ РЅР°С‡Р°Р»Рµ, Рё РІ СЃРµСЂРµРґРёРЅРµ РїСѓС‚Рё.
    $wrapped = "\" + $RelPath + "\"
    foreach ($ex in $Exclude) {
        if ($wrapped -like "*$ex*") { return $true }
    }
    return $false
}

function Test-InIncludePath {
    param([string]$RelPath)

    if ($IncludePath.Count -eq 0) { return $true }
    $wrapped = "\" + $RelPath + "\"
    foreach ($p in $IncludePath) {
        if ($wrapped -like "*$p*") { return $true }
    }
    return $false
}

function Test-FileAllowed {
    param([System.IO.FileInfo]$File)

    $rel = $File.FullName.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\')

    if (Test-PathExcluded  $rel) { return $false }
    if (-not (Test-InIncludePath $rel)) { return $false }
    if ($File.FullName -eq $outFull) { return $false }

    $ext = $File.Extension.ToLower()

    if ($Mode -eq "Include") {
        if ($Include -notcontains $ext) { return $false }
    } else {
        if ($Extensions.Count -gt 0 -and ($Extensions -notcontains $ext)) {
            return $false
        }
    }

    foreach ($pat in $ExcludeNamePatterns) {
        if ($File.Name -match $pat) { return $false }
    }

    if ($File.Length -gt $MaxFileSize) { return $false }

    return $true
}

function Test-DirAllowed {
    param([System.IO.DirectoryInfo]$Dir)
    $rel = $Dir.FullName.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\')

    if (Test-PathExcluded $rel) { return $false }
    if (-not (Test-InIncludePath $rel)) { return $false }
    return $true
}

# ===============================================================
#  Р”РµСЂРµРІРѕ
# ===============================================================

function Get-Tree {
    param([string]$Dir, [string]$Prefix = "")

    $items = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Dir -Force |
             Where-Object { $_.Name -ne "." -and $_.Name -ne ".." } |
             Sort-Object { -not $_.PSIsContainer }, Name |
             Where-Object {
                 if ($_.PSIsContainer) { Test-DirAllowed  $_ }
                 else                  { Test-FileAllowed $_ }
             }

    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $items.Count; $i++) {
        $item   = $items[$i]
        $last   = ($i -eq $items.Count - 1)
        $branch = if ($last) { "+-- " } else { "|-- " }

        "$Prefix$branch$($item.Name)"

        if ($item.PSIsContainer) {
            $np = $Prefix + $(if ($last) { "    " } else { "|   " })
            Get-Tree -Dir $item.FullName -Prefix $np
        }
    }
}

# ===============================================================
#  РЎР±РѕСЂ С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ
# ===============================================================

Write-Host "Mode:        $Mode" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Exclude:     $($Exclude.Count) patterns" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
if ($Mode -eq "Include") {
    Write-Host "Include ext: $($Include -join ', ')" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
}
if ($IncludePath.Count -gt 0) {
    Write-Host "IncludePath: $($IncludePath -join ', ')" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
}
Write-Host "Building tree..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

$files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $root -Recurse -File -Force |
         Where-Object { Test-FileAllowed $_ } |
         Sort-Object FullName

Write-Host "Files found: $($files.Count)" -ForegroundColor Cyan

# ===============================================================
#  РЎР±РѕСЂРєР° РґР°РјРїР°
# ===============================================================

$sb = [System.Text.StringBuilder]::new()

[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine("FOLDER: $root")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("DATE:   $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("MODE:   $Mode")
if ($IncludePath.Count -gt 0) {
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("ONLY:   $($IncludePath -join ', ')")
}
[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine()
[void]$sb.AppendLine((Split-Path $root -Leaf))
[void]$sb.AppendLine((Get-Tree -Dir $root))
[void]$sb.AppendLine()

[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine("FILE CONTENTS")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine()

$i = 0
foreach ($f in $files) {
    $i++
    $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\')
    Write-Host "[$i/$($files.Count)] $rel" -ForegroundColor DarkGray

    [void]$sb.AppendLine("-" * 80)
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("FILE: $rel")
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("SIZE: $($f.Length) bytes")
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("-" * 80)

    try {
        $content = Get-Content -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Raw -ErrorAction Stop
        [void]$sb.AppendLine($content)
        if ($content -and -not $content.EndsWith("`n")) { [void]$sb.AppendLine() }
    } catch {
        [void]$sb.AppendLine("[READ ERROR: $($_.Exception.Message)]")
    }
    [void]$sb.AppendLine()
}

[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($outFull, $sb.ToString(), [System.Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Done!" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "New file: $outFull" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Size: $([math]::Round((Get-Item $outFull).Length / 1KB, 1)) KB" -ForegroundColor Green

<#
.SYNOPSIS
    Dump folder contents to a single text file.

.DESCRIPTION
    Three presets:
      -Preset Overview - minimum to understand a product (README, manifests, entry points).
      -Preset Source   (default) - sources without bundles, templates, locales.
      -Preset Full     - everything, except binaries.

    Large files are handled by -LargeFilePolicy:
      Skip     - not in tree, not in contents.
      Header   - in tree, but contents show only a header with size.
      Truncate - in tree and in contents (first N lines).

.EXAMPLE
    .\dump.ps1 -Path C:\src\drawio -Output drawio-src.txt
    .\dump.ps1 -Path C:\src\drawio -Output drawio-java.txt -IncludePath 'src\main\java\'
    .\dump.ps1 -Path C:\src\drawio -Output drawio.txt -LargeFilePolicy Truncate -TruncateLines 80
#>

param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)]
    [string]$Path,

    [string]$Output = "dump.txt",

    [ValidateSet("Overview", "Source", "Full")]
    [string]$Preset = "Source",

    [string[]]$IncludePath = @(),

    # Threshold for "large file". Anything bigger is handled by LargeFilePolicy.
    [long]$MaxFileSize = 126KB,

    # What to do with files > MaxFileSize.
    [ValidateSet("Skip", "Header", "Truncate")]
    [string]$LargeFilePolicy = "Header",

    # How many lines to show in Truncate mode.
    [int]$TruncateLines = 200,

    # Print top-N largest included files at the end.
    [int]$TopLargest = 10,

    # ============================================================
    #  Common excludes (all presets)
    # ============================================================
    [string[]]$Exclude = @(
        "\.git\",
        "\.github\ISSUE_TEMPLATE\",
        "\node_modules\",
        "\bower_components\",
        "\vendor\",
        "\bin\",
        "\obj\",
        "\dist\",
        "\build\",
        "\target\",
        "\out\",
        "\coverage\",
        "\.gradle\",
        "\.idea\",
        "\.vscode\",
        "\.next\",
        "\.nuxt\",
        "\fonts\",
        "\resources\images\",
        "\images\",
        "\img\",
        "\icons\",
        "\.png\", "\.jpg\", "\.jpeg", "\.gif\", "\.ico\",
        "\.webp\", "\.bmp\", "\.tiff\", "\.psd\",
        "\.woff\", "\.woff2\", "\.ttf\", "\.eot\", "\.otf\",
        "\.pdf\", "\.zip\", "\.tar\", "\.gz\", "\.7z\", "\.jar\",
        "\.class\", "\.exe", "\.dll\", "\.so\", "\.dylib\"
    ),

    # ============================================================
    #  Preset Source: junk folders
    # ============================================================
    [string[]]$SourceExclude = @(
        "\js\mxgraph\",
        "\js\jquery\",
        "\js\MathJax\",
        "\js\bootstrap\",
        "\js\codemirror\",
        "\js\dyo\",
        "\js\sanitizer\",
        "\js\spin\",
        "\js\stencils\",
        "\templates\",
        "\template\",
        "\locales\",
        "\locale\",
        "\i18n\",
        "\l10n\",
        "\translations\",
        "\lang\",
        "\__tests__\",
        "\__mocks__\",
        "\test\fixtures\",
        "\tests\fixtures\",
        "\testdata\",
        "\samples\",
        "\examples\data\"
    ),

    # ============================================================
    #  Preset Source: regex on file name
    # ============================================================
    [string[]]$SourceNamePatterns = @(
        "\.min\.", "-min\.", "_min\.",
        "\.bundle\.", "\.compiled\.", "\.packed\.",
        "\.optimized\.", "\.minified\.",
        "\.chunk\.", "-chunk\.",
        "\.[0-9a-f]{8,}\.(js|css|mjs)$",
        "[._-][0-9a-f]{20,}\.(js|css|mjs)$",
        "\.concat\.(js|css)$", "\.all\.(js|css)$", "\.pack\.(js|css)$",
        "-[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.(js|css|mjs)$",
        "\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.(js|css|mjs)$",
        "\.js\.map$", "\.css\.map$", "\.mjs\.map$", "\.map\.json$",
        "\.d\.ts$", "\.d\.mts$",
        "package-lock\.json$", "yarn\.lock$", "pnpm-lock\.yaml$",
        "npm-shrinkwrap\.json$", "composer\.lock$", "Gemfile\.lock$",
        "\.lock$",
        "\.drawio\.xml$", "\.drawio$",
        "^[A-Za-z]{2}(-[A-Za-z]{2,4})?\.json$",
        "\.snap$"
    ),

    # ============================================================
    #  Preset Full: extension whitelist
    # ============================================================
    [string[]]$Include = @(
        ".java", ".kt", ".groovy", ".scala",
        ".js", ".mjs", ".cjs", ".ts", ".tsx", ".jsx", ".vue", ".svelte",
        ".c", ".cpp", ".cc", ".h", ".hpp",
        ".py", ".rb", ".go", ".rs", ".php",
        ".html", ".htm", ".xhtml",
        ".jsp", ".jspf", ".tag", ".ftl",
        ".css", ".scss", ".sass", ".less",
        ".xml", ".xsd", ".dtd", ".xsl", ".xslt",
        ".gradle", ".kts", ".properties",
        ".yml", ".yaml", ".toml", ".ini", ".cfg", ".conf",
        ".json",
        ".md", ".sql",
        ".sh", ".bash", ".zsh", ".bat", ".cmd", ".ps1"
    ),

    [string[]]$ExcludeNamePatterns = @(
        "\.min\.", "-min\.", "_min\.",
        "\.bundle\.", "\.compiled\.", "\.packed\.",
        "\.optimized\.", "\.minified\.",
        "\.chunk\.", "-chunk\.",
        "\.[0-9a-f]{8,}\.(js|css|mjs)$",
        "[._-][0-9a-f]{20,}\.(js|css|mjs)$",
        "\.concat\.(js|css)$", "\.all\.(js|css)$", "\.pack\.(js|css)$",
        "-[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.(js|css|mjs)$",
        "\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.(js|css|mjs)$",
        "\.js\.map$", "\.css\.map$", "\.mjs\.map$", "\.map\.json$",
        "\.d\.ts$", "\.d\.mts$",
        "package-lock\.json$", "yarn\.lock$", "pnpm-lock\.yaml$",
        "npm-shrinkwrap\.json$", "composer\.lock$", "Gemfile\.lock$",
        "\.lock$",
        "\.drawio\.xml$", "\.drawio$"
    ),

    # ============================================================
    #  Preset Overview: "shop window"
    # ============================================================
    [string[]]$OverviewPatterns = @(
        '^[^/]+\.(md|rst|adoc)$',
        '^LICENSE(\.\w+)?$', '^NOTICE(\.\w+)?$',
        '^CHANGELOG(\.\w+)?$', '^CHANGES(\.\w+)?$',
        '^CONTRIBUTING(\.\w+)?$', '^AUTHORS(\.\w+)?$',
        '^SECURITY(\.\w+)?$', '^COPYING(\.\w+)?$',
        '^package\.json$', '^tsconfig(\.\w+)?\.json$',
        '^pom\.xml$', '^build\.gradle(\.kts)?$', '^settings\.gradle(\.kts)?$',
        '^Cargo\.toml$', '^go\.mod$', '^Gemfile$',
        '^composer\.json$', '^requirements(\.\w+)?\.txt$',
        '^pyproject\.toml$', '^setup\.(py|cfg)$',
        '^Dockerfile(\..+)?$', '^docker-compose(\..+)?\.ya?ml$',
        '^\.dockerignore$', '^\.env\.example$',
        '^\.github/workflows/.+\.ya?ml$',
        '^\.gitlab-ci\.yml$', '^\.travis\.yml$', '^Jenkinsfile$',
        '^\.editorconfig$', '^\.gitignore$', '^\.gitattributes$',
        '^src/main/java/.+/(Application|Main|App)\.java$',
        '^src/(index|main|app)\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx|mjs)$',
        '^src/main\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx)$',
        '^main\.py$', '^app\.py$',
        '^src/main\.rs$', '^cmd/.+/main\.go$',
        '^docs/[^/]+\.(md|rst|txt)$', '^doc/[^/]+\.(md|rst|txt)$'
    )
)

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) { Write-Error "Folder not found: $Path"; exit 1 }

$root    = (Resolve-Path $Path).Path
$outFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Output)

if (Test-Path $outFull) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $outFull -Force }

# ===============================================================
#  Filters
# ===============================================================

function Test-PathExcluded {
    param([string]$RelPath, [string[]]$ExtraPatterns)
    $wrapped = "\" + $RelPath + "\"
    foreach ($ex in $Exclude) {
        if ($wrapped -like "*$ex*") { return $true }
    }
    if ($ExtraPatterns) {
        foreach ($ex in $ExtraPatterns) {
            if ($wrapped -like "*$ex*") { return $true }
        }
    }
    return $false
}

function Test-InIncludePath {
    param([string]$RelPath)
    if ($IncludePath.Count -eq 0) { return $true }
    $wrapped = "\" + $RelPath + "\"
    foreach ($p in $IncludePath) {
        if ($wrapped -like "*$p*") { return $true }
    }
    return $false
}

function Test-OverviewFile {
    param([string]$RelPath)
    $norm = $RelPath.Replace('\', '/')
    foreach ($p in $OverviewPatterns) {
        if ($norm -match $p) { return $true }
    }
    return $false
}

function Test-FileAllowed {
    param([System.IO.FileInfo]$File)

    $rel = $File.FullName.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\')

    if ($File.FullName -eq $outFull) { return $false }
    if (-not (Test-InIncludePath $rel)) { return $false }

    switch ($Preset) {
        "Overview" {
            if (Test-PathExcluded $rel $null) { return $false }
            return Test-OverviewFile $rel
        }
        "Source" {
            if (Test-PathExcluded $rel $SourceExclude) { return $false }
            $ext = $File.Extension.ToLower()
            if ($Include -notcontains $ext) { return $false }
            foreach ($pat in $SourceNamePatterns) {
                if ($File.Name -match $pat) { return $false }
            }
            return $true
        }
        "Full" {
            if (Test-PathExcluded $rel $null) { return $false }
            $ext = $File.Extension.ToLower()
            if ($Include -notcontains $ext) { return $false }
            foreach ($pat in $ExcludeNamePatterns) {
                if ($File.Name -match $pat) { return $false }
            }
            return $true
        }
    }
    return $false
}

function Test-DirExcluded {
    param([string]$RelPath)
    if ($RelPath -eq "") { return $false }
    $extra = if ($Preset -eq "Source") { $SourceExclude } else { $null }
    return (Test-PathExcluded $RelPath $extra)
}

# ===============================================================
#  Collect files
# ===============================================================

Write-Host "Preset:          $Preset" -ForegroundColor Cyan
if ($IncludePath.Count -gt 0) {
    Write-Host "IncludePath:     $($IncludePath -join ', ')" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
}
Write-Host "MaxFileSize:     $([math]::Round($MaxFileSize / 1KB, 0)) KB" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
Write-Host "LargeFilePolicy: $LargeFilePolicy" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
Write-Host "Scanning files..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

$candidates = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $root -Recurse -File -Force |
              Where-Object { Test-FileAllowed $_ } |
              Sort-Object FullName

$files      = @()
$largeFiles = @()
foreach ($f in $candidates) {
    if ($f.Length -gt $MaxFileSize) { $largeFiles += $f }
    else                            { $files      += $f }
}

Write-Host "Small files:     $($files.Count)" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Large files:     $($largeFiles.Count) (policy: $LargeFilePolicy)" -ForegroundColor $(if ($largeFiles.Count) { 'Yellow' } else { 'Cyan' })

$treeFiles = if ($LargeFilePolicy -eq "Skip") { $files } else { $files + $largeFiles }

$includedDirs = [System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]]::new(
    [System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
)
foreach ($f in $treeFiles) {
    $dir = $f.DirectoryName
    while ($dir -and $dir.Length -ge $root.Length) {
        [void]$includedDirs.Add($dir)
        if ($dir -eq $root) { break }
        $dir = Split-Path $dir -Parent
    }
}

# ===============================================================
#  Tree
# ===============================================================

function Get-Tree {
    param([string]$Dir, [string]$Prefix = "")

    $items = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Dir -Force |
             Where-Object { $_.Name -ne "." -and $_.Name -ne ".." } |
             Sort-Object { -not $_.PSIsContainer }, Name |
             Where-Object {
                 $rel = $_.FullName.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\')
                 if (Test-DirExcluded $rel) { return $false }
                 return $includedDirs.Contains($_.FullName)
             }

    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $items.Count; $i++) {
        $item   = $items[$i]
        $last   = ($i -eq $items.Count - 1)
        $branch = if ($last) { "+-- " } else { "|-- " }

        "$Prefix$branch$($item.Name)"

        if ($item.PSIsContainer) {
            $np = $Prefix + $(if ($last) { "    " } else { "|   " })
            Get-Tree -Dir $item.FullName -Prefix $np
        }
    }
}

# ===============================================================
#  Build dump
# ===============================================================

$sb = [System.Text.StringBuilder]::new()

[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine("FOLDER:            $root")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("DATE:              $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("PRESET:            $Preset")
if ($IncludePath.Count -gt 0) {
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("ONLY:              $($IncludePath -join ', ')")
}
[void]$sb.AppendLine("MAX FILE SIZE:     $([math]::Round($MaxFileSize / 1KB, 0)) KB")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("LARGE FILE POLICY: $LargeFilePolicy")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("SMALL FILES:       $($files.Count)")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("LARGE FILES:       $($largeFiles.Count)")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine()
[void]$sb.AppendLine((Split-Path $root -Leaf))
[void]$sb.AppendLine((Get-Tree -Dir $root))
[void]$sb.AppendLine()

[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine("FILE CONTENTS")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine()

$i = 0
foreach ($f in $files) {
    $i++
    $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\')
    Write-Host "[$i/$($files.Count)] $rel" -ForegroundColor DarkGray

    [void]$sb.AppendLine("-" * 80)
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("FILE: $rel")
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("SIZE: $($f.Length) bytes")
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("-" * 80)

    try {
        $content = Get-Content -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Raw -ErrorAction Stop
        [void]$sb.AppendLine($content)
        if ($content -and -not $content.EndsWith("`n")) { [void]$sb.AppendLine() }
    } catch {
        [void]$sb.AppendLine("[READ ERROR: $($_.Exception.Message)]")
    }
    [void]$sb.AppendLine()
}

# ============================================================
#  Large files
# ============================================================

if ($LargeFilePolicy -ne "Skip" -and $largeFiles.Count -gt 0) {
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("LARGE FILES (greater than $([math]::Round($MaxFileSize / 1KB, 0)) KB) - policy: $LargeFilePolicy")
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
    [void]$sb.AppendLine()

    foreach ($f in $largeFiles) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\')

        [void]$sb.AppendLine("-" * 80)
        [void]$sb.AppendLine("FILE: $rel")
        [void]$sb.AppendLine("SIZE: $($f.Length) bytes")
        [void]$sb.AppendLine("-" * 80)

        if ($LargeFilePolicy -eq "Header") {
            [void]$sb.AppendLine("[SKIPPED: $([math]::Round($f.Length / 1KB, 1)) KB greater than $([math]::Round($MaxFileSize / 1KB, 0)) KB]")
        }
        elseif ($LargeFilePolicy -eq "Truncate") {
            try {
                $content = Get-Content -LiteralPath $f.FullName -TotalCount $TruncateLines -ErrorAction Stop
                $content | ForEach-Object { [void]$sb.AppendLine($_) }
                [void]$sb.AppendLine()
                [void]$sb.AppendLine("[TRUNCATED: showing $TruncateLines lines of file $([math]::Round($f.Length / 1KB, 1)) KB]")
            } catch {
                [void]$sb.AppendLine("[READ ERROR: $($_.Exception.Message)]")
            }
        }
        [void]$sb.AppendLine()
    }
}

# ============================================================
#  Summary
# ============================================================

[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine("SUMMARY")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("=" * 80)
[void]$sb.AppendLine()
[void]$sb.AppendLine("Small files included: $($files.Count)")
[void]$sb.AppendLine("Large files:          $($largeFiles.Count) (policy: $LargeFilePolicy)")
[void]$sb.AppendLine()

if ($TopLargest -gt 0 -and $files.Count -gt 0) {
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("Top-$TopLargest largest included files:")
    $files |
        Sort-Object Length -Descending |
        Select-Object -First $TopLargest |
        ForEach-Object {
            $rel = $_.FullName.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\')
            [void]$sb.AppendLine(("  {0,8:N1} KB  {1}" -f ($_.Length / 1KB), $rel))
        }
    [void]$sb.AppendLine()
}

if ($largeFiles.Count -gt 0) {
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("All skipped-as-large files:")
    $largeFiles |
        Sort-Object Length -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            $rel = $_.FullName.Substring($root.Length).TrimStart('\')
            [void]$sb.AppendLine(("  {0,8:N1} KB  {1}" -f ($_.Length / 1KB), $rel))
        }
}

[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($outFull, $sb.ToString(), [System.Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Done!" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "New file: $outFull" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Size: $([math]::Round((Get-Item $outFull).Length / 1KB, 1)) KB" -ForegroundColor Green

# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend updating the configuration to enable type-aware lint rules:

```js
export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...

      // Remove tseslint.configs.recommended and replace with this
      tseslint.configs.recommendedTypeChecked,
      // Alternatively, use this for stricter rules
      tseslint.configs.strictTypeChecked,
      // Optionally, add this for stylistic rules
      tseslint.configs.stylisticTypeChecked,

      // Other configs...
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

You can also install [eslint-plugin-react-x](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-x) and [eslint-plugin-react-dom](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-dom) for React-specific lint rules:

```js
// eslint.config.js
import reactX from 'eslint-plugin-react-x'
import reactDom from 'eslint-plugin-react-dom'

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...
      // Enable lint rules for React
      reactX.configs['recommended-typescript'],
      // Enable lint rules for React DOM
      reactDom.configs.recommended,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

# Ticket Editor - Refactored Architecture

## РћР±Р·РѕСЂ

РџСЂРѕРµРєС‚ РїРµСЂРµСЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РЅ СЃ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°РЅРёРµРј **Clean Architecture** Рё **SOLID** РїСЂРёРЅС†РёРїРѕРІ.

## РђСЂС…РёС‚РµРєС‚СѓСЂР°

```
src/
в”њв”Ђв”Ђ domain/           # Domain Layer - Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃ-Р»РѕРіРёРєР°
в”‚   в”њв”Ђв”Ђ entities/     # РЎСѓС‰РЅРѕСЃС‚Рё (Slide, Element)
в”‚   в”њв”Ђв”Ђ repositories/ # РРЅС‚РµСЂС„РµР№СЃС‹ СЂРµРїРѕР·РёС‚РѕСЂРёРµРІ
в”‚   в””в”Ђв”Ђ events/       # Event Bus РґР»СЏ РєРѕРјРјСѓРЅРёРєР°С†РёРё
в”‚
в”њв”Ђв”Ђ application/      # Application Layer - use cases
в”‚   в”њв”Ђв”Ђ ports/        # РРЅС‚РµСЂС„РµР№СЃС‹ РґР»СЏ РёРЅС„СЂР°СЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂС‹
в”‚   в””в”Ђв”Ђ use-cases/    # РЎС†РµРЅР°СЂРёРё РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ
в”‚
в”њв”Ђв”Ђ infrastructure/   # Infrastructure Layer
в”‚   в””в”Ђв”Ђ store/        # Р РµР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ state management
в”‚
в”њв”Ђв”Ђ presentation/     # Presentation Layer
в”‚   в”њв”Ђв”Ђ components/   # React РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚С‹

в”‚   в””в”Ђв”Ђ providers/    # Context providers
в”‚

    в”њв”Ђв”Ђ types/        # TypeScript С‚РёРїС‹
    в”њв”Ђв”Ђ constants/    # РљРѕРЅСЃС‚Р°РЅС‚С‹
    в””в”Ђв”Ђ utils/        # РЈС‚РёР»РёС‚С‹
```

## РџСЂРёРЅС†РёРїС‹

### 1. SOLID

- **S**ingle Responsibility - РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ РєР»Р°СЃСЃ/РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚ РёРјРµРµС‚ РѕРґРЅСѓ РѕС‚РІРµС‚СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ
- **O**pen/Closed - СЂР°СЃС€РёСЂСЏРµРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ С‡РµСЂРµР· РЅР°СЃР»РµРґРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ/РєРѕРјРїРѕР·РёС†РёСЋ
- **L**iskov Substitution - РїРѕРґС‚РёРїС‹ Р·Р°РјРµРЅСЏСЋС‚ Р±Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Рµ С‚РёРїС‹
- **I**nterface Segregation - СѓР·РєРёРµ РёРЅС‚РµСЂС„РµР№СЃС‹
- **D**ependency Inversion - Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё РѕС‚ Р°Р±СЃС‚СЂР°РєС†РёР№

### 2. Clean Architecture

- **Domain** - РЅРµ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёС‚ РѕС‚ РґСЂСѓРіРёС… СЃР»РѕС‘РІ
- **Application** - Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РѕС‚ Domain
- **Infrastructure** - СЂРµР°Р»РёР·СѓРµС‚ РёРЅС‚РµСЂС„РµР№СЃС‹ Application
- **Presentation** - Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёС‚ РѕС‚ Application Рё Infrastructure

### 3. TypeScript Best Practices

- РЎС‚СЂРѕРіР°СЏ С‚РёРїРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ (no `any`)
- Discriminated unions РґР»СЏ С‚РёРїРѕРІ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ
- Type guards РґР»СЏ runtime РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРѕРє
- Readonly РґР»СЏ РёРјРјСѓС‚Р°Р±РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё

## РљР»СЋС‡РµРІС‹Рµ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ

### Р”Рѕ СЂРµС„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРёРЅРіР°:
- God object `useEditorFacade.ts` (488 СЃС‚СЂРѕРє)
- Context hell СЃ 8 РєРѕРЅС‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°РјРё
- РџРµСЂРµРіСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРЅС‹Рµ С…СѓРєРё
- РњРЅРѕРіРѕ `any` С‚РёРїРѕРІ

### РџРѕСЃР»Рµ СЂРµС„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРёРЅРіР°:
- Р Р°Р·РґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ РЅР° СЃР»РѕРё
- Р§РёСЃС‚С‹Рµ РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚С‹ СЃ РµРґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРѕР№ РѕС‚РІРµС‚СЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ
- РЎС‚СЂРѕРіР°СЏ С‚РёРїРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ
- Dependency Injection
- Event-driven architecture

FILE: scripts\audit-complexity.ps1
SIZE: 2976 bytes

<#
.SYNOPSIS
    Report complexity and max-params warnings from eslint.

.DESCRIPTION
    CQ-SIZE-04, CQ-SIZE-06. Prints files with the most warnings,
    sorted by count.

.EXAMPLE
    .\scripts\audit-complexity.ps1
#>

param(
    [string]$ProjectRoot = (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..'),
    [int]$TopFiles = 20
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$ProjectRoot = (Resolve-Path $ProjectRoot).Path

Push-Location $ProjectRoot
try {
    Write-Host "Running eslint (compact format)..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

    $rawLines = & npx eslint . --format compact 2>&1

    $issues = @()
    foreach ($line in $rawLines) {
        if ($line -match '^(.+?):\s*line (\d+),\s*col (\d+),\s*(Warning|Error)\s*-\s*(.+?)\s*\((.+?)\)\s*$') {
            $issues += [PSCustomObject]@{
                File     = $matches[1]
                Line     = [int]$matches[2]
                Severity = $matches[4]
                Message  = $matches[5].Trim()
                Rule     = $matches[6]
            }
        }
    }

    $complexityIssues = $issues | Where-Object { $_.Rule -eq 'complexity' }
    $paramsIssues     = $issues | Where-Object { $_.Rule -eq 'max-params' }

    $sep = [IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar
    $escapedRoot = [regex]::Escape($ProjectRoot + $sep)

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "=== COMPLEXITY (threshold 15) ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host ""

    $byFile = $complexityIssues | Group-Object File | Sort-Object Count -Descending

    foreach ($group in ($byFile | Select-Object -First $TopFiles)) {
        $rel = $group.Name -replace $escapedRoot, ''
        Write-Host ("  {0} ({1})" -f $rel, $group.Count) -ForegroundColor Yellow
        foreach ($item in ($group.Group | Sort-Object Line)) {
            $complexity = if ($item.Message -match 'complexity of (\d+)') { $matches[1] } else { '?' }
            Write-Host ("    line {0} - complexity {1}" -f $item.Line, $complexity) -ForegroundColor Gray
        }
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "=== MAX-PARAMS (threshold 4) ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host ""

    $byParams = $paramsIssues | Group-Object File | Sort-Object Count -Descending

    foreach ($group in ($byParams | Select-Object -First $TopFiles)) {
        $rel = $group.Name -replace $escapedRoot, ''
        Write-Host ("  {0} ({1})" -f $rel, $group.Count) -ForegroundColor Yellow
        foreach ($item in ($group.Group | Sort-Object Line)) {
            $params = if ($item.Message -match 'too many parameters \((\d+)\)') { $matches[1] } else { '?' }
            Write-Host ("    line {0} - params {1}" -f $item.Line, $params) -ForegroundColor Gray
        }
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "=== SUMMARY ===" -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host ("complexity: {0}" -f $complexityIssues.Count) -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host ("max-params: {0}" -f $paramsIssues.Count) -ForegroundColor Gray
} finally {
    Pop-Location
}

FILE: scripts\check-all.ps1
SIZE: 3902 bytes

<#
.SYNOPSIS
    Full project check: typecheck + typecheck:test + eslint + dep-cruiser + vitest.
.DESCRIPTION
    Returns exit code 1 if anything fails.
.EXAMPLE
    .\scripts\check-all.ps1
#>

param(
    [string]$ProjectRoot = (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..')
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$ProjectRoot = (Resolve-Path $ProjectRoot).Path

Push-Location $ProjectRoot
try {
    $failed = @()
    $sw = [System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()

    # 1. typecheck (src)
    Write-Host "[1/5] typecheck src..." -ForegroundColor Cyan -NoNewline
    $tscOut = & cmd /c "npx tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit 2>&1"
    $tscCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    if ($tscCode -ne 0) {
        Write-Host " FAILED" -ForegroundColor Red
        Write-Host ($tscOut -join "`n")
        $failed += 'typecheck'
    } else {
        Write-Host " ok" -ForegroundColor Green
    }

    # 2. typecheck (tests)
    Write-Host "[2/5] typecheck tests..." -ForegroundColor Cyan -NoNewline
    if (Test-Path 'tsconfig.test.json') {
        $tscTestOut = & cmd /c "npx tsc -p tsconfig.test.json --noEmit 2>&1"
        $tscTestCode = $LASTEXITCODE
        if ($tscTestCode -ne 0) {
            Write-Host " FAILED" -ForegroundColor Red
            Write-Host ($tscTestOut -join "`n")
            $failed += 'typecheck:test'
        } else {
            Write-Host " ok" -ForegroundColor Green
        }
    } else {
        Write-Host " skip (no tsconfig.test.json)" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    }

    # 3. eslint
    Write-Host "[3/5] eslint..." -ForegroundColor Cyan -NoNewline
    $lintOut = & cmd /c "npx eslint . 2>&1"
    $lintCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    if ($lintCode -ne 0) {
        Write-Host " FAILED" -ForegroundColor Red
        Write-Host ($lintOut -join "`n")
        $failed += 'eslint'
    } else {
        $warnCount = ($lintOut -join "`n" | Select-String -Pattern 'warning' -AllMatches).Matches.Count
        Write-Host " ok ($warnCount warnings)" -ForegroundColor Green
    }

    # 4. dep-cruiser
    Write-Host "[4/5] dep-cruiser..." -ForegroundColor Cyan -NoNewline
    if (Test-Path '.dependency-cruiser.cjs') {
        $archOut = & cmd /c "npx depcruise --config .dependency-cruiser.cjs src 2>&1"
        $archCode = $LASTEXITCODE
        if ($archCode -ne 0) {
            Write-Host " FAILED" -ForegroundColor Red
            Write-Host ($archOut -join "`n")
            $failed += 'depcruise'
        } else {
            Write-Host " ok" -ForegroundColor Green
        }
    } else {
        Write-Host " skip (no config)" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    }

    # 5. vitest
    Write-Host "[5/5] vitest..." -ForegroundColor Cyan -NoNewline
    if (Test-Path 'vitest.config.ts') {
        $testOut = & cmd /c "npx vitest run --no-color 2>&1"
        $testCode = $LASTEXITCODE
        if ($testCode -ne 0) {
            Write-Host " FAILED" -ForegroundColor Red
            # РџРµС‡Р°С‚Р°РµРј Р’РЎРЃ С‡С‚Рѕ РµСЃС‚СЊ вЂ” РЅРµ РѕР±СЂРµР·Р°РµРј, РЅРµ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРїСЂРµС‚РёСЂСѓРµРј.
            Write-Host ($testOut -join "`n")
            $failed += 'vitest'
        } else {
            $summary = ($testOut -join "`n" -split "`n" | Where-Object { $_ -match 'Tests\s+\d+' } | Select-Object -First 1)
            Write-Host " ok" -ForegroundColor Green
            if ($summary) { Write-Host "  $($summary.Trim())" -ForegroundColor DarkGray }
        }
    } else {
        Write-Host " skip (no vitest config)" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    }

    $sw.Stop()
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host ("Elapsed: {0:N1}s" -f $sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds) -ForegroundColor DarkGray

    if ($failed.Count -eq 0) {
        Write-Host "ALL PASSED" -ForegroundColor Green
        exit 0
    } else {
        Write-Host ("FAILED: {0}" -f ($failed -join ', ')) -ForegroundColor Red
        exit 1
    }
} finally {
    Pop-Location
}

<#
.SYNOPSIS
    Enable noUncheckedIndexedAccess in tsconfig.json and generate a report.

.DESCRIPTION
    CQ-TS-02. After running, you need to manually fix places where
    array/object access can be undefined.

    Rollback: copy tsconfig.json.bak back.

.PARAMETER ProjectRoot
    Project root folder.

.PARAMETER Force
    Do not ask for confirmation.

.EXAMPLE
    .\scripts\enable-strict-index.ps1
    .\scripts\enable-strict-index.ps1 -Force
#>

param(
    [string]$ProjectRoot = (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..'),
    [switch]$Force
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$ProjectRoot = (Resolve-Path $ProjectRoot).Path
$tsconfigPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot 'tsconfig.json'
$backupPath = "$tsconfigPath.bak"

if (-not (Test-Path $tsconfigPath)) {
    Write-Error "tsconfig.json not found: $tsconfigPath"
    exit 1
}

$content = Get-Content -LiteralPath $tsconfigPath -Raw -Encoding UTF8

if ($content -match '"noUncheckedIndexedAccess"\s*:\s*true') {
    Write-Host "noUncheckedIndexedAccess already enabled." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host "Running tsc for report..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
} else {
    if (-not $Force) {
        Write-Host "Flag will be added to $tsconfigPath" -ForegroundColor Yellow
        Write-Host "This may produce 300+ errors - they go to a report file."
        Write-Host "Continue? (y/N): " -NoNewline
        $answer = Read-Host
        if ($answer -ne 'y' -and $answer -ne 'Y') {
            Write-Host "Cancelled." -ForegroundColor Red
            exit 0
        }
    }

    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $tsconfigPath -Destination $backupPath -Force
    Write-Host "Backup: $backupPath" -ForegroundColor DarkGray

    if ($content -match '"noUncheckedIndexedAccess"\s*:\s*(true|false)') {
        $content = $content -replace '"noUncheckedIndexedAccess"\s*:\s*(true|false)', '"noUncheckedIndexedAccess": true'
    } else {
        $content = $content -replace '("strict"\s*:\s*true,)', "`$1`r`n    `"noUncheckedIndexedAccess`": true,"
    }

    Set-Content -LiteralPath $tsconfigPath -Value $content -NoNewline -Encoding UTF8
    Write-Host "Flag enabled." -ForegroundColor Green
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Running tsc..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
Push-Location $ProjectRoot
try {
    $output = & npx tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit 2>&1 | Out-String
    $reportPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot 'tsc-strict-index-report.txt'
    $output | Set-Content -Path $reportPath -Encoding UTF8

    $errorCount = ([regex]::Matches($output, 'error TS\d+')).Count
    $fileCount = ([regex]::Matches($output, '(?m)^src[\\/][^:]+\.tsx?:')).Count

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "=== RESULT ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    $errColor = if ($errorCount -gt 0) { 'Red' } else { 'Green' }
    $fileColor = if ($fileCount -gt 0) { 'Yellow' } else { 'Green' }
    Write-Host ("Errors:  {0}" -f $errorCount) -ForegroundColor $errColor
    Write-Host ("Files:   {0}" -f $fileCount) -ForegroundColor $fileColor
    Write-Host ("Report:  {0}" -f $reportPath) -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Rollback: Copy-Item '$backupPath' '$tsconfigPath' -Force" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
} finally {
    Pop-Location
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * РџРµСЂРµРєРѕРґРёСЂСѓРµС‚ РІСЃРµ .ts/.tsx/.css/.json/.md РІ UTF-8 РёР· Windows-1251.
 * РџСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµС‚ С„Р°Р№Р»С‹, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ СѓР¶Рµ РІ UTF-8 (СЌРІСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєР° РїРѕ РЅРµРІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Рј
 * РґР»СЏ cp1251 РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЏРј). РРґРµРјРїРѕС‚РµРЅС‚РЅРѕ.
 *
 * Р—Р°РїСѓСЃРє:  node scripts/fix-encoding.mjs [--dry]
 */
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, extname } from 'node:path';

const ROOTS = ['src', 'public'];
const EXTS = new Set(['.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.css', '.json', '.md', '.html']);
const SKIP_DIRS = new Set(['node_modules', 'dist', '.git', 'coverage']);
const DRY = process.argv.includes('--dry');

/** true, РµСЃР»Рё Р±Р°Р№С‚С‹ РІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹ РєР°Рє UTF-8 */
function isValidUtf8(buf) {
  try { new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(buf); return true; }
  catch { return false; }
}

/** true, РµСЃР»Рё РІ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Рµ РµСЃС‚СЊ С‚РёРїРёС‡РЅС‹Рµ mojibake-РјР°СЂРєРµСЂС‹ */
function looksLikeCp1251AsUtf8(text) {
  return /Р [Р†Р…РЎ]|РІР‚|РЎР‚РЎСџ|Р Вµ|РЎР‚Р Вµ/.test(text);
}

function processFile(path) {
  const buf = readFileSync(path);
  const utf8Ok = isValidUtf8(buf);

  if (utf8Ok) {
    const txt = buf.toString('utf-8');
    if (!looksLikeCp1251AsUtf8(txt)) return false;   // СѓР¶Рµ С‡РёСЃС‚Рѕ
    // СѓР¶Рµ utf-8, РЅРѕ СЃ Р±РёС‚С‹РјРё СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Р°РјРё вЂ” РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° В«РґРІРѕР№РЅРѕРіРѕВ» РґРµРєРѕРґР°
    const repaired = Buffer.from(txt, 'latin1').toString('utf-8');
    if (!/пїЅ/.test(repaired)) {
      if (!DRY) writeFileSync(path, repaired, 'utf-8');
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  // С‡РёС‚Р°РµРј РєР°Рє cp1251 в†’ РїРёС€РµРј РєР°Рє utf-8
  const decoded = new TextDecoder('windows-1251').decode(buf);
  if (!DRY) writeFileSync(path, decoded, 'utf-8');
  return true;
}

function walk(dir, hits = []) {
  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {
    if (SKIP_DIRS.has(entry)) continue;
    const full = join(dir, entry);
    const st = statSync(full);
    if (st.isDirectory()) walk(full, hits);
    else if (EXTS.has(extname(entry)) && processFile(full)) hits.push(full);
  }
  return hits;
}

const touched = [];
for (const root of ROOTS) {
  try { walk(root, touched); } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

console.log(DRY ? '[dry]' : '[fix-encoding]', 'files:', touched.length);
for (const f of touched) console.log('  вЂў', f);

# scripts/

Utilities for refactoring and checks.

All scripts are pure ASCII - no encoding issues in PowerShell 5.1 or 7+.

## Apply order

1. `refactor-imports.ps1 -DryRun` - preview changes
2. `refactor-imports.ps1` - apply (creates backup)
3. `check-all.ps1` - full check
4. `audit-complexity.ps1` - remaining warnings
5. `enable-strict-index.ps1` - enable noUncheckedIndexedAccess, collect report

## Scripts

| Script | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `refactor-imports.ps1` | `from '../../shared/X'` -> `from '@/shared/X'` |
| `enable-strict-index.ps1` | enable strict flag, produce `tsc-strict-index-report.txt` |
| `audit-complexity.ps1` | list files with complexity > 15 and max-params > 4 |
| `check-all.ps1` | typecheck + typecheck:test + eslint + depcruise + vitest |

FILE: scripts\refactor-imports.ps1
SIZE: 4082 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    Replace relative imports with path aliases.

.DESCRIPTION
    Replaces 'from "../../shared/X"' with 'from "@/shared/X"'.
    Covers shared, domain, application, infrastructure, lib, presentation.
    Creates a backup of every changed file.

    CQ-IMP-01.

.PARAMETER RootPath
    Root folder to scan. Default: 'src/'.

.PARAMETER DryRun
    Show what would change, do not modify files.

.EXAMPLE
    .\scripts\refactor-imports.ps1 -DryRun
    .\scripts\refactor-imports.ps1
#>

param(
    [string]$RootPath = (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..\src'),
    [switch]$DryRun,
    [switch]$Verbose
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$RootPath = (Resolve-Path $RootPath).Path

$aliasMap = [ordered]@{
    'shared'         = '@/shared'
    'domain'         = '@/domain'
    'application'    = '@/application'
    'infrastructure' = '@/infra'
    'lib'            = '@/lib'
    'presentation'   = '@/ui'
}

$extensions = @('.ts', '.tsx', '.mts', '.cts')
$backupDir = Join-Path $RootPath '.imports-backup'

if (-not $DryRun -and -not (Test-Path $backupDir)) {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $backupDir | Out-Null
}

$script:changedFiles = @()
$script:totalReplacements = 0

function Convert-Imports {
    param([string]$FilePath)

    $content = Get-Content -LiteralPath $FilePath -Raw -Encoding UTF8
    if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($content)) { return 0 }

    $original = $content
    $localReplacements = 0

    foreach ($folder in $aliasMap.Keys) {
        $alias = $aliasMap[$folder]

        $patternFrom = "from '(?:\.\./)+$folder(/|')"
        $replacementFrom = "from '$alias`$1"
        $matches = [regex]::Matches($content, $patternFrom)
        if ($matches.Count -gt 0) {
            $content = [regex]::Replace($content, $patternFrom, $replacementFrom)
            $localReplacements += $matches.Count
        }

        $patternDyn = "import\('(?:\.\./)+$folder(/|')"
        $replacementDyn = "import('$alias`$1"
        $matchesDyn = [regex]::Matches($content, $patternDyn)
        if ($matchesDyn.Count -gt 0) {
            $content = [regex]::Replace($content, $patternDyn, $replacementDyn)
            $localReplacements += $matchesDyn.Count
        }
    }

    if ($content -ne $original) {
        $script:changedFiles += $FilePath
        $script:totalReplacements += $localReplacements

        if ($Verbose) {
            Write-Host ("  {0} (+{1})" -f $FilePath, $localReplacements) -ForegroundColor Yellow
        }

        if (-not $DryRun) {
            $rel = $FilePath.Substring($RootPath.Length).TrimStart('\', '/')
            $backup = Join-Path $backupDir ($rel -replace '[\\/:]', '__')
            Copy-Item -LiteralPath $FilePath -Destination $backup -Force
            Set-Content -LiteralPath $FilePath -Value $content -NoNewline -Encoding UTF8
        }
    }

    return $localReplacements
}

Write-Host "Scan: $RootPath" -ForegroundColor Cyan
if ($DryRun) { Write-Host "DRY RUN" -ForegroundColor Yellow }

$files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $RootPath -Recurse -File |
    Where-Object {
        $extensions -contains $_.Extension.ToLower() -and
        $_.FullName -notmatch '\\node_modules\\'
    }

Write-Host "Files: $($files.Count)" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

foreach ($file in $files) {
    Convert-Imports -FilePath $file.FullName | Out-Null
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "=== TOTAL ===" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Changed files: $($script:changedFiles.Count)" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Replacements:  $($script:totalReplacements)" -ForegroundColor Green

if ($DryRun) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "DRY RUN - nothing changed." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host "Run without -DryRun to apply." -ForegroundColor Yellow
} else {
    if ($script:changedFiles.Count -gt 0) {
        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host "Backup: $backupDir" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
    }
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Next: .\scripts\check-all.ps1" -ForegroundColor Cyan
}

/* ============================================================
   domain/slide/hitTest.ts

   ARC-LAYER-02: домен не знает про React/Konva.
   Чистые функции hit-testing по bbox. Без side effects.
   ============================================================ */

import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';

export interface HitTestOptions {
  /** Типы, которые не участвуют в hit-test (по умолчанию: connector, line, fill). */
  ignoreTypes?: string[];
  /** Учитывать заблокированные элементы? */
  includeLocked?: boolean;
}

const DEFAULT_IGNORE = ['connector', 'line', 'fill'];

/**
 * Ищет верхний элемент под точкой (x, y) в мировых координатах.
 * Возвращает null, если пусто.
 */
export function findElementAt(
  elements: CanvasElement[],
  x: number,
  y: number,
  options: HitTestOptions = {},
): CanvasElement | null {
  const ignore = new Set(options.ignoreTypes ?? DEFAULT_IGNORE);
  for (let i = elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = elements[i];
    if (ignore.has(el.type)) continue;
    if (el.locked && !options.includeLocked) continue;
    if (x >= el.x && x <= el.x + el.width && y >= el.y && y <= el.y + el.height) {
      return el;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Для таблицы — находит конкретную ячейку под точкой (мировые координаты).
 */
export function findTableCellAt(
  el: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number; rows: number; cols: number },
  x: number,
  y: number,
): { row: number; col: number } | null {
  const lx = x - el.x;
  const ly = y - el.y;
  if (lx < 0 || ly < 0 || lx > el.width || ly > el.height) return null;
  const cellW = el.width / el.cols;
  const cellH = el.height / el.rows;
  const col = Math.max(0, Math.min(el.cols - 1, Math.floor(lx / cellW)));
  const row = Math.max(0, Math.min(el.rows - 1, Math.floor(ly / cellH)));
  return { row, col };
}

FILE: src\domain\slide\operations.ts
SIZE: 3584 bytes

/* ============================================================
   domain/slide/operations.ts

   Единственный источник логики Slide. Чистые функции,
   работают с mutable-типами (см. shared/types). Не мутируют вход.

   ARC-LAYER-02, REG-S6.
   ============================================================ */

import type { Slide, CanvasElement, DocFormat, Orientation, Padding } from '@/shared/types';
import { DEFAULT_PADDING } from '@/shared/constants';
import { getFormatSize } from '@/shared/utils/formats';
import { generateId } from '@/shared/utils/id';

export { getFormatSize };

export function createSlide(
  n: number,
  format: DocFormat = 'a4',
  orientation: Orientation = 'portrait',
  customSize?: { w: number; h: number },
  padding: Padding = { ...DEFAULT_PADDING },
): Slide {
  const { w, h } = getFormatSize(format, orientation, customSize);
  return {
    id: generateId(),
    name: `Слайд ${n}`,
    format, orientation,
    width: w, height: h,
    background: '#ffffff',
    padding: { ...padding },
    elements: [],
  };
}

export function cloneSlide(source: Slide): Slide {
  return {
    ...source,
    id: generateId(),
    name: `${source.name} (копия)`,
    padding: { ...source.padding },
    elements: source.elements.map((el) => {
      const copy = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
      copy.id = generateId();
      return copy;
    }),
  };
}

export function reflowElements(
  elements: CanvasElement[],
  newWidth: number,
  newHeight: number,
  padding: Padding,
): CanvasElement[] {
  const printW = Math.max(0, newWidth - padding.left - padding.right);
  const printH = Math.max(0, newHeight - padding.top - padding.bottom);

  return elements.map((el) => {
    if (el.type === 'footer') {
      return { ...el, x: padding.left, y: newHeight - padding.bottom - el.height, width: printW };
    }
    if (el.type === 'placeholder') {
      return { ...el, x: padding.left, y: padding.top, width: printW, height: printH };
    }
    return {
      ...el,
      x: Math.min(el.x, Math.max(0, newWidth - el.width)),
      y: Math.min(el.y, Math.max(0, newHeight - el.height)),
      width: Math.min(el.width, printW),
    };
  });
}

export function changeFormat(
  slide: Slide,
  format: DocFormat,
  customSize?: { w: number; h: number },
): Slide {
  const { w, h } = getFormatSize(format, slide.orientation, customSize);
  return {
    ...slide,
    format, width: w, height: h,
    elements: reflowElements(slide.elements, w, h, slide.padding),
  };
}

export function changeOrientation(slide: Slide, orientation: Orientation): Slide {
  if (slide.orientation === orientation) return slide;
  const customSize = slide.format === 'custom' ? { w: slide.height, h: slide.width } : undefined;
  const { w, h } = getFormatSize(slide.format, orientation, customSize);
  return {
    ...slide,
    orientation, width: w, height: h,
    elements: reflowElements(slide.elements, w, h, slide.padding),
  };
}

export function moveElementLayer(slide: Slide, elementId: string, direction: 1 | -1): Slide {
  const idx = slide.elements.findIndex((e) => e.id === elementId);
  if (idx < 0) return slide;
  const next = idx + direction;
  if (next < 0 || next >= slide.elements.length) return slide;
  const elements = slide.elements.slice();
  [elements[idx], elements[next]] = [elements[next], elements[idx]];
  return { ...slide, elements };
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\infrastructure\state\DocumentStore.ts
SIZE: 9334 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/* ============================================================
   infrastructure/state/DocumentStore.ts
   F-H-01..07, I-05, I-13, F-DATA-07, F-DATA-11.
   NFR-MEM-02, REG-P6: лимит истории и по кол-ву, и по байтам.
   ============================================================ */

import { produce, type Draft } from 'immer';
import { SimpleStore } from '@/shared/store/store';
import type {
  Slide, DataRoot, DataField, PlaceholderElement,
} from '@/shared/types';
import { createSlide } from '@/domain/slide/operations';
import { generateId } from '@/shared/utils/id';

export interface DocumentState {
  slides: Slide[];
  activeId: string;
  data: DataRoot[];
  historyVersion: number;
}

export type Mutator = (draft: Draft<DocumentState>) => void;

interface HistoryEntry {
  state: DocumentState;
  bytes: number;
  mergeKey?: string;
  timestamp: number;
}

const MAX_HISTORY = 100;
const MAX_HISTORY_BYTES = 80 * 1024 * 1024;
const MERGE_TIMEOUT_MS = 600;

/**
 * F-DATA-11: демонстрационные данные для быстрого старта.
 */
function createDefaultData(): DataRoot[] {
  return [
    {
      id: generateId(),
      name: 'event',
      fields: [
        { id: generateId(), name: 'title',    type: 'string', sample: 'ГАЛА-КОНЦЕРТ' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'subtitle', type: 'string', sample: 'Симфонический оркестр' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'day',      type: 'string', sample: '15' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'month',    type: 'string', sample: 'МАРТА' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'time',     type: 'string', sample: '19:00' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'venue',    type: 'string', sample: 'Большой театр' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'address',  type: 'string', sample: 'Театральная пл., 1' },
      ],
    },
    {
      id: generateId(),
      name: 'ticket',
      fields: [
        { id: generateId(), name: 'row',    type: 'string', sample: '12' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'seat',   type: 'string', sample: '8' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'sector', type: 'string', sample: 'Партер' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'price',  type: 'string', sample: '4500 ₽' },
      ],
    },
    {
      id: generateId(),
      name: 'applicant',
      fields: [
        { id: generateId(), name: 'fio',     type: 'string', sample: 'Иванов Иван Иванович' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'address', type: 'string', sample: 'г. Москва, ул. Пушкина, д. 1' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'phone',   type: 'string', sample: '+7 (999) 123-45-67' },
      ],
    },
  ];
}

/**
 * Приблизительная оценка размера snapshot в байтах.
 * Не использует JSON.stringify на main thread.
 */
function estimateBytes(state: DocumentState): number {
  let total = 1024;
  for (const slide of state.slides) {
    total += 512;
    total += slide.elements.length * 512;
  }
  for (const root of state.data) {
    total += 128;
    const walkFields = (fields: DataField[]): number => {
      let n = 0;
      for (const f of fields) {
        n += 1;
        if (f.children && f.children.length) n += walkFields(f.children);
      }
      return n;
    };
    total += walkFields(root.fields) * 64;
  }
  return total;
}

/**
 * F-DATA-06, F-DATA-07: находит sample по пути.
 * Индексы `[N]` в path игнорируются (плейсхолдеры для массивов
 * всегда показывают sample одного «эталонного» элемента).
 */
function resolveSampleFromData(
  data: readonly DataRoot[],
  path: string,
): string | null {
  if (!path) return null;
  const clean = path.replace(/\[\d+\]/g, '');
  const parts = clean.split('.');
  const rootName = parts.shift();
  if (!rootName) return null;
  const root = data.find((d) => d.name === rootName);
  if (!root) return null;

  let fields: readonly DataField[] = root.fields;
  let current: DataField | null = null;
  for (const p of parts) {
    current = fields.find((f) => f.name === p) ?? null;
    if (!current) return null;
    fields = current.children ?? [];
  }
  return current ? current.sample : null;
}

export class DocumentStore extends SimpleStore<DocumentState> {
  private history: HistoryEntry[] = [];
  private historyIndex = -1;
  private historyBytes = 0;

  constructor() {
    const initial = createSlide(1);
    super({
      slides: [initial],
      activeId: initial.id,
      data: createDefaultData(),
      historyVersion: 0,
    });
    this.pushHistory();
  }

  /**
   * F-DATA-07: пересчитать placeholder.sample по актуальным данным.
   * Если поле в data удалено — sample очищается, PlaceholderEl
   * покажет placeholder-заглушку (F-PH-05).
   */
  private applySamplesFromData(draft: Draft<DocumentState>): void {
    for (const slide of draft.slides) {
      for (const el of slide.elements) {
        if (el.type !== 'placeholder') continue;
        const placeholder = el as Draft<PlaceholderElement>;
        if (!placeholder.fieldPath) continue;
        const sample =
          resolveSampleFromData(draft.data, placeholder.fieldPath) ?? '';
        if (sample !== placeholder.sample) {
          placeholder.sample = sample;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  commit(mutator: Mutator, mergeKey?: string): void {
    const prev = this.getState();
    const next = produce(prev, (draft) => {
      mutator(draft);
    });
    if (next === prev) return;

    /*
     * F-DATA-07: если data изменилась — синхронизируем
     * placeholder.sample в том же коммите. Один setState,
     * одна запись в истории, нет лишних ререндеров.
     */
    const final =
      next.data !== prev.data
        ? produce(next, (draft) => this.applySamplesFromData(draft))
        : next;

    this.setState(final);
    this.pushHistory(mergeKey);
  }

  setStateForDrag(mutator: Mutator): void {
    const prev = this.getState();
    const next = produce(prev, mutator);
    if (next === prev) return;
    this.setState(next);
  }

  commitSnapshot(): void {
    this.pushHistory();
  }

  /**
   * @deprecated Автоматически вызывается из commit().
   * Оставлен для случаев явной синхронизации после restore.
   */
  syncSamplesFromData(): void {
    const state = this.getState();
    const next = produce(state, (draft) => this.applySamplesFromData(draft));
    if (next !== state) this.setState(next);
  }

  private pushHistory(mergeKey?: string): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    const last = this.history[this.historyIndex];

    if (
      mergeKey &&
      last?.mergeKey === mergeKey &&
      now - last.timestamp < MERGE_TIMEOUT_MS
    ) {
      const state = this.getState();
      const bytes = estimateBytes(state);
      this.historyBytes += bytes - last.bytes;
      this.history[this.historyIndex] = {
        state,
        bytes,
        mergeKey,
        timestamp: now,
      };
      this.trimHistoryBytes();
      this.bumpHistoryVersion();
      return;
    }

    this.history = this.history.slice(0, this.historyIndex + 1);
    const state = this.getState();
    const bytes = estimateBytes(state);
    this.history.push({ state, bytes, mergeKey, timestamp: now });
    this.historyBytes += bytes;

    if (this.history.length > MAX_HISTORY) {
      const removed = this.history.shift();
      if (removed) this.historyBytes -= removed.bytes;
    } else {
      this.historyIndex++;
    }

    this.trimHistoryBytes();
    this.bumpHistoryVersion();
  }

  private trimHistoryBytes(): void {
    while (this.historyBytes > MAX_HISTORY_BYTES && this.history.length > 1) {
      const removed = this.history.shift();
      if (removed) this.historyBytes -= removed.bytes;
      if (this.historyIndex > 0) this.historyIndex--;
    }
  }

  private bumpHistoryVersion(): void {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      historyVersion: prev.historyVersion + 1,
    }));
  }

  undo(): void {
    if (this.historyIndex <= 0) return;
    this.historyIndex--;
    const prev = this.history[this.historyIndex].state;
    this.setState({
      ...prev,
      historyVersion: this.getState().historyVersion + 1,
    });
  }

  redo(): void {
    if (this.historyIndex >= this.history.length - 1) return;
    this.historyIndex++;
    const next = this.history[this.historyIndex].state;
    this.setState({
      ...next,
      historyVersion: this.getState().historyVersion + 1,
    });
  }

  get canUndo(): boolean {
    return this.historyIndex > 0;
  }

  get canRedo(): boolean {
    return this.historyIndex < this.history.length - 1;
  }
}

export function approximateDocumentBytes(state: DocumentState): number {
  return estimateBytes(state);
}

import type Konva from 'konva';

// РЈРјРµРЅСЊС€РёР»Рё СЃ 2 РґРѕ 1 (РІ 4 СЂР°Р·Р° РјРµРЅСЊС€Рµ РїРёРєСЃРµР»РµР№ РґР»СЏ РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєРё Рё СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂР°)
const FILL_SCALE = 1; 
const MIN_AREA = 100;

export interface FloodFillResult {
  dataUrl: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

function toDataUrl(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement): string {
  try {
    // WebP СЃ РїСЂРѕР·СЂР°С‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ РІРµСЃРёС‚ РІ 3-4 СЂР°Р·Р° РјРµРЅСЊС€Рµ PNG
    const url = canvas.toDataURL('image/webp', 0.85);
    if (url.startsWith('data:image/webp')) return url;
  } catch { /* fallback */ }
  return canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
}

export function rasterizeFloodFill(
  stage: Konva.Stage,
  layerName: string,
  clickX: number,
  clickY: number,
  slideWidth: number,
  slideHeight: number,
  color: string,
): FloodFillResult | null {
  const layer = stage.findOne('.' + layerName) as Konva.Layer | null;
  if (!layer) return null;

  const W = Math.round(slideWidth * FILL_SCALE);
  const H = Math.round(slideHeight * FILL_SCALE);
  if (W <= 0 || H <= 0) return null;

  const sx = Math.floor(clickX * FILL_SCALE);
  const sy = Math.floor(clickY * FILL_SCALE);
  if (sx < 0 || sy < 0 || sx >= W || sy >= H) return null;

  const fillsToHide = layer.find('.is-fill-element');
  const wasVisible = fillsToHide.map((n) => n.visible());
  fillsToHide.forEach((n) => n.visible(false));

  const oldScale = stage.scale();
  const oldPos = stage.position();
  stage.scale({ x: 1, y: 1 });
  stage.position({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  let layerCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
  try {
    layerCanvas = layer.toCanvas({
      x: 0, y: 0,
      width: slideWidth, height: slideHeight,
      pixelRatio: FILL_SCALE,
    });
  } catch {
    stage.scale(oldScale);
    stage.position(oldPos);
    fillsToHide.forEach((n, i) => n.visible(wasVisible[i]));
    return null;
  }

  stage.scale(oldScale);
  stage.position(oldPos);
  fillsToHide.forEach((n, i) => n.visible(wasVisible[i]));

  const offscreen = document.createElement('canvas');
  offscreen.width = W;
  offscreen.height = H;
  const ctx = offscreen.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  if (!ctx) return null;

  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.drawImage(layerCanvas, 0, 0, W, H);

  const img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, W, H);
  const data = img.data;

  // Р‘С‹СЃС‚СЂР°СЏ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°: СЃСѓРјРјР° РєР°РЅР°Р»РѕРІ РјРµРЅСЊС€Рµ 720 (3 * 240) => РїРёРєСЃРµР»СЊ С‚РµРјРЅС‹Р№
  const isDark = (p: number): boolean =>
    data[p] + data[p + 1] + data[p + 2] < 720;

  const seedIdx = sy * W + sx;
  const seedP = seedIdx * 4;
  if (isDark(seedP)) return null;

  const visited = new Uint8Array(W * H);
  const filled = new Uint8Array(W * H);
  const stack: number[] = [seedIdx];
  let count = 0;

  while (stack.length > 0) {
    const i = stack.pop()!;
    if (visited[i]) continue;
    visited[i] = 1;

    const p = i * 4;
    if (isDark(p)) continue;

    filled[i] = 1;
    count++;

    const x = i % W;
    const y = (i - x) / W;
    if (x > 0) stack.push(i - 1);
    if (x < W - 1) stack.push(i + 1);
    if (y > 0) stack.push(i - W);
    if (y < H - 1) stack.push(i + W);
  }

  if (count < MIN_AREA) return null;

  let minX = W, minY = H, maxX = -1, maxY = -1;
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    if (!filled[i]) continue;
    const x = i % W;
    const y = (i - x) / W;
    if (x < minX) minX = x;
    if (y < minY) minY = y;
    if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
    if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
  }

  const cropW = maxX - minX + 1;
  const cropH = maxY - minY + 1;
  if (cropW <= 0 || cropH <= 0) return null;

  const [cr, cg, cb] = hexToRgb(color);

  const resultCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  resultCanvas.width = cropW;
  resultCanvas.height = cropH;
  const rctx = resultCanvas.getContext('2d');
  if (!rctx) return null;

  const cropped = rctx.createImageData(cropW, cropH);
  for (let y = 0; y < cropH; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < cropW; x++) {
      const srcIdx = (minY + y) * W + (minX + x);
      if (!filled[srcIdx]) continue;
      const dp = (y * cropW + x) * 4;
      cropped.data[dp] = cr;
      cropped.data[dp + 1] = cg;
      cropped.data[dp + 2] = cb;
      cropped.data[dp + 3] = 255;
    }
  }
  rctx.putImageData(cropped, 0, 0);

  return {
    dataUrl: toDataUrl(resultCanvas),
    x: minX / FILL_SCALE,
    y: minY / FILL_SCALE,
    width: cropW / FILL_SCALE,
    height: cropH / FILL_SCALE,
  };
}

function hexToRgb(hex: string): [number, number, number] {
  const clean = hex.replace('#', '').trim();
  if (clean.length === 3) {
    return [
      parseInt(clean[0] + clean[0], 16),
      parseInt(clean[1] + clean[1], 16),
      parseInt(clean[2] + clean[2], 16),
    ];
  }
  if (clean.length === 6) {
    return [
      parseInt(clean.slice(0, 2), 16),
      parseInt(clean.slice(2, 4), 16),
      parseInt(clean.slice(4, 6), 16),
    ];
  }
  return [0, 0, 0];
}

// src/lib/connectors.ts
import type { CanvasElement, AnchorSide, ConnectorRouting } from '@/shared/types';
import { uid } from './uid';

export const ANCHOR_OFFSET = 8;
export const GHOST_MIN_GAP = 40;
export const GHOST_MIN_W = 80;
export const GHOST_MIN_H = 40;
export const GHOST_MAX_W = 320;
export const GHOST_MAX_H = 240;

export interface Rect {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/* ---------- anchors ---------- */

export function getAnchorPoint(
  el: CanvasElement,
  side: AnchorSide,
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const cx = el.x + el.width / 2;
  const cy = el.y + el.height / 2;
  const rot = ((el.rotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cos = Math.cos(rot);
  const sin = Math.sin(rot);

  const halfW = el.width / 2;
  const halfH = el.height / 2;

  let lx = 0;
  let ly = 0;
  if (side === 'top') {
    lx = 0;
    ly = -halfH - ANCHOR_OFFSET;
  } else if (side === 'right') {
    lx = halfW + ANCHOR_OFFSET;
    ly = 0;
  } else if (side === 'bottom') {
    lx = 0;
    ly = halfH + ANCHOR_OFFSET;
  } else {
    lx = -halfW - ANCHOR_OFFSET;
    ly = 0;
  }

  return {
    x: cx + lx * cos - ly * sin,
    y: cy + lx * sin + ly * cos,
  };
}

export function getAnchorDirection(side: AnchorSide): { dx: number; dy: number } {
  if (side === 'top') return { dx: 0, dy: -1 };
  if (side === 'right') return { dx: 1, dy: 0 };
  if (side === 'bottom') return { dx: 0, dy: 1 };
  return { dx: -1, dy: 0 };
}

export function oppositeAnchor(side: AnchorSide): AnchorSide {
  if (side === 'top') return 'bottom';
  if (side === 'bottom') return 'top';
  if (side === 'left') return 'right';
  return 'left';
}

/* ---------- routing ---------- */

function routeStraight(
  from: CanvasElement,
  fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
  to: CanvasElement,
  toAnchor: AnchorSide,
): number[] {
  const a = getAnchorPoint(from, fromAnchor);
  const b = getAnchorPoint(to, toAnchor);
  return [a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y];
}

function segmentCrossesRect(
  x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number,
  rect: Rect, padding = 0,
): boolean {
  const rx = rect.x - padding;
  const ry = rect.y - padding;
  const rw = rect.width + padding * 2;
  const rh = rect.height + padding * 2;

  const minX = Math.min(x1, x2);
  const maxX = Math.max(x1, x2);
  const minY = Math.min(y1, y2);
  const maxY = Math.max(y1, y2);

  if (maxX < rx || minX > rx + rw || maxY < ry || minY > ry + rh) return false;
  if (Math.abs(y1 - y2) < 1) {
    return y1 > ry && y1 < ry + rh && maxX > rx && minX < rx + rw;
  }
  if (Math.abs(x1 - x2) < 1) {
    return x1 > rx && x1 < rx + rw && maxY > ry && minY < ry + rh;
  }
  return false;
}

function routeOrthogonal(
  from: CanvasElement, fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
  to: CanvasElement, toAnchor: AnchorSide,
  obstacles: CanvasElement[],
): number[] {
  const a = getAnchorPoint(from, fromAnchor);
  const b = getAnchorPoint(to, toAnchor);

  if (Math.abs(a.x - b.x) < 3) return [a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y];
  if (Math.abs(a.y - b.y) < 3) return [a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y];

  const obs = obstacles.filter((o) => o.id !== from.id && o.id !== to.id);

  const isBlocked = (path: number[]): boolean => {
    for (let i = 0; i < path.length - 2; i += 2) {
      const x1 = path[i], y1 = path[i + 1];
      const x2 = path[i + 2], y2 = path[i + 3];
      for (const o of obs) {
        if (segmentCrossesRect(x1, y1, x2, y2, o, 2)) return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  };

  const isValidDir = (path: number[]): boolean => {
    if (path.length < 4) return true;
    const da = getAnchorDirection(fromAnchor);
    const dx1 = path[2] - path[0];
    const dy1 = path[3] - path[1];
    if (da.dx !== 0 && Math.sign(dx1) !== Math.sign(da.dx) && Math.abs(dx1) > 2) return false;
    if (da.dy !== 0 && Math.sign(dy1) !== Math.sign(da.dy) && Math.abs(dy1) > 2) return false;

    const db = getAnchorDirection(toAnchor);
    const n = path.length;
    const dx2 = path[n - 2] - path[n - 4];
    const dy2 = path[n - 1] - path[n - 3];
    if (db.dx !== 0 && Math.sign(dx2) !== -Math.sign(db.dx) && Math.abs(dx2) > 2) return false;
    if (db.dy !== 0 && Math.sign(dy2) !== -Math.sign(db.dy) && Math.abs(dy2) > 2) return false;
    return true;
  };

  const p1 = [a.x, a.y, b.x, a.y, b.x, b.y];
  const p2 = [a.x, a.y, a.x, b.y, b.x, b.y];

  if (!isBlocked(p1) && isValidDir(p1)) return p1;
  if (!isBlocked(p2) && isValidDir(p2)) return p2;

  const midX = (a.x + b.x) / 2;
  const p3 = [a.x, a.y, midX, a.y, midX, b.y, b.x, b.y];
  if (!isBlocked(p3) && isValidDir(p3)) return p3;

  const midY = (a.y + b.y) / 2;
  const p4 = [a.x, a.y, a.x, midY, b.x, midY, b.x, b.y];
  if (!isBlocked(p4) && isValidDir(p4)) return p4;

  if (obs.length === 0) return p1;

  const minY = Math.min(...obs.map((o) => o.y)) - 20;
  const maxY = Math.max(...obs.map((o) => o.y + o.height)) + 20;
  const minX = Math.min(...obs.map((o) => o.x)) - 20;
  const maxX = Math.max(...obs.map((o) => o.x + o.width)) + 20;

  const d1 = [a.x, a.y, a.x, minY, b.x, minY, b.x, b.y];
  if (!isBlocked(d1) && isValidDir(d1)) return d1;

  const d2 = [a.x, a.y, a.x, maxY, b.x, maxY, b.x, b.y];
  if (!isBlocked(d2) && isValidDir(d2)) return d2;

  const d3 = [a.x, a.y, minX, a.y, minX, b.y, b.x, b.y];
  if (!isBlocked(d3) && isValidDir(d3)) return d3;

  const d4 = [a.x, a.y, maxX, a.y, maxX, b.y, b.x, b.y];
  if (!isBlocked(d4) && isValidDir(d4)) return d4;

  return p1;
}

export function routeConnector(
  from: CanvasElement, fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
  to: CanvasElement, toAnchor: AnchorSide,
  routing: ConnectorRouting,
  obstacles: CanvasElement[] = [],
): number[] {
  if (routing === 'straight') return routeStraight(from, fromAnchor, to, toAnchor);
  return routeOrthogonal(from, fromAnchor, to, toAnchor, obstacles);
}

/* ---------- поиск ---------- */

export function findNearestAnchor(
  el: CanvasElement,
  px: number,
  py: number,
): { side: AnchorSide; distance: number } {
  const sides: AnchorSide[] = ['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left'];
  let best: { side: AnchorSide; distance: number } = { side: 'top', distance: Infinity };
  for (const side of sides) {
    const p = getAnchorPoint(el, side);
    const d = Math.hypot(p.x - px, p.y - py);
    if (d < best.distance) best = { side, distance: d };
  }
  return best;
}

export function findElementAt(
  elements: CanvasElement[],
  px: number,
  py: number,
  excludeIds: string[] = [],
): CanvasElement | null {
  for (let i = elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = elements[i];
    if (el.type === 'connector' || el.type === 'line' || el.type === 'fill') continue;
    if (excludeIds.includes(el.id) || el.locked) continue;
    if (px >= el.x && px <= el.x + el.width && py >= el.y && py <= el.y + el.height) {
      return el;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function findElementWithPadding(
  elements: CanvasElement[],
  px: number,
  py: number,
  padding: number,
  excludeIds: string[] = [],
): CanvasElement | null {
  for (let i = elements.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const el = elements[i];
    if (el.type === 'connector' || el.type === 'line' || el.type === 'fill') continue;
    if (excludeIds.includes(el.id) || el.locked) continue;
    if (
      px >= el.x - padding && px <= el.x + el.width + padding &&
      py >= el.y - padding && py <= el.y + el.height + padding
    ) {
      return el;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function resolveConnectorEnds(
  conn: { fromId: string; toId: string },
  elements: CanvasElement[],
): { from: CanvasElement | null; to: CanvasElement | null } {
  let from: CanvasElement | null = null;
  let to: CanvasElement | null = null;
  for (const el of elements) {
    if (el.id === conn.fromId) from = el;
    if (el.id === conn.toId) to = el;
    if (from && to) break;
  }
  return { from, to };
}

export function midpointOfPath(points: number[]): { x: number; y: number } {
  let totalLen = 0;
  const segs: Array<{ x1: number; y1: number; x2: number; y2: number; len: number }> = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < points.length - 2; i += 2) {
    const x1 = points[i], y1 = points[i + 1];
    const x2 = points[i + 2], y2 = points[i + 3];
    const len = Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1);
    segs.push({ x1, y1, x2, y2, len });
    totalLen += len;
  }
  let target = totalLen / 2;
  for (const s of segs) {
    if (target <= s.len) {
      const t = s.len === 0 ? 0 : target / s.len;
      return { x: s.x1 + (s.x2 - s.x1) * t, y: s.y1 + (s.y2 - s.y1) * t };
    }
    target -= s.len;
  }
  return { x: points[0], y: points[1] };
}

/* ---------- ghost / pull-to-create ---------- */

/**
 * FIX: сохраняем пропорции исходного элемента.
 * Раньше `Math.min(GHOST_MAX_W, source.width)` независимо от высоты
 * приводило к сплющиванию картинки.
 */
export function ghostSizeFor(source: CanvasElement): { w: number; h: number } {
  let w = source.width;
  let h = source.height;

  if (w > GHOST_MAX_W || h > GHOST_MAX_H) {
    const ratio = w / h;
    if (w > GHOST_MAX_W) {
      w = GHOST_MAX_W;
      h = Math.round(w / ratio);
    }
    if (h > GHOST_MAX_H) {
      h = GHOST_MAX_H;
      w = Math.round(h * ratio);
    }
  }

  return {
    w: Math.max(GHOST_MIN_W, Math.round(w)),
    h: Math.max(GHOST_MIN_H, Math.round(h)),
  };
}

export function computeGhostRect(
  source: CanvasElement,
  fromAnchor: AnchorSide,
  mouseX: number,
  mouseY: number,
): Rect {
  const { w, h } = ghostSizeFor(source);

  let left = mouseX - w / 2;
  let top = mouseY - h / 2;

  const srcLeft = source.x - GHOST_MIN_GAP;
  const srcRight = source.x + source.width + GHOST_MIN_GAP;
  const srcTop = source.y - GHOST_MIN_GAP;
  const srcBottom = source.y + source.height + GHOST_MIN_GAP;

  const overlapsX = left < srcRight && left + w > srcLeft;
  const overlapsY = top < srcBottom && top + h > srcTop;

  if (overlapsX && overlapsY) {
    const dir = getAnchorDirection(fromAnchor);
    if (dir.dx > 0) left = srcRight;
    else if (dir.dx < 0) left = srcLeft - w;
    else if (dir.dy > 0) top = srcBottom;
    else top = srcTop - h;
  }

  return { x: Math.round(left), y: Math.round(top), width: w, height: h };
}

export function nearestAnchorForSource(rect: Rect, source: CanvasElement): AnchorSide {
  const cx = rect.x + rect.width / 2;
  const cy = rect.y + rect.height / 2;
  const srcCx = source.x + source.width / 2;
  const srcCy = source.y + source.height / 2;
  const dx = srcCx - cx;
  const dy = srcCy - cy;
  if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) return dx > 0 ? 'right' : 'left';
  return dy > 0 ? 'bottom' : 'top';
}

export function cloneForConnector(rect: Rect, source: CanvasElement): CanvasElement {
  const newId = uid();
  const base = {
    id: newId,
    x: rect.x,
    y: rect.y,
    width: rect.width,
    height: rect.height,
    rotation: 0,
  };

  switch (source.type) {
    case 'shape':
      return { ...source, ...base, text: '' };
    case 'text':
      return { ...source, ...base, text: '', html: undefined };
    case 'image':
      return { ...source, ...base };
    case 'stamp':
      return { ...source, ...base };
    case 'table':
      return {
        ...source,
        ...base,
        data: source.data.map((row) => row.map(() => '')),
      };
    case 'footer':
      return { ...source, ...base, text: '' };
    case 'placeholder':
      return { ...source, ...base, sample: '' };
    default:
      return {
        type: 'shape',
        shape: 'roundRect',
        ...base,
        fill: '#eef2ff',
        stroke: '#6366f1',
        strokeWidth: 2,
        text: '',
        fontSize: 14,
        textFill: '#1e293b',
        radius: 8,
        textAlign: 'center',
        textVerticalAlign: 'middle',
        textPadding: { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 },
        textFontFamily: 'Arial',
        textLineHeight: 1.3,
        textFontStyle: 'normal',
        textDecoration: '',
      } as CanvasElement;
  }
}

export function download(content: string, name: string, mime: string): void {
  const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mime });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = name;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}
