/* ============================================================
   lib/stamps/fillRasterizer.ts
   F-RFILL-04, F-RFILL-12..13.
   NFR-MEM-03: LRU по байтам (32 MB) + по количеству (30).
   NFR-OBS-03: structured logs.
   ============================================================ */

import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import {
  rasterizeCompositeFills,
  type FillSpec,
  type FillError,
} from './rasterFill';
import type {
  FillWorkerRequest,
  FillWorkerResponse,
} from '../../workers/protocol';
import { logger } from '@/shared/logger';

const CACHE_LIMIT = 30;
const CACHE_MAX_BYTES = 32 * 1024 * 1024;

const ENVIRONMENT_REASONS: ReadonlySet<FillError> = new Set<FillError>([
  'no-canvas',
  'no-2d-context',
  'no-path2d',
  'serialize-failed',
]);

export interface FillCacheEntry {
  image: HTMLImageElement | null;
  error: FillError | null;
}

export interface FillRequest {
  key: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
}

type Listener = (key: string, entry: FillCacheEntry) => void;

function estimateEntryBytes(entry: FillCacheEntry): number {
  if (!entry.image) return 256;
  const w = entry.image.naturalWidth || entry.image.width || 256;
  const h = entry.image.naturalHeight || entry.image.height || 256;
  return w * h * 4;
}

class FillRasterizer {
  private worker: Worker | null = null;
  private workerBroken = false;

  private cache = new Map<string, FillCacheEntry>();
  private cacheBytes = 0;
  private inflight = new Set<string>();
  private seqByKey = new Map<number, FillRequest>();
  private listeners = new Set<Listener>();
  private nextSeq = 0;

  constructor() {
    this.createWorker();
  }

  private createWorker(): void {
    try {
      const worker = new Worker(
        new URL('../../workers/fillWorker.ts', import.meta.url),
        { type: 'module' },
      );

      worker.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<FillWorkerResponse>) => {
        void this.handleWorkerResponse(e.data);
      };

      worker.onerror = (err) => {
        this.handleWorkerFailure('worker crashed', err);
        try { worker.terminate(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        this.worker = null;
      };

      this.worker = worker;
    } catch (err) {
      this.handleWorkerFailure('worker unavailable', err);
      this.worker = null;
    }
  }

  private handleWorkerFailure(reason: string, err: unknown): void {
    logger.warn('fill.worker_failure', { reason, error: String(err) });
    this.workerBroken = true;

    const pending = Array.from(this.seqByKey.values());
    this.seqByKey.clear();
    this.inflight.clear();

    for (const req of pending) {
      this.inflight.add(req.key);
      void this.rasterizeOnMainThread(req);
    }
  }

  private async handleWorkerResponse(res: FillWorkerResponse): Promise<void> {
    const req = this.seqByKey.get(res.seq);
    if (!req) return;
    this.seqByKey.delete(res.seq);

    if (!res.ok) {
      if (ENVIRONMENT_REASONS.has(res.reason)) {
        logger.warn('fill.worker_env_failure', { reason: res.reason, key: req.key });
        await this.rasterizeOnMainThread(req);
        return;
      }
      logger.warn('fill.rejected', { reason: res.reason, key: req.key });
      this.store(req.key, { image: null, error: res.reason });
      return;
    }

    const image = await loadImage(res.blob);
    this.store(
      req.key,
      image
        ? { image, error: null }
        : { image: null, error: 'serialize-failed' },
    );
  }

  private async rasterizeOnMainThread(req: FillRequest): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const result = await rasterizeCompositeFills({
        width: req.width,
        height: req.height,
        shapes: req.shapes,
        fills: req.fills,
      });

      if (!result.ok) {
        logger.warn('fill.main_thread_rejected', { reason: result.reason, key: req.key });
        this.store(req.key, { image: null, error: result.reason });
        return;
      }

      const image = await loadImage(result.value);
      this.store(
        req.key,
        image
          ? { image, error: null }
          : { image: null, error: 'serialize-failed' },
      );
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error('fill.main_thread_crash', err, { key: req.key });
      this.store(req.key, { image: null, error: 'all-fills-rejected' });
    }
  }

  private store(key: string, entry: FillCacheEntry): void {
    const prev = this.cache.get(key);
    if (prev) this.cacheBytes -= estimateEntryBytes(prev);
    this.cache.delete(key);

    this.cache.set(key, entry);
    this.cacheBytes += estimateEntryBytes(entry);

    while (
      (this.cache.size > CACHE_LIMIT || this.cacheBytes > CACHE_MAX_BYTES) &&
      this.cache.size > 1
    ) {
      const oldestKey = this.cache.keys().next().value;
      if (oldestKey === undefined) break;
      const oldEntry = this.cache.get(oldestKey);
      if (oldEntry) this.cacheBytes -= estimateEntryBytes(oldEntry);
      this.cache.delete(oldestKey);
    }

    this.inflight.delete(key);
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener(key, entry);
  }

  subscribe(listener: Listener): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
  }

  get(key: string): FillCacheEntry | undefined {
    return this.cache.get(key);
  }

  isInflight(key: string): boolean {
    return this.inflight.has(key);
  }

  request(req: FillRequest): void {
    if (this.cache.has(req.key)) return;
    if (this.inflight.has(req.key)) return;

    this.inflight.add(req.key);

    if (this.worker && !this.workerBroken) {
      const seq = ++this.nextSeq;
      this.seqByKey.set(seq, req);

      const wire: FillWorkerRequest = {
        seq,
        width: req.width,
        height: req.height,
        shapes: req.shapes,
        fills: req.fills,
      };

      try {
        this.worker.postMessage(wire);
        return;
      } catch (err) {
        this.handleWorkerFailure('postMessage failed', err);
        this.seqByKey.delete(seq);
        try { this.worker?.terminate(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        this.worker = null;
      }
    }

    void this.rasterizeOnMainThread(req);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    try { this.worker?.terminate(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    this.worker = null;
    this.cache.clear();
    this.cacheBytes = 0;
    this.inflight.clear();
    this.seqByKey.clear();
    this.listeners.clear();
  }
}

function loadImage(blob: Blob): Promise<HTMLImageElement | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const img = new window.Image();
    img.onload = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); resolve(img); };
    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); resolve(null); };
    img.src = url;
  });
}

let singleton: FillRasterizer | null = null;

export function getFillRasterizer(): FillRasterizer {
  if (!singleton) singleton = new FillRasterizer();
  return singleton;
}

export function resetFillRasterizer(): void {
  singleton?.dispose();
  singleton = null;
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/stamps/geometry/arc.ts
   F-SGEO-01..03, F-STMP-N-09, F-STMP-N-05.
   Направление обхода дуги + точный старт по курсору.
   ============================================================ */

import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';

export const DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG = 320;
export const MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG = 20;
export const MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG = 350;
export const DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET = 6;

export type ArcDirection = 'cw' | 'ccw';

export interface ArcParams {
  arcCx: number;
  arcCy: number;
  arcRadius: number;
  arcRx?: number;
  arcRy?: number;
  arcStartDeg: number;
  arcEndDeg: number;
  arcFlip: boolean;
}

function arcRadiusFor(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  fontSize: number,
  isOutside: boolean,
  arcOffset: number,
): number {
  const baseR = Math.min(host.width, host.height) / 2;
  const safeOffset = Math.max(0, arcOffset);
  if (isOutside) return baseR + safeOffset;
  return Math.max(fontSize * 0.8, baseR - safeOffset);
}

function resolveSpanDeg(
  fontSize: number,
  textLength: number,
  radius: number,
  explicitSpan?: number,
): number {
  if (explicitSpan !== undefined && explicitSpan > 0) {
    return Math.min(MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, Math.max(MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, explicitSpan));
  }
  const charWidth = fontSize * 0.6;
  const textWidth = Math.max(charWidth, textLength * charWidth);
  const raw = (textWidth / Math.max(1, radius)) * (180 / Math.PI);
  return Math.min(MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, Math.max(MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, raw));
}

/**
 * Создание arc-текста «сверху» / «снизу» (используется фабриками).
 * direction применяется для form-параметра spanDeg.
 */
export function computeArcParams(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  fontSize: number,
  textLength: number,
  isTop: boolean,
  ringOffset = 0,
  arcOffset = DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
  arcSpanDeg = DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
  direction: ArcDirection = 'cw',
): ArcParams {
  const cx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const cy = host.y + host.height / 2;
  const radius = arcRadiusFor(host, fontSize, false, arcOffset) - ringOffset * 12;
  const span = resolveSpanDeg(fontSize, textLength, radius, arcSpanDeg);
  const half = span / 2;

  if (isTop) {
    if (direction === 'cw') {
      // верхняя дуга, по часовой (слева-направо над фигурой)
      return {
        arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
        arcStartDeg: -90 - half,
        arcEndDeg: -90 + half,
        arcFlip: false,
      };
    }
    // верхняя дуга, против часовой
    return {
      arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
      arcStartDeg: -90 + half,
      arcEndDeg: -90 - half,
      arcFlip: true,
    };
  }

  if (direction === 'cw') {
    return {
      arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
      arcStartDeg: 90 + half,
      arcEndDeg: 90 - half,
      arcFlip: true,
    };
  }
  return {
    arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
    arcStartDeg: 90 - half,
    arcEndDeg: 90 + half,
    arcFlip: false,
  };
}

/**
 * F-SGEO-02 / F-STMP-E-25: создать/обновить arc-текст по курсору.
 * `arcStartDeg` = ровно точка под курсором (с компенсацией на полсимвола,
 * чтобы первый символ не съезжал), `arcEndDeg = start ± span` в зависимости
 * от `direction`.
 *
 * В canvas-координатах (y вниз) рост угла — по часовой стрелке.
 *   cw:  endDeg > startDeg
 *   ccw: endDeg < startDeg
 */
export function computeArcParamsForAngle(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  angleRad: number,
  fontSize: number,
  isOutside: boolean,
  spanDeg: number,
  arcOffset: number = DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
  direction: ArcDirection = 'cw',
  charCount: number = 1,
): ArcParams {
  const cx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const cy = host.y + host.height / 2;
  const radius = arcRadiusFor(host, fontSize, isOutside, arcOffset);

  const anchorDeg = (angleRad * 180) / Math.PI;
  const clampedSpan = Math.min(MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, Math.max(MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, spanDeg));

  // Первый символ центрируется в anchorDeg:
  // startDeg сдвигаем на -halfChar (cw) или +halfChar (ccw).
  const charSpan = clampedSpan / Math.max(1, charCount);
  const halfChar = charSpan / 2;

  if (direction === 'cw') {
    const startDeg = anchorDeg - halfChar;
    return {
      arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
      arcStartDeg: startDeg,
      arcEndDeg: startDeg + clampedSpan,
      arcFlip: false,
    };
  }
  const startDeg = anchorDeg + halfChar;
  return {
    arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
    arcStartDeg: startDeg,
    arcEndDeg: startDeg - clampedSpan,
    arcFlip: true,
  };
}

export interface ArcBBox {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/** bbox для arc-текста (для hit-area / inline-editor). */
export function arcTextBBox(t: {
  text: string;
  arcCx: number; arcCy: number;
  arcRadius: number;
  arcRx?: number;
  arcRy?: number;
  arcStartDeg: number; arcEndDeg: number;
  fontSize: number;
}): ArcBBox {
  const rx = t.arcRx ?? t.arcRadius;
  const ry = t.arcRy ?? t.arcRadius;

  const chars = t.text.split('');
  if (chars.length === 0) {
    return { x: t.arcCx - 20, y: t.arcCy - 20, width: 40, height: 40 };
  }
  const start = (t.arcStartDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const end = (t.arcEndDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const step = chars.length > 1 ? (end - start) / (chars.length - 1) : 0;

  let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  chars.forEach((_, i) => {
    const a = start + i * step;
    const x = t.arcCx + Math.cos(a) * rx;
    const y = t.arcCy + Math.sin(a) * ry;
    if (x < minX) minX = x;
    if (y < minY) minY = y;
    if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
    if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
  });
  const pad = t.fontSize;
  return {
    x: minX - pad, y: minY - pad,
    width: (maxX - minX) + pad * 2,
    height: (maxY - minY) + pad * 2,
  };
}

import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';

export interface Edge {
  x1: number; y1: number;
  x2: number; y2: number;
  len: number;
  dirX: number; dirY: number;
}

export function makeEdge(ax: number, ay: number, bx: number, by: number): Edge {
  const dx = bx - ax;
  const dy = by - ay;
  const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  return { x1: ax, y1: ay, x2: bx, y2: by, len, dirX: dx / len, dirY: dy / len };
}

/** signedOffset > 0 — внутрь, < 0 — снаружи. */
export function buildRectEdges(w: number, h: number, signedOffset: number): Edge[] {
  const x1 = signedOffset, y1 = signedOffset;
  const x2 = w - signedOffset, y2 = h - signedOffset;
  return [
    makeEdge(x1, y1, x2, y1),
    makeEdge(x2, y1, x2, y2),
    makeEdge(x2, y2, x1, y2),
    makeEdge(x1, y2, x1, y1),
  ];
}

/** signedOffset > 0 — внутрь (к центроиду), < 0 — наружу. */
export function buildTriangleEdges(w: number, h: number, signedOffset: number): Edge[] {
  const cx = w / 2, cy = h / 2;
  const verts: [number, number][] = [[cx, 0], [w, h], [0, h]];
  const shrunk = verts.map(([x, y]) => {
    const dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    const k = (dist - signedOffset) / dist;
    return [cx + dx * k, cy + dy * k] as [number, number];
  });
  return [
    makeEdge(shrunk[0][0], shrunk[0][1], shrunk[1][0], shrunk[1][1]),
    makeEdge(shrunk[1][0], shrunk[1][1], shrunk[2][0], shrunk[2][1]),
    makeEdge(shrunk[2][0], shrunk[2][1], shrunk[0][0], shrunk[0][1]),
  ];
}

export function buildPerimeterEdges(s: StampShapeItem, signedOffset: number): Edge[] {
  if (s.kind === 'rect') return buildRectEdges(s.width, s.height, signedOffset);
  if (s.kind === 'triangle') return buildTriangleEdges(s.width, s.height, signedOffset);
  return [];
}

import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { buildTriangleEdges } from './edges';
import { isPointInTriangle, pointToSegmentDistance } from './math';

export function shapeContainsPoint(s: StampShapeItem, px: number, py: number): boolean {
  if (px < s.x || px > s.x + s.width) return false;
  if (py < s.y || py > s.y + s.height) return false;
  if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') {
    const cx = s.x + s.width / 2;
    const cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
    const rx = s.width / 2;
    const ry = s.height / 2;
    const dx = (px - cx) / Math.max(1e-6, rx);
    const dy = (py - cy) / Math.max(1e-6, ry);
    return dx * dx + dy * dy <= 1;
  }
  if (s.kind === 'triangle') {
    return isPointInTriangle(
      px - s.x, py - s.y,
      [s.width / 2, 0], [s.width, s.height], [0, s.height],
    );
  }
  return true;
}

export function distanceToPerimeter(s: StampShapeItem, px: number, py: number): number {
  const cx = s.x + s.width / 2;
  const cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
  const rx = s.width / 2;
  const ry = s.height / 2;
  const dx = px - cx;
  const dy = py - cy;

  if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') {
    const angle = Math.atan2(dy / Math.max(1e-6, ry), dx / Math.max(1e-6, rx));
    const ex = cx + rx * Math.cos(angle);
    const ey = cy + ry * Math.sin(angle);
    return Math.hypot(px - ex, py - ey);
  }
  if (s.kind === 'triangle') {
    const edges = buildTriangleEdges(s.width, s.height, 0);
    let best = Infinity;
    for (const e of edges) {
      const d = pointToSegmentDistance(px - s.x, py - s.y, e.x1, e.y1, e.x2, e.y2).distance;
      if (d < best) best = d;
    }
    return best;
  }
  return Math.min(
    Math.abs(px - s.x), Math.abs(px - (s.x + s.width)),
    Math.abs(py - s.y), Math.abs(py - (s.y + s.height)),
  );
}

export interface FindShapeResult {
  host: StampShapeItem;
  angleRad: number;
  isOutside: boolean;
}

export function findSnapTarget(
  px: number, py: number,
  excludeId: string,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
  maxDistance?: number,
): FindShapeResult | null {
  let best: StampShapeItem | null = null;
  let bestDist = Infinity;

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.id === excludeId || s.hidden) continue;
    if (s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') continue;
    if (s.width < 30 || s.height < 30) continue;

    const cx = s.x + s.width / 2;
    const cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
    const d = Math.hypot(px - cx, py - cy);
    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = s; }
  }

  if (!best) return null;

  const cx = best.x + best.width / 2;
  const cy = best.y + best.height / 2;
  const rHost = Math.min(best.width, best.height) / 2;
  if (maxDistance !== undefined && bestDist > rHost + maxDistance) return null;

  return {
    host: best,
    angleRad: Math.atan2(py - cy, px - cx),
    isOutside: !shapeContainsPoint(best, px, py),
  };
}

export function findNearestShapeByCursor(
  px: number, py: number,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
  maxDistance = 260,
): FindShapeResult | null {
  let best: StampShapeItem | null = null;
  let bestDist = Infinity;

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.hidden || s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') continue;
    if (s.width < 20 || s.height < 20) continue;

    const d = distanceToPerimeter(s, px, py);
    if (d > maxDistance) continue;
    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = s; }
  }

  if (!best) return null;
  const cx = best.x + best.width / 2;
  const cy = best.y + best.height / 2;
  return {
    host: best,
    angleRad: Math.atan2(py - cy, px - cx),
    isOutside: !shapeContainsPoint(best, px, py),
  };
}

export function findHostShape(
  px: number, py: number,
  excludeId: string, shapes: StampShapeItem[],
): StampShapeItem | null {
  const t = findSnapTarget(px, py, excludeId, shapes);
  return t ? t.host : null;
}

/** Самая маленькая по площади фигура, чей bbox содержит точку. */
export function findContainingShape(
  cx: number, cy: number,
  excludeId: string,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
): StampShapeItem | null {
  let best: StampShapeItem | null = null;
  let bestArea = Infinity;

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.id === excludeId || s.hidden) continue;
    if (s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') continue;
    if (cx < s.x || cx > s.x + s.width) continue;
    if (cy < s.y || cy > s.y + s.height) continue;

    const area = Math.max(1, s.width * s.height);
    if (area < bestArea) { bestArea = area; best = s; }
  }
  return best;
}

/**
 * Все фигуры (не line/fill), содержащие точку P.
 * Порядок — как в массиве shapes (он же z-order).
 */
export function findShapesContaining(
  px: number, py: number,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
): StampShapeItem[] {
  return shapes.filter((s) =>
    s.kind !== 'line' && s.kind !== 'fill'
    && !s.hidden
    && shapeContainsPoint(s, px, py),
  );
}

export function clamp(v: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v));
}

export function isPointInTriangle(
  px: number, py: number,
  a: [number, number], b: [number, number], c: [number, number],
): boolean {
  const sign = (p1: [number, number], p2: [number, number], p3: [number, number]) =>
    (p1[0] - p3[0]) * (p2[1] - p3[1]) - (p2[0] - p3[0]) * (p1[1] - p3[1]);
  const d1 = sign([px, py], a, b);
  const d2 = sign([px, py], b, c);
  const d3 = sign([px, py], c, a);
  const hasNeg = d1 < 0 || d2 < 0 || d3 < 0;
  const hasPos = d1 > 0 || d2 > 0 || d3 > 0;
  return !(hasNeg && hasPos);
}

export interface SegmentDistanceResult {
  distance: number;
  t: number;
  qx: number;
  qy: number;
}

export function pointToSegmentDistance(
  px: number, py: number,
  ax: number, ay: number, bx: number, by: number,
): SegmentDistanceResult {
  const ex = bx - ax;
  const ey = by - ay;
  const lenSq = ex * ex + ey * ey || 1;
  const t = clamp(((px - ax) * ex + (py - ay) * ey) / lenSq, 0, 1);
  const qx = ax + t * ex;
  const qy = ay + t * ey;
  return { distance: Math.hypot(px - qx, py - qy), t, qx, qy };
}

import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { buildRectEdges, buildTriangleEdges, type Edge } from './edges';
import { pointToSegmentDistance } from './math';

function pointOnTrianglePerimeter(
  w: number, h: number, signedOffset: number, angleRad: number,
): [number, number] {
  const edges = buildTriangleEdges(w, h, signedOffset);
  const cx = w / 2, cy = h / 2;
  const cos = Math.cos(angleRad), sin = Math.sin(angleRad);
  let bestT = Infinity;
  let bestPt: [number, number] = [cx, cy];
  for (const e of edges) {
    const ex = e.x2 - e.x1, ey = e.y2 - e.y1;
    const det = cos * (-ey) - (-ex) * sin;
    if (Math.abs(det) < 1e-9) continue;
    const b1 = e.x1 - cx, b2 = e.y1 - cy;
    const t = (b1 * (-ey) - (-ex) * b2) / det;
    const s = (cos * b2 - sin * b1) / det;
    if (t > 0 && s >= 0 && s <= 1 && t < bestT) {
      bestT = t;
      bestPt = [cx + t * cos, cy + t * sin];
    }
  }
  return bestPt;
}

export function pointOnHostPerimeter(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  signedOffset: number,
  angleRad: number,
): [number, number] {
  const cx = host.width / 2, cy = host.height / 2;

  if (host.kind === 'rect') {
    const dxHalf = Math.max(1, host.width / 2 - signedOffset);
    const dyHalf = Math.max(1, host.height / 2 - signedOffset);
    const cos = Math.cos(angleRad), sin = Math.sin(angleRad);
    const t = Math.min(
      dxHalf / Math.max(Math.abs(cos), 1e-9),
      dyHalf / Math.max(Math.abs(sin), 1e-9),
    );
    return [cx + cos * t, cy + sin * t];
  }
  if (host.kind === 'triangle') {
    return pointOnTrianglePerimeter(host.width, host.height, signedOffset, angleRad);
  }
  const r = Math.max(1, Math.min(host.width, host.height) / 2 - signedOffset);
  return [cx + Math.cos(angleRad) * r, cy + Math.sin(angleRad) * r];
}

export function closestPointOnHostPerimeter(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  signedOffset: number,
  px: number, py: number,
): [number, number] {
  const lx = px - host.x;
  const ly = py - host.y;

  if (host.kind === 'rect') {
    const x1 = signedOffset, y1 = signedOffset;
    const x2 = host.width - signedOffset, y2 = host.height - signedOffset;
    const edges: [number, number, number, number][] = [
      [x1, y1, x2, y1], [x2, y1, x2, y2],
      [x2, y2, x1, y2], [x1, y2, x1, y1],
    ];
    let best = Infinity;
    let bp: [number, number] = [x1, y1];
    for (const [ax, ay, bx, by] of edges) {
      const r = pointToSegmentDistance(lx, ly, ax, ay, bx, by);
      if (r.distance < best) { best = r.distance; bp = [r.qx, r.qy]; }
    }
    return [host.x + bp[0], host.y + bp[1]];
  }

  if (host.kind === 'triangle') {
    const edges = buildTriangleEdges(host.width, host.height, signedOffset);
    let best = Infinity;
    let bp: [number, number] = [0, 0];
    for (const e of edges) {
      const r = pointToSegmentDistance(lx, ly, e.x1, e.y1, e.x2, e.y2);
      if (r.distance < best) { best = r.distance; bp = [r.qx, r.qy]; }
    }
    return [host.x + bp[0], host.y + bp[1]];
  }

  const cx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const cy = host.y + host.height / 2;
  const r = Math.max(1, Math.min(host.width, host.height) / 2 - signedOffset);
  const angle = Math.atan2(py - cy, px - cx);
  return [cx + r * Math.cos(angle), cy + r * Math.sin(angle)];
}

export interface PerimeterTextOptions {
  host: StampShapeItem;
  arcOffset: number;
  isOutside: boolean;
  centerDeg: number;
  text: string;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  fill: string;
}

function findPerimeterPosition(edges: Edge[], tx: number, ty: number): number {
  let bestDist = Infinity;
  let bestPos = 0;
  let cum = 0;
  for (const e of edges) {
    const r = pointToSegmentDistance(tx, ty, e.x1, e.y1, e.x2, e.y2);
    if (r.distance < bestDist) {
      bestDist = r.distance;
      bestPos = cum + Math.hypot(r.qx - e.x1, r.qy - e.y1);
    }
    cum += e.len;
  }
  return bestPos;
}

function findEdgeAt(edges: Edge[], L: number): { edge: Edge | null; posOnEdge: number } {
  const total = edges.reduce((s, e) => s + e.len, 0);
  if (total <= 0) return { edge: null, posOnEdge: 0 };
  const wrapL = ((L % total) + total) % total;
  let cum = 0;
  for (const e of edges) {
    if (wrapL >= cum && wrapL < cum + e.len) return { edge: e, posOnEdge: wrapL - cum };
    cum += e.len;
  }
  const last = edges[edges.length - 1];
  return { edge: last, posOnEdge: last.len };
}

export function drawPerimeterText(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  opts: PerimeterTextOptions,
): void {
  const {
    host, arcOffset, isOutside, centerDeg,
    text, fontSize, fontFamily, fontStyle, fill,
  } = opts;
  if (!text) return;

  const fontParts: string[] = [];
  if (fontStyle.includes('italic')) fontParts.push('italic');
  if (fontStyle.includes('bold')) fontParts.push('bold');
  fontParts.push(`${fontSize}px`, `${fontFamily}, Arial, sans-serif`);

  ctx.save();
  ctx.font = fontParts.join(' ');
  ctx.fillStyle = fill;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';

  const signed = isOutside ? -Math.max(0, arcOffset) : Math.max(0, arcOffset);
  const edges = host.kind === 'rect'
    ? buildRectEdges(host.width, host.height, signed)
    : buildTriangleEdges(host.width, host.height, signed);

  if (edges.reduce((s, e) => s + e.len, 0) <= 1) { ctx.restore(); return; }

  const chars = Array.from(text);
  const widths = chars.map((ch) => Math.max(fontSize * 0.3, ctx.measureText(ch).width));
  const totalTextLen = widths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);

  const centerRad = (centerDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const [tx, ty] = pointOnHostPerimeter(host, signed, centerRad);
  const centerPos = findPerimeterPosition(edges, tx, ty);
  let cursor = centerPos - totalTextLen / 2;

  for (let i = 0; i < chars.length; i++) {
    const mid = cursor + widths[i] / 2;
    const { edge, posOnEdge } = findEdgeAt(edges, mid);
    if (edge) {
      const px = edge.x1 + edge.dirX * posOnEdge;
      const py = edge.y1 + edge.dirY * posOnEdge;
      const angle = Math.atan2(edge.dirY, edge.dirX);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(px, py);
      ctx.rotate(angle);
      ctx.fillText(chars[i], 0, 0);
      ctx.restore();
    }
    cursor += widths[i];
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';

export const SNAP_TO_GRID = 10;
export const SNAP_THRESHOLD = 6;
export const GRID_SNAP_DIST = 2;

export function snapPosition(
  x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number,
  excludeId: string, shapes: StampShapeItem[],
  canvasW: number, canvasH: number, enableSnap: boolean,
): { x: number; y: number } {
  if (!enableSnap) return { x: Math.round(x), y: Math.round(y) };

  let fx = x, fy = y;

  const gridX = Math.round(x / SNAP_TO_GRID) * SNAP_TO_GRID;
  const gridY = Math.round(y / SNAP_TO_GRID) * SNAP_TO_GRID;
  if (Math.abs(gridX - x) <= GRID_SNAP_DIST) fx = gridX;
  if (Math.abs(gridY - y) <= GRID_SNAP_DIST) fy = gridY;

  const cx = canvasW / 2, cy = canvasH / 2;
  if (Math.abs((fx + w / 2) - cx) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fx = cx - w / 2;
  if (Math.abs((fy + h / 2) - cy) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fy = cy - h / 2;

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.id === excludeId || s.hidden) continue;
    if (s.kind === 'line') continue;
    const pts = [
      { x: s.x, y: s.y },
      { x: s.x + s.width / 2, y: s.y + s.height / 2 },
      { x: s.x + s.width, y: s.y + s.height },
      { x: s.x + s.width, y: s.y },
      { x: s.x, y: s.y + s.height },
    ];
    for (const p of pts) {
      if (Math.abs(fx - p.x) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fx = p.x;
      if (Math.abs(fy - p.y) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fy = p.y;
      if (Math.abs((fx + w / 2) - p.x) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fx = p.x - w / 2;
      if (Math.abs((fy + h / 2) - p.y) <= SNAP_THRESHOLD) fy = p.y - h / 2;
    }
  }
  return { x: Math.round(fx), y: Math.round(fy) };
}

export interface HostCenterSnapResult {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  snappedX: boolean;
  snappedY: boolean;
}

/** Центрирует фигуру относительно host с адаптивным порогом. */
export function snapToHostCenter(
  x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number,
  host: StampShapeItem | null,
  thresholdOverride?: number,
): HostCenterSnapResult {
  if (!host) return { x, y, snappedX: false, snappedY: false };

  const threshold = thresholdOverride ?? Math.max(30, Math.min(w, h) * 0.4);
  const hCx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const hCy = host.y + host.height / 2;
  const cx = x + w / 2;
  const cy = y + h / 2;

  const snappedX = Math.abs(cx - hCx) <= threshold;
  const snappedY = Math.abs(cy - hCy) <= threshold;

  return {
    x: snappedX ? hCx - w / 2 : x,
    y: snappedY ? hCy - h / 2 : y,
    snappedX, snappedY,
  };
}

export type PlacementMode = 'inside' | 'outside' | 'next';

export function computeNewShapeGeometry(
  host: StampShapeItem | null,
  baseW: number, baseH: number,
  canvasW: number, canvasH: number,
  mode: PlacementMode,
  padding = 12,
): { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number } {
  if (!host) {
    return {
      x: Math.round((canvasW - baseW) / 2),
      y: Math.round((canvasH - baseH) / 2),
      width: baseW, height: baseH,
    };
  }

  const hostCx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const hostCy = host.y + host.height / 2;

  if (mode === 'inside') {
    const scale = 0.6;
    const w = Math.round(host.width * scale);
    const h = Math.round(host.height * scale);
    return { x: Math.round(hostCx - w / 2), y: Math.round(hostCy - h / 2), width: w, height: h };
  }

  if (mode === 'outside') {
    const w = Math.round(host.width + padding * 2);
    const h = Math.round(host.height + padding * 2);
    return { x: Math.round(hostCx - w / 2), y: Math.round(hostCy - h / 2), width: w, height: h };
  }

  return {
    x: Math.round(host.x + host.width + padding),
    y: Math.round(host.y),
    width: baseW, height: baseH,
  };
}

  'arcStartDeg', 'arcEndDeg',
  'fontSize', 'strokeWidth', 'cornerRadius', 'opacity',
  'fill', 'stroke',

/** РџРѕР»СЏ, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ РІСЃРµРіРґР° СЃРѕР·РґР°СЋС‚ РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ undo-С€Р°Рі (РґРёСЃРєСЂРµС‚РЅС‹Рµ). */
const DISCRETE_FIELDS = new Set<string>([
  'x', 'y', 'width', 'height', 'rotation',
  'arcCx', 'arcCy',
  'text', 'mode', 'name',
  'fillTargetShapeId', 'fillMode', 'fillOuterId', 'fillHoleIds',
  'locked', 'hidden',
  'fontStyle', 'textDecoration', 'align', 'fontFamily',
  'arcFlip', 'arcOutside',
  'src', 'fit',
]);

/**
 * Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ mergeKey РґР»СЏ `useStampHistory.push`, РµСЃР»Рё РїР°С‚С‡
 * СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ В«РЅРµРїСЂРµСЂС‹РІРЅС‹РµВ» РїРѕР»СЏ. Р”Р»СЏ РґРёСЃРєСЂРµС‚РЅС‹С… РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№
 * (РёР»Рё СЃРјРµС€Р°РЅРЅС‹С…) РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ `undefined` вЂ” РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ undo-С€Р°Рі.
 */
export function autoKey(id: string, patch: object): string | undefined {
  const keys = Object.keys(patch);
  if (keys.length === 0) return undefined;
  if (keys.some((k) => DISCRETE_FIELDS.has(k))) return undefined;
  if (!keys.some((k) => CONTINUOUS_FIELDS.has(k))) return undefined;
  return `${id}:${keys.slice().sort().join(',')}`;
}

        fontSize: 22, fontStyle: 'bold', fill: '#1e40af',
      }),
    ],

/* ============================================================
   Пользовательские штампы
   ============================================================ */

export function loadCustomStamps(): StampTemplate[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : [];
  } catch { return []; }
}

export function saveCustomStamps(stamps: StampTemplate[]): void {
  try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(stamps)); } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Определение встроенности
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */

const BUILTIN_IDS = new Set<string>([
  ...BUILTIN_STAMPS.map((s) => s.id),
  ...BANKING_STAMPS.map((s) => s.id),
  ...BANKING_STAMPS_FILLED.map((s) => s.id),
  ...SERVICE_STAMPS.map((s) => s.id),
  ...SPECIAL_STAMPS.map((s) => s.id),
  ...BANKING_STAMPS_WITH_IMAGES.map((s) => s.id),
]);

export function isBuiltinStamp(id: string): boolean {
  return BUILTIN_IDS.has(id);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Публичный доступ
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */

export function getAllStamps(): StampTemplate[] {
  const banking = BANKING_STAMPS.map((s) => ({ ...s, banking: true }));
  const bankingFilled = BANKING_STAMPS_FILLED.map((s) => ({ ...s, banking: true }));
  const services = SERVICE_STAMPS.map((s) => ({ ...s, banking: true }));
  const specials = SPECIAL_STAMPS.map((s) => ({ ...s, banking: true }));
  const withImages = BANKING_STAMPS_WITH_IMAGES.map((s) => ({ ...s, banking: true }));
  return [
    ...BUILTIN_STAMPS,
    ...banking,
    ...bankingFilled,
    ...services,
    ...specials,
    ...withImages,
    ...loadCustomStamps(),
  ];
}

export function addCustomStamp(t: StampTemplate): void {
  const list = loadCustomStamps();
  list.push({ ...t, custom: true, banking: false });
  saveCustomStamps(list);
}

export function removeCustomStamp(id: string): void {
  const list = loadCustomStamps().filter((s) => s.id !== id);
  saveCustomStamps(list);
}

export function updateCustomStamp(id: string, patch: Partial<StampTemplate>): void {
  const list = loadCustomStamps();
  const idx = list.findIndex((s) => s.id === id);
  if (idx < 0) return;
  list[idx] = { ...list[idx], ...patch };
  saveCustomStamps(list);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Создание пользовательской копии из встроенного.
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */

export function forkStampAsCustom(source: StampTemplate): StampTemplate {
  const copy: StampTemplate = {
    ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(source)),
    id: `custom-${Date.now()}`,
    name: `${source.name} (моя)`,
    custom: true,
    banking: false,
  };
  copy.shapes = copy.shapes.map((s) => ({ ...s, id: uid() }));
  copy.texts = copy.texts.map((t) => ({ ...t, id: uid() }));
  copy.images = (copy.images || []).map((im) => ({ ...im, id: uid() }));
  return copy;
}

FILE: src\lib\stamps\rasterFill.ts

/* ============================================================
   lib/stamps/rasterFill.ts

   Чистая async-функция растеризации заливок (§50).
   Работает и в Worker (OffscreenCanvas), и в main thread (HTMLCanvas).

   Контракт:
   - никогда не бросает;
   - возвращает Result<Blob, FillError>;
   - для anchor-fill (fill.target != null) маска target гарантирует
     заливку области, поэтому проверки touchesBorder / MAX_AREA_RATIO
     применяются только к legacy-fill без target.

   F-RFILL-05..11.
   ============================================================ */

import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { getShapeStrategy } from './shapes/registry';

const SCALE = 4;
const BORDER_THRESHOLD = 200;
const MIN_AREA = 16;
const MAX_AREA_RATIO = 0.85;
const SEED_SEARCH_RADIUS = 20;

const LBL_NONE    = 0;
const LBL_INSIDE  = 1;
const LBL_OUTSIDE = 2;
const LBL_NEUTRAL = 3;

export type FillError =
  | 'no-canvas'
  | 'no-2d-context'
  | 'no-path2d'
  | 'get-image-data-failed'
  | 'empty-target-mask'
  | 'no-seed-pixel'
  | 'zero-pixels'
  | 'area-too-small'
  | 'area-too-big'
  | 'open-region'
  | 'all-fills-rejected'
  | 'serialize-failed';

export type RasterizeResult =
  | { ok: true; value: Blob }
  | { ok: false; reason: FillError };

export interface FillSpec {
  px: number;
  py: number;
  color: string;
  target: StampShapeItem | null;
}

export interface CompositeFillOptions {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
}

/* ---------- canvas ---------- */

type AnyCanvas = HTMLCanvasElement | OffscreenCanvas;
type Ctx2D = CanvasRenderingContext2D | OffscreenCanvasRenderingContext2D;

function createCanvas(w: number, h: number): { canvas: AnyCanvas; ctx: Ctx2D } | null {
  if (typeof OffscreenCanvas !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const canvas = new OffscreenCanvas(w, h);
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      if (ctx) return { canvas, ctx };
    } catch { /* fall through */ }
  }
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = w;
    canvas.height = h;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
    if (ctx) return { canvas, ctx };
  }
  return null;
}

async function canvasToBlob(canvas: AnyCanvas): Promise<RasterizeResult> {
  const anyCanvas = canvas as unknown as {
    convertToBlob?: (o?: { type?: string }) => Promise<Blob>;
    toBlob?: (cb: (b: Blob | null) => void, type?: string) => void;
  };

  if (typeof anyCanvas.convertToBlob === 'function') {
    try {
      const blob = await anyCanvas.convertToBlob({ type: 'image/png' });
      return { ok: true, value: blob };
    } catch {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'serialize-failed' };
    }
  }

  if (typeof anyCanvas.toBlob === 'function') {
    return new Promise<RasterizeResult>((resolve) => {
      anyCanvas.toBlob!((blob) => {
        resolve(blob
          ? { ok: true, value: blob }
          : { ok: false, reason: 'serialize-failed' });
      }, 'image/png');
    });
  }

  return { ok: false, reason: 'serialize-failed' };
}

/* ---------- shape tracing ---------- */

function traceShapePath(ctx: Ctx2D, s: StampShapeItem): void {
  ctx.beginPath();
  const { x, y, width: w, height: h } = s;

  if (s.kind === 'rect') {
    const r = Math.max(0, Math.min(s.cornerRadius || 0, w / 2, h / 2));
    if (r > 0) {
      ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
      ctx.lineTo(x + w - r, y);
      ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r, r);
      ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h - r);
      ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - r, y + h, r);
      ctx.lineTo(x + r, y + h);
      ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - r, r);
      ctx.lineTo(x, y + r);
      ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + r, y, r);
      ctx.closePath();
    } else {
      ctx.rect(x, y, w, h);
    }
    return;
  }

  if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') {
    ctx.ellipse(
      x + w / 2, y + h / 2,
      Math.max(1, w / 2), Math.max(1, h / 2),
      0, 0, Math.PI * 2,
    );
    return;
  }

  if (s.kind === 'triangle') {
    ctx.moveTo(x + w / 2, y);
    ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h);
    ctx.lineTo(x, y + h);
    ctx.closePath();
    return;
  }

  if (s.kind === 'line') {
    const pts = s.points || [0, h / 2, w, h / 2];
    if (pts.length >= 4) {
      ctx.moveTo(x + pts[0], y + pts[1]);
      for (let i = 2; i + 1 < pts.length; i += 2) {
        ctx.lineTo(x + pts[i], y + pts[i + 1]);
      }
    }
  }
}

/* ---------- target mask ---------- */

function buildTargetMask(
  W: number,
  H: number,
  target: StampShapeItem,
): { ok: true; value: Uint8Array } | { ok: false; reason: FillError } {
  const made = createCanvas(W, H);
  if (!made) return { ok: false, reason: 'no-canvas' };

  made.ctx.scale(SCALE, SCALE);

  let path: Path2D;
  try {
    path = getShapeStrategy(target.kind).toPath(target);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'no-path2d' };
  }

  made.ctx.fillStyle = '#000000';
  made.ctx.fill(path);

  let img: ImageData;
  try {
    img = made.ctx.getImageData(0, 0, W, H);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'get-image-data-failed' };
  }

  const mask = new Uint8Array(W * H);
  for (let i = 0; i < mask.length; i++) {
    if (img.data[i * 4 + 3] > 128) mask[i] = 1;
  }
  return { ok: true, value: mask };
}

/* ---------- flood fill ---------- */

function floodFillLightFromSeed(
  data: Uint8ClampedArray,
  labels: Uint8Array,
  W: number,
  H: number,
  seedIdx: number,
  tag: number,
  targetMask: Uint8Array | null,
): { touchesBorder: boolean; count: number } {
  if (targetMask && !targetMask[seedIdx]) return { touchesBorder: false, count: 0 };

  const stack: number[] = [seedIdx];
  let touchesBorder = false;
  let count = 0;

  while (stack.length > 0) {
    const i = stack.pop()!;
    if (labels[i] !== LBL_NONE) continue;
    if (targetMask && !targetMask[i]) continue;

    const p = i * 4;
    if ((data[p] + data[p + 1] + data[p + 2]) / 3 < BORDER_THRESHOLD) continue;

    labels[i] = tag;
    count++;

    const x = i % W;
    const y = (i - x) / W;
    if (x === 0 || y === 0 || x === W - 1 || y === H - 1) touchesBorder = true;

    if (x > 0)     stack.push(i - 1);
    if (x < W - 1) stack.push(i + 1);
    if (y > 0)     stack.push(i - W);
    if (y < H - 1) stack.push(i + W);
  }
  return { touchesBorder, count };
}

function floodFillLightFromMany(
  data: Uint8ClampedArray,
  labels: Uint8Array,
  W: number,
  H: number,
  seedIdxs: number[],
  tag: number,
): void {
  const stack = seedIdxs.slice();
  while (stack.length > 0) {
    const i = stack.pop()!;
    if (labels[i] !== LBL_NONE) continue;

    const p = i * 4;
    if ((data[p] + data[p + 1] + data[p + 2]) / 3 < BORDER_THRESHOLD) continue;

    labels[i] = tag;
    const x = i % W;
    const y = (i - x) / W;
    if (x > 0)     stack.push(i - 1);
    if (x < W - 1) stack.push(i + 1);
    if (y > 0)     stack.push(i - W);
    if (y < H - 1) stack.push(i + W);
  }
}

function markNeutralWhiteRegions(data: Uint8ClampedArray, labels: Uint8Array): void {
  for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {
    if (labels[i] !== LBL_NONE) continue;
    const p = i * 4;
    if ((data[p] + data[p + 1] + data[p + 2]) / 3 >= BORDER_THRESHOLD) {
      labels[i] = LBL_NEUTRAL;
    }
  }
}

function watershedByDistance(
  data: Uint8ClampedArray,
  labels: Uint8Array,
  W: number,
  H: number,
): void {
  const total = W * H;
  const di = new Int32Array(total).fill(-1);
  const dout = new Int32Array(total).fill(-1);

  const bfs = (dist: Int32Array, from: number): void => {
    const q: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) if (labels[i] === from) { dist[i] = 0; q.push(i); }

    let h = 0;
    while (h < q.length) {
      const i = q[h++];
      const d = dist[i];
      const x = i % W;
      const y = (i - x) / W;

      const tryN = (ni: number): void => {
        if (ni < 0 || ni >= total) return;
        if (dist[ni] !== -1) return;
        if (labels[ni] !== LBL_NONE) return;
        const p = ni * 4;
        if ((data[p] + data[p + 1] + data[p + 2]) / 3 >= BORDER_THRESHOLD) return;
        dist[ni] = d + 1;
        q.push(ni);
      };

      if (x > 0)     tryN(i - 1);
      if (x < W - 1) tryN(i + 1);
      if (y > 0)     tryN(i - W);
      if (y < H - 1) tryN(i + W);
    }
  };

  bfs(di, LBL_INSIDE);
  bfs(dout, LBL_OUTSIDE);

  for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
    if (labels[i] !== LBL_NONE) continue;
    const a = di[i];
    const b = dout[i];
    if (a === -1 && b === -1) continue;
    if (a === -1)  { labels[i] = LBL_OUTSIDE; continue; }
    if (b === -1)  { labels[i] = LBL_INSIDE;  continue; }
    labels[i] = a <= b ? LBL_INSIDE : LBL_OUTSIDE;
  }
}

function findNearbyLightPixel(
  data: Uint8ClampedArray,
  W: number,
  H: number,
  cx: number,
  cy: number,
  maxR: number,
  targetMask: Uint8Array | null,
): number | null {
  const isLight = (i: number): boolean => {
    if (targetMask && !targetMask[i]) return false;
    const p = i * 4;
    return (data[p] + data[p + 1] + data[p + 2]) / 3 >= BORDER_THRESHOLD;
  };

  const start = cy * W + cx;
  if (isLight(start)) return start;

  for (let r = 1; r <= maxR; r++) {
    for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
      for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
        if (Math.abs(dx) !== r && Math.abs(dy) !== r) continue;
        const x = cx + dx;
        const y = cy + dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= W || y >= H) continue;
        const i = y * W + x;
        if (isLight(i)) return i;
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function computeMaskForFill(
  data: Uint8ClampedArray,
  W: number,
  H: number,
  px: number,
  py: number,
  target: StampShapeItem | null,
): { ok: true; value: Uint8Array } | { ok: false; reason: FillError } {
  const ax = Math.floor(px * SCALE);
  const ay = Math.floor(py * SCALE);
  if (ax < 0 || ay < 0 || ax >= W || ay >= H) return { ok: false, reason: 'no-seed-pixel' };

  let targetMask: Uint8Array | null = null;
  if (target) {
    const maskRes = buildTargetMask(W, H, target);
    if (!maskRes.ok) return maskRes;

    let any = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < maskRes.value.length; i++) {
      if (maskRes.value[i]) { any = true; break; }
    }
    if (!any) return { ok: false, reason: 'empty-target-mask' };
    targetMask = maskRes.value;
  }

  const start = findNearbyLightPixel(data, W, H, ax, ay, SEED_SEARCH_RADIUS, targetMask);
  if (start === null) return { ok: false, reason: 'no-seed-pixel' };

  const labels = new Uint8Array(W * H);
  if (targetMask) {
    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {
      if (!targetMask[i]) labels[i] = LBL_OUTSIDE;
    }
  }

  const { touchesBorder, count } = floodFillLightFromSeed(
    data, labels, W, H, start, LBL_INSIDE, targetMask,
  );
  if (count === 0) return { ok: false, reason: 'zero-pixels' };

  if (!targetMask) {
    if (touchesBorder) return { ok: false, reason: 'open-region' };

    const seeds: number[] = [];
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) { seeds.push(x); seeds.push((H - 1) * W + x); }
    for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) { seeds.push(y * W); seeds.push(y * W + W - 1); }
    floodFillLightFromMany(data, labels, W, H, seeds, LBL_OUTSIDE);
  }

  markNeutralWhiteRegions(data, labels);
  watershedByDistance(data, labels, W, H);

  const mask = new Uint8Array(W * H);
  let c = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {
    if (labels[i] === LBL_INSIDE) { mask[i] = 1; c++; }
  }

  if (c < MIN_AREA * SCALE * SCALE) return { ok: false, reason: 'area-too-small' };
  if (!targetMask && c > W * H * MAX_AREA_RATIO) return { ok: false, reason: 'area-too-big' };

  return { ok: true, value: mask };
}

/* ---------- main ---------- */

export async function rasterizeCompositeFills(
  opts: CompositeFillOptions,
): Promise<RasterizeResult> {
  const { width, height, shapes, fills } = opts;

  if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return { ok: false, reason: 'no-canvas' };
  if (!fills.length) return { ok: false, reason: 'all-fills-rejected' };
  if (typeof Path2D === 'undefined') return { ok: false, reason: 'no-path2d' };

  const W = width * SCALE;
  const H = height * SCALE;

  const made = createCanvas(W, H);
  if (!made) return { ok: false, reason: 'no-canvas' };

  const { canvas, ctx } = made;

  ctx.scale(SCALE, SCALE);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  ctx.strokeStyle = '#000000';
  ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.hidden) continue;
    if (s.kind === 'fill') continue;
    traceShapePath(ctx, s);
    ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2 / SCALE, s.strokeWidth || 2);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  let img: ImageData;
  try {
    img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, W, H);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'get-image-data-failed' };
  }

  const data = img.data;
  const resultR = new Uint8ClampedArray(W * H);
  const resultG = new Uint8ClampedArray(W * H);
  const resultB = new Uint8ClampedArray(W * H);
  const resultA = new Uint8Array(W * H);

  let anyPainted = false;
  let lastError: FillError = 'all-fills-rejected';

  for (const fill of fills) {
    const maskRes = computeMaskForFill(data, W, H, fill.px, fill.py, fill.target);
    if (!maskRes.ok) { lastError = maskRes.reason; continue; }

    const [cr, cg, cb] = hexToRgb(fill.color);
    const mask = maskRes.value;
    for (let i = 0; i < mask.length; i++) {
      if (mask[i]) {
        resultR[i] = cr;
        resultG[i] = cg;
        resultB[i] = cb;
        resultA[i] = 255;
      }
    }
    anyPainted = true;
  }

  if (!anyPainted) return { ok: false, reason: lastError };

  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    const p = i * 4;
    if (resultA[i]) {
      data[p]     = resultR[i];
      data[p + 1] = resultG[i];
      data[p + 2] = resultB[i];
      data[p + 3] = 255;
    } else {
      data[p + 3] = 0;
    }
  }

  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);

  return canvasToBlob(canvas);
}

/**
 * F-RFILL-10: парсер цвета.
 * Поддерживает #RGB и #RRGGBB (как приходит из <input type="color">,
 * который всегда отдаёт #rrggbb, но запасной вариант не помешает).
 */
function hexToRgb(hex: string): [number, number, number] {
  const clean = hex.replace('#', '').trim();
  if (clean.length === 3) {
    const r = parseInt(clean[0] + clean[0], 16);
    const g = parseInt(clean[1] + clean[1], 16);
    const b = parseInt(clean[2] + clean[2], 16);
    if (Number.isFinite(r) && Number.isFinite(g) && Number.isFinite(b)) {
      return [r, g, b];
    }
  }
  if (clean.length === 6) {
    const r = parseInt(clean.slice(0, 2), 16);
    const g = parseInt(clean.slice(2, 4), 16);
    const b = parseInt(clean.slice(4, 6), 16);
    if (Number.isFinite(r) && Number.isFinite(g) && Number.isFinite(b)) {
      return [r, g, b];
    }
  }
  return [0, 0, 0];
}

import type { DashType } from '@/shared/types';

export const STAMP_DASH_MAP: Record<DashType, number[] | undefined> = {
  solid: undefined,
  dashed: [10, 6],
  dotted: [2, 5],
  dashdot: [10, 5, 2, 5],
  longdash: [20, 8],
};

export const konvaStyle = (s: string): string => {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (s.includes('bold')) parts.push('bold');
  if (s.includes('italic')) parts.push('italic');
  return parts.join(' ') || 'normal';
};

export const canvasFont = (family: string, style: string, size: number): string => {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (style.includes('italic')) parts.push('italic');
  if (style.includes('bold')) parts.push('bold');
  parts.push(`${size}px`);
  parts.push(family);
  return parts.join(' ');
};

export interface ArcTextParams {
  text: string;
  cx: number;
  cy: number;
  radius: number;
  radiusX?: number;
  radiusY?: number;
  startDeg: number;
  endDeg: number;
  flip: boolean;
  font: string;
  fill: string;
}

/* ============================================================
   Утилиты для эллипса
   ============================================================ */

/**
 * Длина эллиптической дуги от угла a0 до угла a1.
 * Численное интегрирование методом Симпсона по 64 шагам.
 * Используется для равномерной расстановки букв по длине.
 */
function ellipseArcLength(
  rx: number, ry: number,
  a0: number, a1: number,
): number {
  const N = 64;
  const h = (a1 - a0) / N;
  let sum = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {
    const a = a0 + i * h;
    const speed = Math.hypot(rx * Math.sin(a), ry * Math.cos(a));
    const w = (i === 0 || i === N) ? 1 : (i % 2 === 0 ? 2 : 4);
    sum += w * speed;
  }
  return Math.abs((h / 3) * sum);
}

/**
 * Найти угол a такой, что длина дуги от startDeg до a
 * равна target (доли от полной длины 0..1).
 * Бинарный поиск.
 */
function angleAtArcFraction(
  rx: number, ry: number,
  aStart: number, aEnd: number,
  fraction: number,
): number {
  const total = ellipseArcLength(rx, ry, aStart, aEnd);
  if (total === 0) return aStart;
  const target = total * fraction;

  let lo = 0, hi = 1;
  for (let it = 0; it < 40; it++) {
    const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
    const a = aStart + (aEnd - aStart) * mid;
    const len = ellipseArcLength(rx, ry, aStart, a);
    if (len < target) lo = mid; else hi = mid;
  }
  return aStart + (aEnd - aStart) * (lo + hi) / 2;
}

/* ============================================================
   Отрисовка текста по эллиптической дуге
   ============================================================ */

/**
 * Текст ложится вдоль эллипса с радиусами (rx, ry).
 *
 * Правила:
 *  - Для круга (rx == ry) поведение совпадает с классическим.
 *  - Буквы распределены РАВНОМЕРНО ПО ДЛИНЕ ДУГИ, а не по углу.
 *  - Каждая буква ориентирована по касательной к эллипсу
 *    («верх» буквы смотрит наружу от центра).
 *  - Значение flip игнорируется: положение уже задано углами
 *    startDeg/endDeg, а направление чтения — порядком букв.
 */
export function drawArcText(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, p: ArcTextParams): void {
  if (!p.text) return;

  const rx = p.radiusX ?? p.radius;
  const ry = p.radiusY ?? p.radius;
  if (!rx || !ry) return;

  const startRad = (p.startDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const endRad = (p.endDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const chars = Array.from(p.text);
  const n = chars.length;
  if (n === 0) return;

  /* Полная длина дуги — для равномерного распределения */
  const totalLen = ellipseArcLength(rx, ry, startRad, endRad);
  if (totalLen === 0) return;

  ctx.save();
  ctx.font = p.font;
  ctx.fillStyle = p.fill;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';

  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const ch = chars[i];
    if (ch === ' ' || ch === '\t' || ch === '\n') continue;

    /* Доля от начала до ЦЕНТРА этой буквы */
    const fraction = n > 1 ? (i + 0.5) / n : 0.5;
    const a = angleAtArcFraction(rx, ry, startRad, endRad, fraction);

    const cosA = Math.cos(a);
    const sinA = Math.sin(a);
    const x = p.cx + cosA * rx;
    const y = p.cy + sinA * ry;

    /* Касательная к эллипсу: d/da (rx·cos a, ry·sin a) = (-rx·sin a, ry·cos a) */
    const tx = -rx * sinA;
    const ty = ry * cosA;
    const rot = Math.atan2(ty, tx);

    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(x, y);
    ctx.rotate(rot);
    ctx.fillText(ch, 0, 0);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  ctx.restore();
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/stamps/serviceStamps.ts

import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { uid } from '../uid';
import type { StampTemplate } from './types';

const INK = '#0a2540';
const SOFT = '#374151';
const WHITE = '#ffffff';
const GREEN = '#15803d';
const GREEN_LIGHT = '#dcfce7';
const RED = '#b91c1c';
const RED_LIGHT = '#fecaca';
const BLUE = '#1e40af';
const BLUE_LIGHT = '#dbeafe';
const YELLOW_LIGHT = '#fef9c3';
const PURPLE = '#6b21a8';
const PURPLE_LIGHT = '#f3e8ff';

/* ============================================================
   Фабрики (локальные)
   ============================================================ */

const shape = (
  o: Partial<StampShapeItem> & { kind: StampShapeItem['kind'] },
): StampShapeItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 100,
  rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
  stroke: INK, strokeWidth: 1, dash: 'solid',
  fill: undefined,
  ...o,
});

const text = (
  o: Partial<StampTextItem> & { text: string; mode: 'straight' | 'arc' },
): StampTextItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, align: 'center',
  arcCx: 100, arcCy: 100, arcRadius: 60,
  arcStartDeg: -90, arcEndDeg: 90, arcFlip: false,
  arcOffset: 6, arcSpanDeg: 140,
  fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
  textDecoration: '', fill: INK,
  ...o,
});

/**
 * Anchor-заливка: полностью повторяет геометрию целевого контейнера.
 * bbox = весь штамп, seed задаётся в долях от bbox контейнера.
 */
function anchorFill(
  container: StampShapeItem,
  color: string,
  anchorLocal: { x: number; y: number },
  stampW: number,
  stampH: number,
  name = 'Заливка',
): StampShapeItem {
  return {
    id: uid(),
    kind: 'fill',
    x: 0, y: 0,
    width: stampW, height: stampH,
    rotation: 0,
    cornerRadius: 0,
    stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
    fill: color,
    name,
    fillTargetShapeId: container.id,
    fillAnchorLocal: anchorLocal,
  };
}

export * from './types';

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\lib\stamps\shapes\registry.ts
SIZE: 761 bytes

import type { StampShapeKind } from '@/shared/types';
import type { ShapeStrategy } from './types';
import {
  rectStrategy, circleStrategy, ellipseStrategy,
  triangleStrategy, lineStrategy, fillStrategy,
} from './strategies';

const ALL: ShapeStrategy[] = [
  rectStrategy, circleStrategy, ellipseStrategy,
  triangleStrategy, lineStrategy, fillStrategy,
];

const registry = new Map<StampShapeKind, ShapeStrategy>();
for (const s of ALL) registry.set(s.kind, s);

export function getShapeStrategy(kind: StampShapeKind): ShapeStrategy {
  const s = registry.get(kind);
  if (!s) throw new Error(`[stamps] no strategy for kind="${kind}"`);
  return s;
}

export function getAllStrategies(): readonly ShapeStrategy[] {
  return ALL;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\lib\stamps\shapes\strategies.ts
SIZE: 6646 bytes

import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import type { ShapeStrategy, TransformInput, TransformOutput, Geometry } from './types';
import { findContainingShape, snapToHostCenter } from '../geometry';

/* ============================================================
   Path builders
   ============================================================ */

function buildRectPath(s: StampShapeItem): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D();
  const { x, y, width: w, height: h } = s;
  const r = Math.max(0, s.cornerRadius || 0);
  const rr = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
  if (rr > 0) {
    p.moveTo(x + rr, y);
    p.lineTo(x + w - rr, y);
    p.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + rr, rr);
    p.lineTo(x + w, y + h - rr);
    p.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - rr, y + h, rr);
    p.lineTo(x + rr, y + h);
    p.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - rr, rr);
    p.lineTo(x, y + rr);
    p.arcTo(x, y, x + rr, y, rr);
    p.closePath();
  } else {
    p.rect(x, y, w, h);
  }
  return p;
}

function buildEllipsePath(s: StampShapeItem): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D();
  const cx = s.x + s.width / 2;
  const cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
  p.ellipse(cx, cy, Math.max(1, s.width / 2), Math.max(1, s.height / 2), 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  return p;
}

function buildCirclePath(s: StampShapeItem): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D();
  const cx = s.x + s.width / 2;
  const cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
  const r = Math.max(1, Math.min(s.width, s.height) / 2);
  p.ellipse(cx, cy, r, r, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  return p;
}

function buildTrianglePath(s: StampShapeItem): Path2D {
  const p = new Path2D();
  const { x, y, width: w, height: h } = s;
  p.moveTo(x + w / 2, y);
  p.lineTo(x + w, y + h);
  p.lineTo(x, y + h);
  p.closePath();
  return p;
}

/* ============================================================
   Общая трансформация для closed-фигур
   ============================================================ */

interface TransformOpts {
  keepSquare?: boolean;
  noRotation?: boolean;
  noResize?: boolean;
}

function applyShapeTransform(input: TransformInput, opts: TransformOpts): TransformOutput {
  const { shape: s, nodeX, nodeY, nodeRotation, scaleX, scaleY, ctrl, shapes } = input;

  if (opts.noResize) {
    let nx = Math.round(nodeX - s.width / 2);
    let ny = Math.round(nodeY - s.height / 2);
    if (!ctrl) {
      const host = findContainingShape(nx + s.width / 2, ny + s.height / 2, s.id, shapes);
      const cs = snapToHostCenter(nx, ny, s.width, s.height, host);
      nx = Math.round(cs.x);
      ny = Math.round(cs.y);
    }
    return {
      patch: { x: nx, y: ny, rotation: opts.noRotation ? 0 : Math.round(nodeRotation) },
      resetScale: true,
    };
  }

  const rawW = Math.max(8, s.width * scaleX);
  const rawH = Math.max(8, s.height * scaleY);
  const fw = opts.keepSquare ? Math.max(rawW, rawH) : rawW;
  const fh = opts.keepSquare ? Math.max(rawW, rawH) : rawH;

  let nx = Math.round(nodeX - fw / 2);
  let ny = Math.round(nodeY - fh / 2);

  if (!ctrl) {
    const host = findContainingShape(nx + fw / 2, ny + fh / 2, s.id, shapes);
    const cs = snapToHostCenter(nx, ny, fw, fh, host);
    nx = Math.round(cs.x);
    ny = Math.round(cs.y);
  }

  return {
    patch: {
      x: nx, y: ny,
      rotation: Math.round(nodeRotation),
      width: Math.round(fw),
      height: Math.round(fh),
    },
    resetScale: true,
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   Стратегии
   ============================================================ */

export const rectStrategy: ShapeStrategy = {
  kind: 'rect',
  defaultSize: { w: 160, h: 100, corner: 12 },
  normalizeGeometry: (g: Geometry) => g,
  toPath: buildRectPath,
  applyTransform: (i) => applyShapeTransform(i, {}),
};

export const circleStrategy: ShapeStrategy = {
  kind: 'circle',
  defaultSize: { w: 140, h: 140, corner: 0 },
  normalizeGeometry: (g: Geometry) => {
    const side = Math.min(g.width, g.height);
    return { ...g, width: side, height: side };
  },
  toPath: buildCirclePath,
  applyTransform: (i) => applyShapeTransform(i, { keepSquare: true }),
};

export const ellipseStrategy: ShapeStrategy = {
  kind: 'ellipse',
  defaultSize: { w: 160, h: 160, corner: 0 },
  normalizeGeometry: (g: Geometry) => g,
  toPath: buildEllipsePath,
  applyTransform: (i) => applyShapeTransform(i, {}),
};

export const triangleStrategy: ShapeStrategy = {
  kind: 'triangle',
  defaultSize: { w: 140, h: 120, corner: 0 },
  normalizeGeometry: (g: Geometry) => g,
  toPath: buildTrianglePath,
  applyTransform: (i) => applyShapeTransform(i, {}),
};

export const lineStrategy: ShapeStrategy = {
  kind: 'line',
  defaultSize: { w: 160, h: 20, corner: 0 },
  normalizeGeometry: (g: Geometry) => g,
  toPath: () => new Path2D(),
  applyTransform: (i) => {
    const { shape: s, nodeX, nodeY, nodeRotation, scaleX, scaleY, ctrl, shapes } = i;

    const w = Math.max(1, s.width);
    const h = Math.max(1, s.height);

    const kx = scaleX;
    const ky = scaleY;

    const srcPoints = (s.points && s.points.length >= 4)
      ? s.points
      : [0, h / 2, w, h / 2];

    const scaledPoints = srcPoints.map((v, idx) =>
      idx % 2 === 0 ? v * kx : v * ky,
    );

    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
    for (let k = 0; k < scaledPoints.length; k += 2) {
      const px = scaledPoints[k];
      const py = scaledPoints[k + 1];
      if (px < minX) minX = px;
      if (py < minY) minY = py;
      if (px > maxX) maxX = px;
      if (py > maxY) maxY = py;
    }
    const newW = Math.max(8, maxX - minX);
    const newH = Math.max(8, maxY - minY);

    let nx = Math.round(nodeX - newW / 2);
    let ny = Math.round(nodeY - newH / 2);

    if (!ctrl) {
      const host = findContainingShape(nx + newW / 2, ny + newH / 2, s.id, shapes);
      const cs = snapToHostCenter(nx, ny, newW, newH, host);
      nx = Math.round(cs.x);
      ny = Math.round(cs.y);
    }

    return {
      patch: {
        x: nx,
        y: ny,
        rotation: Math.round(nodeRotation),
        width: newW,
        height: newH,
        points: scaledPoints.map((v) => Math.round(v)),
      },
      resetScale: true,
    };
  },
};

export const fillStrategy: ShapeStrategy = {
  kind: 'fill',
  defaultSize: { w: 0, h: 0, corner: 0 },
  normalizeGeometry: (g: Geometry) => g,
  toPath: () => new Path2D(),
  applyTransform: () => ({ patch: {}, resetScale: false }),
};

import type { StampShapeItem, StampShapeKind } from '@/shared/types';

export interface Geometry {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export interface TransformInput {
  shape: StampShapeItem;
  /** Позиция центра (node.x, node.y). */
  nodeX: number;
  nodeY: number;
  /** Угол node.rotation. */
  nodeRotation: number;
  scaleX: number;
  scaleY: number;
  /** Зажат Ctrl / Cmd — свободный режим. */
  ctrl: boolean;
  /** Все фигуры в штампе. */
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
}

export interface TransformOutput {
  patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>;
  /** true — сбросить node.scaleX/scaleY в 1 (обычно да). */
  resetScale: boolean;
}

export interface ShapeStrategy {
  kind: StampShapeKind;

  /** Размеры и corner при создании. */
  defaultSize: { w: number; h: number; corner: number };

  /** Нормализует геометрию после computeNewShapeGeometry (например, круг → квадрат). */
  normalizeGeometry(geom: Geometry): Geometry;

  /** Path2D по фигуре для fill/clip. */
  toPath(shape: StampShapeItem): Path2D;

  /** Трансформация после Konva.Transformer. */
  applyTransform(input: TransformInput): TransformOutput;
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/stamps/specialStamps.ts

   Специальные штампы:
   - треугольные (медицина, командировки, корреспонденция);
   - со сложной заливкой (пересечения, двойные плашки);
   - круговые с двумя кольцами и эмблемой;
   - овальные с дуговым текстом.

   Технология та же: StampShapeItem + StampTextItem +
   anchor-заливка через fillTargetShapeId / fillAnchorLocal.

   Про заливку перекрытий:
   Маска заливки строится по ОДНОЙ фигуре-контейнеру.
   Если нужно залить пересечение двух фигур — привязываем fill
   к одной из них, а сид ставим в точке, достижимой только
   внутри пересечения. Вторая фигура играет роль «стены»,
   потому что её штрих попадает в offscreen-рендер и
   останавливает flood fill.

   Про дуговой текст:
   Дуга используется только там, где контейнер криволинейный:
   круглые и овальные печати. Верхняя надпись идёт по верхней
   дуге (arcFlip: false), нижняя — по нижней (arcFlip: true).
   В прямоугольных и треугольных штампах дуга не применяется —
   там работают прямые строки.
   ============================================================ */

import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { uid } from '../uid';
import type { StampTemplate } from './types';

const INK = '#0a2540';
const SOFT = '#374151';
const WHITE = '#ffffff';
const GREEN = '#15803d';
const GREEN_LIGHT = '#dcfce7';
const BLUE = '#1e40af';
const BLUE_LIGHT = '#dbeafe';
const YELLOW_LIGHT = '#fef9c3';

/* ============================================================
   Фабрики
   ============================================================ */

const shape = (
  o: Partial<StampShapeItem> & { kind: StampShapeItem['kind'] },
): StampShapeItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 100,
  rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
  stroke: INK, strokeWidth: 1, dash: 'solid',
  fill: undefined,
  ...o,
});

const text = (
  o: Partial<StampTextItem> & { text: string; mode: 'straight' | 'arc' },
): StampTextItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, align: 'center',
  arcCx: 100, arcCy: 100, arcRadius: 60,
  arcStartDeg: -90, arcEndDeg: 90, arcFlip: false,
  arcOffset: 6, arcSpanDeg: 140,
  fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
  textDecoration: '', fill: INK,
  ...o,
});

function anchorFill(
  container: StampShapeItem,
  color: string,
  anchorLocal: { x: number; y: number },
  stampW: number,
  stampH: number,
  name = 'Заливка',
): StampShapeItem {
  return {
    id: uid(),
    kind: 'fill',
    x: 0, y: 0,
    width: stampW, height: stampH,
    rotation: 0,
    cornerRadius: 0,
    stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
    fill: color,
    name,
    fillTargetShapeId: container.id,
    fillAnchorLocal: anchorLocal,
  };
}

function vennSeal(): StampTemplate {

  const R = 72;
  const CY = 100;
  const CX_L = 72;
  const CX_R = W - 72;

  const leftCircle = shape({
    kind: 'ellipse',
    x: CX_L - R, y: CY - R,
    width: R * 2, height: R * 2,
    strokeWidth: 2,
  });
  const rightCircle = shape({
    kind: 'ellipse',
    x: CX_R - R, y: CY - R,
    width: R * 2, height: R * 2,
    strokeWidth: 2,
  });

  /* Левая заливка — крайняя левая точка левого круга */
  const leftFill = anchorFill(
    leftCircle, BLUE_LIGHT,
    { x: 0.1, y: 0.5 },
    W, H, 'Левый круг',
  );

  /* Правая заливка — крайняя правая точка правого круга */
  const rightFill = anchorFill(
    rightCircle, GREEN_LIGHT,
    { x: 0.9, y: 0.5 },
    W, H, 'Правый круг',
  );

  /* Пересечение — сид внутри обоих кругов,
     привязка к левому, штрих правого отсекает лишнее. */
  const overlapFill = anchorFill(
    leftCircle, YELLOW_LIGHT,
    { x: 0.75, y: 0.5 },
    W, H, 'Пересечение',
  );

  return {
    id: 'special-venn-seal',
    name: 'Печать с пересечением',

    icon: '⚭',
    width: W, height: H,
    shapes: [leftFill, rightFill, overlapFill, leftCircle, rightCircle],
    texts: [
      text({
        text: 'ПАО БАНК',
        mode: 'straight',
        x: 20, y: 16, width: W - 40,
        fontSize: 14, fontStyle: 'bold', align: 'center',
      }),

function triangleCircleOverlap(): StampTemplate {

    icon: '◬',
    width: W, height: H,
    shapes: [overlapFill, circle, triangle],
    texts: [
      /* Название — внутри пересечения, надписью по центру треугольника. */
      text({
        text: 'ЗОНА КОНТРОЛЯ', mode: 'straight',

        fontSize: 10, fontStyle: 'bold', align: 'center', fill: INK,

        fontSize: 9, align: 'center', fill: SOFT,
      }),
      /* Подпись в нижней части круга, вне треугольника. */
      text({
        text: 'Участок № 5', mode: 'straight',
        x: CX - 55, y: CY + 65, width: 110,
        fontSize: 10, align: 'center', fill: SOFT,
      }),
    ],
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/stamps/types.ts

   Общие типы штампов. Вынесено из library.ts, чтобы убрать
   циклы: library.ts импортирует данные из bankingStamps.ts,
   а bankingStamps.ts импортирует StampTemplate из ./types.
   ============================================================ */

import type {
  StampShapeItem,
  StampTextItem,
  StampImageItem,
} from '@/shared/types';

export interface StampTemplate {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  icon?: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images?: StampImageItem[];
  custom?: boolean;
  /** Помечаем штампы из банковской библиотеки для группировки/подсветки. */
  banking?: boolean;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\lib\svg\escape.ts
SIZE: 463 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/* ============================================================
   lib/svg/escape.ts
   F-SVG-15: XML-escape РІСЃРµС… Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёР№.
   ============================================================ */

export function escapeXml(str: string | undefined | null): string {
  if (!str) return '';
  return str
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&apos;');
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\lib\svg\exporters.ts
SIZE: 12382 bytes
