/* ============================================================
   lib/svg/exporters.ts
   F-SVG-03..14: Сериализация всех типов элементов.

   Fix ARC-LAYER: computeTableLayout теперь из lib/richText/tableLayout.
   ============================================================ */

import type {
  TextElement, ShapeElement, LineElement, TableElement, ImageElement,
  FooterElement, PlaceholderElement, FillElement, ConnectorElement,
  DiagramElement, StampElement, CanvasElement,
} from '@/shared/types';
import { escapeXml } from './escape';
import { resolveConnectorEnds, routeConnector } from '../connectors';
import { parseHtmlToBlocks } from '../richText/parser';
import { layoutRuns } from '../richText/layout';
import { computeTableLayout } from '../richText/tableLayout';

/* ---------- Text ---------- */

export function toSvgText(el: TextElement): string {
  const padding = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, el.width - padding.left - padding.right);

  const sourceHtml = el.html && el.html.trim() ? el.html : `<p>${escapeXml(el.text || '')}</p>`;

  const blocks = parseHtmlToBlocks(sourceHtml, {
    fontSize: el.fontSize,
    fontFamily: el.fontFamily,
    fontStyle: el.fontStyle || 'normal',
    textDecoration: el.textDecoration || '',
    fill: el.fill || '#000000',
  });

  const lineHeightCoef = el.lineHeight || 1.5;

  let cursorY = el.y + padding.top;
  let svgContent = '';

  for (const block of blocks) {
    if (block.kind === 'text') {
      const layout = layoutRuns(block.runs, innerWidth, lineHeightCoef);
      if (layout.lines.length === 0) continue;

      layout.lines.forEach((line) => {
        const lineWidth = line.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.width, 0);
        let lineOffsetX = 0;
        if (el.align === 'center') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, (innerWidth - lineWidth) / 2);
        else if (el.align === 'right') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, innerWidth - lineWidth);

        line.forEach((run) => {
          const rx = el.x + padding.left + lineOffsetX + run.x;
          const ry = cursorY + run.y + run.actualFontSize;

          const textDecoration =
            run.underline && run.strike ? 'underline line-through'
            : run.underline ? 'underline'
            : run.strike ? 'line-through'
            : 'none';

          svgContent += `<text x="${rx}" y="${ry}" font-size="${run.actualFontSize}" font-family="${run.fontFamily || el.fontFamily}" font-style="${run.italic ? 'italic' : 'normal'}" font-weight="${run.bold ? 'bold' : 'normal'}" fill="${run.color || el.fill || '#000'}" text-decoration="${textDecoration}">${escapeXml(run.text)}</text>`;
        });
      });

      cursorY += layout.totalHeight;
    } else if (block.kind === 'table') {
      const layout = computeTableLayout(block.data, innerWidth, el.fontSize, lineHeightCoef);
      const tableX = el.x + padding.left;
      const tableY = cursorY;

      block.data.cells.forEach((cell) => {
        const cellX = tableX + layout.colOffsets[cell.colIndex];
        const cellY = tableY + layout.rowOffsets[cell.rowIndex];
        const cellW = layout.colOffsets[Math.min(cell.colIndex + cell.colspan, layout.colOffsets.length - 1)] - layout.colOffsets[cell.colIndex];
        const cellH = layout.rowOffsets[Math.min(cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan, layout.rowOffsets.length - 1)] - layout.rowOffsets[cell.rowIndex];

        const bg = cell.bgColor || (cell.isHeader ? '#f1f5f9' : '#ffffff');
        const border = cell.borderColor || '#cbd5e1';
        const bw = cell.borderWidth ?? 1;

        svgContent += `<rect x="${cellX}" y="${cellY}" width="${cellW}" height="${cellH}" fill="${bg}" stroke="${border}" stroke-width="${bw}" />`;

        const items = layout.cellItems.get(cell);
        if (items) {
          let itemY = cellY + (cell.padding?.top ?? 8);
          items.forEach((item) => {
            if (item.kind === 'text') {
              item.layout.lines.forEach((line) => {
                const lineW = line.reduce((s, r) => s + r.width, 0);
                let lineOffsetX = 0;
                if (cell.align === 'center') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, (cellW - lineW) / 2);
                else if (cell.align === 'right') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, cellW - lineW);

                line.forEach((run) => {
                  const rx = cellX + (cell.padding?.left ?? 10) + lineOffsetX + run.x;
                  const ry = itemY + run.y + run.actualFontSize;
                  svgContent += `<text x="${rx}" y="${ry}" font-size="${run.actualFontSize}" font-family="${run.fontFamily || el.fontFamily}" font-weight="${run.bold || cell.isHeader ? 'bold' : 'normal'}" fill="${run.color || '#000'}">${escapeXml(run.text)}</text>`;
                });
              });
              itemY += item.height + 8;
            }
          });
        }
      });

      cursorY += layout.totalHeight;
    }
  }

  return svgContent;
}

/* ---------- Shape ---------- */

export function toSvgShape(el: ShapeElement): string {
  const { x, y, width: w, height: h, fill, stroke, strokeWidth, text, textFill, fontSize } = el;
  const fillAttr = fill === 'transparent' ? 'none' : fill;
  let shapeSvg = '';

  if (el.shape === 'rect' || el.shape === 'roundRect') {
    const rx = el.shape === 'roundRect' ? (el.radius || 16) : 0;
    shapeSvg = `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" rx="${rx}" fill="${fillAttr}" stroke="${stroke}" stroke-width="${strokeWidth}" />`;
  } else if (el.shape === 'circle') {
    shapeSvg = `<circle cx="${x + w / 2}" cy="${y + h / 2}" r="${Math.min(w, h) / 2}" fill="${fillAttr}" stroke="${stroke}" stroke-width="${strokeWidth}" />`;
  } else if (el.shape === 'triangle') {
    shapeSvg = `<polygon points="${x + w / 2},${y} ${x + w},${y + h} ${x},${y + h}" fill="${fillAttr}" stroke="${stroke}" stroke-width="${strokeWidth}" />`;
  } else {
    shapeSvg = `<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="${fillAttr}" stroke="${stroke}" stroke-width="${strokeWidth}" />`;
  }

  if (text) {
    const textAnchor = el.textAlign === 'center' ? 'middle' : el.textAlign === 'right' ? 'end' : 'start';
    const tx = el.textAlign === 'center' ? x + w / 2 : el.textAlign === 'right' ? x + w - 8 : x + 8;
    const ty = y + h / 2 + (fontSize || 18) / 2;
    shapeSvg += `<text x="${tx}" y="${ty}" font-size="${fontSize || 18}" font-family="Arial" fill="${textFill || '#000'}" text-anchor="${textAnchor}">${escapeXml(text)}</text>`;
  }

  return shapeSvg;
}

/* ---------- Line ---------- */

export function toSvgLine(el: LineElement): string {
  const pts = el.points.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v + el.x : v + el.y));
  const pointsStr: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) pointsStr.push(`${pts[i]},${pts[i + 1]}`);

  return `<polyline points="${pointsStr.join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="${el.stroke}" stroke-width="${el.strokeWidth}" />`;
}

/* ---------- Table (Standalone) ---------- */

export function toSvgTable(el: TableElement): string {
  const { x, y, width: w, height: h, rows, cols, data, borderColor, headerBg, fontSize } = el;
  const cellW = w / cols;
  const cellH = h / rows;
  let svg = '';

  for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
      const bg = r === 0 ? headerBg : '#ffffff';
      const cx = x + c * cellW;
      const cy = y + r * cellH;

      svg += `<rect x="${cx}" y="${cy}" width="${cellW}" height="${cellH}" fill="${bg}" stroke="${borderColor}" stroke-width="1" />`;

      const text = data[r]?.[c] || '';
      if (text) {
        svg += `<text x="${cx + 4}" y="${cy + fontSize + 4}" font-size="${fontSize}" font-family="Arial" fill="#111">${escapeXml(text)}</text>`;
      }
    }
  }
  return svg;
}

/* ---------- Image / Footer / Placeholder ---------- */

export function toSvgImage(el: ImageElement): string {
  return `<image x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" href="${escapeXml(el.src)}" />`;
}

export function toSvgFooter(el: FooterElement): string {
  return `
    <rect x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" fill="${el.bgColor}" />
    <text x="${el.x + el.width / 2}" y="${el.y + el.height / 2 + el.fontSize / 2}" font-size="${el.fontSize}" font-family="Arial" fill="${el.fill}" text-anchor="middle">${escapeXml(el.text)}</text>
  `;
}

export function toSvgPlaceholder(el: PlaceholderElement): string {
  return `
    <rect x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" fill="${el.background}" stroke="#6366f1" stroke-dasharray="6,4" />
    <text x="${el.x + 8}" y="${el.y + el.fontSize + 8}" font-size="${el.fontSize}" font-family="${el.fontFamily}" fill="${el.fill}">${escapeXml(el.sample || '—')}</text>
  `;
}

/* ---------- Fill ---------- */

export function toSvgFill(el: FillElement): string {
  const pts = el.points.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v + el.x : v + el.y));
  const pointsStr: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) pointsStr.push(`${pts[i]},${pts[i + 1]}`);
  return `<polygon points="${pointsStr.join(' ')}" fill="${el.fill}" opacity="${el.opacity || 0.6}" />`;
}

/* ---------- Connector ---------- */

export function toSvgConnector(el: ConnectorElement, allElements: CanvasElement[]): string {
  const { from, to } = resolveConnectorEnds(el, allElements);
  if (!from || !to) return '';

  const obstacles = allElements.filter((e) =>
    e.id !== el.fromId && e.id !== el.toId
    && e.type !== 'connector' && e.type !== 'fill' && e.type !== 'line'
    && !e.locked,
  );

  const points = routeConnector(from, el.fromAnchor, to, el.toAnchor, el.routing, obstacles);
  const pointsStr: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i += 2) pointsStr.push(`${points[i]},${points[i + 1]}`);

  let svg = `<polyline points="${pointsStr.join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="${el.stroke}" stroke-width="${el.strokeWidth}" />`;

  if (el.isArrow) {
    const n = points.length;
    const x1 = points[n - 4], y1 = points[n - 3];
    const x2 = points[n - 2], y2 = points[n - 1];
    const angle = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);
    const len = 12;
    const spread = 0.45;

    const arrowHead = [
      x2 + len * Math.cos(angle + Math.PI - spread),
      y2 + len * Math.sin(angle + Math.PI - spread),
      x2, y2,
      x2 + len * Math.cos(angle + Math.PI + spread),
      y2 + len * Math.sin(angle + Math.PI + spread),
    ];

    svg += `<polyline points="${arrowHead.join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="${el.stroke}" stroke-width="${el.strokeWidth}" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" />`;
  }

  return svg;
}

/* ---------- Diagram ---------- */

export function toSvgDiagram(el: DiagramElement): string {
  return el.svg || `<rect x="${el.x}" y="${el.y}" width="${el.width}" height="${el.height}" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="#cbd5e1" stroke-dasharray="6,4" />`;
}

/* ---------- Stamp ---------- */

export function toSvgStamp(el: StampElement): string {
  let svg = `<g transform="translate(${el.x}, ${el.y})">`;

  if (el.shapes) {
    el.shapes.forEach((s) => {
      if (s.kind === 'rect') {
        svg += `<rect x="${s.x}" y="${s.y}" width="${s.width}" height="${s.height}" fill="${s.fill || 'transparent'}" stroke="${s.stroke}" stroke-width="${s.strokeWidth}" />`;
      } else if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') {
        svg += `<ellipse cx="${s.x + s.width / 2}" cy="${s.y + s.height / 2}" rx="${s.width / 2}" ry="${s.height / 2}" fill="${s.fill || 'transparent'}" stroke="${s.stroke}" stroke-width="${s.strokeWidth}" />`;
      } else if (s.kind === 'line') {
        const pts = s.points || [0, 0, s.width, 0];
        svg += `<polyline points="${pts.join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="${s.stroke}" stroke-width="${s.strokeWidth}" />`;
      }
    });
  }

  if (el.texts) {
    el.texts.forEach((t) => {
      if (t.mode === 'straight') {
        svg += `<text x="${t.x}" y="${t.y + t.fontSize}" font-size="${t.fontSize}" font-family="${t.fontFamily}" fill="${t.fill}" text-anchor="middle">${escapeXml(t.text)}</text>`;
      }
    });
  }

  svg += `</g>`;
  return svg;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\lib\svg\sanitize.ts
SIZE: 815 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/* ============================================================
   sanitize.ts
   NFR-SEC-04: РЈРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ РѕРїР°СЃРЅС‹С… С‚РµРіРѕРІ Рё Р°С‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚РѕРІ РёР· SVG.
   ============================================================ */

export function sanitizeSvg(svg: string): string {
  if (!svg) return '';
  return svg
    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј <script>
    .replace(/<script\b[^<]*(?:(?!<\/script>)<[^<]*)*<\/script>/gi, '')
    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј on* Р°С‚СЂРёР±СѓС‚С‹ (onclick, onload Рё С‚.Рґ.)
    .replace(/\s+on\w+\s*=\s*["'][^"']*["']/gi, '')
    .replace(/\s+on\w+\s*=\s*[^\s>]+/gi, '')
    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј javascript: РІ href Рё xlink:href
    .replace(/href\s*=\s*["']javascript:[^"']*["']/gi, 'href="#"')
    .replace(/xlink:href\s*=\s*["']javascript:[^"']*["']/gi, 'xlink:href="#"');
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/svg/serialize.ts
   F-SVG-01, F-SVG-02: Сборка полного SVG слайда.
   ============================================================ */

import type { Slide } from '@/shared/types';
import { escapeXml } from './escape';
import {
  toSvgText, toSvgShape, toSvgLine, toSvgTable, toSvgImage,
  toSvgFooter, toSvgPlaceholder, toSvgFill, toSvgConnector,
  toSvgDiagram, toSvgStamp
} from './exporters';

export function serializeSlideToSvg(slide: Slide): string {
  const { width, height, background, elements } = slide;

  // F-SVG-02: Строгий порядок отрисовки
  const fills = elements.filter((e) => e.type === 'fill');
  const connectors = elements.filter((e) => e.type === 'connector');
  const others = elements.filter((e) => e.type !== 'fill' && e.type !== 'connector');

  let elementsSvg = '';

  // 1. Заливки (fills) — opacity 0.6
  fills.forEach((el) => {
    if (el.type === 'fill') elementsSvg += toSvgFill(el);
  });

  // 2. Коннекторы (connectors)
  connectors.forEach((el) => {
    if (el.type === 'connector') elementsSvg += toSvgConnector(el, elements);
  });

  // 3. Остальные элементы
  others.forEach((el) => {
    switch (el.type) {
      case 'text': elementsSvg += toSvgText(el); break;
      case 'shape': elementsSvg += toSvgShape(el); break;
      case 'line': elementsSvg += toSvgLine(el); break;
      case 'table': elementsSvg += toSvgTable(el); break;
      case 'image': elementsSvg += toSvgImage(el); break;
      case 'footer': elementsSvg += toSvgFooter(el); break;
      case 'placeholder': elementsSvg += toSvgPlaceholder(el); break;
      case 'diagram': elementsSvg += toSvgDiagram(el); break;
      case 'stamp': elementsSvg += toSvgStamp(el); break;
      default: break;
    }
  });

  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" width="${width}" height="${height}" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}">
  <rect width="${width}" height="${height}" fill="${escapeXml(background)}" />
  ${elementsSvg}
</svg>`;
}

// src/presentation/components/Canvas.tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Stage, Layer, Rect, Transformer } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { Slide, AnchorSide, CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { useStore } from '@/shared/store/store';
import {
  useDocumentStore,
  useSelectionStore,
  useModalsStore,
} from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { ElementRenderer } from './ElementRenderer';
import { GridLayer } from './GridLayer';
import { CanvasPreviewLayer } from './canvas/CanvasPreviewLayer';
import { useCanvasViewport } from '../hooks/canvas/useCanvasViewport';
import {
  useCanvasDrag,
  useCanvasRubberBand,
  useCanvasFillMode,
  useCanvasConnectorDraft,
  useCanvasDrop,
  useCanvasHoverAnchor,
  useCanvasDblClick,
} from '../hooks/canvas';
import type { ElementSelectEvent } from './elements/types';

interface Props {
  slide: Slide;
  zoom: number;
  onZoomChange: (z: number | ((prev: number) => number)) => void;
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
}

function isMultiSelectEvent(evt: unknown): boolean {
  if (!(evt instanceof MouseEvent) && !(evt instanceof PointerEvent)) return false;
  return evt.shiftKey || evt.metaKey || evt.ctrlKey;
}

export const Canvas: React.FC<Props> = ({ slide, zoom, onZoomChange, wrapRef }) => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();

  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);
  const isDraggingRef = useRef(false);

  const elementsRef = useRef(slide.elements);
  if (elementsRef.current !== slide.elements) elementsRef.current = slide.elements;

  const selectedIds = useStore(selStore, (s) => s.selectedIds);
  const editing = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.editing);
  const fillMode = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.fillMode);
  const showGrid = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.showGrid);
  const showMargins = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.showMargins);

  const viewport = useCanvasViewport({ wrapRef, zoom, onZoomChange, stageRef });
  const drag = useCanvasDrag({ docStore, selStore, modalsStore, slide });
  const rubber = useCanvasRubberBand({ selStore });
  const fill = useCanvasFillMode({ docStore, modalsStore, slide, stageRef });
  const draft = useCanvasConnectorDraft({ docStore, slide });
  const drop = useCanvasDrop({ docStore, stageRef, zoom });
  const hover = useCanvasHoverAnchor({ slide });
  const dbl = useCanvasDblClick({ modalsStore, zoom });

  const singleSelection = selectedIds.length === 1;
  const selectedEl = singleSelection
    ? slide.elements.find((e) => e.id === selectedIds[0]) ?? null
    : null;

  // Ключ размеров для принудительного обновления Transformer
  const sizeKey = selectedEl ? `${selectedEl.width}x${selectedEl.height}` : '';
  const prevSizeKey = useRef('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!tr || !stage) return;
    if (!singleSelection) { tr.nodes([]); return; }

    const id = selectedIds[0];
    for (const l of stage.getLayers()) {
      const n = l.findOne('#' + id);
      if (n) {
        tr.nodes([n]);
        if (prevSizeKey.current !== sizeKey) {
          tr.forceUpdate();
          tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw();
          prevSizeKey.current = sizeKey;
        }
        return;
      }
    }
    tr.nodes([]);
  }, [selectedIds, singleSelection, sizeKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onGlobalDblClick = (): void => {
      if (viewport.isPanning) return;
      if (fill.points().length >= 6) fill.closePolygon();
    };
    window.addEventListener('dblclick', onGlobalDblClick);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('dblclick', onGlobalDblClick);
  }, [fill, viewport.isPanning]);

  const handleElementDblClick = useCallback(
    (el: CanvasElement, e: ElementSelectEvent | undefined) => {
      if (!e) return;
      const stage = stageRef.current;
      if (!stage) return;
      const evt = e.evt;
      const clientX = 'clientX' in evt ? evt.clientX : 0;
      const clientY = 'clientY' in evt ? evt.clientY : 0;
      const rect = stage.container().getBoundingClientRect();
      dbl.handle(el, {
        viewportLeft: rect.left,
        viewportTop: rect.top,
        clientX,
        clientY,
      });
    },
    [dbl],
  );

  const handleStartDraft = useCallback(
    (id: string, side: AnchorSide, _clientX: number, _clientY: number) => {
      if (viewport.isPanning) return;
      const stage = stageRef.current;
      if (!stage) return;
      const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
      if (!pointer) return;
      draft.startDraft(id, side, pointer.x / zoom, pointer.y / zoom);
    },
    [zoom, draft, viewport.isPanning],
  );

  const onStageDown = useCallback(
    (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => {
      if (viewport.isPanning) {
        viewport.handleMouseDown(e.evt);
        return;
      }
      const stage = e.target.getStage();
      const isStage = e.target === stage;
      const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
      if (!p) return;

      const wx = p.x / zoom;
      const wy = p.y / zoom;

      if (fillMode) {
        if (isStage) fill.applyFill(wx, wy);
        return;
      }

      if (isStage) {
        selStore.clear();
        modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);
        rubber.begin(wx, wy);
      }
    },
    [zoom, fillMode, fill, selStore, modalsStore, rubber, viewport],
  );

  const onStageMove = useCallback(
    (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => {
      if (viewport.isPanning) return;
      if (isDraggingRef.current) return;
      const stage = e.target.getStage();
      const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
      if (!p) return;
      const wx = p.x / zoom;
      const wy = p.y / zoom;
      draft.update(wx, wy);
      rubber.update(wx, wy);
      hover.update(e, wx, wy);
    },
    [zoom, draft, rubber, hover, viewport.isPanning],
  );

  const onStageUp = useCallback(
    (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => {
      if (viewport.isPanning) return;
      if (draft.isDrafting()) {
        const stage = e.target.getStage();
        const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
        if (p) draft.commit(p.x / zoom, p.y / zoom);
      }
      if (rubber.isActive()) rubber.finish(slide.elements);
    },
    [zoom, draft, rubber, slide.elements, viewport.isPanning],
  );

  return (
    <div
      className="stage-wrap"
      data-fill-mode={fillMode || undefined}
      role="presentation"
      aria-label={`Холст, ${slide.elements.length} элементов`}
      onDragOver={drop.onDragOver}
      onDrop={drop.onDrop}
    >
      <Stage
        ref={stageRef}
        width={slide.width * zoom}
        height={slide.height * zoom}
        scaleX={zoom}
        scaleY={zoom}
        onMouseDown={onStageDown}
        onMouseMove={onStageMove}
        onMouseUp={onStageUp}
        onContextMenu={(e) => {
          e.evt.preventDefault();
          modalsStore.setContextMenu({
            x: e.evt.clientX,
            y: e.evt.clientY,
            elementId: null,
          });
        }}
        style={{
          cursor: viewport.isPanning ? 'grab' : fillMode ? 'crosshair' : 'default',
        }}
      >
        <Layer listening={false}>
          <Rect
            x={0}
            y={0}
            width={slide.width}
            height={slide.height}
            fill={slide.background}
          />
          {showGrid && <GridLayer width={slide.width} height={slide.height} />}
          {showMargins && (
            <Rect
              x={slide.padding.left}
              y={slide.padding.top}
              width={Math.max(0, slide.width - slide.padding.left - slide.padding.right)}
              height={Math.max(0, slide.height - slide.padding.top - slide.padding.bottom)}
              stroke="#3b82f6"
              strokeWidth={1 / zoom}
              dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
              listening={false}
            />
          )}
        </Layer>

        <Layer name="elements-layer" listening={!fillMode}>
          {slide.elements.map((el) => (
            <ElementRenderer
              key={el.id}
              element={el}
              elements={el.type === 'connector' ? elementsRef.current : undefined}
              isSelected={selectedIds.includes(el.id)}
              isEditing={editing?.id === el.id}
              onSelect={(e) => {
                if (viewport.isPanning) return;
                const nativeEvt = e && 'evt' in e ? e.evt : undefined;
                const multi = isMultiSelectEvent(nativeEvt);
                const current = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
                if (multi) selStore.select(el.id, true);
                else if (!current.includes(el.id)) selStore.select(el.id);
              }}
              onUpdate={(patch, newElement) => {
                docStore.commit((d) => {
                  const s = d.slides.find((x) => x.id === d.activeId);
                  const target = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === el.id);
                  if (target) Object.assign(target, patch);
                  if (newElement && s) s.elements.push(newElement);
                }, `upd-${el.id}`);
              }}
              onDblClick={(e) => handleElementDblClick(el, e)}
              onDragStart={(e) => {
                if (draft.isDrafting() || viewport.isPanning) {
                  e.target.stopDrag();
                  return;
                }
                isDraggingRef.current = true;
                drag.onDragStart(el, e);
              }}
              onDragMove={(e) => drag.onDragMove(e)}
              onDragEnd={(e) => {
                drag.onDragEnd(e);
                isDraggingRef.current = false;
              }}
              onContextMenu={(ev: Konva.KonvaEventObject<PointerEvent>) => {
                ev.evt.preventDefault();
                if (!selectedIds.includes(el.id)) selStore.select(el.id);
                modalsStore.setContextMenu({
                  x: ev.evt.clientX,
                  y: ev.evt.clientY,
                  elementId: el.id,
                });
              }}
            />
          ))}
        </Layer>

        <Layer name="transformer-layer" listening={!fillMode}>
          {singleSelection && (
            <Transformer
              ref={trRef}
              rotateEnabled
              resizeEnabled
              keepRatio={false}
              draggable={false}
              rotationSnaps={[
                0, 15, 30, 45, 60, 75, 90, 105, 120, 135, 150, 165,
                180, 195, 210, 225, 240, 255, 270, 285, 300, 315, 330, 345,
              ]}
              rotationSnapTolerance={6}
              borderStroke="#f59e0b"
              anchorStroke="#f59e0b"
              anchorFill="#fbbf24"
              anchorSize={10}
              shouldOverdrawWholeArea={false}
              enabledAnchors={
                selectedEl?.type === 'text'
                  ? ['middle-left', 'middle-right']
                  : undefined
              }
              boundBoxFunc={(oldBox, newBox) => {
                if (newBox.width < 20 || newBox.height < 20) return oldBox;
                return newBox;
              }}
              onTransformEnd={(e) => {
                const node = e.target;
                const id = node.id();
                const sx = node.scaleX();
                const sy = node.scaleY();
                const rotation = node.rotation();
                if (sx === 1 && sy === 1 && rotation === 0) return;
                node.scaleX(1);
                node.scaleY(1);
                docStore.commit((d) => {
                  const s = d.slides.find((x) => x.id === d.activeId);
                  const target = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === id);
                  if (!target) return;
                  target.x = node.x();
                  target.y = node.y();
                  target.rotation = rotation;
                  if (target.type === 'text') {
                    target.width = Math.max(20, target.width * sx);
                  } else {
                    target.width = Math.max(20, target.width * sx);
                    target.height = Math.max(20, target.height * sy);
                  }
                  if (target.type === 'line') {
                    target.points = target.points.map((v, i) =>
                      i % 2 === 0 ? v * sx : v * sy,
                    );
                  }
                }, `trans-${id}`);
              }}
            />
          )}
        </Layer>

        <CanvasPreviewLayer
          zoom={zoom}
          fillPoints={fill.points()}
          draft={draft.current()}
          elements={slide.elements}
          rubberBand={rubber.ref()}
          onStartDraft={handleStartDraft}
          ghostRect={draft.getGhostRect()}
          selectedIds={selectedIds}
          slideWidth={slide.width}
          slideHeight={slide.height}
          listening={!fillMode}
        />
      </Stage>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { Layer, Line, Rect, Circle } from 'react-konva';
import type { CanvasElement, AnchorSide } from '@/shared/types';
import type { Rect as RectType } from '@/lib/connectors';
import { getAnchorPoint } from '@/lib/connectors';
import { guidesStore } from '../../stores/GuidesStore';
import { dragLiveStore } from '../../stores/DragLiveStore';
import { hoverStore } from '../../stores/HoverStore';

interface DraftState {
  fromId: string;
  fromSide: AnchorSide;
  toX: number;
  toY: number;
}

interface RubberBandBox {
  x1: number;
  y1: number;
  x2: number;
  y2: number;
}

interface Props {
  zoom: number;
  fillPoints: number[];
  draft: DraftState | null;
  elements: CanvasElement[];
  rubberBand: RubberBandBox | null;
  onStartDraft: (id: string, side: AnchorSide, clientX: number, clientY: number) => void;
  ghostRect: RectType | null;
  selectedIds: string[];
  slideWidth: number;
  slideHeight: number;
  listening?: boolean;
}

export const CanvasPreviewLayer: React.FC<Props> = ({
  zoom, fillPoints, draft, elements, rubberBand,
  onStartDraft, ghostRect, selectedIds, slideWidth, slideHeight, listening = true,
}) => {
  // REG-B3: hover читаем из микро-store, а не из props.
  const hoveredId = useSyncExternalStore(hoverStore.subscribe, hoverStore.getSnapshot);
  const guides = useSyncExternalStore(guidesStore.subscribe, guidesStore.getSnapshot);
  useSyncExternalStore(dragLiveStore.subscribe, dragLiveStore.getSnapshot);

  const from = draft ? elements.find((x) => x.id === draft.fromId) : null;
  const fromPoint = from && draft ? getAnchorPoint(from, draft.fromSide) : null;
  const hoveredEl = hoveredId
    ? elements.find((x) => x.id === hoveredId) ?? null
    : null;

  const showAnchors =
    !!hoveredEl && !draft && !rubberBand && !selectedIds.includes(hoveredEl.id);

  let groupBBox: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number } | null = null;
  if (selectedIds.length > 1) {
    const chosen = selectedIds
      .map((id) => {
        const el = elements.find((e) => e.id === id);
        if (!el) return null;
        const live = dragLiveStore.get(id);
        return live ? { ...el, ...live } : el;
      })
      .filter((e): e is CanvasElement => !!e);
    if (chosen.length > 1) {
      const minX = Math.min(...chosen.map((e) => e.x));
      const minY = Math.min(...chosen.map((e) => e.y));
      const maxX = Math.max(...chosen.map((e) => e.x + e.width));
      const maxY = Math.max(...chosen.map((e) => e.y + e.height));
      groupBBox = { x: minX, y: minY, w: maxX - minX, h: maxY - minY };
    }
  }

  return (
    <Layer listening={listening}>
      {guides.v.map((x, i) => (
        <Line
          key={`gv-${i}`}
          points={[x, 0, x, slideHeight]}
          stroke="#ef4444"
          strokeWidth={1 / zoom}
          dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
          listening={false}
        />
      ))}
      {guides.h.map((y, i) => (
        <Line
          key={`gh-${i}`}
          points={[0, y, slideWidth, y]}
          stroke="#ef4444"
          strokeWidth={1 / zoom}
          dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
          listening={false}
        />
      ))}

      {groupBBox && (
        <Rect
          x={groupBBox.x}
          y={groupBBox.y}
          width={groupBBox.w}
          height={groupBBox.h}
          stroke="#f59e0b"
          strokeWidth={1.5 / zoom}
          dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
          listening={false}
        />
      )}

      {fillPoints.length >= 2 && (
        <Line
          points={fillPoints}
          stroke="#6366f1"
          strokeWidth={1.5 / zoom}
          dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
          listening={false}
        />
      )}
      {fillPoints.length >= 2 && Array.from({ length: fillPoints.length / 2 }).map((_, i) => (
        <Rect
          key={`fp-${i}`}
          x={(fillPoints[i * 2] ?? 0) - 3 / zoom}
          y={(fillPoints[i * 2 + 1] ?? 0) - 3 / zoom}
          width={6 / zoom}
          height={6 / zoom}
          fill="#6366f1"
          listening={false}
        />
      ))}

      {draft && fromPoint && (
        <Line
          points={[fromPoint.x, fromPoint.y, draft.toX, draft.toY]}
          stroke="#6366f1"
          strokeWidth={1.5 / zoom}
          dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
          listening={false}
        />
      )}

      {ghostRect && (
        <Rect
          x={ghostRect.x}
          y={ghostRect.y}
          width={ghostRect.width}
          height={ghostRect.height}
          stroke="#6366f1"
          strokeWidth={2 / zoom}
          dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
          fill="rgba(99,102,241,0.1)"
          listening={false}
        />
      )}

      {showAnchors && (['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left'] as AnchorSide[]).map((side) => {
        const pos = getAnchorPoint(hoveredEl, side);
        return (
          <Circle
            key={side}
            x={pos.x}
            y={pos.y}
            radius={6 / zoom}
            fill="#fbbf24"
            stroke="#f59e0b"
            strokeWidth={1.5 / zoom}
            onMouseDown={(e) => {
              e.cancelBubble = true;
              onStartDraft(hoveredEl.id, side, e.evt.clientX, e.evt.clientY);
            }}
            onMouseEnter={(e) => {
              const stage = e.target.getStage();
              if (stage) stage.container().style.cursor = 'crosshair';
            }}
            onMouseLeave={(e) => {
              const stage = e.target.getStage();
              if (stage) stage.container().style.cursor = 'default';
            }}
          />
        );
      })}

      {rubberBand && (
        <Rect
          x={Math.min(rubberBand.x1, rubberBand.x2)}
          y={Math.min(rubberBand.y1, rubberBand.y2)}
          width={Math.abs(rubberBand.x2 - rubberBand.x1)}
          height={Math.abs(rubberBand.y2 - rubberBand.y1)}
          fill="rgba(99,102,241,0.12)"
          stroke="#6366f1"
          strokeWidth={1.5 / zoom}
          dash={[6 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
          listening={false}
        />
      )}
    </Layer>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';

interface ContextMenuProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  elementId: string | null;
  selectedElements: CanvasElement[];
  onClose: () => void;
  onCut: () => void;
  onCopy: () => void;
  onPaste: () => void;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  onBringToFront: () => void;
  onBringForward: () => void;
  onSendBackward: () => void;
  onSendToBack: () => void;
  onCopyStyle: () => void;
  onApplyStyle: () => void;
  canPaste: boolean;
  hasStyleCopied: boolean;
}

export const ContextMenu: React.FC<ContextMenuProps> = ({
  x, y, selectedElements, onClose, onCut, onCopy, onPaste, onDuplicate,
  onDelete, onBringToFront, onBringForward, onSendBackward, onSendToBack,
  onCopyStyle, onApplyStyle, canPaste, hasStyleCopied,
}) => {
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    };
    const handleEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  const clampedX = Math.min(x, window.innerWidth - 230);
  const clampedY = Math.min(y, window.innerHeight - 350);
  const hasSelection = selectedElements.length > 0;
  const isLine = selectedElements.length === 1 && selectedElements[0].type === 'line';

  return (
    <div ref={menuRef} className="context-menu" style={{ left: clampedX, top: clampedY }}>
      {hasSelection && (
        <>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onCut}>
            <span>Вырезать</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+X</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onCopy}>
            <span>Копировать</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+C</span>
          </div>
          <div className={`context-menu-item ${!canPaste ? 'disabled' : ''}`} onClick={canPaste ? onPaste : undefined}>
            <span>Вставить</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+V</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onDuplicate}>
            <span>Дублировать</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Ctrl+D</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-divider" />
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onCopyStyle}>
            <span>Копировать стиль</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Alt+C</span>
          </div>
          <div className={`context-menu-item ${!hasStyleCopied ? 'disabled' : ''}`} onClick={hasStyleCopied ? onApplyStyle : undefined}>
            <span>Применить стиль</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Alt+V</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-divider" />
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onBringToFront}>
            <span>На самый верх</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onBringForward}>
            <span>Переместить вперед</span>
            <span className="shortcut">]</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onSendBackward}>
            <span>Переместить назад</span>
            <span className="shortcut">[</span>
          </div>
          <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onSendToBack}>
            <span>На самый низ</span>
          </div>
          {isLine && (
            <>
              <div className="context-menu-divider" />
              <div className="context-menu-item">
                <span>Добавить точку</span>
              </div>
              <div className="context-menu-item">
                <span>Удалить последнюю точку</span>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
          <div className="context-menu-divider" />
          <div className="context-menu-item danger" onClick={onDelete}>
            <span>Удалить</span>
            <span className="shortcut">Del</span>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { DataRoot, DataField, DataFieldType } from '@/shared/types';
import { useStore } from '@/shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { generateId } from '@/shared/utils/id';
import { download } from '@/lib/download';

const FIELD_TYPES: DataFieldType[] = [
  'string', 'number', 'date', 'boolean', 'image', 'object', 'array',
];

const FIELD_TYPE_LABEL: Record<DataFieldType, string> = {
  string: 'Строка',
  number: 'Число',
  date: 'Дата',
  boolean: 'Да/Нет',
  image: 'Картинка',
  object: 'Объект',
  array: 'Массив',
};

function buildDemoData(): DataRoot[] {
  return [
    {
      id: generateId(),
      name: 'event',
      fields: [
        { id: generateId(), name: 'title',    type: 'string', sample: 'ГАЛА-КОНЦЕРТ' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'subtitle', type: 'string', sample: 'Симфонический оркестр' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'day',      type: 'string', sample: '15' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'month',    type: 'string', sample: 'МАРТА' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'time',     type: 'string', sample: '19:00' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'venue',    type: 'string', sample: 'Большой театр' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'address',  type: 'string', sample: 'Театральная пл., 1' },
      ],
    },
    {
      id: generateId(),
      name: 'ticket',
      fields: [
        { id: generateId(), name: 'row',    type: 'string', sample: '12' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'seat',   type: 'string', sample: '8' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'sector', type: 'string', sample: 'Партер' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'price',  type: 'string', sample: '4500 ₽' },
      ],
    },
    {
      id: generateId(),
      name: 'applicant',
      fields: [
        { id: generateId(), name: 'fio',     type: 'string', sample: 'Иванов Иван Иванович' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'address', type: 'string', sample: 'г. Москва, ул. Пушкина, д. 1' },
        { id: generateId(), name: 'phone',   type: 'string', sample: '+7 (999) 123-45-67' },
      ],
    },
  ];
}

export const DataPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const data = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.data);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const commit = (recipe: (draft: DataRoot[]) => void) =>
    docStore.commit((draft) => recipe(draft.data), 'data-edit');

  const addRoot = () => {
    commit((d) => {
      d.push({ id: generateId(), name: 'Новый корень', fields: [] });
    });
  };

  const loadDemo = () => {
    commit((d) => {
      d.length = 0;
      d.push(...buildDemoData());
    });
  };

  const updateRoot = (rootId: string, patch: Partial<DataRoot>) => {
    commit((d) => {
      const r = d.find((x) => x.id === rootId);
      if (r) Object.assign(r, patch);
    });
  };

  const removeRoot = (rootId: string) => {
    commit((d) => {
      const i = d.findIndex((x) => x.id === rootId);
      if (i >= 0) d.splice(i, 1);
    });
  };

  const updateField = (rootId: string, fieldId: string, patch: Partial<DataField>) => {
    const walk = (fields: DataField[]): boolean => {
      for (const f of fields) {
        if (f.id === fieldId) {
          Object.assign(f, patch);
          return true;
        }
        if (f.children && walk(f.children)) return true;
      }
      return false;
    };
    commit((d) => {
      const r = d.find((x) => x.id === rootId);
      if (r) walk(r.fields);
    });
  };

  const addField = (rootId: string, parentId?: string) => {
    const newField: DataField = {
      id: generateId(),
      name: 'Поле',
      type: 'string',
      sample: '',
    };
    commit((d) => {
      const r = d.find((x) => x.id === rootId);
      if (!r) return;
      if (parentId) {
        const walk = (fields: DataField[]): boolean => {
          for (const f of fields) {
            if (f.id === parentId) {
              (f.children ??= []).push(newField);
              return true;
            }
            if (f.children && walk(f.children)) return true;
          }
          return false;
        };
        walk(r.fields);
      } else {
        r.fields.push(newField);
      }
    });
  };

  const removeField = (rootId: string, fieldId: string) => {
    const walk = (fields: DataField[]): boolean => {
      const i = fields.findIndex((f) => f.id === fieldId);
      if (i >= 0) {
        fields.splice(i, 1);
        return true;
      }
      for (const f of fields) {
        if (f.children && walk(f.children)) return true;
      }
      return false;
    };
    commit((d) => {
      const r = d.find((x) => x.id === rootId);
      if (r) walk(r.fields);
    });
  };

  const onDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, payload: object) => {
    e.dataTransfer.setData('application/json', JSON.stringify(payload));
    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy';
  };

  const handleExport = () => {
    const payload = { version: '1.0', data };
    download(
      JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2),
      `data-schema-${Date.now()}.json`,
      'application/json',
    );
  };

  const handleImport = () => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = '.json';
    input.onchange = async () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      try {
        const text = await file.text();
        const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
        const newData: DataRoot[] = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : (parsed.data || []);
        docStore.commit((draft) => { draft.data = newData; }, 'import-data');
        alert('Данные успешно импортированы');
      } catch {
        alert('Ошибка импорта: невалидный JSON');
      }
    };
    input.click();
  };

  const renderField = (
    root: DataRoot,
    field: DataField,
    path: string,
    depth = 0,
  ): React.ReactNode => {
    const isContainer = field.type === 'object' || field.type === 'array';
    const ownPath = `${path}.${field.name}`;
    const isOpen = expanded[field.id] !== false;

    return (
      <div key={field.id} style={{ paddingLeft: depth * 12, marginBottom: 4 }}>
        {/* Имя и тип */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          {isContainer ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 10 }}
              onClick={() => setExpanded((e) => ({ ...e, [field.id]: !isOpen }))}
            >
              {isOpen ? '▼' : '▶'}
            </button>
          ) : <span style={{ width: 16 }} />}

          <input
            style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 40, padding: '4px 6px', fontSize: 12, border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 4 }}
            value={field.name}
            onChange={(e) => updateField(root.id, field.id, { name: e.target.value })}
          />
          <select
            style={{ width: 80, padding: '4px', fontSize: 11, border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 4 }}
            value={field.type}
            onChange={(e) => updateField(root.id, field.id, { type: e.target.value as DataFieldType })}
          >
            {FIELD_TYPES.map((t) => (
              <option key={t} value={t}>{FIELD_TYPE_LABEL[t]}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <button
            type="button"
            style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#dc2626' }}
            onClick={() => removeField(root.id, field.id)}
            title="Удалить поле"
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Пример и drag-chip */}
        {!isContainer && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, marginLeft: 20, marginTop: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <input
              style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 40, padding: '3px 6px', fontSize: 11, border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: 4, background: '#f8fafc' }}
              placeholder="Пример заполнения"
              value={field.sample}
              onChange={(e) => updateField(root.id, field.id, { sample: e.target.value })}
            />
            <div
              draggable
              onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, {
                kind: 'field',
                path: ownPath,
                fieldType: field.type,
                sample: field.sample,
              })}
              style={{
                display: 'inline-flex',
                padding: '3px 8px',
                background: '#dbeafe',
                border: '1px solid #93c5fd',
                borderRadius: 12,
                fontSize: 11,
                cursor: 'grab',
                flexShrink: 0,
              }}
              title="Перетащите на холст"
            >
              🏷️
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {field.type === 'array' && (
          <div
            draggable
            onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, {
              kind: 'array',
              path: ownPath,
              arrayName: field.name,
              sampleCount: field.sampleCount ?? 3,
              arrayChildren: (field.children ?? []).map((c) => ({ name: c.name, type: c.type })),
            })}
            style={{
              display: 'inline-block',
              margin: '4px 0 4px 22px',
              padding: '3px 8px',
              background: '#fef3c7',
              border: '1px solid #fcd34d',
              borderRadius: 12,
              fontSize: 11,
              cursor: 'grab',
            }}
            title="Перетащите на холст, чтобы создать таблицу"
          >
            🏷️ {ownPath} <span style={{ color: '#92400e', fontSize: 9 }}>→ таблица</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {isContainer && isOpen && (
          <div style={{ marginLeft: 4, borderLeft: '2px solid #e2e8f0', paddingLeft: 4 }}>
            {field.children?.map((c) => renderField(root, c, ownPath, depth + 1))}
            <button
              type="button"
              style={{
                margin: '4px 0 4px 22px',
                padding: '2px 8px',
                fontSize: 10,
                border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1',
                borderRadius: 4,
                background: 'transparent',
                cursor: 'pointer',
              }}
              onClick={() => addField(root.id, field.id)}
            >
              + поле
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 10, overflowY: 'auto', height: '100%' }}>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, marginBottom: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <button
          type="button"
          style={{
            flex: '1 1 100px',
            padding: '6px 4px',
            background: '#6366f1',
            color: '#fff',
            border: 'none',
            borderRadius: 4,
            cursor: 'pointer',
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
          onClick={addRoot}
        >
          + Корень
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          style={{
            flex: '1 1 80px',
            padding: '6px 4px',
            background: '#f8fafc',
            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
            borderRadius: 4,
            cursor: 'pointer',
            fontSize: 12,
          }}
          onClick={loadDemo}
          title="Загрузить демонстрационные данные"
        >
          📋 Пример
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          style={{
            flex: '0 0 36px',
            padding: '6px 0',
            background: '#f8fafc',
            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
            borderRadius: 4,
            cursor: 'pointer',
            fontSize: 14,
          }}
          onClick={handleImport}
          title="Импорт данных (JSON)"
        >
          📥
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          style={{
            flex: '0 0 36px',
            padding: '6px 0',
            background: '#f8fafc',
            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
            borderRadius: 4,
            cursor: 'pointer',
            fontSize: 14,
          }}
          onClick={handleExport}
          title="Экспорт данных (JSON)"
        >
          📤
        </button>
      </div>

      {data.length === 0 && (
        <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8', textAlign: 'center', margin: 20 }}>
          Нет данных. Нажмите «+ Корень» или «📋 Пример», чтобы начать.
        </p>
      )}

      {data.map((root) => {
        const isOpen = expanded[root.id] !== false;
        return (
          <div
            key={root.id}
            style={{
              marginBottom: 8,
              border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
              borderRadius: 6,
              background: '#fff',
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                gap: 4,
                padding: 6,
                background: '#eef2ff',
                borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
              }}
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 10 }}
                onClick={() => setExpanded((e) => ({ ...e, [root.id]: !isOpen }))}
              >
                {isOpen ? '▼' : '▶'}
              </button>
              <input
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  fontSize: 13,
                  padding: '3px 6px',
                  border: '1px solid transparent',

/* ============================================================
   DiagramEditor.tsx — полный

   F-DG-01..11, F-ERR-02.
   draw.io embed-протокол (JSON-сообщения) вместо устаревших строк.
   ============================================================ */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

interface DiagramEditorProps {
  svg: string;
  xml: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (svg: string, xml: string) => void;
}

import { CONFIG } from '@/shared/config';

const DRAWIO_URL = CONFIG.DRAWIO_URL;

export const DiagramEditor: React.FC<DiagramEditorProps> = ({
  svg: _initialSvg,
  xml: initialXml,
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSave,
}) => {
  const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const lastXmlRef = useRef<string>(initialXml);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handleMessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {
      const allowed = (() => {
        try { return event.origin === new URL(DRAWIO_URL).origin; }
        catch { return false; }
      })();
      if (!allowed) return;

      let msg: any;
      try {
        msg = typeof event.data === 'string' ? JSON.parse(event.data) : event.data;
      } catch {
        return;
      }
      if (!msg || typeof msg !== 'object') return;

      if (msg.event === 'init') {
        setIsLoading(false);
        iframeRef.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage(
          JSON.stringify({ action: 'load', autosave: 1, xml: lastXmlRef.current || '<mxGraphModel/>' }),
          '*',
        );
      } else if (msg.event === 'autosave' || msg.event === 'save') {
        if (typeof msg.xml === 'string') lastXmlRef.current = msg.xml;
        iframeRef.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage(
          JSON.stringify({ action: 'export', format: 'svg', xml: lastXmlRef.current }),
          '*',
        );
      } else if (msg.event === 'export') {
        const svgOut = decodeURIComponent(msg.data || msg.xml || '');
        onSave(svgOut, lastXmlRef.current);
      } else if (msg.event === 'exit') {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('message', handleMessage);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('message', handleMessage);
  }, [isOpen, onSave, onClose]);

  const handleSave = useCallback(() => {
    iframeRef.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage(
      JSON.stringify({ action: 'save', xml: lastXmlRef.current }),
      '*',
    );
  }, []);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="diagram-editor-backdrop">
      <div className="diagram-editor-modal">
        <div className="diagram-editor-header">
          <h3>Diagram Editor</h3>
          <div className="diagram-editor-actions">
            <button className="diagram-btn" onClick={handleSave}>💾 Save</button>
            <button className="diagram-btn" onClick={onClose}>✕ Close</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="diagram-editor-body">
          {isLoading && (
            <div className="diagram-loading">
              <div className="diagram-spinner" />
              <p>Loading editor...</p>
            </div>
          )}
          <iframe
            ref={iframeRef}
            src={`${DRAWIO_URL}?embed=1&proto=json&spin=1&configure=1`}
            className="diagram-iframe"
            sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-popups allow-forms"
            allow="fullscreen"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

DiagramEditor.displayName = 'DiagramEditor';

interface Props {

  element,
  onClose,
  onSave,
}) => (
  <DiagramEditor
    svg={element.svg}
    xml={element.xml}
    isOpen
    onClose={onClose}

  />

/* ---------- pure helpers ---------- */

function buildTextPatch(patch: TextStylePatch): Partial<TextElement> {
  const out: Partial<TextElement> = {};
  if (patch.fontSize !== undefined) out.fontSize = patch.fontSize;
  if (patch.fontFamily !== undefined) out.fontFamily = patch.fontFamily;
  if (patch.fontStyle !== undefined) out.fontStyle = patch.fontStyle;
  if (patch.textDecoration !== undefined) out.textDecoration = patch.textDecoration;
  if (patch.fill !== undefined) out.fill = patch.fill;
  if (patch.align !== undefined) out.align = patch.align;
  return out;
}

function buildFooterPatch(patch: TextStylePatch): Partial<FooterElement> {
  const out: Partial<FooterElement> = {};
  if (patch.fontSize !== undefined) out.fontSize = patch.fontSize;
  if (patch.fill !== undefined) out.fill = patch.fill;
  return out;
}

function buildShapePatch(patch: TextStylePatch): Partial<ShapeElement> {
  const out: Partial<ShapeElement> = {};
  if (patch.fontSize !== undefined) out.fontSize = patch.fontSize;
  if (patch.fontFamily !== undefined) out.textFontFamily = patch.fontFamily;
  if (patch.fontStyle !== undefined) out.textFontStyle = patch.fontStyle;
  if (patch.textDecoration !== undefined) out.textDecoration = patch.textDecoration;
  if (patch.fill !== undefined) out.textFill = patch.fill;
  if (patch.align !== undefined) out.textAlign = patch.align;
  return out;
}

function applyTextStylePatch(
  el: CanvasElement,
  patch: TextStylePatch,
): Partial<CanvasElement> {
  if (el.type === 'text') return buildTextPatch(patch);
  if (el.type === 'footer') return buildFooterPatch(patch);
  if (el.type === 'shape') return buildShapePatch(patch);
  return {};
}

/* ---------- layout ---------- */

const EditorLayout: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();

  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);
  const data = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.data);
  const selectedIds = useStore(selStore, (s) => s.selectedIds);
  const editing = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.editing);
  const floatingPos = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.floatingPos);
  const contextMenu = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.contextMenu);
  const stampEditorTarget = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.stampEditorTarget);
  const diagramEditorTarget = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.diagramEditorTarget);

  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [liveMessage, setLiveMessage] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = loadAutoSave();
    if (
      saved &&
      window.confirm(
        `Восстановить сессию от ${new Date(saved.savedAt).toLocaleString()}?`,
      )
    ) {
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        draft.slides = saved.slides;
        draft.activeId = saved.activeSlideId || saved.slides[0].id;
        draft.data = saved.data;
      }, 'restore-autosave');
      clearAutoSave();
    } else if (saved) {
      clearAutoSave();
    }
  }, [docStore]);

  const activeSlide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);

  useAutoFitZoom({
    wrapRef,
    slideWidth: activeSlide?.width ?? 0,
    slideHeight: activeSlide?.height ?? 0,
    setZoom,
  });

  useAutoSave(slides, data, activeId ?? null);
  useEditorShortcuts({ docStore, selStore, modalsStore });

  const commitEditing = useEditingCommit(docStore);
  const commitStamp = useStampCommit(docStore);
  const commitDiagram = useDiagramCommit(docStore);
  const { copy, cut, paste, duplicate } = useClipboardActions(docStore, selStore, modalsStore);
  const { copyStyle, applyStyle } = useStyleClipboard(docStore, selStore, modalsStore);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeSlide) {
      setLiveMessage('');
      return;
    }
    const n = selectedIds.length;
    if (n === 0) setLiveMessage('Ничего не выбрано');
    else if (n === 1) setLiveMessage('Выбран 1 элемент');
    else setLiveMessage(`Выбрано ${n} элементов`);
  }, [selectedIds, activeSlide]);

  const handleToolbarChange = useCallback(
    (patch: TextStylePatch) => {
      if (!editing) return;
      modalsStore.setEditing({
        ...editing,
        ...(patch.fontSize !== undefined && { fontSize: patch.fontSize }),
        ...(patch.fontFamily !== undefined && { fontFamily: patch.fontFamily }),
        ...(patch.fontStyle !== undefined && { fontStyle: patch.fontStyle }),
        ...(patch.textDecoration !== undefined && { textDecoration: patch.textDecoration }),
        ...(patch.fill !== undefined && { fill: patch.fill }),
        ...(patch.align !== undefined && { align: patch.align }),
      });

      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        const el = s?.elements.find((e) => e.id === editing.id);
        if (!el) return;
        Object.assign(el, applyTextStylePatch(el, patch));
      }, `toolbar-${editing.id}`);
    },
    [editing, docStore, modalsStore],
  );

  const handleToolbarCommit = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editing) return;
    modalsStore.closeEditor();
  }, [editing, modalsStore]);

  const selectedElement = useMemo<CanvasElement | null>(
    () => activeSlide?.elements.find((e) => e.id === selectedIds[0]) ?? null,
    [activeSlide, selectedIds],
  );

  const stampElement = useMemo<CanvasElement | null>(() => {
    if (stampEditorTarget == null || !activeSlide) return null;
    return activeSlide.elements.find((e) => e.id === stampEditorTarget) ?? null;
  }, [stampEditorTarget, activeSlide]);

  const diagramElement = useMemo<CanvasElement | null>(() => {
    if (diagramEditorTarget == null || !activeSlide) return null;
    return activeSlide.elements.find((e) => e.id === diagramEditorTarget) ?? null;
  }, [diagramEditorTarget, activeSlide]);

  if (!activeSlide) {
    return <div style={{ padding: 40 }}>Нет активного слайда</div>;
  }

  const contextSelected = activeSlide.elements.filter((e) => selectedIds.includes(e.id));
  const hasStyleCopied = modalsStore.getStyleClipboard() != null;
  const canPaste = modalsStore.getClipboard().length > 0;

  const closeContextMenu = () => {
    modalsStore.setContextMenu(null);
    modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);
  };

  const moveLayer = (dir: 'forward' | 'backward' | 'front' | 'back', targetId?: string) => {
    const id = targetId || selectedIds[0];
    if (!id) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      
      const i = s.elements.findIndex((e) => e.id === id);
      if (i < 0) return;

      const el = s.elements[i];
      s.elements.splice(i, 1);

      if (dir === 'front') {
        s.elements.push(el);
      } else if (dir === 'back') {
        s.elements.unshift(el);
      } else if (dir === 'forward') {
        const j = Math.min(s.elements.length, i + 1);
        s.elements.splice(j, 0, el);
      } else if (dir === 'backward') {
        const j = Math.max(0, i - 1);
        s.elements.splice(j, 0, el);
      }
    }, `layer-${id}`);
  };

  const deleteSelected = () => {
    if (!selectedIds.length) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => {
        if (selectedIds.includes(el.id)) return false;
        if (
          el.type === 'connector' &&
          (selectedIds.includes(el.fromId) || selectedIds.includes(el.toId))
        ) return false;
        return true;
      });
    }, 'delete');
    selStore.clear();
    closeContextMenu();
  };

  const updateSelected = (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    if (!selectedElement) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === selectedElement.id);
      if (el) Object.assign(el, patch);
    }, `update-${selectedElement.id}`);
  };

  const simpleTextEditing =
    !!editing &&
    !editing.isRich &&
    (editing.kind === 'text' || editing.kind === 'shape');

  const showStampEditor =
    !!stampElement &&
    stampElement.type === 'stamp' &&
    isFeatureEnabled('FEATURE_STAMP_EDITOR');

  const showDiagramEditor =
    !!diagramElement &&
    diagramElement.type === 'diagram' &&
    isFeatureEnabled('FEATURE_DIAGRAM');

  return (
    <div className="editor-shell" role="application" aria-label="Редактор билетов">
      {isFeatureEnabled('FEATURE_SHOW_MIGRATION_BANNER') && (
        <MigrationBanner
          rollbackUrl={
            isFeatureEnabled('FEATURE_SHOW_ROLLBACK_BUTTON')
              ? '/?legacy=1'
              : undefined
          }
        />
      )}

      <Toolbar />
      <div className="editor-layout">
        <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Ошибка в левой панели</div>}>
          <LeftPanel />
        </ErrorBoundary>

        <main className="canvas-area" ref={wrapRef}>
          <ZoomBar
            zoom={zoom}
            onChange={setZoom}
            selectedCount={selectedIds.length}
            fillMode={modalsStore.getState().fillMode}
          />
          <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Ошибка в холсте</div>}>
            <Canvas
              slide={activeSlide}
              zoom={zoom}
              onZoomChange={setZoom}
              wrapRef={wrapRef}
            />
          </ErrorBoundary>

          {simpleTextEditing && editing && (
            <Suspense fallback={null}>
              <TextToolbar
                x={editing.x}
                y={editing.y}
                elementHeight={editing.height}
                fontSize={editing.fontSize}
                fontFamily={editing.fontFamily}
                fontStyle={editing.fontStyle}
                textDecoration={editing.textDecoration}
                fill={editing.fill}
                align={editing.align}
                onChange={handleToolbarChange}
                onCommit={handleToolbarCommit}
              />
            </Suspense>
          )}
        </main>

        <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Ошибка в правой панели</div>}>
          <RightPanel />
        </ErrorBoundary>

        <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Ошибка в панели слайдов</div>}>
          <SlidesBar />
        </ErrorBoundary>
      </div>

      {contextMenu && (
        <ContextMenu
          x={contextMenu.x}
          y={contextMenu.y}
          elementId={contextMenu.elementId}
          selectedElements={contextSelected}
          onClose={closeContextMenu}
          onCut={() => { cut(contextSelected); closeContextMenu(); }}
          onCopy={() => { copy(contextSelected); closeContextMenu(); }}
          onPaste={() => { paste(); closeContextMenu(); }}
          onDuplicate={() => { duplicate(contextSelected); closeContextMenu(); }}
          onDelete={deleteSelected}
          onBringToFront={() => { moveLayer('front', contextMenu.elementId || undefined); closeContextMenu(); }}
          onBringForward={() => { moveLayer('forward', contextMenu.elementId || undefined); closeContextMenu(); }}
          onSendBackward={() => { moveLayer('backward', contextMenu.elementId || undefined); closeContextMenu(); }}
          onSendToBack={() => { moveLayer('back', contextMenu.elementId || undefined); closeContextMenu(); }}
          onCopyStyle={() => { if (selectedElement) copyStyle(selectedElement); closeContextMenu(); }}
          onApplyStyle={() => { applyStyle(); closeContextMenu(); }}
          canPaste={canPaste}
          hasStyleCopied={hasStyleCopied}
        />
      )}

      {floatingPos && selectedElement && !editing && (
        <FloatingProps
          x={floatingPos.x}
          y={floatingPos.y}
          element={selectedElement}
          onUpdate={updateSelected}
          onClose={() => modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null)}
          onDelete={deleteSelected}
        />
      )}

      {editing && (
        <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Ошибка в редакторе текста</div>}>
          <Suspense fallback={null}>
            <InlineEditorHost
              key={editing.id}
              editing={editing}
              onClose={() => modalsStore.closeEditor()}
              onCommit={(html, text) => {
                commitEditing(editing, html, text);
                modalsStore.closeEditor();
              }}
            />
          </Suspense>
        </ErrorBoundary>
      )}

      {showStampEditor && stampElement && stampElement.type === 'stamp' && (
        <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Ошибка в редакторе штампа</div>}>
          <Suspense fallback={null}>
            <StampEditorHost
              element={stampElement}
              onClose={() => modalsStore.closeStampEditor()}
              onSave={(patch) => {
                commitStamp(stampElement.id, patch);
                modalsStore.closeStampEditor();
              }}
            />
          </Suspense>
        </ErrorBoundary>
      )}

      {showDiagramEditor && diagramElement && diagramElement.type === 'diagram' && (
        <ErrorBoundary fallback={<div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Ошибка в редакторе диаграммы</div>}>
          <Suspense fallback={null}>
            <DiagramEditorHost
              element={diagramElement}
              onClose={() => modalsStore.closeDiagramEditor()}
              onSave={(patch) => {
                commitDiagram(diagramElement.id, patch);
                modalsStore.closeDiagramEditor();
              }}
            />
          </Suspense>
        </ErrorBoundary>
      )}

      <div
        className="sr-only"
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
        aria-atomic="true"
      >
        {liveMessage}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export const Editor: React.FC = () => {
  const stores = useMemo(
    () => ({
      document: new DocumentStore(),
      selection: new SelectionStore(),
      modals: new ModalsStore(),
    }),
    [],
  );
  return (
    <EditorProvider stores={stores}>
      <EditorLayout />
    </EditorProvider>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { getElementStrategy } from './elements/registry';
import type { ElementSelectEvent } from './elements/types';

export interface ElementRendererProps {
  element: CanvasElement;
  elements?: CanvasElement[];
  isSelected: boolean;
  isEditing?: boolean;
  onSelect: (e?: ElementSelectEvent) => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>, newElement?: CanvasElement) => void;
  onDblClick?: (e?: ElementSelectEvent) => void;
  onContextMenu?: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<PointerEvent>) => void;
  onDragStart?: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onDragMove?: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onDragEnd?: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onCellDblClick?: (r: number, c: number) => void;
}

const NOOP = (): void => {};

const ElementRendererInner: React.FC<ElementRendererProps> = ({
  element, elements, isSelected, isEditing,
  onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick, onContextMenu,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onCellDblClick,
}) => {
  const strategy = getElementStrategy(element.type);
  if (!strategy) return null;

  const View = strategy.KonvaView;
  const dbl = onDblClick ?? NOOP;
  const ctx = onContextMenu ?? NOOP;
  const ds = onDragStart ?? NOOP;
  const dm = onDragMove ?? NOOP;
  const de = onDragEnd ?? NOOP;

  return (
    <View
      el={element as never}
      elements={elements ?? []}
      isSelected={isSelected}
      isEditing={!!isEditing}
      onSelect={onSelect}
      onUpdate={onUpdate as never}
      onDblClick={dbl}
      onContextMenu={ctx}
      onDragStart={ds}
      onDragMove={dm}
      onDragEnd={de}
      onCellDblClick={onCellDblClick}
    />
  );
};

export const ElementRenderer = React.memo(ElementRendererInner, (prev, next) => {
  if (prev.element !== next.element) return false;
  if (prev.isSelected !== next.isSelected) return false;
  if (prev.isEditing !== next.isEditing) return false;
  if (next.element.type === 'connector') {
    if (prev.elements !== next.elements) return false;
  }
  return true;
});

import React, { useMemo, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { Group, Line, Text as KonvaText, Circle } from 'react-konva';
import type { ConnectorElement, CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP } from '@/lib/stamps/render';
import {
  routeConnector, midpointOfPath, resolveConnectorEnds, findNearestAnchor,
} from '@/lib/connectors';
import { dragLiveStore } from '../../stores/DragLiveStore';

interface Props extends CommonProps<ConnectorElement> {
  el: ConnectorElement;
  elements: CanvasElement[];
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<ConnectorElement>) => void;
}

export const ConnectorEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, elements, onSelect, onContextMenu, isSelected, onUpdate,
}) => {
  const dragVersion = useSyncExternalStore(dragLiveStore.subscribe, dragLiveStore.getSnapshot);

  const { from, to } = useMemo(
    () => resolveConnectorEnds(el, elements),
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [el.fromId, el.toId, elements],
  );

  const obstacles = useMemo(
    () => elements.filter((e) =>
      e.type !== 'connector' && e.type !== 'fill' && e.type !== 'line' && !e.locked
    ),
    [elements],
  );

  const [dragState, setDragState] = useState<{ endpoint: 'from' | 'to'; x: number; y: number } | null>(null);

  const points = useMemo(() => {
    if (!from || !to) return null;

    const liveFrom = dragLiveStore.get(el.fromId);
    const liveTo = dragLiveStore.get(el.toId);
    const fromEff = liveFrom ? { ...from, ...liveFrom } : from;
    const toEff = liveTo ? { ...to, ...liveTo } : to;

    let pts = routeConnector(fromEff, el.fromAnchor, toEff, el.toAnchor, el.routing, obstacles);

    if (dragState) {
      pts = [...pts];
      if (dragState.endpoint === 'from') { pts[0] = dragState.x; pts[1] = dragState.y; }
      else { pts[pts.length - 2] = dragState.x; pts[pts.length - 1] = dragState.y; }
    }
    return pts;
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [from, to, el.routing, el.fromAnchor, el.toAnchor, obstacles, dragState, el.fromId, el.toId, dragVersion]);

  const arrowHead = useMemo(() => {
    if (!el.isArrow || !points) return null;
    const n = points.length;
    const x2 = points[n - 2], y2 = points[n - 1];
    let i = n - 4;
    while (i > 0 && Math.hypot(x2 - points[i], y2 - points[i + 1]) < 2) i -= 2;
    const x1 = points[i], y1 = points[i + 1];
    const angle = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);
    const len = 14, spread = 0.4;
    return [
      x2 + len * Math.cos(angle + Math.PI - spread), y2 + len * Math.sin(angle + Math.PI - spread),
      x2, y2,
      x2 + len * Math.cos(angle + Math.PI + spread), y2 + len * Math.sin(angle + Math.PI + spread),
    ];
  }, [el.isArrow, points]);

  const labelPos = useMemo(() => {
    if (!el.label || !points) return null;
    const m = midpointOfPath(points);
    return { x: m.x + (el.labelOffsetX || 0), y: m.y + (el.labelOffsetY || 0) };
  }, [el.label, el.labelOffsetX, el.labelOffsetY, points]);

  if (!points || !from || !to) return null;

  const dashArr = STAMP_DASH_MAP[el.dash];
  const startX = points[0], startY = points[1];
  const endX = points[points.length - 2], endY = points[points.length - 1];

  const handleDragMove = (endpoint: 'from' | 'to', e: any) => {
    setDragState({ endpoint, x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() });
  };

  const handleDragEnd = (endpoint: 'from' | 'to', e: any) => {
    const stage = e.target.getStage();
    if (!stage) return;
    const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
    if (!pointer) { setDragState(null); return; }
    const transform = stage.getAbsoluteTransform().copy().invert();
    const pos = transform.point(pointer);
    const wx = pos.x, wy = pos.y;
    const targetEl = elements.find(
      (e) => e.id !== (endpoint === 'from' ? el.toId : el.fromId) && e.type !== 'connector' && !e.locked &&
      wx >= e.x && wx <= e.x + e.width && wy >= e.y && wy <= e.y + e.height
    );
    if (targetEl) {
      const nearest = findNearestAnchor(targetEl, wx, wy);
      if (nearest.distance < 40) {
        if (endpoint === 'from') onUpdate({ fromId: targetEl.id, fromAnchor: nearest.side });
        else onUpdate({ toId: targetEl.id, toAnchor: nearest.side });
      }
    }
    setDragState(null);
  };

  return (
    <Group id={el.id} name="element" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }} onContextMenu={onContextMenu}>
      <Line points={points} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" strokeWidth={20} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" listening />
      <Line points={points} stroke={el.stroke} strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth} dash={dashArr} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" listening={false} />
      {arrowHead && <Line points={arrowHead} stroke={el.stroke} strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" listening={false} />}
      {labelPos && el.label && (
        <KonvaText x={labelPos.x} y={labelPos.y} text={el.label} fontSize={el.labelFontSize || 12} fontFamily="Arial" fill={el.labelFill || el.stroke} offsetX={el.label.length * (el.labelFontSize || 12) * 0.3} offsetY={(el.labelFontSize || 12) / 2} listening={false} />
      )}
      {isSelected && (
        <>
          <Circle x={startX} y={startY} radius={8} fill="#fbbf24" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={2} draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragStart={(e) => handleDragMove('from', e)} onDragMove={(e) => handleDragMove('from', e)} onDragEnd={(e) => handleDragEnd('from', e)} />
          <Circle x={endX} y={endY} radius={8} fill="#fbbf24" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={2} draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragStart={(e) => handleDragMove('to', e)} onDragMove={(e) => handleDragMove('to', e)} onDragEnd={(e) => handleDragEnd('to', e)} />
        </>
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\elements\DiagramEl.tsx
