import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { DiagramElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps<DiagramElement> {
  el: DiagramElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<DiagramElement>) => void;
  onDblClick: () => void;
}

export const DiagramEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!el.svg) { setImg(null); return; }
    const blob = new Blob([el.svg], { type: 'image/svg+xml' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const image = new window.Image();
    image.onload = () => { setImg(image); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); };
    image.onerror = () => { setImg(null); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); };
    image.src = url;
  }, [el.svg]);

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(40, el.width * sx),
          height: Math.max(40, el.height * sy),
        });
      }}
    >
      <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
      {img
        ? <KImage x={0} y={0} width={el.width} height={el.height} image={img} listening={false} />
        : <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="#f8fafc" stroke="#cbd5e1" strokeWidth={1} dash={[6, 4]} />}
      {isSelected && (
        <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Circle, Line } from 'react-konva';
import type { FillElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps<FillElement> {
  el: FillElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<FillElement>) => void;
}

export const FillEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const points = el.points || [];
  if (points.length < 6) return null;

  const updatePoint = (idx: number, x: number, y: number) => {
    const np = [...points];
    np[idx * 2] = x;
    np[idx * 2 + 1] = y;
    onUpdate({ points: np });
  };

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element is-fill-element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      <Line
        points={points}
        closed
        fill={el.fill}
        opacity={1}
        stroke={el.stroke || undefined}
        strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth || 0}
        listening
      />
      {isSelected && points.map((_, i) => {
        if (i % 2 !== 0) return null;
        return (
          <Circle
            key={i}
            x={points[i]} y={points[i + 1]}
            radius={5} fill="#fbbf24" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth={1.5} draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragMove={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; updatePoint(i / 2, e.target.x(), e.target.y()); }}
            onDragEnd={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text as KonvaText } from 'react-konva';

interface Props extends CommonProps<FooterElement> {

}

export const FooterEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd,
  onContextMenu, onDblClick, isEditing,
}) => (
  <Group
    id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
    draggable
    onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
    onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
    onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
    onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
    onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
    onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
    onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
  >

    {!isEditing && (
      <KonvaText x={0} y={el.height / 2 - el.fontSize / 2} width={el.width} align="center" text={el.text} fontSize={el.fontSize} fill={el.fill} />

  </Group>

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { ImageElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps<ImageElement> {
  el: ImageElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<ImageElement>) => void;
}

export const ImageEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate: _onUpdate, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
}) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.src = el.src;
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
  }, [el.src]);

  if (!img) return null;

  return (
    <KImage
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} width={el.width} height={el.height} image={img} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      perfectDrawEnabled={false} // ОГРОМНЫЙ БУСТ FPS
    />
  );
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\presentation\components\elements\LineEl.tsx
SIZE: 3848 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { Group } from 'react-konva';
import type { LineElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import { PolylineRenderer } from '../shapes/PolylineRenderer';
import { ensureMinimalPoints } from '@/lib/polyline';

interface Props extends Omit<CommonProps<LineElement>, 'onDblClick'> {
  el: LineElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<LineElement>) => void;
  onDblClick?: (stageX: number, stageY: number) => void;
}

export const LineEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDblClick,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const points = ensureMinimalPoints(el.points, el.width, el.height);

  const handleDblClick = (e: any) => {
    e.cancelBubble = true;
    if (!onDblClick) return;
    const stage = e.target.getStage();
    const p = stage?.getPointerPosition();
    if (!p || !stage) return;

    const sx = p.x / stage.scaleX();
    const sy = p.y / stage.scaleY();
    const localX = sx - el.x;
    const localY = sy - el.y;

    let bestIdx = 2, bestDist = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i + 3 < points.length; i += 2) {
      const x1 = points[i], y1 = points[i + 1];
      const x2 = points[i + 2], y2 = points[i + 3];
      const dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1;
      const len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy || 1;
      const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((localX - x1) * dx + (localY - y1) * dy) / len2));
      const px = x1 + t * dx, py = y1 + t * dy;
      const d = Math.hypot(localX - px, localY - py);
      if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; bestIdx = i + 2; }
    }
    onUpdate({ points: [...points.slice(0, bestIdx), localX, localY, ...points.slice(bestIdx)] });
  };

  const handlePointMove = (index: number, x: number, y: number) => {
    const np = [...points];
    np[index * 2] = x;
    np[index * 2 + 1] = y;
    onUpdate({ points: np });
  };

  const handlePointContextMenu = (index: number) => {
    if (points.length <= 4) return;
    const np = [...points];
    np.splice(index * 2, 2);
    onUpdate({ points: np });
  };

  const handleTransformEnd = (e: any) => {
    const n = e.target;
    const sx = n.scaleX();
    const sy = n.scaleY();
    n.scaleX(1);
    n.scaleY(1);

    const scaledPoints = points.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v * sx : v * sy));

    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < scaledPoints.length; i += 2) {
      const px = scaledPoints[i] ?? 0;
      const py = scaledPoints[i + 1] ?? 0;
      if (px < minX) minX = px;
      if (py < minY) minY = py;
      if (px > maxX) maxX = px;
      if (py > maxY) maxY = py;
    }
    const newW = Math.max(10, maxX - minX);
    const newH = Math.max(10, maxY - minY);

    onUpdate({
      x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
      width: newW, height: newH, points: scaledPoints,
    });
  };

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onDblClick={handleDblClick}
      onDblTap={handleDblClick}
      onTransformEnd={handleTransformEnd}
    >
      <PolylineRenderer
        points={points}
        stroke={el.stroke}
        strokeWidth={el.strokeWidth}
        dash={el.dash}
        isArrow={el.isArrow}
        isSelected={!!isSelected}
        onPointMove={handlePointMove}
        onPointContextMenu={handlePointContextMenu}
      />
    </Group>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { PlaceholderElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps<PlaceholderElement> {
  el: PlaceholderElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<PlaceholderElement>) => void;
}

export const PlaceholderEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate,
  onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  const textRef = useRef<Konva.Text>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (el.fieldType !== 'image' || !el.sample) { setImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    i.src = el.sample;
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
    i.onerror = () => setImg(null);
  }, [el.sample, el.fieldType]);

  const pad = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerW = Math.max(10, el.width - pad.left - pad.right);
  const pathLabel = `{{${el.fieldPath}}}`;
  const sampleText = el.sample || '—';

  const konvaStyle = [
    (el.fontStyle || '').includes('bold') ? 'bold' : '',
    (el.fontStyle || '').includes('italic') ? 'italic' : '',
  ].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || 'normal';

  useEffect(() => {
    if (el.fieldType === 'image') return;
    if (el.inline) return;
    const node = textRef.current;
    if (!node) return;
    const measured = Math.ceil(node.height() + pad.top + pad.bottom + 24);
    if (Math.abs(measured - el.height) > 2) onUpdate({ height: measured });
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [el.sample, el.fontSize, el.width, el.fontFamily, el.fontStyle, el.lineHeight, el.align, el.padding, el.fieldType, el.inline]);

  if (el.inline) {
    if (el.fieldType === 'image') {
      return (
        <Group
          id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
          onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
          onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
          onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
          onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
          onTransformEnd={(e) => {
            const n = e.target;
            const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
            n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
            onUpdate({
              x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
              width: Math.max(20, el.width * sx),
              height: Math.max(20, el.height * sy),
            });
          }}
        >
          <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
          {img ? <KImage x={0} y={0} width={el.width} height={el.height} image={img} listening={false} />
               : <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="#f0f0f0" listening={false} />}
          {isSelected && (
            <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height}
              stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />
          )}
        </Group>
      );
    }
    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
        onTransformEnd={(e) => {
          const n = e.target;
          const sx = n.scaleX();
          n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
          onUpdate({
            x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
            width: Math.max(20, el.width * sx),
          });
        }}
      >
        {el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' && (
          <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill={el.background} />
        )}
        <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
        {isSelected && (
          <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height}
            stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />
        )}
        <Text
          ref={textRef}
          x={pad.left} y={pad.top}
          width={innerW}
          text={sampleText}
          fontSize={el.fontSize}
          fontFamily={el.fontFamily || 'Arial'}
          fontStyle={konvaStyle}
          textDecoration={el.textDecoration || ''}
          align={el.align}
          lineHeight={el.lineHeight || 1.2}
          fill={el.fill}
          wrap="word"
          listening={false}
        />
      </Group>
    );
  }

  if (el.fieldType === 'image') {
    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
        onTransformEnd={(e) => {
          const n = e.target;
          const sx = n.scaleX(); const sy = n.scaleY();
          n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
          onUpdate({
            x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
            width: Math.max(40, el.width * sx),
            height: Math.max(40, el.height * sy),
          });
        }}
      >
        <Rect
          width={el.width} height={el.height}
          fill="#f8fafc" stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} cornerRadius={6}
        />
        <Rect width={el.width} height={22} fill="#6366f1" cornerRadius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />
        <Text
          x={8} y={6} width={el.width - 16}
          text={`🖼 ${pathLabel}`}
          fontSize={11} fontFamily="monospace" fill="#ffffff" listening={false}
        />
        {img ? (
          <KImage
            x={pad.left} y={22 + pad.top}
            width={innerW}
            height={Math.max(10, el.height - 22 - pad.top - pad.bottom)}
            image={img}
            listening={false}
          />
        ) : (
          <Text
            x={pad.left} y={(el.height + 22) / 2} width={innerW}
            align="center"
            text="(картинка не загружена)"
            fontSize={12} fill="#94a3b8" listening={false}
          />
        )}
      </Group>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onUpdate({ x: e.target.x(), y: e.target.y() }); onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      onTransformEnd={(e) => {
        const n = e.target;
        const sx = n.scaleX();
        n.scaleX(1); n.scaleY(1);
        onUpdate({
          x: n.x(), y: n.y(), rotation: n.rotation(),
          width: Math.max(60, el.width * sx),
        });
      }}
    >
      <Rect
        width={el.width} height={el.height}
        fill={el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' ? el.background : '#f8fafc'}
        stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} cornerRadius={6}
      />
      <Rect width={el.width} height={20} fill="#6366f1" cornerRadius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />
      <Text
        x={8} y={4} width={el.width - 16}
        text={pathLabel}
        fontSize={11} fontFamily="monospace" fill="#ffffff" listening={false}
      />
      <Text
        ref={textRef}
        x={pad.left} y={20 + pad.top}
        width={innerW}
        text={sampleText}
        fontSize={el.fontSize}
        fontFamily={el.fontFamily || 'Arial'}
        fontStyle={konvaStyle}
        textDecoration={el.textDecoration || ''}
        align={el.align}
        verticalAlign={el.verticalAlign || 'top'}
        lineHeight={el.lineHeight || 1.2}
        fill={el.fill}
        wrap="word"
        listening={false}
      />
    </Group>
  );
};

export const StampProps: React.FC<{ el: StampElement }> = ({ el }) => {
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();
  return (
    <Group title="Штамп">
      <p style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
        {(el.shapes?.length ?? 0)} фигур · {(el.texts?.length ?? 0)} текстов · {(el.images?.length ?? 0)} картинок

        Открыть редактор штампа
      </button>

import React from 'react';
import type { ShapeElement, TextAlign, TextVerticalAlign } from '@/shared/types';

export const ShapeProps: React.FC<{ el: ShapeElement; update: (p: Partial<ShapeElement>) => void }> = ({ el, update }) => {
  const pad = el.textPadding || { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 };

  const updatePad = (key: keyof typeof pad, val: number) => {
    update({ textPadding: { ...pad, [key]: val } });
  };

  return (
    <>
      <Group title="Фигура">
        <L label="Заливка"><input style={inputStyle} type="color" value={normalizeColor(el.fill)} onChange={(e) => update({ fill: e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="Обводка"><input style={inputStyle} type="color" value={normalizeColor(el.stroke)} onChange={(e) => update({ stroke: e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="Толщина"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" min={0} value={el.strokeWidth} onChange={(e) => update({ strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} /></L>
      </Group>

      <Group title="Текст внутри">
        <L label="Содержимое">
          <textarea style={{ ...inputStyle, minHeight: 60 }} value={el.text || ''} onChange={(e) => update({ text: e.target.value })} />
        </L>
        
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6, marginBottom: 6 }}>

          <L label="Цвет"><input style={inputStyle} type="color" value={normalizeColor(el.textFill)} onChange={(e) => update({ textFill: e.target.value })} /></L>
        </div>

        <L label="Горизонтальное выравнивание">
          <select style={inputStyle} value={el.textAlign || 'center'} onChange={(e) => update({ textAlign: e.target.value as TextAlign })}>
            <option value="left">По левому краю</option>

            <option value="right">По правому краю</option>

          </select>
        </L>

        <L label="Вертикальное выравнивание">
          <select style={inputStyle} value={el.textVerticalAlign || 'middle'} onChange={(e) => update({ textVerticalAlign: e.target.value as TextVerticalAlign })}>
            <option value="top">Сверху</option>
            <option value="middle">По центру</option>
            <option value="bottom">Снизу</option>

          <L label="Справа"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={pad.right} onChange={(e) => updatePad('right', +e.target.value)} /></L>

import React from 'react';
import type { TableElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { Group, L, inputStyle, normalizeColor } from './ui';

export const TableProps: React.FC<{ el: TableElement; update: (p: Partial<TableElement>) => void }> = ({ el, update }) => (
  <Group title="Таблица">
    <L label="Строк"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" min={1} value={el.rows} onChange={(e) => {
      const rows = Math.max(1, +e.target.value);
      const data = el.data.slice(0, rows);
      while (data.length < rows) data.push(Array(el.cols).fill(''));
      update({ rows, data });
    }} /></L>
    <L label="Колонок"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" min={1} value={el.cols} onChange={(e) => {
      const cols = Math.max(1, +e.target.value);
      const data = el.data.map((r) => {
        const nr = r.slice(0, cols);
        while (nr.length < cols) nr.push('');
        return nr;
      });
      update({ cols, data });
    }} /></L>
    <L label="Цвет границ"><input style={inputStyle} type="color" value={normalizeColor(el.borderColor)} onChange={(e) => update({ borderColor: e.target.value })} /></L>
    <L label="Цвет шапки"><input style={inputStyle} type="color" value={normalizeColor(el.headerBg)} onChange={(e) => update({ headerBg: e.target.value })} /></L>
  </Group>
);

import React from 'react';

export function normalizeColor(c: string | undefined | null): string {
  if (!c || c === 'transparent') return '#ffffff';
  if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(c)) {
    return '#' + c[1] + c[1] + c[2] + c[2] + c[3] + c[3];
  }
  return c;
}

export const inputStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: '100%', padding: '5px 8px', fontSize: 12,
  border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: 4, boxSizing: 'border-box',
};

export const Group: React.FC<{ title: string; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ title, children }) => (
  <div style={{ marginBottom: 12, paddingBottom: 12, borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
    <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 8px', fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>{title}</h4>
    {children}
  </div>
);

export const L: React.FC<{ label: string; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ label, children }) => (
  <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b', marginBottom: 4 }}>
    <span style={{ display: 'block', marginBottom: 2 }}>{label}</span>
    {children}
  </label>
);

/* ============================================================

import { registerElement } from './registry';

import { TextEl } from './TextEl';
import { ShapeEl } from './ShapeEl';
import { LineEl } from './LineEl';
import { TableEl } from './TableEl';
import { FooterEl } from './FooterEl';
import { ImageEl } from './ImageEl';
import { PlaceholderEl } from './PlaceholderEl';
import { FillEl } from './FillEl';
import { ConnectorEl } from './ConnectorEl';
import { DiagramEl } from './DiagramEl';
import { StampEl } from './StampEl';

import { TextProps } from './properties/TextProps';
import { ShapeProps } from './properties/ShapeProps';
import { TableProps } from './properties/TableProps';
import {
  LineProps, ImageProps, FooterProps, PlaceholderProps,
  FillProps, ConnectorProps, StampProps, DiagramProps,
} from './properties/MiscProps';

import {
  toSvgText, toSvgShape, toSvgLine, toSvgTable, toSvgImage,
  toSvgFooter, toSvgPlaceholder, toSvgFill, toSvgConnector,
  toSvgDiagram, toSvgStamp,
} from '@/lib/svg/exporters';

import type { ConnectorElement } from '@/shared/types';

export function registerAllElements(): void {
  registerElement({ type: 'text',        KonvaView: TextEl        as never, PropertiesPanel: TextProps        as never, toSvg: toSvgText });
  registerElement({ type: 'shape',       KonvaView: ShapeEl       as never, PropertiesPanel: ShapeProps       as never, toSvg: toSvgShape });
  registerElement({ type: 'line',        KonvaView: LineEl        as never, PropertiesPanel: LineProps        as never, toSvg: toSvgLine });
  registerElement({ type: 'table',       KonvaView: TableEl       as never, PropertiesPanel: TableProps       as never, toSvg: toSvgTable });
  registerElement({ type: 'footer',      KonvaView: FooterEl      as never, PropertiesPanel: FooterProps      as never, toSvg: toSvgFooter });
  registerElement({ type: 'image',       KonvaView: ImageEl       as never, PropertiesPanel: ImageProps       as never, toSvg: toSvgImage });
  registerElement({ type: 'placeholder', KonvaView: PlaceholderEl as never, PropertiesPanel: PlaceholderProps as never, toSvg: toSvgPlaceholder });
  registerElement({ type: 'fill',        KonvaView: FillEl        as never, PropertiesPanel: FillProps        as never, toSvg: toSvgFill });
  registerElement({
    type: 'connector',
    KonvaView: ConnectorEl as never,
    PropertiesPanel: ConnectorProps as never,
    toSvg: (el, ctx) => toSvgConnector(el as ConnectorElement, ctx?.allElements ?? []),
  });
  registerElement({ type: 'diagram', KonvaView: DiagramEl as never, PropertiesPanel: DiagramProps as never, toSvg: toSvgDiagram });
  registerElement({ type: 'stamp',   KonvaView: StampEl   as never, PropertiesPanel: StampProps   as never, toSvg: toSvgStamp });
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/components/elements/registry.ts
   ARC-PATTERN-07. CQ-TS-04: без any.
   ============================================================ */

import type React from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { ElementSelectEvent } from './types';

export interface CommonHandlers<T extends CanvasElement = CanvasElement> {
  onSelect: (e?: ElementSelectEvent) => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<T>, newElement?: CanvasElement) => void;
  onDragStart: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onDragMove: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onDragEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onContextMenu: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<PointerEvent>) => void;
  isSelected: boolean;
  onDblClick?: (e?: ElementSelectEvent) => void;
}

export interface ElementViewProps<T extends CanvasElement = CanvasElement> {
  el: T;
  handlers: CommonHandlers<T>;
  elements: CanvasElement[];
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

export interface ElementStrategy<T extends CanvasElement = CanvasElement> {
  type: T['type'];
  KonvaView: React.ComponentType<{
    el: T;
    elements?: CanvasElement[];
    isSelected?: boolean;
    isEditing?: boolean;
    onSelect: (e?: ElementSelectEvent) => void;
    onUpdate: (patch: Partial<T>, newElement?: CanvasElement) => void;
    onDragStart: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
    onDragMove: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
    onDragEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
    onContextMenu: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<PointerEvent>) => void;
    onDblClick?: (e?: ElementSelectEvent) => void;
    onCellDblClick?: (row: number, col: number) => void;
  }>;
  PropertiesPanel?: React.ComponentType<{
    el: T;
    update: (p: Partial<T>) => void;
  }>;
  toSvg: (el: T, ctx?: { allElements: CanvasElement[] }) => string;
}

const registry = new Map<string, ElementStrategy>();

export function registerElement<T extends CanvasElement>(strategy: ElementStrategy<T>): void {
  registry.set(strategy.type, strategy as unknown as ElementStrategy);
}

export function getElementStrategy(type: string): ElementStrategy | null {
  return registry.get(type) ?? null;
}

FILE: src\presentation\components\elements\RichTable.tsx

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text as KonvaText } from 'react-konva';
import type {
  RichTableData, RichTableCell, PositionedRun,
} from '@/lib/richText/types';
import {
  computeTableLayout, cellPad, measureTableHeight, lineWidth,
  CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN, MAX_NEST_DEPTH, type CellBlockItem,
} from './tableLayout';

export { measureTableHeight };

interface Props {
  data: RichTableData;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fallbackFontSize: number;
  fallbackFill: string;
  lineHeightCoef?: number;
  depth?: number;
}

export const RichTable: React.FC<Props> = ({
  data, x, y, width, fontFamily, fallbackFontSize, fallbackFill,
  lineHeightCoef = 1.5, depth = 0,
}) => {
  const layout = useMemo(
    () => computeTableLayout(data, width, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef),
    [data, width, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef],
  );

  if (!data.cells.length) return null;
  const { colOffsets, rowOffsets, cellItems, totalWidth, totalHeight } = layout;

  return (
    <Group x={x} y={y} listening={false}>
      {data.cells.map((cell, i) => (
        <CellBackground key={`bg-${i}`} cell={cell} colOffsets={colOffsets} rowOffsets={rowOffsets} />
      ))}
      {data.cells.map((cell, i) => (
        <CellContent
          key={`ct-${i}`}
          cell={cell}
          items={cellItems.get(cell)}
          colOffsets={colOffsets}
          rowOffsets={rowOffsets}
          fontFamily={fontFamily}
          fallbackFontSize={fallbackFontSize}
          fallbackFill={fallbackFill}
          lineHeightCoef={lineHeightCoef}
          depth={depth}
        />
      ))}
      <Rect
        x={0} y={0}
        width={totalWidth} height={totalHeight}
        stroke="#94a3b8" strokeWidth={1}
        listening={false}
      />
    </Group>
  );
};

const CellBackground: React.FC<{
  cell: RichTableCell;
  colOffsets: number[];
  rowOffsets: number[];
}> = ({ cell, colOffsets, rowOffsets }) => {
  const cx = colOffsets[cell.colIndex];
  const cy = rowOffsets[cell.rowIndex];
  const cw = colOffsets[Math.min(cell.colIndex + cell.colspan, colOffsets.length - 1)] - cx;
  const ch = rowOffsets[Math.min(cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan, rowOffsets.length - 1)] - cy;

  const fill = cell.bgColor || (cell.isHeader ? '#f1f5f9' : '#ffffff');
  const borderColor = cell.borderColor || '#cbd5e1';
  const borderWidth = cell.borderWidth ?? 1;
  const dash =
    cell.borderStyle === 'dashed' ? [4, 4]
    : cell.borderStyle === 'dotted' ? [2, 3]
    : undefined;

  return (
    <Rect
      x={cx} y={cy}
      width={cw} height={ch}
      fill={fill}
      stroke={borderWidth > 0 ? borderColor : undefined}
      strokeWidth={borderWidth}
      dash={dash}
      listening={false}
    />
  );
};

interface CellContentProps {
  cell: RichTableCell;
  items: CellBlockItem[] | undefined;
  colOffsets: number[];
  rowOffsets: number[];
  fontFamily: string;
  fallbackFontSize: number;
  fallbackFill: string;
  lineHeightCoef: number;
  depth: number;
}

const CellContent: React.FC<CellContentProps> = ({
  cell, items, colOffsets, rowOffsets,
  fontFamily, fallbackFontSize, fallbackFill, lineHeightCoef, depth,
}) => {
  if (!items || !items.length) return null;

  const p = cellPad(cell);
  const cellLeft = colOffsets[cell.colIndex];
  const cellTop = rowOffsets[cell.rowIndex];
  const cellRight = colOffsets[Math.min(cell.colIndex + cell.colspan, colOffsets.length - 1)];
  const cellBottom = rowOffsets[Math.min(cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan, rowOffsets.length - 1)];

  const innerLeft = cellLeft + p.left;
  const innerRight = cellRight - p.right;
  const innerTop = cellTop + p.top;
  const innerBottom = cellBottom - p.bottom;
  const innerWidth = Math.max(10, innerRight - innerLeft);
  const innerHeight = Math.max(10, innerBottom - innerTop);

  const totalContentHeight =
    items.reduce((sum, it) => sum + it.height, 0) +
    Math.max(0, items.length - 1) * CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN;

  const va = cell.verticalAlign ?? 'top';
  let topY: number;
  if (va === 'middle') {
    topY = innerTop + Math.max(0, (innerHeight - totalContentHeight) / 2);
  } else if (va === 'bottom') {
    topY = innerTop + Math.max(0, innerHeight - totalContentHeight);
  } else {
    topY = innerTop;
  }

  const align = cell.align ?? 'left';
  let cursorY = topY;

  return (
    <>
      {items.map((item, bi) => {
        const blockTop = cursorY;
        cursorY += item.height + CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN;

        if (item.kind === 'text') {
          return (
            <TextLayoutRuns
              key={`t-${bi}`}
              lines={item.layout.lines}
              innerLeft={innerLeft}
              innerWidth={innerWidth}
              y={blockTop}
              align={align}
              bold={cell.isHeader}
              fontFamily={fontFamily}
              fallbackFontSize={fallbackFontSize}
              fallbackFill={fallbackFill}
            />
          );
        }

        if (depth + 1 > MAX_NEST_DEPTH) return null;

        return (
          <RichTable
            key={`nt-${bi}`}
            data={item.data}
            x={innerLeft}
            y={blockTop}
            width={innerWidth}
            fontFamily={fontFamily}
            fallbackFontSize={fallbackFontSize}
            fallbackFill={fallbackFill}
            lineHeightCoef={lineHeightCoef}
            depth={depth + 1}
          />
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
};

interface TextRunsProps {
  lines: PositionedRun[][];
  innerLeft: number;
  innerWidth: number;
  y: number;
  align: 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify';
  bold: boolean;
  fontFamily: string;
  fallbackFontSize: number;
  fallbackFill: string;
}

const TextLayoutRuns: React.FC<TextRunsProps> = ({
  lines, innerLeft, innerWidth, y, align,
  bold, fontFamily, fallbackFontSize, fallbackFill,
}) => (
  <>
    {lines.map((line, ri) => {
      const lw = lineWidth(line);
      let lineOffsetX = 0;
      if (align === 'center') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, (innerWidth - lw) / 2);
      else if (align === 'right') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, innerWidth - lw);

      return line.map((run, rj) => {
        const fs = run.actualFontSize || fallbackFontSize;
        const isBold = bold || run.bold;
        const fontStyle =
          isBold && run.italic ? 'italic bold'
          : isBold ? 'bold'
          : run.italic ? 'italic'
          : 'normal';
        const textDecoration =
          run.underline && run.strike ? 'underline line-through'
          : run.underline ? 'underline'
          : run.strike ? 'line-through'
          : '';

        return (
          <KonvaText
            key={`${ri}-${rj}`}
            x={innerLeft + lineOffsetX + run.x}
            y={y + run.y}
            text={run.text}
            fontSize={fs}
            fontFamily={run.fontFamily || fontFamily}
            fontStyle={fontStyle}
            textDecoration={textDecoration}
            fill={run.color || fallbackFill}
            listening={false}
          />
        );
      });
    })}
  </>
);


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\presentation\components\elements\ShapeEl.tsx
SIZE: 4377 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Circle, Line, Text, Star, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { ShapeElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps<ShapeElement> {
  el: ShapeElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<ShapeElement>) => void;
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

export const ShapeEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd,
  onContextMenu, onDblClick,
}) => {
  const w = el.width, h = el.height;
  const common = {
    stroke: el.stroke || '#000',
    strokeWidth: el.strokeWidth || 2,
    fill: el.fill === 'transparent' ? undefined : el.fill,
  };

  const [rasterImg, setRasterImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  const rasterSrc = el.rasterFill?.src;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!rasterSrc) { setRasterImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.src = rasterSrc;
    i.onload = () => setRasterImg(i);
  }, [rasterSrc]);

  const textStr = String(el.text || '');
  const hasText = textStr.length > 0;
  const fontSize = el.fontSize || 18;
  const textPad = el.textPadding || { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 };
  const textAlign = el.textAlign || 'center';
  const textVerticalAlign = el.textVerticalAlign || 'middle';
  const textFontFamily = el.textFontFamily || 'Arial';
  const textLineHeight = el.textLineHeight || 1.3;
  const textFontStyle = el.textFontStyle || 'normal';
  const textDecoration = el.textDecoration || '';

  const innerW = Math.max(1, w - textPad.left - textPad.right);
  const innerH = Math.max(1, h - textPad.top - textPad.bottom);
  let tx = textPad.left, ty = textPad.top, tw = innerW, th = innerH;
  if (el.shape === 'circle') {
    const d = Math.min(w, h);
    tw = d * 0.7; th = d * 0.7;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = (h - th) / 2;
  } else if (el.shape === 'triangle') {
    tw = w * 0.55; th = h * 0.45;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = h * 0.5;
  } else if (el.shape === 'star') {
    tw = w * 0.5; th = h * 0.5;
    tx = (w - tw) / 2; ty = (h - th) / 2;
  }

  const lineBoxOffset = textVerticalAlign === 'top' ? ((textLineHeight - 1) * fontSize) / 2 : 0;

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      dragDistance={3}
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      {el.rasterFill && rasterImg && (
        <KImage
          x={el.rasterFill.relX * w}
          y={el.rasterFill.relY * h}
          width={el.rasterFill.relW * w}
          height={el.rasterFill.relH * h}
          image={rasterImg}
          listening={false}
          perfectDrawEnabled={false}
        />
      )}

      {el.shape === 'rect' && <Rect width={w} height={h} cornerRadius={el.radius || 0} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'roundRect' && <Rect width={w} height={h} cornerRadius={el.radius ?? 16} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'circle' && <Circle x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radius={Math.min(w, h) / 2} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'triangle' && <Line points={[w / 2, 0, w, h, 0, h, w / 2, 0]} closed {...common} lineJoin="round" />}
      {el.shape === 'star' && <Star x={w / 2} y={h / 2} numPoints={5} innerRadius={Math.min(w, h) / 4} outerRadius={Math.min(w, h) / 2} {...common} />}
      {el.shape === 'arrow' && <Line points={[0, h / 2, w - 20, h / 2, w - 20, h / 2 - 15, w, h / 2, w - 20, h / 2 + 15, w - 20, h / 2]} {...common} lineJoin="round" />}

      {hasText && (
        <Text
          x={tx} y={ty + lineBoxOffset} width={tw} height={th}
          text={textStr} fontSize={fontSize} fontFamily={textFontFamily}
          fontStyle={textFontStyle} textDecoration={textDecoration}
          fill={el.textFill || '#000000'} align={textAlign} verticalAlign={textVerticalAlign}
          lineHeight={textLineHeight} wrap="word" listening={false}
        />
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Circle, Ellipse, Line, Text as KonvaText, Shape as KonvaShape, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampElement, StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import { drawArcText, canvasFont, konvaStyle, STAMP_DASH_MAP } from '@/lib/stamps/render';
import { CompositeFillLayer } from '../stamps/canvas/CompositeFillLayer';

interface Props extends CommonProps<StampElement> {
  el: StampElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<StampElement>) => void;
}

export const StampEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate: _onUpdate, onDblClick, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  // Мемоизируем массивы, чтобы useMemo ниже не срабатывал на каждый рендер.
  const shapes = useMemo(() => el.shapes ?? [], [el.shapes]);
  const texts = useMemo(() => el.texts ?? [], [el.texts]);
  const images = useMemo(() => el.images ?? [], [el.images]);

  const regularShapes = useMemo(() => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill'), [shapes]);
  const legacyFills = useMemo(() => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === 'fill' && !s.fillTargetShapeId), [shapes]);

  // === LEGACY STAMP ===
  if (shapes.length === 0 && el.shape) {
    const {
      shape, stampColor, strokeWidth, strokeDash, showInner, innerStrokeWidth, innerStrokeDash, topText, centerText, bottomText,
      topFontSize, topFontFamily, topFontStyle, topTextDecoration, topColor,
      centerFontSize, centerFontFamily, centerFontStyle, centerTextDecoration, centerColor,
      bottomFontSize, bottomFontFamily, bottomFontStyle, bottomTextDecoration, bottomColor,
    } = el;
    const w = el.width, h = el.height;
    const pad = 8;

    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
        onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
        onDragStart={onDragStart}
        onDragMove={onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      >
        <Rect width={w} height={h} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening />

        {shape === 'circle' && <Circle x={w/2} y={h/2} radius={Math.min(w,h)/2} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[strokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}
        {shape === 'oval' && <Ellipse x={w/2} y={h/2} radiusX={w/2} radiusY={h/2} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[strokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}
        {(shape === 'rect') && <Rect width={w} height={h} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[strokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" cornerRadius={12} />}
        {(shape === 'triangle') && <Line points={[w/2, 0, w, h, 0, h]} closed stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[strokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}

        {showInner && shape === 'circle' && <Circle x={w/2} y={h/2} radius={Math.min(w,h)/2 - pad} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[innerStrokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}
        {showInner && shape === 'oval' && <Ellipse x={w/2} y={h/2} radiusX={w/2 - pad} radiusY={h/2 - pad} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[innerStrokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}
        {showInner && shape === 'rect' && <Rect x={pad} y={pad} width={w - pad*2} height={h - pad*2} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[innerStrokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" cornerRadius={6} />}
        {showInner && shape === 'triangle' && <Line points={[w/2, pad, w - pad, h - pad, pad, h - pad]} closed stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[innerStrokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}

        {(shape === 'circle' || shape === 'oval') && topText && (
          <KonvaShape sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
            drawArcText((ctx as any)._context, { text: topText, cx: w/2, cy: h/2, radius: Math.min(w,h)/2 - 15, startDeg: -150, endDeg: -30, flip: false, font: canvasFont(topFontFamily||'Arial', topFontStyle||'bold', topFontSize||14), fill: topColor||stampColor||'#000' });
          }} listening={false} />
        )}
        {(shape === 'circle' || shape === 'oval') && bottomText && (
          <KonvaShape sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
            drawArcText((ctx as any)._context, { text: bottomText, cx: w/2, cy: h/2, radius: Math.min(w,h)/2 - 15, startDeg: 150, endDeg: 30, flip: true, font: canvasFont(bottomFontFamily||'Arial', bottomFontStyle||'normal', bottomFontSize||12), fill: bottomColor||stampColor||'#000' });
          }} listening={false} />
        )}

        {shape === 'rect' && topText && <KonvaText x={0} y={10} width={w} text={topText} fontSize={topFontSize||14} fontFamily={topFontFamily||'Arial'} fontStyle={topFontStyle||'bold'} textDecoration={topTextDecoration||''} fill={topColor||stampColor||'#000'} align="center" />}
        {shape === 'rect' && bottomText && <KonvaText x={0} y={h - (bottomFontSize||12) - 10} width={w} text={bottomText} fontSize={bottomFontSize||12} fontFamily={bottomFontFamily||'Arial'} fontStyle={bottomFontStyle||'normal'} textDecoration={bottomTextDecoration||''} fill={bottomColor||stampColor||'#000'} align="center" />}
        {centerText && <KonvaText x={0} y={h/2 - (centerFontSize||20)/2} width={w} text={centerText} fontSize={centerFontSize||20} fontFamily={centerFontFamily||'Arial'} fontStyle={centerFontStyle||'bold'} textDecoration={centerTextDecoration||''} fill={centerColor||stampColor||'#000'} align="center" />}

        {isSelected && <Rect width={w} height={h} stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />}
      </Group>
    );
  }

  // === NEW STAMP ===
  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      <Rect width={Math.max(1, el.width)} height={Math.max(1, el.height)} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening />
      <CompositeFillLayer width={el.width} height={el.height} shapes={shapes} />
      {legacyFills.map((s) => s.hidden ? null : <LegacyFillShape key={s.id} shape={s} allShapes={shapes} />)}
      {regularShapes.map((s) => s.hidden ? null : <ShapeNode key={s.id} shape={s} allShapes={shapes} />)}
      {images.map((im) => im.hidden ? null : <ImageNode key={im.id} im={im} />)}
      {texts.map((t) => t.hidden ? null : <TextNode key={t.id} t={t} />)}
      {isSelected && <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />}
    </Group>
  );
};

const ShapeNode: React.FC<{ shape: StampShapeItem; allShapes: StampShapeItem[] }> = ({ shape }) => {
  const cx = shape.x + shape.width / 2;
  const cy = shape.y + shape.height / 2;
  const common = { stroke: shape.stroke, strokeWidth: shape.strokeWidth, dash: STAMP_DASH_MAP[shape.dash], fill: shape.fill || 'transparent', listening: false };
  if (shape.kind === 'rect') return <Rect x={shape.x} y={shape.y} width={shape.width} height={shape.height} cornerRadius={shape.cornerRadius || 0} {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'circle') return <Ellipse x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={Math.min(shape.width, shape.height)/2} radiusY={Math.min(shape.width, shape.height)/2} {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'ellipse') return <Ellipse x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={shape.width/2} radiusY={shape.height/2} {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'triangle') return <Line points={[cx, shape.y, shape.x + shape.width, shape.y + shape.height, shape.x, shape.y + shape.height]} closed lineJoin="round" {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'line') {
    const pts = shape.points && shape.points.length >= 4 ? shape.points.slice() : [0, shape.height / 2, shape.width, shape.height / 2];
    return <Line x={shape.x} y={shape.y} points={pts} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" {...common} />;
  }
  return null;
};

const LegacyFillShape: React.FC<{ shape: StampShapeItem; allShapes: StampShapeItem[] }> = ({ shape, allShapes }) => {
  const outer = shape.fillOuterId ? allShapes.find((x) => x.id === shape.fillOuterId) : null;
  if (!outer) return null;
  const holes = (shape.fillHoleIds || []).map((id) => allShapes.find((x) => x.id === id)).filter((x): x is StampShapeItem => !!x && !x.hidden);
  const mode = shape.fillMode || 'subtract';
  return (
    <KonvaShape listening={false} sceneFunc={(c) => {
      const raw = (c as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
      const all = [outer, ...holes];
      const minX = Math.min(...all.map((p) => p.x));
      const minY = Math.min(...all.map((p) => p.y));
      const maxX = Math.max(...all.map((p) => p.x + p.width));
      const maxY = Math.max(...all.map((p) => p.y + p.height));
      raw.save();
      if (mode === 'intersect' && holes.length > 0) {
        const op = (s: StampShapeItem) => {
          const path = new Path2D();
          const { x, y, width: w, height: h } = s;
          if (s.kind === 'rect') path.rect(x, y, w, h);
          else if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') path.ellipse(x + w / 2, y + h / 2, w / 2, h / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          else if (s.kind === 'triangle') { path.moveTo(x + w / 2, y); path.lineTo(x + w, y + h); path.lineTo(x, y + h); path.closePath(); }
          return path;
        };
        raw.clip(op(outer));
        for (const h of holes) raw.clip(op(h));
      } else {
        const combined = new Path2D();
        const addPath = (s: StampShapeItem) => {
          const { x, y, width: w, height: h } = s;
          if (s.kind === 'rect') combined.rect(x, y, w, h);
          else if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') combined.ellipse(x + w / 2, y + h / 2, w / 2, h / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          else if (s.kind === 'triangle') { combined.moveTo(x + w / 2, y); combined.lineTo(x + w, y + h); combined.lineTo(x, y + h); combined.closePath(); }
        };
        addPath(outer);
        for (const h of holes) addPath(h);
        raw.clip(combined, 'evenodd');
      }
      raw.fillStyle = shape.fill || '#bfdbfe';
      raw.fillRect(minX, minY, maxX - minX, maxY - minY);
      raw.restore();
    }} />
  );
};

const ImageNode: React.FC<{ im: StampImageItem }> = ({ im }) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!im.src) { setImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
    i.onerror = () => setImg(null);
    i.src = im.src;
  }, [im.src]);
  if (!img) return null;
  return <KImage x={im.x} y={im.y} width={im.width} height={im.height} image={img} listening={false} />;
};

const TextNode: React.FC<{ t: StampTextItem }> = ({ t }) => {
  if (t.mode === 'straight') {
    return <KonvaText x={t.x} y={t.y} width={t.width} text={t.text} fontSize={t.fontSize} fontFamily={t.fontFamily} fontStyle={konvaStyle(t.fontStyle || 'normal')} textDecoration={t.textDecoration} fill={t.fill} align={t.align} listening={false} />;
  }
  const rx = t.arcRx ?? t.arcRadius;
  const ry = t.arcRy ?? t.arcRadius;
  return (
    <KonvaShape listening={false} sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
      const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
      drawArcText(c, { text: t.text, cx: t.arcCx, cy: t.arcCy, radius: t.arcRadius, radiusX: rx, radiusY: ry, startDeg: t.arcStartDeg, endDeg: t.arcEndDeg, flip: t.arcFlip, font: canvasFont(t.fontFamily, t.fontStyle, t.fontSize), fill: t.fill });
    }} />
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text } from 'react-konva';
import type { TableElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps<TableElement> {
  el: TableElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<TableElement>) => void;
}

export const TableEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd,
  onContextMenu, onDblClick,
}) => {
  const cellW = el.width / el.cols;
  const cellH = el.height / el.rows;
  const cells: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  const texts: React.ReactNode[] = [];

  for (let r = 0; r < el.rows; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < el.cols; c++) {
      const bg = r === 0 ? el.headerBg : '#fff';
      cells.push(
        <Rect key={`r-${r}-${c}`} x={c * cellW} y={r * cellH} width={cellW} height={cellH} fill={bg} stroke={el.borderColor} strokeWidth={1} />
      );
      const cellText = el.data[r]?.[c] ?? '';
      const isPh = /\{\{[^}]+\}\}/.test(cellText);
      texts.push(
        <Text key={`t-${r}-${c}`} x={c * cellW + 6} y={r * cellH + 6} width={Math.max(1, cellW - 12)} height={Math.max(1, cellH - 12)} text={cellText} fontSize={el.fontSize} fill={isPh ? '#2563eb' : '#111'} fontStyle={isPh ? 'italic' : 'normal'} wrap="word" ellipsis listening={false} />
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
      {cells}
      {texts}
    </Group>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\elements\TextEl.tsx

import React, { useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text as KonvaText } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { TextElement } from '@/shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import {
  parseHtmlToBlocks,
  layoutRuns,
  plainToHtml,
  type PositionedRun,
  type RichTableData,
} from '@/lib/richText';
import { RichTable, measureTableHeight } from './RichTable';
import { TEXT_PLACEHOLDER } from '@/shared/constants';

const BLOCK_MARGIN = 0;
const HIT_RECT_NAME = 'hit-rect';

interface Props extends CommonProps<TextElement> {
  el: TextElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<TextElement>) => void;
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

type PreparedBlock =
  | { kind: 'text'; layout: ReturnType<typeof layoutRuns>; height: number }
  | { kind: 'table'; data: RichTableData; height: number };

export const TextEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd,
  onContextMenu, onDblClick, isEditing,
}) => {
  const groupRef = useRef<Konva.Group>(null);

  const padding = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, el.width - padding.left - padding.right);
  const innerHeight = Math.max(1, el.height - padding.top - padding.bottom);

  const isPlaceholder = useMemo(
    () => !el.html && (el.text === TEXT_PLACEHOLDER || !el.text?.trim()),
    [el.html, el.text],
  );

  const blocks = useMemo(() => {
    const baseFontStyle = el.fontStyle || 'normal';
    const resolvedFontStyle = isPlaceholder ? `${baseFontStyle} italic` : baseFontStyle;
    const resolvedFill = isPlaceholder ? '#9ca3af' : (el.fill || '#000000');
    const sourceHtml = el.html && el.html.trim() ? el.html : plainToHtml(el.text || '');
    return parseHtmlToBlocks(sourceHtml, {
      fontSize: el.fontSize,
      fontFamily: el.fontFamily,
      fontStyle: resolvedFontStyle,
      textDecoration: el.textDecoration || '',
      fill: resolvedFill,
    });
  }, [el.html, el.text, el.fontSize, el.fontFamily, el.fontStyle, el.textDecoration, el.fill, isPlaceholder]);

  const lineHeightCoef = el.lineHeight || 1.5;

  const prepared = useMemo(() => {
    const items: PreparedBlock[] = [];
    let totalHeight = 0;
    for (const block of blocks) {
      if (block.kind === 'text') {
        const layout = layoutRuns(block.runs, innerWidth, lineHeightCoef);
        if (layout.lines.length === 0) continue;
        items.push({ kind: 'text', layout, height: layout.totalHeight });
        totalHeight += layout.totalHeight;
      } else {
        const h = measureTableHeight(block.data, innerWidth, el.fontSize, lineHeightCoef);
        items.push({ kind: 'table', data: block.data, height: h });
        totalHeight += h;
      }
    }
    if (items.length > 1 && BLOCK_MARGIN > 0) totalHeight += (items.length - 1) * BLOCK_MARGIN;
    return { items, totalHeight };
  }, [blocks, innerWidth, lineHeightCoef, el.fontSize]);

  const contentHeight = padding.top + prepared.totalHeight + padding.bottom;

  /**
   * Auto-grow. Измеряем по фактическим Konva-узлам (getClientRect),
   * а не только по вычисленной contentHeight — так получаем точный
   * размер отрендеренного текста, включая paragraph gaps и переносы
   * (которые layoutRuns может недооценить).
   *
   * useLayoutEffect, не useEffect: измерение должно произойти до
   * paint, чтобы Transformer (bbox считается по дочерним узлам)
   * не успел показать «прыжок».
   */
    useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const g = groupRef.current;
    if (!g) return;

    let maxBottom = 0;
    for (const child of g.getChildren()) {
      if (child.name() === HIT_RECT_NAME) continue;
      if (!child.visible()) continue;
      const r = child.getClientRect({
        skipTransform: true,
        skipShadow: true,
        skipStroke: true,
      });
      const bottom = r.y + r.height;
      if (bottom > maxBottom) maxBottom = bottom;
    }

    const measured = Math.ceil(Math.max(maxBottom, contentHeight));

    // Auto-fit: растёт И сжимается вместе с контентом.
    // Текстовый элемент не должен «висеть» большим, если удалили абзацы.
    if (Math.abs(measured - el.height) > 2) {
      onUpdate({ height: measured });
    }
  }, [contentHeight, el.height, el.width, el.padding, el.fontSize, onUpdate]);

  let offsetY = padding.top;
  if (el.verticalAlign === 'middle') offsetY = padding.top + Math.max(0, (innerHeight - prepared.totalHeight) / 2);
  else if (el.verticalAlign === 'bottom') offsetY = padding.top + Math.max(0, innerHeight - prepared.totalHeight);

  const effectiveFill = isPlaceholder ? '#9ca3af' : (el.fill || '#000000');
  const bg = el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' ? el.background : 'transparent';
  const hitFill = bg === 'transparent' ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.001)' : bg;

  let cursorY = offsetY;

  return (
    <Group
      ref={groupRef}
      id={el.id}
      name="element"
      x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      <Rect
        name={HIT_RECT_NAME}
        width={el.width}
        height={el.height}
        fill={hitFill}
        listening
      />
      {!isEditing && prepared.items.map((item, bi) => {
        const blockTop = cursorY;
        cursorY += item.height;
        if (bi < prepared.items.length - 1) cursorY += BLOCK_MARGIN;

        if (item.kind === 'text') {
          return (
            <React.Fragment key={`b-${bi}`}>
              {item.layout.lines.map((line: PositionedRun[], i: number) => {
                const lw = line.reduce((acc: number, r: PositionedRun) => acc + r.width, 0);
                let lineOffsetX = 0;
                if (el.align === 'center') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, (innerWidth - lw) / 2);
                else if (el.align === 'right') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, innerWidth - lw);
                return line.map((run, j) => {
                  const x = padding.left + lineOffsetX + run.x;
                  const y = blockTop + run.y;
                  const resolvedFontStyle = run.bold && run.italic
                    ? 'italic bold'
                    : run.bold ? 'bold'
                    : run.italic ? 'italic'
                    : isPlaceholder ? 'italic'
                    : 'normal';
                  return (
                    <React.Fragment key={`${i}-${j}`}>
                      {run.bgColor && (
                        <Rect
                          x={x} y={y}
                          width={run.width} height={run.actualFontSize * 1.2}
                          fill={run.bgColor} listening={false}
                        />
                      )}
                      <KonvaText
                        x={x} y={y}
                        text={run.text}
                        fontSize={run.actualFontSize}
                        fontFamily={run.fontFamily || el.fontFamily}
                        fontStyle={resolvedFontStyle}
                        textDecoration={
                          run.underline && run.strike ? 'underline line-through'
                          : run.underline ? 'underline'
                          : run.strike ? 'line-through'
                          : ''
                        }
                        fill={run.color || effectiveFill}
                        listening={false}
                      />
                    </React.Fragment>
                  );
                });
              })}
            </React.Fragment>
          );
        }
        return (
          <RichTable
            key={`table-${bi}`}
            data={item.data}
            x={padding.left}
            y={blockTop}
            width={innerWidth}
            fontFamily={el.fontFamily}
            fallbackFontSize={el.fontSize}
            fallbackFill={el.fill}
            lineHeightCoef={lineHeightCoef}
          />
        );
      })}
    </Group>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   presentation/components/elements/types.ts
   CQ-TS-04: без any; CommonProps<T> обобщён по типу элемента.
   ============================================================ */

import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';

/**
 * Konva для onMouseDown/onTap обычно даёт KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>
 * или <TouchEvent>, для onDblClick/onDblTap на Group — <Event>,
 * для pointer-events — <PointerEvent>. Все четыре варианта включены,
 * чтобы не ловить ложные несовместимости.
 */
export type ElementSelectEvent =
  | Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>
  | Konva.KonvaEventObject<TouchEvent>
  | Konva.KonvaEventObject<PointerEvent>
  | Konva.KonvaEventObject<Event>;

export interface CommonProps<T extends CanvasElement = CanvasElement> {
  onSelect: (e?: ElementSelectEvent) => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<T>, newElement?: CanvasElement) => void;
  onDragStart?: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onDragMove?: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onDragEnd?: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onContextMenu?: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<PointerEvent>) => void;
  isSelected?: boolean;
  isEditing?: boolean;
  onDblClick?: (e?: ElementSelectEvent) => void;
  onCellDblClick?: (row: number, col: number) => void;
}

/* ============================================================
   ErrorBoundary.tsx
   NFR-OBS-01: перехват ошибок на уровне App и панелей.
   NFR-OBS-02: prod-ошибки → logger (Sentry-хук подключается позже).
   ============================================================ */

import React, { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { logger } from '@/shared/logger';

interface Props {
  children: ReactNode;
  fallback?: ReactNode;
}

interface State {
  hasError: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  public override state: State = { hasError: false, error: null };

  public static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  public override componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo): void {
    logger.error('ui.crash', error, {
      componentStack: errorInfo.componentStack ?? undefined,
    });
  }

  public override render(): ReactNode {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return this.props.fallback || (
        <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center', color: '#dc2626', fontFamily: 'sans-serif' }}>
          <h2>Что-то пошло не так</h2>
          <p style={{ color: '#64748b' }}>Попробуйте обновить страницу.</p>
          <pre style={{ marginTop: 20, padding: 12, background: '#fef2f2', borderRadius: 6, textAlign: 'left', fontSize: 12, overflowX: 'auto' }}>
            {this.state.error?.message}
          </pre>
          <button
            onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
            style={{ marginTop: 20, padding: '8px 16px', background: '#6366f1', color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 6, cursor: 'pointer' }}
          >
            Обновить страницу
          </button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

FILE: src\presentation\components\FloatingProps.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';

interface FloatingPropsProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  element: CanvasElement;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}

const normalizeColor = (c: string | undefined | null): string => {
  if (!c || c === 'transparent') return '#ffffff';
  if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(c)) {
    return '#' + c[1] + c[1] + c[2] + c[2] + c[3] + c[3];
  }
  return c;
};

export const FloatingProps: React.FC<FloatingPropsProps> = ({
  x, y, element, onUpdate, onClose, onDelete,
}) => {
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [position, setPosition] = useState({ x, y });

  useEffect(() => {
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    if (!panel) return;
    const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
    let newX = x;
    let newY = y - 50;
    if (newX + rect.width > window.innerWidth) newX = window.innerWidth - rect.width - 10;
    if (newX < 10) newX = 10;
    if (newY < 10) newY = y + element.height + 10;
    if (newY + rect.height > window.innerHeight) newY = window.innerHeight - rect.height - 10;
    setPosition({ x: newX, y: newY });
  }, [x, y, element.height]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const down = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (panelRef.current && !panelRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    };
    setTimeout(() => document.addEventListener('mousedown', down), 0);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', down);
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div ref={panelRef} className="floating-props" style={{ left: position.x, top: position.y }}>
      <div className="fp-group">
        <button onClick={onDelete} title="Удалить">🗑</button>
      </div>

      {element.type === 'shape' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Заливка</label>
            <input
              type="color"
              value={normalizeColor(element.fill)}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })}
            />
            <button onClick={() => onUpdate({ fill: 'transparent' })}>∅</button>
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Обводка</label>
            <input
              type="color"
              value={normalizeColor(element.stroke)}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ stroke: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {element.type === 'text' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Цвет</label>
            <input
              type="color"
              value={normalizeColor(element.fill)}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Размер</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={element.fontSize}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fontSize: Number(e.target.value) })}
            />
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

/**
 * Grid Layer Component - Presentation Layer
 * Renders canvas grid
 */

import React from 'react';
import { Shape } from 'react-konva';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '@/shared/constants';

interface GridLayerProps {
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export const GridLayer: React.FC<GridLayerProps> = ({ width, height }) => {
  return (
    <Shape
      listening={false}
      sceneFunc={(ctx, shape) => {
        const context = ctx._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        context.beginPath();

        // Vertical lines
        for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += GRID_SIZE) {
          context.moveTo(x, 0);
          context.lineTo(x, height);
        }

        // Horizontal lines
        for (let y = 0; y <= height; y += GRID_SIZE) {
          context.moveTo(0, y);
          context.lineTo(width, y);
        }

        context.strokeStyle = '#eef2f7';
        context.lineWidth = 1;
        context.stroke();

        ctx.strokeShape(shape);
      }}
    />
  );
};

GridLayer.displayName = 'GridLayer';

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from 'react';

interface InlineEditorProps {
  x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number;
  value: string; html?: string;
  fontSize: number; fontFamily: string; fontStyle: string; textDecoration: string;
  fill: string; align: 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify';
  verticalAlign: 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
  lineHeight: number; padding: { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number };
  background: string; isOpen: boolean;
  onChange: (value: { value?: string; html?: string }) => void;
  onCommit: () => void; onCancel: () => void;
}

export const InlineEditor: React.FC<InlineEditorProps> = ({
  x, y, width, height, value, html,
  fontSize, fontFamily, fontStyle, textDecoration,
  fill, align, verticalAlign, lineHeight, padding, background,
  isOpen, onChange, onCommit, onCancel,
}) => {
  const editorRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [isComposing, setIsComposing] = useState(false);
  const committedRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !editorRef.current) return;
    committedRef.current = false;
    const editor = editorRef.current;
    editor.innerHTML = html || value || '<p><br></p>';
    editor.focus();

    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(editor);
    const selection = window.getSelection();
    if (selection) {
      selection.removeAllRanges();
      selection.addRange(range);
    }
    // html Рё value вЂ” initial values; СЌС„С„РµРєС‚ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СЃСЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°С‚СЊ РўРћР›Р¬РљРћ
    // РїСЂРё РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёРё, Р° РЅРµ РЅР° РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ РІРІРѕРґ (РёРЅР°С‡Рµ РєР°СЂРµС‚РєР° Р±СѓРґРµС‚ СЃР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°С‚СЊСЃСЏ).
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [isOpen]);

  const handleInput = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editorRef.current) return;
    onChange({ value: editorRef.current.innerText, html: editorRef.current.innerHTML });
  }, [onChange]);

  const handleCommit = useCallback(() => {
    if (committedRef.current) return;
    committedRef.current = true;
    onCommit();
  }, [onCommit]);

  const handleKeyDown = useCallback((e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (isComposing) return;
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); handleCommit(); }
    else if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); onCancel(); }
  }, [isComposing, handleCommit, onCancel]);

  const handleBlur = useCallback(() => setTimeout(() => handleCommit(), 100), [handleCommit]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="inline-editor-overlay" style={{ position: 'fixed', left: x, top: y, width, height, zIndex: 1000 }}>
      <div
        ref={editorRef}
        className="inline-editor"
        contentEditable
        suppressContentEditableWarning
        onInput={handleInput}
        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
        onBlur={handleBlur}
        onCompositionStart={() => setIsComposing(true)}
        onCompositionEnd={() => setIsComposing(false)}
        style={{
          width: '100%', height: '100%', padding: `${padding.top}px ${padding.right}px ${padding.bottom}px ${padding.left}px`,
          fontSize: `${fontSize}px`, fontFamily, fontStyle, textDecoration, color: fill,
          backgroundColor: background === 'transparent' ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)' : background,
          textAlign: align, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column',
          justifyContent: verticalAlign === 'middle' ? 'center' : verticalAlign === 'bottom' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start',
          lineHeight, outline: 'none', border: '2px solid #3b82f6', borderRadius: '4px',
          boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)', overflow: 'auto', wordWrap: 'break-word', whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\InlineEditorHost.tsx

import React, { useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { RichTextEditor } from './RichTextEditor';
import { InlineEditor } from './InlineEditor';
import type { EditingState } from '../stores/ModalsStore';

interface Props {
  editing: EditingState;
  onClose: () => void;
  onCommit: (html: string | undefined, text: string) => void;
}

export const InlineEditorHost: React.FC<Props> = ({ editing, onClose, onCommit }) => {
  const valueRef = useRef(editing.value || '');

  const handlePlainCommit = useCallback(() => {
    onCommit(undefined, valueRef.current);
  }, [onCommit]);

  if (editing.isRich) {
    return (
      <RichTextEditor
        isOpen
        initialHtml={editing.html ?? `<p>${escapeHtml(editing.value ?? '')}</p>`}
        onClose={onClose}
        onSave={(html) => {
          const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
          let text = doc.body.textContent ?? '';
          text = text.replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ');
          onCommit(html, text);
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <InlineEditor
      isOpen
      x={editing.x} y={editing.y}
      width={editing.width} height={editing.height}
      value={editing.value}
      html={editing.html}
      fontSize={editing.fontSize} fontFamily={editing.fontFamily}
      fontStyle={editing.fontStyle} textDecoration={editing.textDecoration}
      fill={editing.fill} align={editing.align} verticalAlign={editing.verticalAlign}
      lineHeight={editing.lineHeight} padding={editing.padding} background={editing.background}
      onChange={(val) => {
        if (val.value !== undefined) valueRef.current = val.value;
      }}
      onCommit={handlePlainCommit}
      onCancel={onClose}
    />
  );
};

function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) =>
    ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]!));
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { DocFormat, Orientation, Padding, CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { FORMATS } from '@/shared/constants';
import { useStore } from '@/shared/store/store';
import {
  useDocumentStore,
  useSelectionStore,
  useModalsStore,
} from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { StampLibraryPanel } from './StampLibraryPanel';
import { changeFormat, changeOrientation } from '@/domain/slide/operations';
import {
  newText,
  newSimpleText,
  newFooter,
  newTable,
  newLine,
  newShape,
} from '@/lib/factories';
import { TYPICAL_LAYOUTS, BUNDLES } from '../../templates';
