/* ---------- Compound Components ---------- */

const Section: React.FC<{
  title: string;
  icon?: string;
  defaultOpen?: boolean;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}> = ({ title, icon, defaultOpen = false, children }) => {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(defaultOpen);
  return (
    <div className={`tool-section ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}>
      <button type="button" className="section-head" onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}>
        <span className="section-ico">{icon}</span>
        <span className="section-title">{title}</span>
        <span className="section-chev">{open ? '▼' : '▶'}</span>
      </button>
      {open && <div className="section-body">{children}</div>}
    </div>
  );
};

const PaddingEditor: React.FC<{
  value: Padding;
  onChange: (p: Padding) => void;
}> = ({ value, onChange }) => {
  const keys: Array<{ k: keyof Padding; label: string }> = [
    { k: 'top', label: 'Верх' },
    { k: 'bottom', label: 'Низ' },
    { k: 'left', label: 'Лево' },
    { k: 'right', label: 'Право' },
  ];
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
      {keys.map(({ k, label }) => (
        <label key={k} className="field-label">
          {label}
          <input
            type="number"
            min={0}
            value={value[k]}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, [k]: +e.target.value })}
            className="field"
          />
        </label>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};

/* ---------- LeftPanel ---------- */

export const LeftPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();
  const fileIO = useFileIO();

  const activeSlide = useStore(docStore, (s) =>
    s.slides.find((x) => x.id === s.activeId),
  );
  const fillMode = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.fillMode);
  const fillColor = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.fillColor);
  const showGrid = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.showGrid);

  const canUndo = docStore.canUndo;
  const canRedo = docStore.canRedo;

  if (!activeSlide) return null;

  const add = (el: CanvasElement) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (s) s.elements.push(el);
    }, 'add-element');
    selStore.select(el.id);
  };

  const resetAfterLoad = () => {
    selStore.clear();
    modalsStore.closeEditor();
    modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);
    modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
  };

  const handleFormat = (format: DocFormat) => {
    if (format === activeSlide.format) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const i = draft.slides.findIndex((s) => s.id === draft.activeId);
      if (i < 0) return;
      draft.slides[i] = changeFormat(draft.slides[i], format);
    }, 'change-format');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const handleOrientation = (orientation: Orientation) => {
    if (orientation === activeSlide.orientation) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const i = draft.slides.findIndex((s) => s.id === draft.activeId);
      if (i < 0) return;
      draft.slides[i] = changeOrientation(draft.slides[i], orientation);
    }, 'change-orientation');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const updatePadding = (padding: Padding) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (s) s.padding = padding;
    }, 'padding');
  };

  const changeCustomSize = (w: number, h: number) => {
    if (activeSlide.format !== 'custom') return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      const newW = s.orientation === 'portrait' ? w : h;
      const newH = s.orientation === 'portrait' ? h : w;
      s.width = newW;
      s.height = newH;
    }, 'custom-size');
  };

  const applyLayout = (layoutId: string) => {
    const layout = TYPICAL_LAYOUTS.find((l) => l.id === layoutId);
    if (!layout) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const slide = layout.build();
      draft.slides = [slide];
      draft.activeId = slide.id;
    }, 'apply-layout');
    resetAfterLoad();
  };

  const applyBundle = (bundleId: string) => {
    const bundle = BUNDLES.find((b) => b.id === bundleId);
    if (!bundle) return;
    const { slide, data: bundleData } = bundle.build();
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      draft.slides = [slide];
      draft.activeId = slide.id;
      draft.data = bundleData;
    }, 'apply-bundle');
    resetAfterLoad();
  };

  const startDragNew = (e: React.DragEvent, elementType: string) => {
    e.dataTransfer.setData(
      'application/json',
      JSON.stringify({ kind: 'new-element', elementType }),
    );
    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy';
  };

  return (
    <aside className="left-panel">
      {/* 1. Файл */}
      <Section title="Файл" icon="📁" defaultOpen>
        <button type="button" className="tool-btn" onClick={fileIO.importJson}>
          📥 Импорт JSON
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="tool-btn" onClick={fileIO.exportJson}>
          📤 Экспорт JSON
        </button>
        <div style={{ height: 10 }} />
        <button type="button" className="tool-btn" onClick={fileIO.exportSvgLocal}>
          🖼 SVG (локально)
        </button>
      </Section>

      {/* 2. Макеты */}
      <Section title="Макеты" icon="🎨">
        {TYPICAL_LAYOUTS.map((l) => (
          <button
            key={l.id}
            type="button"
            className="tool-btn"
            title={l.description}
            onClick={() => applyLayout(l.id)}
          >
            {l.icon} {l.name}
          </button>
        ))}
      </Section>

      {/* 3. Документы (BUNDLES с данными) */}
      <Section title="Документы" icon="📄">
        {BUNDLES.map((b) => (
          <button
            key={b.id}
            type="button"
            className="tool-btn"
            title={b.description}
            onClick={() => applyBundle(b.id)}
          >
            {b.icon} {b.name}
          </button>
        ))}
      </Section>

        <label className="field-label">Размер</label>
        <select
          className="field"
          value={activeSlide.format}
          onChange={(e) => handleFormat(e.target.value as DocFormat)}
        >
          {Object.entries(FORMATS).map(([k, v]) => (
            <option key={k} value={k}>{v.label}</option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <label className="field-label">Ориентация</label>
        <div className="segmented">
          <button
            type="button"

            onClick={() => handleOrientation('portrait')}
          >
            Книжная
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={activeSlide.orientation === 'landscape' ? 'active' : ''}
            onClick={() => handleOrientation('landscape')}
          >
            Альбомная
          </button>
        </div>

        {activeSlide.format === 'custom' && (

              />
            </label>
            <label className="field-label">
              Высота
              <input
                className="field"
                type="number"
                min={50}
                value={activeSlide.height}
                onChange={(e) => changeCustomSize(activeSlide.width, +e.target.value)}
              />
            </label>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="hint-sm">
          {activeSlide.width} × {activeSlide.height} px
        </div>

        <h4 style={{ margin: '10px 0 4px', fontSize: 11, color: '#666', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
          Отступы страницы
        </h4>
        <PaddingEditor value={activeSlide.padding} onChange={updatePadding} />

        />

/* ============================================================
   presentation/components/MigrationBanner.tsx
   MIG-UI-01, MIG-UI-02. РЎРєСЂРѕРјРЅС‹Р№ Р±Р°РЅРЅРµСЂ СЃ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕР№ СЃРІСЏР·СЊСЋ + rollback.
   ============================================================ */

import React, { useState } from 'react';

interface Props {
  reportUrl?: string;
  rollbackUrl?: string;
}

const DISMISS_KEY = 'ticket-editor:banner-dismissed';

export const MigrationBanner: React.FC<Props> = ({
  reportUrl = 'mailto:support@example.com?subject=ticket-editor%20feedback',
  rollbackUrl,
}) => {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(() => {
    try {
      return localStorage.getItem(DISMISS_KEY) !== '1';
    } catch {
      return true;
    }
  });

  if (!visible) return null;

  const dismiss = (): void => {
    try { localStorage.setItem(DISMISS_KEY, '1'); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    setVisible(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="migration-banner" role="status" aria-live="polite">
      <span>
        Р’С‹ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚Рµ РЅРѕРІСѓСЋ РІРµСЂСЃРёСЋ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР°.{' '}
        <a href={reportUrl} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">РЎРѕРѕР±С‰РёС‚СЊ Рѕ РїСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјРµ в†’</a>
      </span>
      {rollbackUrl && (
        <a href={rollbackUrl} className="migration-banner__rollback">РЎС‚Р°СЂР°СЏ РІРµСЂСЃРёСЏ</a>
      )}
      <button
        type="button"
        className="migration-banner__close"
        onClick={dismiss}
        aria-label="РЎРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ"
      >Г—</button>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\PropertiesPanel.tsx

import React from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { useDocumentStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { useSelectedElement } from '../hooks/useSelectedElement';
import { getElementStrategy } from './elements/registry';
import { Group, L, inputStyle } from './elements/properties/ui';

export const PropertiesPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const el = useSelectedElement();

  if (!el) {
    return <div style={{ padding: 20, color: '#94a3b8', textAlign: 'center' }}>Выделите элемент</div>;
  }

  const update = (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const target = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === el.id);
      if (target) Object.assign(target, patch);
    }, `update-${el.id}`);
  };

  const strategy = getElementStrategy(el.type);
  const PropsComponent = strategy?.PropertiesPanel;

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 12, overflowY: 'auto', height: '100%' }}>
      {/* Общие свойства для всех элементов */}
      <Group title="Положение">
        <L label="X"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.x)} onChange={(e) => update({ x: +e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="Y"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.y)} onChange={(e) => update({ y: +e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="Ширина"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.width)} onChange={(e) => update({ width: +e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="Высота"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.height)} onChange={(e) => update({ height: +e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="Поворот"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.rotation)} onChange={(e) => update({ rotation: +e.target.value })} /></L>
      </Group>

      {/* Специфичные свойства элемента через Strategy */}
      {PropsComponent && <PropsComponent el={el as any} update={update as any} />}
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

interface Props {
  title: string;
  colors: string[];
  onPick: (color: string) => void;
  onReset?: () => void;
  resetLabel?: string;
}

export const ColorPickerPopover: React.FC<Props> = ({
  title, colors, onPick, onReset, resetLabel = 'РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ',
}) => (
  <div className="rt-popover">
    <div className="rt-popover-title">{title}</div>
    <div className="rt-color-grid">
      {colors.map((c) => (
        <button
          key={c}
          type="button"
          className="rt-color-cell"
          style={{ background: c }}
          title={c}
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onPick(c); }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
    {onReset && (
      <button
        type="button"
        className="rt-popover-action"
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onReset(); }}
      >
        {resetLabel}
      </button>
    )}
  </div>
);

/* ============================================================
   richText/extensions.ts
   CQ-TS-04: Р±РµР· any; TipTap-РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєРё С‚РёРїРёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅС‹ С‡РµСЂРµР·
   CommandProps Рё Record<string, unknown>.
   ============================================================ */

import { Extension, type CommandProps } from '@tiptap/core';
import TableCell from '@tiptap/extension-table-cell';
import TableHeader from '@tiptap/extension-table-header';

declare module '@tiptap/core' {
  interface Commands<ReturnType> {
    fontSize: {
      setFontSize: (fontSize: string) => ReturnType;
      unsetFontSize: () => ReturnType;
    };
    indent: {
      indent: () => ReturnType;
      outdent: () => ReturnType;
    };
  }
}

/** РЈРЅРёРІРµСЂСЃР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ С‚РёРї РґР»СЏ attributes РІ renderHTML TipTap. */
type HtmlAttrs = Record<string, unknown>;

const asStr = (v: unknown): string =>
  typeof v === 'string' || typeof v === 'number' ? String(v) : '';

/* ============================================================
   FontSize
   ============================================================ */

export const FontSize = Extension.create({
  name: 'fontSize',
  addOptions() {
    return { types: ['textStyle'] as string[] };
  },
  addGlobalAttributes() {
    return [
      {
        types: this.options.types,
        attributes: {
          fontSize: {
            default: null,
            parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement): string | null =>
              el.style.fontSize?.replace(/['"]+/g, '') || null,
            renderHTML: (attrs: HtmlAttrs) =>
              attrs.fontSize ? { style: `font-size: ${asStr(attrs.fontSize)}` } : {},
          },
        },
      },
    ];
  },
  addCommands() {
    return {
      setFontSize: (fontSize: string) => ({ chain }: CommandProps) =>
        chain().setMark('textStyle', { fontSize }).run(),
      unsetFontSize: () => ({ chain }: CommandProps) =>
        chain().setMark('textStyle', { fontSize: null }).removeEmptyTextStyle().run(),
      // TipTap's addCommands typing requires Partial<RawCommands>; augmented
      // commands are visible only after `declare module`, and TS's object-literal
      // excess-property check rejects them, so we use the ecosystem-standard cast.
      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
    } as any;
  },
});

/* ============================================================
   Cell attributes (bgColor, borderColor, ...)
   ============================================================ */

const CELL_ATTRIBUTES = {
  bgColor: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement): string | null => el.style.backgroundColor || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: HtmlAttrs) =>
      attrs.bgColor ? { style: `background-color: ${asStr(attrs.bgColor)}` } : {},
  },
  borderColor: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement): string | null => el.style.borderColor || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: HtmlAttrs) =>
      attrs.borderColor ? { style: `border-color: ${asStr(attrs.borderColor)}` } : {},
  },
  borderWidth: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement): string | null => el.style.borderWidth || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: HtmlAttrs) =>
      attrs.borderWidth ? { style: `border-width: ${asStr(attrs.borderWidth)}` } : {},
  },
  borderStyle: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement): string | null => el.style.borderStyle || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: HtmlAttrs) =>
      attrs.borderStyle ? { style: `border-style: ${asStr(attrs.borderStyle)}` } : {},
  },
  cellPadding: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement): string | null => el.style.padding || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: HtmlAttrs) =>
      attrs.cellPadding ? { style: `padding: ${asStr(attrs.cellPadding)}` } : {},
  },
  textAlign: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement): string | null => el.style.textAlign || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: HtmlAttrs) =>
      attrs.textAlign ? { style: `text-align: ${asStr(attrs.textAlign)}` } : {},
  },
  verticalAlign: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement): string | null => el.style.verticalAlign || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: HtmlAttrs) =>
      attrs.verticalAlign ? { style: `vertical-align: ${asStr(attrs.verticalAlign)}` } : {},
  },
};

export const CustomTableCell = TableCell.extend({
  addAttributes() {
    return { ...this.parent?.(), ...CELL_ATTRIBUTES };
  },
});

export const CustomTableHeader = TableHeader.extend({
  addAttributes() {
    return { ...this.parent?.(), ...CELL_ATTRIBUTES };
  },
});

/* ============================================================
   Indent
   ============================================================ */

export const Indent = Extension.create({
  name: 'indent',
  addOptions() {
    return { types: ['paragraph', 'heading'] as string[] };
  },
  addGlobalAttributes() {
    return [
      {
        types: this.options.types,
        attributes: {
          indent: {
            default: 0,
            parseHTML: (element: HTMLElement): number =>
              parseInt(element.style.paddingLeft) / 20 || 0,
            renderHTML: (attributes: HtmlAttrs) => {
              const n = typeof attributes.indent === 'number' ? attributes.indent : 0;
              if (!n) return {};
              return { style: `padding-left: ${n * 20}px` };
            },
          },
        },
      },
    ];
  },
  addCommands() {
    return {
      indent: () => ({ tr, state, dispatch }: CommandProps) => {
        const { selection } = state;
        const { from, to } = selection;
        state.doc.nodesBetween(from, to, (node, pos) => {
          if (this.options.types.includes(node.type.name)) {
            const current = typeof node.attrs.indent === 'number' ? node.attrs.indent : 0;
            const next = Math.min(current + 1, 10);
            tr.setNodeMarkup(pos, undefined, { ...node.attrs, indent: next });
          }
        });
        if (dispatch) dispatch(tr);
        return true;
      },
      outdent: () => ({ tr, state, dispatch }: CommandProps) => {
        const { selection } = state;
        const { from, to } = selection;
        state.doc.nodesBetween(from, to, (node, pos) => {
          if (this.options.types.includes(node.type.name)) {
            const current = typeof node.attrs.indent === 'number' ? node.attrs.indent : 0;
            const next = Math.max(current - 1, 0);
            tr.setNodeMarkup(pos, undefined, { ...node.attrs, indent: next });
          }
        });
        if (dispatch) dispatch(tr);
        return true;
      },
      // TipTap's addCommands typing requires Partial<RawCommands>; augmented
      // commands are visible only after `declare module`, and TS's object-literal
      // excess-property check rejects them, so we use the ecosystem-standard cast.
      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
    } as any;
  },
});

        { v: 'solid', label: 'вЂ”вЂ”' },
        { v: 'dashed', label: '- - -' },
        { v: 'dotted', label: 'В·В·В·' },
      ].map((s) => (
        <button key={s.v} type="button" className="rt-btn"
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onSet({ borderStyle: s.v }); }}>
          {s.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
    <button type="button" className="rt-popover-action"
      onMouseDown={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        onSet({ borderColor: null, borderWidth: null, borderStyle: null });
      }}>
      РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ РіСЂР°РЅРёС†С‹
    </button>
  </div>
);

export const CellPadPopover: React.FC<{ onSet: (padding: string | null) => void }> = ({ onSet }) => (
  <div className="rt-popover" style={{ minWidth: 200 }}>
    <div className="rt-popover-title">РћС‚СЃС‚СѓРїС‹ (padding)</div>

          {p}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
    <button type="button" className="rt-popover-action"
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onSet(null); }}>
      РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ
    </button>
  </div>
);

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { Editor } from '@tiptap/react';
import { TableSizePicker } from './TableSizePicker';
import { CellFillPopover } from './TableCellPopovers';

export const TableOperationsToolbar: React.FC<{ editor: Editor }> = ({ editor }) => {
  const [pop, setPop] = useState<null | 'size' | 'fill'>(null);
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const toggle = (k: 'size' | 'fill') => setPop((v) => (v === k ? null : k));
  const insideTable = editor.isActive('table');

  /* Р—Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РїР°Р»РёС‚СЂС‹ РїРѕ РєР»РёРєСѓ РІРЅРµ РµС‘ (F-TT-07) */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pop) return;
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setPop(null);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, [pop]);

  const setCellAttrs = (attrs: Record<string, any>) => {
    editor.chain().focus().updateAttributes('tableCell', attrs).run();
    editor.chain().focus().updateAttributes('tableHeader', attrs).run();
  };

  const handleInsertTable = (r: number, c: number) => {
    editor.chain().focus().insertTable({ rows: r, cols: c, withHeaderRow: true }).run();

    const { doc } = editor.state;
    const lastNode = doc.lastChild;
    if (lastNode && lastNode.type.name === 'table') {
      editor.chain().focus().insertContentAt(doc.content.size, '<p></p>').run();
    }

    setPop(null);
  };

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} style={{ display: 'contents' }}>
      {/* Р’РЎРўРђР’РљРђ РўРђР‘Р›РР¦Р« */}
      <div className="rt-sep" />
      <div className="rt-popover-wrap">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="rt-btn"
          title="Р’СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Сѓ"
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); toggle('size'); }}
        >
          вЉћ РўР°Р±Р»РёС†Р°
        </button>
        {pop === 'size' && <TableSizePicker onPick={handleInsertTable} />}
      </div>

      {insideTable && (
        <>
          {/* РЎРўР РћРљР Р РљРћР›РћРќРљР */}
          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().addRowAfter().run(); }}>+РЎС‚СЂ</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().deleteRow().run(); }}>-РЎС‚СЂ</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().addColumnAfter().run(); }}>+РљРѕР»</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().deleteColumn().run(); }}>-РљРѕР»</button>
          </div>

          {/* РћР‘РЄР•Р”РРќР•РќРР• / Р РђР—Р”Р•Р›Р•РќРР• */}
          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РћР±СЉРµРґРёРЅРёС‚СЊ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().mergeCells().run(); }}>РћР±СЉРµРґ</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="Р Р°Р·РґРµР»РёС‚СЊ СЏС‡РµР№РєСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); editor.chain().focus().splitCell().run(); }}>Р Р°Р·СЉРµРґ</button>
          </div>

          {/* Р’Р«Р РђР’РќРР’РђРќРР• Р’ РЇР§Р•Р™РљР• */}
          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РџРѕ Р»РµРІРѕРјСѓ РєСЂР°СЋ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setCellAttrs({ textAlign: 'left' }); }}>в—§</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РџРѕ С†РµРЅС‚СЂСѓ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setCellAttrs({ textAlign: 'center' }); }}>в—«</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РџРѕ РїСЂР°РІРѕРјСѓ РєСЂР°СЋ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setCellAttrs({ textAlign: 'right' }); }}>в—Ё</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РџРѕ С€РёСЂРёРЅРµ" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setCellAttrs({ textAlign: 'justify' }); }}>в°</button>
          </div>

          {/* РћРўРЎРўРЈРџР« Р’ РЇР§Р•Р™РљР• */}
          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РЈРјРµРЅСЊС€РёС‚СЊ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setCellAttrs({ cellPadding: '4px' }); }}>в†ђРџР°Рґ</button>
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="РЈРІРµР»РёС‡РёС‚СЊ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setCellAttrs({ cellPadding: '12px' }); }}>в†’РџР°Рґ</button>
          </div>

          {/* Р—РђР›РР’РљРђ */}
          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <div className="rt-popover-wrap">
              <button type="button" className="rt-btn" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); toggle('fill'); }}>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°</button>
              {pop === 'fill' && (
                <CellFillPopover onSet={(c) => { setCellAttrs({ bgColor: c }); setPop(null); }} />
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';

interface Props {
  onPick: (rows: number, cols: number) => void;
}

const MAX = 6;

export const TableSizePicker: React.FC<Props> = ({ onPick }) => {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState({ r: -1, c: -1 });

  return (
    <div className="rt-popover">
      <div className="rt-popover-title">Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹</div>
      <div className="rt-table-grid">
        {Array.from({ length: MAX }, (_, r) =>
          Array.from({ length: MAX }, (_, c) => {
            const active = r <= hover.r && c <= hover.c && hover.r >= 0 && hover.c >= 0;
            return (
              <button
                key={`${r}-${c}`}
                type="button"
                className={`rt-table-cell ${active ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onMouseEnter={() => setHover({ r, c })}
                onMouseLeave={() => setHover({ r: -1, c: -1 })}
                onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onPick(r + 1, c + 1); }}
              />
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="rt-popover-hint">
        {hover.r >= 0 ? `${hover.r + 1} Г— ${hover.c + 1}` : 'РќР°РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂ'}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useEditor, EditorContent } from '@tiptap/react';
import StarterKit from '@tiptap/starter-kit';
import Underline from '@tiptap/extension-underline';
import TextAlign from '@tiptap/extension-text-align';
import TextStyle from '@tiptap/extension-text-style';
import Color from '@tiptap/extension-color';
import Highlight from '@tiptap/extension-highlight';
import Link from '@tiptap/extension-link';
import Table from '@tiptap/extension-table';
import TableRow from '@tiptap/extension-table-row';
import FontFamily from '@tiptap/extension-font-family';

// Gapcursor РІС…РѕРґРёС‚ РІ StarterKit (v2.6.6) вЂ” СЏРІРЅР°СЏ СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ РґР°С‘С‚
// warning "Duplicate extension names found: ['gapCursor']".
// РџРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚ Рё СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ СѓР±СЂР°РЅС‹.

import { FontSize, CustomTableCell, CustomTableHeader, Indent } from './richText/extensions';
import { ColorPickerPopover } from './richText/ColorPickerPopover';
import { TableOperationsToolbar } from './richText/TableOperationsToolbar';
import { TEXT_COLORS, BG_COLORS, FONT_SIZES, FONT_FAMILIES } from './richText/palettes';

interface Props {
  isOpen: boolean;
  initialHtml: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (html: string) => void;
}

type PopKind = null | 'color' | 'bg';

interface BtnProps {
  onClick: () => void;
  active?: boolean;
  title?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

/**
 * РљРЅРѕРїРєР° С‚СѓР»Р±Р°СЂР° РІС‹РЅРµСЃРµРЅР° РёР· С‚РµР»Р° РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚Р° вЂ” РёРЅР°С‡Рµ React
 * РІРёРґРёС‚ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚ РЅР° РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂ Рё РїРµСЂРµСЃРѕР·РґР°С‘С‚ DOM
 * (С‚РµСЂСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ С„РѕРєСѓСЃ Сѓ СЃРѕСЃРµРґРЅРёС… РєРЅРѕРїРѕРє).
 */
const Btn: React.FC<BtnProps> = ({ onClick, active, title, children }) => (
  <button
    type="button"
    className={`rt-btn ${active ? 'active' : ''}`}
    onClick={onClick}
    title={title}
  >
    {children}
  </button>
);

export const RichTextEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({ isOpen, initialHtml, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const [pop, setPop] = useState<PopKind>(null);
  const toolbarRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const isPlaceholder = initialHtml.includes('Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ С‚РµРєСЃС‚') || !initialHtml.trim();
  let cleanHtml = isPlaceholder ? '<p></p>' : initialHtml;

  if (cleanHtml.includes('<table')) {
    if (!cleanHtml.startsWith('<p>') && !cleanHtml.startsWith('<div>')) {
      cleanHtml = '<p><br></p>' + cleanHtml;
    }
    if (!cleanHtml.endsWith('</p>') && !cleanHtml.endsWith('</div>')) {
      cleanHtml += '<p><br></p>';
    }
  }

  const editor = useEditor({
    extensions: [
      StarterKit.configure({ heading: { levels: [1, 2, 3] } }),
      Underline,
      TextAlign.configure({ types: ['heading', 'paragraph'] }),
      TextStyle,
      Color,
      FontFamily,
      FontSize,
      Highlight.configure({ multicolor: true }),
      Link.configure({ openOnClick: false }),
      Table.configure({ resizable: true }),
      TableRow,
      CustomTableHeader,
      CustomTableCell,
      Indent,
    ],
    content: cleanHtml,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen && editor) {
      editor.commands.setContent(cleanHtml);
      setTimeout(() => {
        try {
          editor.commands.focus('end');
        } catch {
          /* noop: С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РЅРµ РєСЂРёС‚РёС‡РµРЅ, РµСЃР»Рё editor СЂР°Р·РјРѕРЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ */
        }
      }, 50);
    }
  }, [isOpen, cleanHtml, editor]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent): void => {
      if (toolbarRef.current && !toolbarRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setPop(null);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const handleSave = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editor) return;
    const html = editor.getHTML();
    const isEmpty = html === '<p></p>' || html === '<p><br></p>';
    onSave(isEmpty ? '' : html);
    onClose();
  }, [editor, onSave, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen || !editor) return null;

  const currentFontSize = (editor.getAttributes('textStyle').fontSize || '16px').replace('px', '');
  const currentColor = editor.getAttributes('textStyle').color || '#000000';
  const currentHighlight = editor.getAttributes('highlight').color || null;

  return (
    <div className="rt-modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={handleSave}>
      <div className="rt-modal" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="rt-header">
          <h3>Р РµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°</h3>
          <button type="button" className="rt-close" onClick={handleSave}>Г—</button>
        </div>

        <div className="rt-toolbar" ref={toolbarRef}>
          <div className="rt-group">
            <select
              className="rt-select"
              value={
                editor.isActive('heading', { level: 1 }) ? 'h1'
                  : editor.isActive('heading', { level: 2 }) ? 'h2'
                  : editor.isActive('heading', { level: 3 }) ? 'h3'
                  : 'p'
              }
              onChange={(e) => {
                const v = e.target.value;
                if (v === 'p') editor.chain().focus().setParagraph().run();
                else editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: +v[1] as 1 | 2 | 3 }).run();
              }}
            >
              <option value="p">РћР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№</option>
              <option value="h1">Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє 1</option>
              <option value="h2">Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє 2</option>
              <option value="h3">Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє 3</option>
            </select>
            <select
              className="rt-select"
              value={editor.getAttributes('textStyle').fontFamily || 'Arial'}
              onChange={(e) => editor.chain().focus().setFontFamily(e.target.value).run()}
            >
              {FONT_FAMILIES.map((f) => (
                <option key={f.value} value={f.value}>{f.label}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <select
              className="rt-select"
              style={{ width: 60 }}
              value={currentFontSize}
              onChange={(e) => editor.chain().focus().setFontSize(`${e.target.value}px`).run()}
            >
              {FONT_SIZES.map((s) => (
                <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBold().run()}
              active={editor.isActive('bold')}
              title="Р–РёСЂРЅС‹Р№"
            ><b>B</b></Btn>
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()}
              active={editor.isActive('italic')}
              title="РљСѓСЂСЃРёРІ"
            ><i>I</i></Btn>
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleUnderline().run()}
              active={editor.isActive('underline')}
              title="РџРѕРґС‡С‘СЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№"
            ><u>U</u></Btn>
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()}
              active={editor.isActive('strike')}
              title="Р—Р°С‡С‘СЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№"
            ><s>S</s></Btn>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-popover-wrap">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="rt-btn"
              title="Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°"
              onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setPop((p) => (p === 'color' ? null : 'color')); }}
            >
              <span className="rt-letter" style={{ borderBottomColor: currentColor }}>A</span>
            </button>
            {pop === 'color' && (
              <ColorPickerPopover
                title="Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°"
                colors={TEXT_COLORS.flat()}
                onPick={(c) => { editor.chain().focus().setColor(c).run(); setPop(null); }}
                onReset={() => { editor.chain().focus().unsetColor().run(); setPop(null); }}
              />
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="rt-popover-wrap">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="rt-btn"
              title="Р¤РѕРЅ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°"
              onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setPop((p) => (p === 'bg' ? null : 'bg')); }}
            >
              <span
                className="rt-letter rt-bg-letter"
                style={{
                  background: currentHighlight || 'transparent',
                  borderBottomColor: currentHighlight || '#9ca3af',
                }}
              >A</span>
            </button>
            {pop === 'bg' && (
              <ColorPickerPopover
                title="Р¤РѕРЅ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°"
                colors={BG_COLORS.flat()}
                onPick={(c) => { editor.chain().focus().setHighlight({ color: c }).run(); setPop(null); }}
                onReset={() => { editor.chain().focus().unsetHighlight().run(); setPop(null); }}
              />
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('left').run()}
              active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'left' })}
              title="РџРѕ Р»РµРІРѕРјСѓ РєСЂР°СЋ"
            >в—§</Btn>
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('center').run()}
              active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'center' })}
              title="РџРѕ С†РµРЅС‚СЂСѓ"
            >в—«</Btn>
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('right').run()}
              active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'right' })}
              title="РџРѕ РїСЂР°РІРѕРјСѓ РєСЂР°СЋ"
            >в—Ё</Btn>
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('justify').run()}
              active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'justify' })}
              title="РџРѕ С€РёСЂРёРЅРµ"
            >в°</Btn>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBulletList().run()}
              active={editor.isActive('bulletList')}
              title="РњР°СЂРєРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє"
            >вЂў РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє</Btn>
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleOrderedList().run()}
              active={editor.isActive('orderedList')}
              title="РќСѓРјРµСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє"
            >1. РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє</Btn>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().outdent().run()}
              title="РЈРјРµРЅСЊС€РёС‚СЊ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї"
            >в†ђ РћС‚СЃС‚СѓРї</Btn>
            <Btn
              onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().indent().run()}
              title="РЈРІРµР»РёС‡РёС‚СЊ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї"
            >в†’ РћС‚СЃС‚СѓРї</Btn>
          </div>

          <TableOperationsToolbar editor={editor} />
        </div>

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '@/shared/store/store';
import { useSelectionStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { DataPanel } from './DataPanel';
import { PropertiesPanel } from './PropertiesPanel';

export const RightPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const selectedIds = useStore(selStore, (s) => s.selectedIds);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'data' | 'props'>('props');

  return (
    <aside className="right-panel">
      <div className="rp-tabs">
        <button className={`rp-tab ${tab === 'data' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTab('data')}>📊 Данные</button>
        <button className={`rp-tab ${tab === 'props' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTab('props')}>
          ⚙️ Свойства {selectedIds.length > 0 && <span className="rp-tab-badge">{selectedIds.length}</span>}
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="rp-body">
        {tab === 'data' && <DataPanel />}
        {tab === 'props' && <PropertiesPanel />}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\shapes\PolylineRenderer.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { Line, Circle } from 'react-konva';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP as DASH_MAP } from '@/lib/stamps/render';
import type { DashType } from '@/shared/types';

export interface PolylineRendererProps {
  points: number[];
  stroke: string;
  strokeWidth: number;
  dash?: DashType;
  isArrow?: boolean;
  isSelected?: boolean;
  hitStrokeWidth?: number;
  handleRadius?: number;
  onPointMove?: (index: number, x: number, y: number) => void;
  onPointMoveEnd?: (index: number, x: number, y: number) => void;
  onPointContextMenu?: (index: number, e: any) => void;
}

export const PolylineRenderer: React.FC<PolylineRendererProps> = ({
  points,
  stroke,
  strokeWidth,
  dash = 'solid',
  isArrow = false,
  isSelected = false,
  hitStrokeWidth = 20,
  handleRadius = 6,
  onPointMove,
  onPointMoveEnd,
  onPointContextMenu,
}) => {
  if (!points || points.length < 4) return null;

  const dashArr = DASH_MAP[dash];

  const arrowNode = (() => {
    if (!isArrow || points.length < 4) return null;
    const n = points.length;
    const x1 = points[n - 4], y1 = points[n - 3];
    const x2 = points[n - 2], y2 = points[n - 1];
    const angle = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);
    const len = 14;
    const a1 = angle + Math.PI - 0.35;
    const a2 = angle + Math.PI + 0.35;
    return (
      <Line
        points={[
          x2 + len * Math.cos(a1), y2 + len * Math.sin(a1),
          x2, y2,
          x2 + len * Math.cos(a2), y2 + len * Math.sin(a2),
        ]}
        stroke={stroke}
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        lineCap="round"
        lineJoin="round"
        listening={false}
      />
    );
  })();

  const pointCount = points.length / 2;

  return (
    <>
      <Line
        points={points}
        stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)"
        strokeWidth={hitStrokeWidth}
        lineCap="round"
        lineJoin="round"
        listening={true}
      />
      <Line
        points={points}
        stroke={stroke}
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        dash={dashArr}
        lineCap="round"
        lineJoin="round"
        listening={false}
      />
      {arrowNode}
      {isSelected && Array.from({ length: pointCount }).map((_, i) => {
        const px = points[i * 2];
        const py = points[i * 2 + 1];
        return (
          <Circle
            key={i}
            x={px}
            y={py}
            radius={handleRadius}
            fill="#fbbf24"
            stroke="#f59e0b"
            strokeWidth={1.5}
            draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragMove={(e) => {
              e.cancelBubble = true;
              onPointMove?.(i, e.target.x(), e.target.y());
            }}
            onDragEnd={(e) => {
              e.cancelBubble = true;
              onPointMoveEnd?.(i, e.target.x(), e.target.y());
            }}
            onContextMenu={(e) => {
              if (!onPointContextMenu) return;
              e.evt.preventDefault();
              e.cancelBubble = true;
              onPointContextMenu(i, e);
            }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\SlidesBar.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '@/shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { createSlide, cloneSlide } from '@/domain/slide/operations';

export const SlidesBar: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);

  const addSlide = () => {
    const current = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);
    const next = createSlide(
      slides.length + 1,
      current?.format ?? 'a4',
      current?.orientation ?? 'portrait',
      undefined,
      current?.padding,
    );
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      draft.slides.push(next);
      draft.activeId = next.id;
    }, 'add-slide');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const duplicateSlide = (id: string) => {
    const src = slides.find((s) => s.id === id);
    if (!src) return;
    const clone = cloneSlide(src);
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      draft.slides.push(clone);
      draft.activeId = clone.id;
    }, 'dup-slide');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const removeSlide = (id: string) => {
    if (slides.length <= 1) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const i = draft.slides.findIndex((s) => s.id === id);
      if (i < 0) return;
      draft.slides.splice(i, 1);
      if (draft.activeId === id) draft.activeId = draft.slides[0].id;
    }, 'remove-slide');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const setActive = (id: string) => {
    docStore.setState((s) => ({ ...s, activeId: id }));
    selStore.clear();
  };

  return (
    <footer className="slides-panel">
      {slides.map((s, i) => (
        <div
          key={s.id}
          className={`slide-thumb ${s.id === activeId ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActive(s.id)}
          onDoubleClick={() => duplicateSlide(s.id)}
        >
          {i + 1}
          {slides.length > 1 && (
            <button
              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); removeSlide(s.id); }}
              style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 2, right: 4, border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#94a3b8' }}
            >×</button>
          )}
        </div>
      ))}
      <button className="add-slide" onClick={addSlide}>+ Слайд</button>
    </footer>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   stamps/canvas/CompositeFillLayer.tsx
   F-STMP-N-07, F-STMP-N-11, F-RFILL-12..15, §50.
   React.memo — при стабильных props не перерисовывается.
   ============================================================ */

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import type { FillSpec } from '@/lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import { useFillRasterizer } from '../../../hooks/useFillRasterizer';

interface Props {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  enabled?: boolean;
}

function anchorToWorld(
  target: StampShapeItem,
  local: { x: number; y: number },
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const lx = target.x + local.x * target.width;
  const ly = target.y + local.y * target.height;
  const cx = target.x + target.width / 2;
  const cy = target.y + target.height / 2;
  const rot = ((target.rotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dx = lx - cx;
  const dy = ly - cy;

  return {
    x: cx + dx * Math.cos(rot) - dy * Math.sin(rot),
    y: cy + dx * Math.sin(rot) + dy * Math.cos(rot),
  };
}

const CompositeFillLayerInner: React.FC<Props> = ({
  width,
  height,
  shapes,
  enabled = true,
}) => {
  const fills: FillSpec[] = useMemo(() => {
    const out: FillSpec[] = [];
    for (const s of shapes) {
      if (s.kind !== 'fill') continue;
      if (s.hidden) continue;
      if (!s.fillTargetShapeId) continue;

      const target = shapes.find((t) => t.id === s.fillTargetShapeId);
      if (!target) continue;

      const local = s.fillAnchorLocal ?? { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 };
      const { x, y } = anchorToWorld(target, local);

      out.push({
        px: x,
        py: y,
        color: s.fill || '#fbbf24',
        target,
      });
    }
    return out;
  }, [shapes]);

  const signature = useMemo(() => {
    const geo = shapes
      .filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden)
      .map(
        (s) =>
          `${s.kind}:${Math.round(s.x)}|${Math.round(s.y)}|${Math.round(
            s.width,
          )}|${Math.round(s.height)}|${Math.round(s.rotation || 0)}|${(
            s.points || []
          )
            .map((v) => Math.round(v))
            .join(',')}|${s.strokeWidth}`,
      )
      .join('||');

    const f = fills
      .map(
        (x) =>
          `${Math.round(x.px)},${Math.round(x.py)},${x.color},${
            x.target?.id ?? '-'
          }`,
      )
      .join(';');

    return geo + '###' + f;
  }, [shapes, fills]);

  const entry = useFillRasterizer({
    key: signature,
    width,
    height,
    shapes,
    fills,
    enabled: enabled && fills.length > 0,
  });

  const img = entry?.image ?? null;
  if (!img) return null;

  return (
    <KImage
      x={0}
      y={0}
      width={width}
      height={height}
      image={img}
      listening={false}
      perfectDrawEnabled={false}
    />
  );
};

export const CompositeFillLayer = React.memo(CompositeFillLayerInner);

/* ============================================================
   stamps/canvas/StampBackgroundLayer.tsx

   F-STMP-E-22: белый фон + тень + сетка + cross-hairs + border.
   F-STMP-E-04: сетка 10px, default on.

   Исправлено: раньше grid был невидим на белом — цвет #eef2f7 почти
   совпадал с фоном, а 1/zoom при zoom > 1 давало линии < 1px.
   Теперь #d5dbe3 + минимальная толщина 0.75px + clamp 1/zoom.
   ============================================================ */

import React from 'react';
import { Layer, Rect, Line, Shape } from 'react-konva';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '@/shared/constants';

interface Props {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  zoom: number;
  showGrid: boolean;
}

export const StampBackgroundLayer: React.FC<Props> = ({
  width, height, zoom, showGrid,
}) => {
  const strokeWidth = Math.max(0.75, 1 / zoom);

  return (
    <Layer>
      <Rect
        x={0}
        y={0}
        width={width}
        height={height}
        fill="#ffffff"
        shadowColor="#000"
        shadowBlur={14}
        shadowOpacity={0.12}
        listening={false}
      />

      {showGrid && (
        <Shape
          listening={false}
          sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
            const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
            c.beginPath();

            // Малые линии каждые GRID_SIZE
            for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += GRID_SIZE) {
              c.moveTo(x, 0);
              c.lineTo(x, height);
            }
            for (let y = 0; y <= height; y += GRID_SIZE) {
              c.moveTo(0, y);
              c.lineTo(width, y);
            }
            c.strokeStyle = '#d5dbe3';
            c.lineWidth = strokeWidth;
            c.stroke();

            // Усиленные линии каждые 5×GRID_SIZE — визуальный ориентир
            c.beginPath();
            for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += GRID_SIZE * 5) {
              c.moveTo(x, 0);
              c.lineTo(x, height);
            }
            for (let y = 0; y <= height; y += GRID_SIZE * 5) {
              c.moveTo(0, y);
              c.lineTo(width, y);
            }
            c.strokeStyle = '#b8c2cf';
            c.lineWidth = strokeWidth * 1.25;
            c.stroke();
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Cross-hairs по центру — F-STMP-E-22 */}
      <Line
        points={[width / 2, 0, width / 2, height]}
        stroke="#93c5fd"
        strokeWidth={Math.max(0.75, 1 / zoom)}
        dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
        listening={false}
      />
      <Line
        points={[0, height / 2, width, height / 2]}
        stroke="#93c5fd"
        strokeWidth={Math.max(0.75, 1 / zoom)}
        dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]}
        listening={false}
      />

      {/* Внешняя граница — F-STMP-E-22 */}
      <Rect
        x={0}
        y={0}
        width={width}
        height={height}
        stroke="#94a3b8"
        strokeWidth={Math.max(1, 1.5 / zoom)}
        listening={false}
      />
    </Layer>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   stamps/canvas/StampElementsLayer.tsx

import React, { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Layer, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type {
  StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem,
} from '@/shared/types';
import { StampShapeView } from '../StampShapeView';
import { StampStraightTextView } from '../StampStraightTextView';
import { StampArcTextView } from '../StampArcTextView';
import { StampTransformer } from './StampTransformer';
import { CompositeFillLayer } from './CompositeFillLayer';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Props {
  layerRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Layer>;
  trRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Transformer>;
  stampW: number;
  stampH: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  imageCache: Record<string, HTMLImageElement>;
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedType: SelectionType;
  selectedShape: StampShapeItem | null;
  snap: boolean;
  listening?: boolean;
  onSelect: (id: string, type: Exclude<SelectionType, null>) => void;
  onShapeDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onTextDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, text: StampTextItem) => void;
  onImageDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, image: StampImageItem) => void;
  onTransformEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<Event>) => void;
  onOpenTextEditor: (id: string) => void;
  onUpdateText: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
  onPointsChange?: (id: string, points: number[]) => void;
  onPointContextMenu?: (
    shapeId: string,
    pointIndex: number,
    clientX: number,
    clientY: number,
  ) => void;
}

const StampElementsLayerInner: React.FC<Props> = ({
  layerRef, trRef, stampW, stampH, shapes, texts, images, imageCache,
  selectedId, selectedType, selectedShape, snap, listening = true,
  onSelect, onShapeDragEnd, onTextDragEnd, onImageDragEnd, onTransformEnd,
  onOpenTextEditor, onUpdateText, onPointsChange, onPointContextMenu,
}) => {
  const regularShapes = useMemo(
    () => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill'),
    [shapes],
  );
  const legacyFills = useMemo(
    () => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === 'fill' && !s.fillTargetShapeId),
    [shapes],
  );

  const handleShapeSelect = useCallback(
    (id: string) => onSelect(id, 'shape'),
    [onSelect],
  );
  const handleLegacyFillSelect = useCallback(
    (id: string) => onSelect(id, 'shape'),
    [onSelect],
  );
  const handleStraightTextSelect = useCallback(
    (id: string) => onSelect(id, 'text'),
    [onSelect],
  );
  const handleArcTextSelect = useCallback(
    (id: string) => onSelect(id, 'text'),
    [onSelect],
  );
  const handleImageSelect = useCallback(
    (id: string) => onSelect(id, 'image'),
    [onSelect],
  );
  const handleStraightTextDblClick = useCallback(
    (id: string) => { onSelect(id, 'text'); onOpenTextEditor(id); },
    [onSelect, onOpenTextEditor],
  );
  const handleArcTextDblClick = useCallback(
    (id: string) => { onSelect(id, 'text'); onOpenTextEditor(id); },
    [onSelect, onOpenTextEditor],
  );

  return (
    <Layer ref={layerRef as any} listening={listening}>
      <CompositeFillLayer width={stampW} height={stampH} shapes={shapes} />

      {legacyFills.map((s) =>
        s.hidden ? null : (
          <StampShapeView
            key={s.id}
            shape={s}
            allShapes={shapes}
            draggable={false}
            onSelect={handleLegacyFillSelect}
            onDragEnd={onShapeDragEnd}
          />
        ),
      )}

      {regularShapes.map((s) =>
        s.hidden ? null : (
          <StampShapeView
            key={s.id}
            shape={s}
            allShapes={shapes}
            draggable={!s.locked}
            isSelected={selectedId === s.id && selectedType === 'shape'}
            onSelect={handleShapeSelect}
            onDragEnd={onShapeDragEnd}
            onPointsChange={onPointsChange}
            onPointContextMenu={onPointContextMenu}
          />
        ),
      )}

      {images.map((im) => {
        if (im.hidden) return null;
        const img = imageCache[im.src];
        if (!img) return null;
        return (
          <KImage
            key={im.id}
            id={im.id}
            name="image"
            x={im.x}
            y={im.y}
            width={im.width}
            height={im.height}
            image={img}
            draggable={!im.locked}
            perfectDrawEnabled={false}
            onClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; handleImageSelect(im.id); }}
            onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; handleImageSelect(im.id); }}
            onDragEnd={(e) => onImageDragEnd(im.id, e, im)}
          />
        );
      })}

      {texts.map((t) =>
        t.hidden ? null : t.mode === 'straight' ? (
          <StampStraightTextView
            key={t.id}
            text={t}
            draggable={!t.locked}
            onSelect={handleStraightTextSelect as any}
            onDblClick={handleStraightTextDblClick as any}
            onDragEnd={(e) => onTextDragEnd(t.id, e, t)}
          />
        ) : (
          <StampArcTextView
            key={t.id}
            text={t}
            shapes={shapes}
            snapEnabled={snap}
            draggable={!t.locked}
            onSelect={handleArcTextSelect as any}
            onDblClick={handleArcTextDblClick as any}
            onUpdate={(patch) => onUpdateText(t.id, patch)}
          />
        ),
      )}

      {listening && (
        <StampTransformer
          trRef={trRef}
          selectedShape={selectedShape}
          onTransformEnd={onTransformEnd}
        />
      )}
    </Layer>
  );
};

export const StampElementsLayer = React.memo(StampElementsLayerInner);

    onTransformEnd={onTransformEnd}
  />
);

/* ============================================================
   stamps/LineContextMenu.tsx
   F-LN-09, F-CM-03..04, F-CM-06..08.
   ============================================================ */

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export interface LineContextMenuState {
  clientX: number;
  clientY: number;
  shapeId: string;
  pointIndex: number;
}

interface Props {
  state: LineContextMenuState;
  canRemove: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onRemovePoint: () => void;
  onAddPoint: () => void;
}

const MARGIN = 8;

export const LineContextMenu: React.FC<Props> = ({
  state, canRemove, onClose, onRemovePoint, onAddPoint,
}) => {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onDown = (e: MouseEvent): void => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    };
    const onEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onDown);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onEsc);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onDown);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onEsc);
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  const vw = window.innerWidth;
  const vh = window.innerHeight;
  const left = Math.min(state.clientX, vw - 220 - MARGIN);
  const top = Math.min(state.clientY, vh - 120 - MARGIN);

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className="context-menu"
      style={{ left, top, zIndex: 3000, position: 'fixed' }}
    >
      <div className="context-menu-item" onClick={onAddPoint}>
        <span>Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ</span>
      </div>
      <div
        className={`context-menu-item ${canRemove ? 'danger' : 'disabled'}`}
        onClick={canRemove ? onRemovePoint : undefined}
      >
        <span>РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { StampTextItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { arcTextBBox } from '@/lib/stamps/geometry';

interface Props { text: StampTextItem; zoom: number; pan: { x: number; y: number }; onChange: (v: string) => void; onClose: () => void; }

export const StampInlineTextEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({ text, zoom, pan, onChange, onClose }) => {
  let left = 0, top = 0, width = 200, height = 30;
  if (text.mode === 'straight') {
    left = text.x * zoom + pan.x; top = text.y * zoom + pan.y;
    width = Math.max(80, text.width * zoom); height = Math.max(20, text.fontSize * 1.5 * zoom);
  } else {
    const rx = text.arcRx ?? text.arcRadius; const ry = text.arcRy ?? text.arcRadius;
    const bb = arcTextBBox({ text: text.text, arcCx: text.arcCx, arcCy: text.arcCy, arcRadius: text.arcRadius, arcRx: rx, arcRy: ry, arcStartDeg: text.arcStartDeg, arcEndDeg: text.arcEndDeg, fontSize: text.fontSize });
    const cx = bb.x + bb.width / 2; const cy = bb.y + bb.height / 2;
    width = Math.max(140, Math.min(280, bb.width * zoom)); height = Math.max(24, text.fontSize * 1.6 * zoom);
    left = cx * zoom + pan.x - width / 2; top = cy * zoom + pan.y - height / 2;
  }
  return (
    <textarea autoFocus value={text.text} onFocus={(e) => e.currentTarget.select()} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} onBlur={onClose}
      onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); onClose(); } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); (e.target as HTMLTextAreaElement).blur(); } }}
      onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      style={{ position: 'absolute', left, top, width, minHeight: height, zIndex: 50, boxSizing: 'border-box', padding: '2px 6px', border: '2px solid #6366f1', borderRadius: 4, outline: 'none', resize: 'none', background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.98)', boxShadow: '0 0 0 4px rgba(99,102,241,0.15)', fontSize: Math.max(10, text.fontSize * zoom), fontFamily: text.fontFamily, fontWeight: text.fontStyle.includes('bold') ? 700 : 400, fontStyle: text.fontStyle.includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal', textDecoration: text.textDecoration, color: text.fill, textAlign: text.align as any, lineHeight: 1.3, whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}
    />
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   stamps/shapes/registry.ts
   F-SSTRAT-06, F-SSTRAT-07.
   ============================================================ */

import React from 'react';
import type { StampShapeKind } from '@/shared/types';
import type { ShapeViewStrategy, ShapePanelContext } from './types';
import {
  RectView, CircleView, EllipseView, TriangleView, LineView, FillView,
} from './ShapeViews';
import {
  ClosedShapePanel, LinePanel, FillPanel,
} from './ShapePanels';

const RectPanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = (props) =>
  React.createElement(ClosedShapePanel, { ...props, showCornerRadius: true });

export const shapeViews: Record<StampShapeKind, ShapeViewStrategy> = {
  rect:     { kind: 'rect',     View: RectView,     Panel: RectPanel },
  circle:   { kind: 'circle',   View: CircleView,   Panel: ClosedShapePanel },
  ellipse:  { kind: 'ellipse',  View: EllipseView,  Panel: ClosedShapePanel },
  triangle: { kind: 'triangle', View: TriangleView, Panel: ClosedShapePanel },
  line:     { kind: 'line',     View: LineView,     Panel: LinePanel },
  fill:     { kind: 'fill',     View: FillView,     Panel: FillPanel },
};

export function getShapeView(kind: StampShapeKind): ShapeViewStrategy {
  const s = shapeViews[kind];
  if (!s) throw new Error(`[stamps] no view strategy for kind="${kind}"`);
  return s;
}

import React from 'react';
import type {
  StampShapeKind, DashType, StampFillMode,
} from '@/shared/types';
import type { ShapePanelContext } from './types';

const KIND_LABEL: Record<StampShapeKind, string> = {
  ellipse: 'Эллипс',
  circle: 'Круг',
  rect: 'Прямоугольник',
  triangle: 'Треугольник',
  line: 'Линия',
  fill: 'Заливка',
};

export const ClosedShapePanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext & { showCornerRadius?: boolean }> =
  ({ shape, onChange, showCornerRadius }) => (
    <>
      <h4>Свойства</h4>
      <label>
        Имя
        <input value={shape.name || ''} placeholder={KIND_LABEL[shape.kind]} onChange={(e) => onChange({ name: e.target.value })} />
      </label>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, marginTop: 6 }}>
        <label className="check" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={!!shape.locked} onChange={(e) => onChange({ locked: e.target.checked })} /> Закрепить

      </div>
      {showCornerRadius && (
        <label>Радиус <input type="number" min="0" value={shape.cornerRadius} onChange={(e) => onChange({ cornerRadius: +e.target.value })} /></label>
      )}

      <h4>Обводка</h4>
      <label>Цвет <input type="color" value={shape.stroke || '#000000'} onChange={(e) => onChange({ stroke: e.target.value })} /></label>
      <label>Толщина <input type="number" min="0.25" max="40" step="0.25" value={shape.strokeWidth} onChange={(e) => onChange({ strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} /></label>

        Стиль

    </>

export const LinePanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = ({ shape, onChange }) => {
  const points = shape.points && shape.points.length >= 4
    ? shape.points
    : [0, shape.height / 2, shape.width, shape.height / 2];
  const pointCount = points.length / 2;

  const setPoint = (index: number, x: number, y: number) => {
    const next = [...points];
    next[index * 2] = x;
    next[index * 2 + 1] = y;
    onChange({ points: next });
  };

  const addPointAtEnd = () => {
    const lastX = points[points.length - 2];
    const lastY = points[points.length - 1];
    const prevX = points[points.length - 4];
    const prevY = points[points.length - 3];
    onChange({ points: [...points, lastX + (lastX - prevX), lastY + (lastY - prevY)] });
  };

  const removePointAt = (index: number) => {
    if (pointCount <= 2) return;
    const next = points.slice();
    next.splice(index * 2, 2);
    onChange({ points: next });
  };

  return (
    <>
      <h4>Линия</h4>
      <label>
        Имя
        <input value={shape.name || ''} placeholder="Линия" onChange={(e) => onChange({ name: e.target.value })} />
      </label>

      <h4>Точки ({pointCount})</h4>
      <p className="hint-sm" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
        Двойной клик — добавить точку. ПКМ по точке — удалить.
      </p>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, margin: '8px 0' }}>
        {Array.from({ length: pointCount }).map((_, i) => (
          <div key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#6b7280', width: 20 }}>#{i + 1}</span>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={Math.round(points[i * 2])}
              onChange={(e) => setPoint(i, +e.target.value, points[i * 2 + 1])}
              style={{ width: 60, padding: '4px 6px', fontSize: 11 }}
            />
            <input
              type="number"
              value={Math.round(points[i * 2 + 1])}
              onChange={(e) => setPoint(i, points[i * 2], +e.target.value)}
              style={{ width: 60, padding: '4px 6px', fontSize: 11 }}
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={pointCount <= 2}
              onClick={() => removePointAt(i)}
              style={{
                padding: '4px 8px', fontSize: 11,
                color: pointCount <= 2 ? '#9ca3af' : '#dc2626',
                border: '1px solid #e5e7eb', borderRadius: 4, background: '#fff',
              }}
            >—</button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <button type="button" className="tool-btn" onClick={addPointAtEnd}>+ Добавить точку</button>

      <h4>Обводка</h4>
      <label>Цвет <input type="color" value={shape.stroke || '#000000'} onChange={(e) => onChange({ stroke: e.target.value })} /></label>
      <label>Толщина <input type="number" min="0.25" max="40" step="0.25" value={shape.strokeWidth} onChange={(e) => onChange({ strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} /></label>
      <label>
        Стиль
        <select value={shape.dash} onChange={(e) => onChange({ dash: e.target.value as DashType })}>
          <option value="solid">Сплошная</option>
          <option value="dashed">Штрих</option>
          <option value="dotted">Точки</option>
          <option value="dashdot">Штрих-пунктир</option>
          <option value="longdash">Длинный штрих</option>
        </select>
      </label>

      <label className="check" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          checked={!!shape.isArrow}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ isArrow: e.target.checked })}
        /> Стрелка
      </label>
    </>
  );
};

const AnchorFillPanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = ({ shape, allShapes, onChange }) => {
  const target = shape.fillTargetShapeId
    ? allShapes.find((s) => s.id === shape.fillTargetShapeId) || null
    : null;
  return (
    <>
      <h4>Заливка области</h4>
      {target && <p className="hint-sm">Привязана к <b>{target.name || target.kind}</b>.</p>}
      <label>
        Цвет
        <input
          type="color"
          value={(shape.fill && shape.fill.startsWith('#')) ? shape.fill : '#fbbf24'}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })}
        />
      </label>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, marginTop: 8 }}>
        <label className="check" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={!!shape.locked} onChange={(e) => onChange({ locked: e.target.checked })} /> Закрепить
        </label>
        <label className="check" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={!!shape.hidden} onChange={(e) => onChange({ hidden: e.target.checked })} /> Скрыть
        </label>
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

const LegacyPathFillPanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = ({ shape, allShapes, onChange }) => {
  const fillMode: StampFillMode = shape.fillMode || 'subtract';
  const outer = shape.fillOuterId
    ? allShapes.find((s) => s.id === shape.fillOuterId) || null
    : null;
  const holeIds = shape.fillHoleIds || [];
  const candidates = allShapes.filter((s) => s.id !== shape.id && s.kind !== 'fill');

  const toggleHole = (id: string) => {
    const next = holeIds.includes(id)
      ? holeIds.filter((x) => x !== id)
      : [...holeIds, id];
    onChange({ fillHoleIds: next });
  };

  return (
    <>
      <h4>Заливка области</h4>
      <label>
        Режим
        <select value={fillMode} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fillMode: e.target.value as StampFillMode })}>
          <option value="subtract">Вычесть</option>
          <option value="intersect">Пересечение</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <label>
        Цвет
        <input
          type="color"
          value={(shape.fill && shape.fill.startsWith('#')) ? shape.fill : '#bfdbfe'}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })}
        />
      </label>

      <h4>Фигура-основа</h4>
      <select value={shape.fillOuterId || ''} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fillOuterId: e.target.value || undefined })}>
        <option value="">-- не выбрана --</option>
        {candidates.map((s) => (
          <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
            {s.name || KIND_LABEL[s.kind]} ({s.id.slice(0, 4)}…)
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      {!outer && <p className="hint-sm" style={{ color: '#c00' }}>Выберите фигуру.</p>}

      <h4>{fillMode === 'intersect' ? 'Пересечение с' : 'Вычесть'}</h4>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
        {candidates.filter((s) => s.id !== shape.fillOuterId).map((s) => (
          <label key={s.id} className="check" style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={holeIds.includes(s.id)}
              onChange={() => toggleHole(s.id)}
            />
            <span>
              {s.name || KIND_LABEL[s.kind]}{' '}
              <span style={{ color: '#999', fontSize: 10 }}>{s.id.slice(0, 4)}…</span>
            </span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

export const FillPanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = (props) => {
  if (props.shape.fillTargetShapeId) return <AnchorFillPanel {...props} />;
  return <LegacyPathFillPanel {...props} />;
};

/* ============================================================

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Ellipse, Rect, Line, Shape } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP } from '@/lib/stamps/render';
import { paintFill } from '@/lib/stamps/fillGeometry';
import type { ShapeViewContext } from './types';
import { PolylineRenderer } from '../../shapes/PolylineRenderer';
import {
  ensureMinimalPoints,
  parsePoints,
  serializePoints,
  insertPoint,
} from '@/lib/polyline';

function useGroupProps(ctx: ShapeViewContext) {
  const { shape: s } = ctx;
  const w = Math.max(1, s.width);
  const h = Math.max(1, s.height);

  const handleClick = (e: any) => {
    e.cancelBubble = true;
    ctx.onSelect(s.id);
  };

  const handleDragEnd = (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => {
    ctx.onDragEnd(s.id, e);
  };

  return {
    group: {
      id: s.id,
      name: 'shape',
      x: s.x + w / 2,
      y: s.y + h / 2,
      offsetX: w / 2,
      offsetY: h / 2,
      rotation: s.rotation || 0,
      draggable: ctx.draggable,
      onDragEnd: handleDragEnd,
      onClick: handleClick,
      onTap: handleClick,
    },
    w,
    h,
  };
}

function useBaseStyle(s: StampShapeItem) {
  return {
    stroke: s.stroke,
    strokeWidth: s.strokeWidth,
    dash: STAMP_DASH_MAP[s.dash],
    fill: s.fill || 'transparent',
    listening: true,
    perfectDrawEnabled: false as const,
  };
}

const RectViewInner: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx);
  const base = useBaseStyle(ctx.shape);
  return (
    <Group {...group}>
      <Rect x={0} y={0} width={w} height={h} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening />
      <Rect x={0} y={0} width={w} height={h} cornerRadius={ctx.shape.cornerRadius} {...base} />
    </Group>
  );
};
export const RectView = React.memo(RectViewInner);

const CircleViewInner: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx);
  const base = useBaseStyle(ctx.shape);
  const r = Math.max(1, Math.min(w, h) / 2);
  return (
    <Group {...group}>
      <Ellipse x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radiusX={r} radiusY={r} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening />
      <Ellipse x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radiusX={r} radiusY={r} {...base} />
    </Group>
  );
};
export const CircleView = React.memo(CircleViewInner);

const EllipseViewInner: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx);
  const base = useBaseStyle(ctx.shape);
  const rx = Math.max(1, w / 2);
  const ry = Math.max(1, h / 2);
  return (
    <Group {...group}>
      <Ellipse x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radiusX={rx} radiusY={ry} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening />
      <Ellipse x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radiusX={rx} radiusY={ry} {...base} />
    </Group>
  );
};
export const EllipseView = React.memo(EllipseViewInner);

const TriangleViewInner: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx);
  const base = useBaseStyle(ctx.shape);
  const points = [w / 2, 0, w, h, 0, h, w / 2, 0];
  return (
    <Group {...group}>
      <Line points={points} closed fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening />
      <Line points={points} closed lineJoin="round" {...base} />
    </Group>
  );
};
export const TriangleView = React.memo(TriangleViewInner);

/* F-LN-02..09 */
const LineViewInner: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx);
  const s = ctx.shape;
  const points = ensureMinimalPoints(s.points, w, h);

  const handleDblClick = (e: any) => {
    if (!ctx.onPointsChange) return;
    e.cancelBubble = true;

    const stage = e.target.getStage();
    const pointer = stage?.getPointerPosition();
    if (!stage || !pointer) return;

    let node: any = e.target;
    while (node && typeof node.name === 'function' && node.name() !== 'shape') {
      node = node.getParent?.();
    }
    if (!node) return;

    const inv = node.getAbsoluteTransform().copy().invert();
    const local = inv.point(pointer);
    const zoom = stage.scaleX() || 1;
    const snapRadius = 5 / zoom;

    const pts = parsePoints(points);
    const next = insertPoint(pts, local.x, local.y, snapRadius);
    if (next === pts) return;
    ctx.onPointsChange(s.id, serializePoints(next));
  };

  const handlePointMove = (index: number, x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!ctx.onPointsChange) return;
    const next = [...points];
    next[index * 2] = x;
    next[index * 2 + 1] = y;
    ctx.onPointsChange(s.id, next);
  };

  /**
   * F-LN-03 / F-LN-09: не удаляем сразу — показываем меню.
   * Fallback: если родитель не передал onPointContextMenu, удаляем
   * как раньше (обратная совместимость).
   */
  const handlePointContextMenu = (index: number, e: any) => {
    if (ctx.onPointContextMenu) {
      const clientX: number = e.evt?.clientX ?? 0;
      const clientY: number = e.evt?.clientY ?? 0;
      ctx.onPointContextMenu(s.id, index, clientX, clientY);
      return;
    }
    if (!ctx.onPointsChange) return;
    const pts = parsePoints(points);
    if (pts.length <= 2) return;
    const next = pts.slice();
    next.splice(index, 1);
    ctx.onPointsChange(s.id, serializePoints(next));
  };

  return (
    <Group {...group} onDblClick={handleDblClick} onDblTap={handleDblClick}>
      <PolylineRenderer
        points={points}
        stroke={s.stroke}
        strokeWidth={s.strokeWidth}
        dash={s.dash}
        isArrow={!!s.isArrow}
        isSelected={!!ctx.isSelected}
        onPointMove={handlePointMove}
        onPointContextMenu={handlePointContextMenu}
      />
    </Group>
  );
};
export const LineView = React.memo(LineViewInner);

/* F-LFILL-09: legacy path fill. */
const PathFillInner: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = ({ shape: s, allShapes, onSelect }) => {
  const outer = s.fillOuterId
    ? allShapes.find((x) => x.id === s.fillOuterId) || null
    : null;
  if (!outer) return null;

  const holes = (s.fillHoleIds || [])
    .map((id) => allShapes.find((x) => x.id === id))
    .filter((x): x is StampShapeItem => !!x && !x.hidden && x.kind !== 'fill');

  const mode = s.fillMode || 'subtract';

  return (
    <Shape
      id={s.id}
      name="shape"
      listening
      draggable={false}
      perfectDrawEnabled={false}
      onClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(s.id); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(s.id); }}
      sceneFunc={(c) => {
        const raw = (c as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        paintFill(raw, { outer, holes, mode }, s.fill || '#bfdbfe');
      }}
      hitFunc={(c) => {
        const raw = (c as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        paintFill(raw, { outer, holes, mode }, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.001)');
      }}
    />
  );
};
const PathFill = React.memo(PathFillInner);

const FillViewInner: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  if (ctx.shape.fillTargetShapeId) return null;
  return <PathFill {...ctx} />;
};
export const FillView = React.memo(FillViewInner);

import type React from 'react';
