/* ============================================================
   stamps/StampArcTextView.tsx
   F-STMP-N-08..09, F-SGEO-01..06, F-STMP-E-25.
   Направление (cw/ccw) + точный старт по курсору.
   ============================================================ */

import React, { useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Line, Shape } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampTextItem, StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { canvasFont, drawArcText } from '@/lib/stamps/render';
import {
  arcTextBBox,
  findNearestShapeByCursor,
  computeArcParamsForAngle,
  closestPointOnHostPerimeter,
  drawPerimeterText,
  DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
  DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
  type ArcDirection,
} from './stampSnap';

interface Props {
  text: StampTextItem;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  snapEnabled: boolean;
  draggable: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onDblClick: () => void;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
}

function resolveArcRadii(
  text: StampTextItem,
  host: StampShapeItem | null,
  arcOffset: number,
): { rx: number; ry: number } {
  if (text.arcRx != null || text.arcRy != null) {
    return { rx: text.arcRx ?? text.arcRadius, ry: text.arcRy ?? text.arcRadius };
  }
  if (host && host.kind === 'ellipse' && host.width !== host.height) {
    const sign = text.arcOutside ? 1 : -1;
    const minR = text.fontSize * 0.8;
    return {
      rx: Math.max(minR, host.width / 2 + sign * arcOffset),
      ry: Math.max(minR, host.height / 2 + sign * arcOffset),
    };
  }
  return { rx: text.arcRadius, ry: text.arcRadius };
}

export const StampArcTextView: React.FC<Props> = ({
  text, shapes, snapEnabled, draggable, onSelect, onDblClick, onUpdate,
}) => {
  const groupRef = useRef<Konva.Group>(null);

  const host = text.arcHostId
    ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === text.arcHostId) || null
    : shapes.find((s) => {
        if (s.hidden || s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') return false;
        return (
          Math.abs(s.x + s.width / 2 - text.arcCx) < 1 &&
          Math.abs(s.y + s.height / 2 - text.arcCy) < 1
        );
      }) || null;

  const arcOffset = text.arcOffset ?? DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET;
  const isOutside = !!text.arcOutside;
  const direction: ArcDirection = text.arcDirection ?? 'cw';
  const spanDeg =
    text.arcSpanDeg ??
    Math.abs(text.arcEndDeg - text.arcStartDeg) ??
    DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG;
  const centerDeg = (text.arcStartDeg + text.arcEndDeg) / 2;

  const onPerimeter = !!host && (host.kind === 'rect' || host.kind === 'triangle');
  const { rx, ry } = resolveArcRadii(text, host, arcOffset);

  const bbox = arcTextBBox({
    text: text.text,
    arcCx: text.arcCx,
    arcCy: text.arcCy,
    arcRadius: text.arcRadius,
    arcRx: rx,
    arcRy: ry,
    arcStartDeg: text.arcStartDeg,
    arcEndDeg: text.arcEndDeg,
    fontSize: text.fontSize,
  });

  const arcHitPoints = useMemo(() => {
    if (onPerimeter) return [] as number[];
    const steps = 64;
    const start = (text.arcStartDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
    const end = (text.arcEndDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
    const out: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
      const a = start + (end - start) * (i / steps);
      out.push(text.arcCx + Math.cos(a) * rx, text.arcCy + Math.sin(a) * ry);
    }
    return out;
  }, [onPerimeter, text.arcCx, text.arcCy, rx, ry, text.arcStartDeg, text.arcEndDeg]);

  const handlers = useMemo(() => ({
    onClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },
    onTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },
    onDblClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); },
    onDblTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); },
  }), [onSelect, onDblClick]);

  const handleDragEnd = (e: any) => {
    const g = groupRef.current;
    if (!g) return;
    const dx = g.x();
    const dy = g.y();
    g.x(0);
    g.y(0);

    const nx = text.arcCx + dx;
    const ny = text.arcCy + dy;

    if (snapEnabled) {
      const stage = e.target.getStage();
      const pos = stage?.getRelativePointerPosition?.();
      if (pos) {
        const target = findNearestShapeByCursor(pos.x, pos.y, shapes, 260);
        if (target) {
          const signed = target.isOutside ? -arcOffset : arcOffset;
          const [cpx, cpy] = closestPointOnHostPerimeter(
            target.host, signed, pos.x, pos.y,
          );
          const hostCx = target.host.x + target.host.width / 2;
          const hostCy = target.host.y + target.host.height / 2;
          const angleRad = Math.atan2(cpy - hostCy, cpx - hostCx);
          const params = computeArcParamsForAngle(
            target.host,
            angleRad,
            text.fontSize,
            target.isOutside,
            spanDeg,
            arcOffset,
            direction,
            Math.max(1, text.text.length),
          );
          onUpdate({
            ...params,
            arcHostId: target.host.id,
            arcOutside: target.isOutside,
            arcDirection: direction,
          });
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    onUpdate({ arcCx: Math.round(nx), arcCy: Math.round(ny) });
  };

  let hitPoints: number[] = [];
  if (host) {
    if (host.kind === 'rect') {
      hitPoints = [
        host.x, host.y,
        host.x + host.width, host.y,
        host.x + host.width, host.y + host.height,
        host.x, host.y + host.height,
      ];
    } else if (host.kind === 'triangle') {
      hitPoints = [
        host.x + host.width / 2, host.y,
        host.x + host.width, host.y + host.height,
        host.x, host.y + host.height,
      ];
    }
  }

  return (
    <Group
      ref={groupRef}
      id={text.id}
      name="text-arc"
      x={0}
      y={0}
      draggable={draggable}
      onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}
      {...handlers}
    >
      {onPerimeter && host ? (
        <>
          <Group x={host.x} y={host.y} listening={false}>
            <Shape
              listening={false}
              perfectDrawEnabled={false}
              sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
                const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
                drawPerimeterText(c, {
                  host,
                  arcOffset,
                  isOutside,
                  centerDeg,
                  text: text.text,
                  fontSize: text.fontSize,
                  fontFamily: text.fontFamily,
                  fontStyle: text.fontStyle,
                  fill: text.fill,
                });
              }}
            />
          </Group>
          <Line
            points={hitPoints}
            closed
            stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)"
            strokeWidth={1}
            hitStrokeWidth={Math.max(12, text.fontSize * 1.2)}
            lineJoin="round"
            lineCap="round"
            listening
          />
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Shape
            listening={false}
            perfectDrawEnabled={false}
            sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
              const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
              drawArcText(c, {
                text: text.text,
                cx: text.arcCx,
                cy: text.arcCy,
                radius: text.arcRadius,
                radiusX: rx,
                radiusY: ry,
                startDeg: text.arcStartDeg,
                endDeg: text.arcEndDeg,
                flip: text.arcFlip,
                font: canvasFont(text.fontFamily, text.fontStyle, text.fontSize),
                fill: text.fill,
              });
            }}
          />
          <Line
            points={arcHitPoints}
            stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)"
            strokeWidth={1}
            hitStrokeWidth={Math.max(14, text.fontSize * 1.5)}
            lineCap="round"
            lineJoin="round"
            listening
          />
          <Rect
            x={bbox.x + bbox.width / 2 - 8}
            y={bbox.y + bbox.height / 2 - 8}
            width={16}
            height={16}
            fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)"
            listening
          />
        </>
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};

export default React.memo(StampArcTextView);

/* ============================================================

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { Stage } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type {
  StampShapeItem,
  StampTextItem,
  StampImageItem,
} from '@/shared/types';
import { StampBackgroundLayer } from './canvas/StampBackgroundLayer';
import { StampElementsLayer } from './canvas/StampElementsLayer';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Props {
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
  stageRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
  trRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Transformer>;
  layerRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Layer>;
  wrapSize: { w: number; h: number };
  width: number;
  height: number;
  zoom: number;
  pan: { x: number; y: number };
  onWheel: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<WheelEvent>) => void;
  onStageMouseDown: () => void;
  showGrid: boolean;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  imageCache: Record<string, HTMLImageElement>;
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedType: SelectionType;
  selectedShape: StampShapeItem | null;
  snap: boolean;
  fillMode: boolean;
  onFillClick: (px: number, py: number) => void;
  onSelect: (id: string, type: Exclude<SelectionType, null>) => void;
  onShapeDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onTextDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, text: StampTextItem) => void;
  onImageDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, image: StampImageItem) => void;
  onTransformEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<Event>) => void;
  onOpenTextEditor: (id: string) => void;
  onUpdateText: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
  onPointsChange?: (id: string, points: number[]) => void;
  onPointContextMenu?: (
    shapeId: string,
    pointIndex: number,
    clientX: number,
    clientY: number,
  ) => void;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const StampCanvas: React.FC<Props> = ({
  wrapRef, stageRef, trRef, layerRef, wrapSize, width, height, zoom, pan,
  onWheel, onStageMouseDown, showGrid, shapes, texts, images, imageCache,
  selectedId, selectedType, selectedShape, snap, fillMode, onFillClick,
  onSelect, onShapeDragEnd, onTextDragEnd, onImageDragEnd, onTransformEnd,
  onOpenTextEditor, onUpdateText, onPointsChange, onPointContextMenu, children,
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = wrapRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const prev = el.style.cursor;
    el.style.cursor = fillMode ? 'crosshair' : '';
    return () => { el.style.cursor = prev; };
  }, [fillMode, wrapRef]);

  return (
    <div className="stamp-editor-canvas" ref={wrapRef}>
      <Stage
        ref={stageRef as any}
        width={wrapSize.w}
        height={wrapSize.h}
        x={pan.x}
        y={pan.y}
        scaleX={zoom}
        scaleY={zoom}
        onWheel={onWheel}
        onMouseDown={(e) => {
          const stage = e.target.getStage();
          const pos = stage?.getRelativePointerPosition();
          if (fillMode && pos) {
            onFillClick(pos.x, pos.y);
            return;
          }
          if (e.target === stage) {
            onStageMouseDown();
          }
        }}
      >
        <StampBackgroundLayer width={width} height={height} zoom={zoom} showGrid={showGrid} />
        <StampElementsLayer
          layerRef={layerRef}
          trRef={trRef}
          stampW={width}
          stampH={height}
          shapes={shapes}
          texts={texts}
          images={images}
          imageCache={imageCache}
          selectedId={selectedId}
          selectedType={selectedType}
          selectedShape={selectedShape}
          snap={snap}
          listening={!fillMode}
          onSelect={onSelect}
          onShapeDragEnd={onShapeDragEnd}
          onTextDragEnd={onTextDragEnd}
          onImageDragEnd={onImageDragEnd}
          onTransformEnd={onTransformEnd}
          onOpenTextEditor={onOpenTextEditor}
          onUpdateText={onUpdateText}
          onPointsChange={onPointsChange}
          onPointContextMenu={onPointContextMenu}
        />
      </Stage>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
};

import type { PlacementMode } from '@/lib/stamps/geometry';
import { autoKey } from '@/lib/stamps/history/autoKey';
import { parsePoints, serializePoints } from '@/lib/polyline';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Props {
  initialWidth: number;
  initialHeight: number;
  initialShapes: StampShapeItem[];
  initialTexts: StampTextItem[];
  initialImages?: StampImageItem[];
  initialName?: string;
  onSave: (data: {

    texts: StampTextItem[];
    images: StampImageItem[];
  }) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export const StampEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({
  initialWidth, initialHeight, initialShapes, initialTexts, initialImages,
  initialName, onSave, onCancel,
}) => {
  const [name, setName] = useState(initialName || 'Новый штамп');
  const [width, setWidth] = useState(initialWidth);
  const [height, setHeight] = useState(initialHeight);
  const [placementMode, setPlacementMode] = useState<PlacementMode>('inside');
  const [showGrid, setShowGrid] = useState(true);
  const [snap, setSnap] = useState(true);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<SelectionType>(null);
  const [lineContextMenu, setLineContextMenu] = useState<LineContextMenuState | null>(null);

  const select = useCallback((id: string | null, type: SelectionType) => {
    setSelectedId(id);
    setSelectedType(type);
    setLineContextMenu(null);
  }, []);
  const clearSelection = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedId(null);
    setSelectedType(null);
    setLineContextMenu(null);
  }, []);

  const stageWrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);
  const layerRef = useRef<Konva.Layer>(null);

  const history = useStampHistory({
    shapes: initialShapes,
    texts: initialTexts,
    images: initialImages || [],
  });
  const { shapes, texts, images } = history.state;

  const zp = useStampZoomPan(stageRef);
  const wrapSize = useStampCanvasWrap(stageWrapRef as React.RefObject<HTMLElement>);

  useEffect(() => {
    zp.zoomFit(wrapSize.w, wrapSize.h, width, height);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [width, height, wrapSize.w, wrapSize.h]);

  const imageCache = useStampImageCache(images);

  const commit = useCallback(
    (
      s: StampShapeItem[],
      t: StampTextItem[],
      i: StampImageItem[],
      mergeKey?: string,
    ) => history.push({ shapes: s, texts: t, images: i }, mergeKey),
    [history],
  );

  const updateShape = useCallback(
    (id: string, patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => {
      const key = autoKey(id, patch);
      commit(
        shapes.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, ...patch } : s)),
        texts,
        images,
        key,
      );
    },
    [shapes, texts, images, commit],
  );

  const updateImage = useCallback(
    (id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => {
      const key = autoKey(id, patch);
      commit(
        shapes,
        texts,
        images.map((im) => (im.id === id ? { ...im, ...patch } : im)),
        key,
      );
    },
    [shapes, texts, images, commit],
  );

  const updateText = useStampTextUpdate({ shapes, texts, images, commit });

  const textEditor = useStampTextEditor({
    shapes, texts, images, commit, replace: history.replace,
  });
  const drag = useStampDragHandlers({
    shapes, width, height, snapEnabled: snap,
    updateShape, updateText, updateImage,
  });
  const handleTransform = useStampTransform({
    shapes, images, selectedType, updateShape, updateImage,
  });
  const layerActions = useStampLayerActions({
    shapes, texts, images, selectedId, commit, clearSelection,
  });
  const toolbar = useStampToolbarActions({
    shapes, texts, images, width, height, placementMode,
    selectedId, selectedType, commit, select,
  });
  const crud = useStampCRUD({
    shapes, texts, images, selectedId, selectedType, commit, select, clearSelection,
  });
  const uploadImage = useStampImageUpload({
    shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select,
  });
  const replaceImage = useStampImageReplace({
    updateImage, stampW: width, stampH: height,
  });
  const fill = useStampFillMode({
    shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select,
  });

  const withFillOff = useCallback(
    <A extends unknown[]>(fn: (...args: A) => void) =>
      (...args: A) => {
        fill.setFillMode(false);
        fn(...args);
      },
    [fill],
  );

  const addShape = useMemo(() => withFillOff(toolbar.addShape), [toolbar.addShape, withFillOff]);
  const addStraightText = useMemo(() => withFillOff(toolbar.addStraightText), [toolbar.addStraightText, withFillOff]);
  const addArcText = useMemo(() => withFillOff(toolbar.addArcText), [toolbar.addArcText, withFillOff]);
  const addImage = useMemo(() => withFillOff(uploadImage), [uploadImage, withFillOff]);

  const { undo: handleUndo, redo: handleRedo } = useStampUndoRedo({
    history, clearSelection,
  });

  useStampShortcuts({
    editingTextId: textEditor.editingTextId,
    selectedId,
    onCloseTextEditor: textEditor.closeTextEditor,
    onClearSelection: clearSelection,
    onDelete: crud.deleteSelected,
    onDuplicate: crud.duplicateSelected,
    onUndo: handleUndo,
    onRedo: handleRedo,
    onCloseEditor: onCancel,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current;
    const layer = layerRef.current;
    if (!tr || !layer) return;
    const isFill =
      selectedType === 'shape' &&
      shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId)?.kind === 'fill';
    if (!selectedId || isFill || (selectedType !== 'shape' && selectedType !== 'image')) {
      tr.nodes([]);
      layer.batchDraw();
      return;
    }
    const node = layer.findOne('#' + selectedId);
    if (node) tr.nodes([node]);
    else tr.nodes([]);
    layer.batchDraw();
  }, [selectedId, selectedType, shapes, texts, images]);

  /* ---------- F-LN-09: контекстное меню для точки линии ---------- */

  const handlePointContextMenu = useCallback(
    (shapeId: string, pointIndex: number, clientX: number, clientY: number) => {
      setLineContextMenu({ shapeId, pointIndex, clientX, clientY });
    },
    [],
  );

  const closeLineContextMenu = useCallback(() => setLineContextMenu(null), []);

  const handleRemoveLinePoint = useCallback(() => {
    if (!lineContextMenu) return;
    const shape = shapes.find((s) => s.id === lineContextMenu.shapeId);
    if (!shape || shape.kind !== 'line') return;
    const pts = parsePoints(shape.points);
    if (pts.length <= 2) return;
    const next = pts.slice();
    next.splice(lineContextMenu.pointIndex, 1);
    updateShape(shape.id, { points: serializePoints(next) });
    setLineContextMenu(null);
  }, [lineContextMenu, shapes, updateShape]);

  const handleAddLinePoint = useCallback(() => {
    if (!lineContextMenu) return;
    const shape = shapes.find((s) => s.id === lineContextMenu.shapeId);
    if (!shape || shape.kind !== 'line') return;
    const pts = parsePoints(shape.points);
    if (pts.length === 0) {
      setLineContextMenu(null);
      return;
    }
    const last = pts[pts.length - 1];
    const prev = pts.length > 1 ? pts[pts.length - 2] : last;
    const next = [...pts, {
      x: last.x + (last.x - prev.x),
      y: last.y + (last.y - prev.y),
    }];
    updateShape(shape.id, { points: serializePoints(next) });
    setLineContextMenu(null);
  }, [lineContextMenu, shapes, updateShape]);

  const selectedShape = selectedType === 'shape'
    ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) || null
    : null;
  const selectedText = selectedType === 'text'
    ? texts.find((t) => t.id === selectedId) || null
    : null;
  const selectedImage = selectedType === 'image'
    ? images.find((im) => im.id === selectedId) || null
    : null;
  const editingText = textEditor.editingTextId
    ? texts.find((t) => t.id === textEditor.editingTextId) || null
    : null;

  const handleSave = () =>
    onSave({ name, width, height, shapes, texts, images });

  return (
    <div className="stamp-editor-backdrop" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
      <div className="stamp-editor-modal" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <StampHead
          name={name} width={width} height={height}
          onChangeName={setName} onChangeWidth={setWidth} onChangeHeight={setHeight}
          onSave={handleSave} onCancel={onCancel}
        />
        <StampToolbar
          placementMode={placementMode}
          zoom={zp.zoom} minZoom={zp.MIN_ZOOM} maxZoom={zp.MAX_ZOOM}
          canUndo={history.canUndo} canRedo={history.canRedo}
          showGrid={showGrid} snap={snap}
          fillMode={fill.fillMode} fillColor={fill.fillColor}
          onChangePlacement={setPlacementMode}
          onAddShape={addShape}
          onAddStraightText={addStraightText}
          onAddArcText={addArcText}
          onAddImage={addImage}
          onToggleFillMode={() => fill.setFillMode((v) => !v)}
          onFillColorChange={fill.setFillColor}
          onUndo={handleUndo} onRedo={handleRedo}
          onZoomIn={zp.zoomIn} onZoomOut={zp.zoomOut}
          onZoomFit={() => zp.zoomFit(wrapSize.w, wrapSize.h, width, height)}
          onToggleGrid={setShowGrid} onToggleSnap={setSnap}
        />
        <div className="stamp-editor-body">
          <div className="stamp-editor-left">
            <StampLayersPanel
              shapes={shapes} texts={texts} images={images}
              selectedId={selectedId} selectedType={selectedType}
              onSelect={select}
              onToggleHidden={layerActions.toggleHidden}
              onToggleLocked={layerActions.toggleLocked}
              onDelete={layerActions.removeLayer}
              onReorder={layerActions.reorder}
            />
          </div>
          <StampCanvas
            wrapRef={stageWrapRef as React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>}
            stageRef={stageRef}
            trRef={trRef}
            layerRef={layerRef}
            wrapSize={wrapSize}
            width={width} height={height}
            zoom={zp.zoom} pan={zp.pan}
            onWheel={zp.onWheel}
            onStageMouseDown={() => {
              if (textEditor.editingTextId) textEditor.closeTextEditor();
              clearSelection();
            }}
            showGrid={showGrid}
            shapes={shapes} texts={texts} images={images}
            imageCache={imageCache}
            selectedId={selectedId} selectedType={selectedType}
            selectedShape={selectedShape}
            snap={snap}
            fillMode={fill.fillMode}
            onFillClick={fill.handleCanvasClick}
            onSelect={(id, type) => select(id, type)}
            onShapeDragEnd={drag.handleShapeDragEnd}
            onTextDragEnd={drag.handleTextDragEnd}
            onImageDragEnd={drag.handleImageDragEnd}
            onTransformEnd={handleTransform}
            onOpenTextEditor={textEditor.openTextEditor}
            onUpdateText={(id, patch) => updateText(id, patch)}
            onPointsChange={(id, points) => updateShape(id, { points })}
            onPointContextMenu={handlePointContextMenu}
          >
            {editingText && (
              <StampInlineTextEditor
                text={editingText}
                zoom={zp.zoom}
                pan={zp.pan}
                onChange={textEditor.changeEditingText}
                onClose={textEditor.closeTextEditor}
              />
            )}
            <div className="stamp-editor-info">
              {width}×{height} · {Math.round(zp.zoom * 100)}% · Элементов:{' '}
              {shapes.length + texts.length + images.length}
            </div>
          </StampCanvas>
          <div className="stamp-editor-right">
            {selectedShape && (
              <ShapePropsPanel
                shape={selectedShape}
                allShapes={shapes}
                onChange={(p) => updateShape(selectedShape.id, p)}
              />
            )}
            {selectedText && (
              <TextPropsPanel
                text={selectedText}
                onChange={(p) => updateText(selectedText.id, p)}
              />
            )}
            {selectedImage && (
              <StampImagePropsPanel
                image={selectedImage}
                onChange={(p) => updateImage(selectedImage.id, p)}
                onReplace={() => replaceImage(selectedImage.id)}
              />
            )}
            {!selectedShape && !selectedText && !selectedImage && (
              <p className="stamp-editor-empty">
                <b>Выберите элемент</b>, чтобы редактировать.
                <br /><br />
                <b>Ctrl</b> при перемещении/масштабировании — свободно.
                <br />
                <b>Двойной клик по тексту</b> — редактирование.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {lineContextMenu && (
        <LineContextMenu
          state={lineContextMenu}
          canRemove={(() => {
            const sh = shapes.find((s) => s.id === lineContextMenu.shapeId);
            if (!sh || sh.kind !== 'line') return false;
            return parsePoints(sh.points).length > 2;
          })()}
          onClose={closeLineContextMenu}
          onRemovePoint={handleRemoveLinePoint}
          onAddPoint={handleAddLinePoint}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';

interface Props { image: StampImageItem; onChange: (p: Partial<StampImageItem>) => void; onReplace: () => void; }

export const StampImagePropsPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ image, onChange, onReplace }) => (
  <>
    <h4>Свойства</h4>
    <label>Имя <input value={image.name || ''} placeholder="Картинка" onChange={(e) => onChange({ name: e.target.value })} /></label>
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, marginTop: 6 }}>
      <label className="check" style={{ flex: 1 }}><input type="checkbox" checked={!!image.locked} onChange={(e) => onChange({ locked: e.target.checked })} /> Закрыть</label>
      <label className="check" style={{ flex: 1 }}><input type="checkbox" checked={!!image.hidden} onChange={(e) => onChange({ hidden: e.target.checked })} /> Скрыть</label>
    </div>
    <h4>Геометрия</h4>
    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
      <label>X <input type="number" value={Math.round(image.x)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ x: +e.target.value })} /></label>
      <label>Y <input type="number" value={Math.round(image.y)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ y: +e.target.value })} /></label>
      <label>Ширина <input type="number" min="1" value={Math.round(image.width)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ width: Math.max(1, +e.target.value) })} /></label>
      <label>Высота <input type="number" min="1" value={Math.round(image.height)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ height: Math.max(1, +e.target.value) })} /></label>
    </div>
    <h4>Вписывание</h4>
    <label>Режим <select value={image.fit || 'contain'} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fit: e.target.value as any })}><option value="contain">Вписать (contain)</option><option value="cover">Заполнить (cover)</option><option value="fill">Растянуть (fill)</option></select></label>
    <h4>Файл</h4>
    <button onClick={onReplace}>🖼 Заменить картинку</button>
  </>
);

import React from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';

type LayerKind = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image';
interface LayerItem { id: string; type: LayerKind; name: string; icon: string; hidden?: boolean; locked?: boolean; }
interface Props {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images?: StampImageItem[];
  selectedId: string | null; selectedType: LayerKind | null;
  onSelect: (id: string, type: LayerKind) => void; onToggleHidden: (id: string, type: LayerKind) => void;
  onToggleLocked: (id: string, type: LayerKind) => void; onDelete: (id: string, type: LayerKind) => void; onReorder: (id: string, type: LayerKind, dir: 1 | -1) => void;
}

export const StampLayersPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ shapes, texts, images = [], selectedId, selectedType, onSelect, onToggleHidden, onToggleLocked, onDelete, onReorder }) => {
  const items: LayerItem[] = [
    ...texts.map((t) => ({ id: t.id, type: 'text' as const, name: t.name || (t.mode === 'arc' ? 'Текст (дуга)' : 'Текст'), icon: t.mode === 'arc' ? '◯' : 'T', hidden: t.hidden, locked: t.locked })),
    ...images.map((im) => ({ id: im.id, type: 'image' as const, name: im.name || 'Картинка', icon: '🖼', hidden: im.hidden, locked: im.locked })),
    ...shapes.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, type: 'shape' as const, name: s.name || ({ ellipse: 'Эллипс', rect: 'Прямоугольник', triangle: 'Треугольник', line: 'Линия', circle: 'Круг' } as any)[s.kind] || 'Фигура', icon: s.kind === 'ellipse' || s.kind === 'circle' ? '○' : s.kind === 'rect' ? '▭' : s.kind === 'triangle' ? '△' : '╱', hidden: s.hidden, locked: s.locked })),
  ];

  return (
    <div className="stamp-layers">
      <div className="stamp-layers-head">Слои ({items.length})</div>
      <div className="stamp-layers-list">
        {items.length === 0 && <p className="stamp-editor-empty">Пусто. Добавьте фигуру, текст или картинку.</p>}
        {items.map((item) => {
          const active = item.id === selectedId && item.type === selectedType;
          return (
            <div key={item.id} className={`stamp-layer ${active ? 'active' : ''} ${item.hidden ? 'hidden' : ''}`} onClick={() => onSelect(item.id, item.type)}>
              <span className="stamp-layer-icon">{item.icon}</span>
              <span className="stamp-layer-name">{item.name}</span>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title={item.hidden ? 'Показать' : 'Скрыть'} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleHidden(item.id, item.type); }}>{item.hidden ? '👁' : '👁‍🗨'}</button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title={item.locked ? 'Разблокировать' : 'Заблокировать'} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleLocked(item.id, item.type); }}>{item.locked ? '🔒' : '🔓'}</button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title="Выше" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onReorder(item.id, item.type, 1); }}>▴</button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title="Ниже" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onReorder(item.id, item.type, -1); }}>▾</button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn danger" title="Удалить" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onDelete(item.id, item.type); }}>×</button>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { StampShape, StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';
import {
  getAllStamps, addCustomStamp, updateCustomStamp, removeCustomStamp, isBuiltinStamp, forkStampAsCustom, type StampTemplate,
} from '@/lib/stamps/library';
import { newStampFromTemplate, newLegacyStamp } from '@/lib/stamps/factories';
import { StampEditor } from './StampEditor';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../../providers/EditorProvider';

const LEGACY_QUICK: { shape: StampShape; label: string; icon: string }[] = [
  { shape: 'circle',   label: 'Круглая',       icon: '⬤' },
  { shape: 'oval',     label: 'Овал',          icon: '⬭' },
  { shape: 'rect',     label: 'Прямоугольная', icon: '▭' },
  { shape: 'triangle', label: 'Треугольная',   icon: '△' },
];

interface EditorState {
  mode: 'create' | 'edit'; sourceId?: string; sourceIsBuiltin?: boolean; customId?: string;
  width: number; height: number; shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[]; name?: string;
}

function toDragPayload(tpl: StampTemplate) {
  return JSON.stringify({ kind: 'stamp-template', template: { id: tpl.id, name: tpl.name, description: tpl.description, icon: tpl.icon, width: tpl.width, height: tpl.height, shapes: tpl.shapes, texts: tpl.texts, images: tpl.images || [] } });
}

export const StampLibraryPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(0);
  const [editorState, setEditorState] = useState<EditorState | null>(null);

  const stamps = useMemo(() => {
  // `version` — cache-buster: после add/remove/update custom stamp
  // localStorage меняется, useMemo должен пересчитать.
  void version;
  return getAllStamps();
}, [version]);
  const generalStamps = useMemo(() => stamps.filter((s) => !s.custom && !s.banking), [stamps]);
  const bankingStamps = useMemo(() => stamps.filter((s) => !s.custom && s.banking), [stamps]);
  const customStamps  = useMemo(() => stamps.filter((s) => s.custom), [stamps]);

  const addElement = (el: StampShapeItem | StampTextItem | StampImageItem | any) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (s) s.elements.push(el);
    }, 'add-stamp-element');
    selStore.select(el.id);
  };

  const applyStamp = (tpl: StampTemplate) => addElement(newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200));
  const addLegacy = (shape: StampShape) => addElement(newLegacyStamp(shape, 200, 200));

  const openNew = () => { setEditorState({ mode: 'create', width: 200, height: 200, shapes: [], texts: [], images: [], name: 'Мой штамп' }); };
  const openEdit = (tpl: StampTemplate) => {
    const builtin = isBuiltinStamp(tpl.id);
    setEditorState({ mode: 'edit', sourceId: tpl.id, sourceIsBuiltin: builtin, customId: builtin ? undefined : tpl.id, width: tpl.width, height: tpl.height, shapes: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.shapes)), texts: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.texts)), images: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.images || [])), name: tpl.name });
  };

  const handleSave = (data: { name: string; width: number; height: number; shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[]; }) => {
    if (!editorState) return;
    if (editorState.mode === 'edit') {
      if (editorState.sourceIsBuiltin) {
        const source = stamps.find((s) => s.id === editorState.sourceId);
        if (source) addCustomStamp(forkStampAsCustom({ ...source, name: data.name, width: data.width, height: data.height, shapes: data.shapes, texts: data.texts, images: data.images }));
      } else if (editorState.customId) {
        updateCustomStamp(editorState.customId, { name: data.name, width: data.width, height: data.height, shapes: data.shapes, texts: data.texts, images: data.images });
      }
      setVersion((v) => v + 1); setEditorState(null); return;
    }
    const tpl: StampTemplate = { id: `custom-${Date.now()}`, name: data.name, icon: '🖼', width: data.width, height: data.height, shapes: data.shapes, texts: data.texts, images: data.images, custom: true };
    addCustomStamp(tpl); setVersion((v) => v + 1); setEditorState(null); addElement(newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200));
  };

  const deleteStamp = (tpl: StampTemplate) => {
    if (!tpl.custom) return;
    if (!window.confirm(`Удалить штамп «${tpl.name}»?`)) return;
    removeCustomStamp(tpl.id); setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  };

  const renderItem = (s: StampTemplate, opts: { editable: boolean; deletable: boolean }) => (
    <div key={s.id} style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
      <button className="tool-tile" title={s.description || s.name} draggable onDragStart={(e) => { e.dataTransfer.setData('application/json', toDragPayload(s)); e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy'; }} onClick={() => applyStamp(s)} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
        <span className="tile-ico">{s.icon || '🖼'}</span><span>{s.name}</span>
      </button>
      {opts.editable && <button title="Редактировать" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); openEdit(s); }} style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 2, right: 2, width: 18, height: 18, padding: 0, fontSize: 10, lineHeight: '16px', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 3, background: 'var(--panel)', color: 'var(--text)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>✎</button>}
      {opts.deletable && <button title="Удалить" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); deleteStamp(s); }} style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 2, right: 22, width: 18, height: 18, padding: 0, fontSize: 11, lineHeight: '16px', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 3, background: 'var(--panel)', color: 'var(--danger, #ef4444)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>×</button>}
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <>
      <label className="field-label">Быстрые (устаревшие)</label>
      <div className="tool-grid">{LEGACY_QUICK.map((q) => <button key={q.shape} className="tool-tile" onClick={() => addLegacy(q.shape)}><span className="tile-ico">{q.icon}</span><span>{q.label}</span></button>)}</div>
      <label className="field-label" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>Встроенные</label>
      <div className="tool-grid">{generalStamps.map((s) => renderItem(s, { editable: true, deletable: false }))}</div>
      <label className="field-label" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>Банковские</label>
      <div className="tool-grid">{bankingStamps.map((s) => renderItem(s, { editable: true, deletable: false }))}</div>
      <label className="field-label" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>Мои штампы</label>
      <div className="tool-grid">
        {customStamps.map((s) => renderItem(s, { editable: true, deletable: true }))}
        {customStamps.length === 0 && <p className="hint-sm" style={{ gridColumn: '1 / -1', margin: 0 }}>Пока пусто.</p>}
      </div>
      <button className="tool-btn" style={{ marginTop: 8 }} onClick={openNew}>+ Создать штамп…</button>
      {editorState && <StampEditor initialWidth={editorState.width} initialHeight={editorState.height} initialShapes={editorState.shapes} initialTexts={editorState.texts} initialImages={editorState.images} initialName={editorState.name} onSave={handleSave} onCancel={() => setEditorState(null)} />}
    </>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { getShapeView } from './shapes';

interface Props {
  shape: StampShapeItem;
  allShapes?: StampShapeItem[];
  draggable: boolean;
  isSelected?: boolean;
  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
  onDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onPointsChange?: (id: string, points: number[]) => void;
  onPointContextMenu?: (
    shapeId: string,
    pointIndex: number,
    clientX: number,
    clientY: number,
  ) => void;
}

const StampShapeViewInner: React.FC<Props> = ({
  shape, allShapes = [], draggable, isSelected,
  onSelect, onDragEnd, onPointsChange, onPointContextMenu,
}) => {
  const strategy = getShapeView(shape.kind);
  const View = strategy.View;
  return (
    <View
      shape={shape}
      allShapes={allShapes}
      draggable={draggable}
      isSelected={isSelected}
      onSelect={onSelect}
      onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
      onPointsChange={onPointsChange}
      onPointContextMenu={onPointContextMenu}
    />
  );
};

export const StampShapeView = React.memo(StampShapeViewInner, (prev, next) => (
  prev.shape === next.shape
  && prev.draggable === next.draggable
  && prev.isSelected === next.isSelected
  && prev.allShapes === next.allShapes
  && prev.onSelect === next.onSelect
  && prev.onDragEnd === next.onDragEnd
  && prev.onPointsChange === next.onPointsChange
  && prev.onPointContextMenu === next.onPointContextMenu
));

/* ============================================================
   stamps/StampStraightTextView.tsx
   F-STMP-E-07, REG-B3. React.memo.
   ============================================================ */

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Text } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampTextItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { konvaStyle } from '@/lib/stamps/render';

interface Props {
  text: StampTextItem;
  draggable: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onDblClick: () => void;
  onDragEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
}

const StampStraightTextViewInner: React.FC<Props> = ({
  text, draggable, onSelect, onDblClick, onDragEnd,
}) => {
  const handlers = React.useMemo(() => ({
    onClick: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },
    onTap: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); },
    onDblClick: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); },
    onDblTap: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); },
  }), [onSelect, onDblClick]);

  return (
    <Group
      id={text.id}
      name="text"
      x={text.x}
      y={text.y}
      draggable={draggable}
      onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
      {...handlers}
    >
      <Text
        x={0}
        y={0}
        width={text.width}
        height={text.fontSize * 1.4}
        text={text.text}
        fontSize={text.fontSize}
        fontFamily={text.fontFamily}
        fontStyle={konvaStyle(text.fontStyle)}
        textDecoration={text.textDecoration}
        fill={text.fill}
        align={text.align}
        listening
        hitStrokeWidth={0}
        perfectDrawEnabled={false}
      />
    </Group>
  );
};

export const StampStraightTextView = React.memo(StampStraightTextViewInner);

import React from 'react';
import type { StampTextItem, TextAlign } from '@/shared/types';
import {
  MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, type ArcDirection,
} from './stampSnap';

interface Props {
  text: StampTextItem;
  onChange: (p: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
}

export const TextPropsPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ text, onChange }) => {
  const hasBold = (text.fontStyle || '').includes('bold');
  const hasItalic = (text.fontStyle || '').includes('italic');
  const hasUnderline = (text.textDecoration || '').includes('underline');
  const direction: ArcDirection = text.arcDirection ?? 'cw';

  const toggle = (flag: 'bold' | 'italic') => {
    const parts = (text.fontStyle || '').split(' ').filter(Boolean);
    const next = parts.includes(flag)
      ? parts.filter((p) => p !== flag)
      : [...parts, flag];
    onChange({ fontStyle: next.join(' ') || 'normal' });
  };

  const rx = text.arcRx ?? text.arcRadius;
  const ry = text.arcRy ?? text.arcRadius;

  return (
    <>
      <h4>Текст</h4>
      <label>
        Имя
        <input
          value={text.name || ''}
          placeholder="Текст"
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ name: e.target.value })}
        />
      </label>
      <label>
        Содержимое
        <textarea
          value={text.text}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ text: e.target.value })}
        />
      </label>
      <label>
        Режим
        <select
          value={text.mode}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ mode: e.target.value as 'straight' | 'arc' })}
        >
          <option value="straight">Прямой</option>
          <option value="arc">По дуге</option>
        </select>
      </label>

      {text.mode === 'straight' ? (
        <>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
            <label>X <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.x)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ x: +e.target.value })} /></label>
            <label>Y <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.y)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ y: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          </div>
          <label>Ширина <input type="number" min="10" value={Math.round(text.width)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ width: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          <label>
            Выравнивание
            <select value={text.align} onChange={(e) => onChange({ align: e.target.value as TextAlign })}>
              <option value="left">Влево</option>
              <option value="center">По центру</option>
              <option value="right">Вправо</option>
            </select>
          </label>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <h4>Дуга</h4>
          <label>
            Направление
            <select
              value={direction}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcDirection: e.target.value as ArcDirection })}
            >
              <option value="cw">По часовой</option>
              <option value="ccw">Против часовой</option>
            </select>
          </label>
          <label>
            Угол: {Math.round(text.arcSpanDeg ?? 320)}°
            <input
              type="range"
              min={MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG}
              max={MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG}
              value={text.arcSpanDeg ?? 320}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcSpanDeg: +e.target.value })}
            />
          </label>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
            <label>
              Градус
              <input
                type="number"
                min={MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG}
                max={MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG}
                value={Math.round(text.arcSpanDeg ?? 320)}
                onChange={(e) => onChange({
                  arcSpanDeg: Math.max(
                    MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
                    Math.min(MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, +e.target.value),
                  ),
                })}
              />
            </label>
            <label>
              Отступ px
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0"
                max="100"
                value={Math.round(text.arcOffset ?? 6)}
                onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcOffset: Math.max(0, +e.target.value) })}
              />
            </label>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
            <label>
              Радиус X
              <input
                type="number"
                value={Math.round(rx)}
                onChange={(e) => onChange({
                  arcRx: +e.target.value,
                  arcRy: text.arcRy ?? text.arcRadius,
                })}
              />
            </label>
            <label>
              Радиус Y
              <input
                type="number"
                value={Math.round(ry)}
                onChange={(e) => onChange({
                  arcRy: +e.target.value,
                  arcRx: text.arcRx ?? text.arcRadius,
                })}
              />
            </label>
          </div>
          <label className="check" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={!!text.arcOutside}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcOutside: e.target.checked })}
            />{' '}
            Снаружи
          </label>
          <h4>Центр дуги</h4>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
            <label>X <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.arcCx)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcCx: +e.target.value })} /></label>
            <label>Y <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.arcCy)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcCy: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
            <label>Начало° <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.arcStartDeg)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcStartDeg: +e.target.value })} /></label>
            <label>Конец° <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.arcEndDeg)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcEndDeg: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      <h4>Стиль</h4>
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
        <label>Размер <input type="number" min="6" value={text.fontSize} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontSize: +e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label>
          Шрифт
          <select value={text.fontFamily} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontFamily: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="Arial">Arial</option>
            <option value="Times New Roman">Times New Roman</option>
            <option value="Courier New">Courier New</option>
            <option value="Georgia">Georgia</option>
            <option value="Verdana">Verdana</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, marginTop: 6 }}>
        <button className={hasBold ? 'active' : ''} style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={() => toggle('bold')}><b>B</b></button>
        <button className={hasItalic ? 'active' : ''} style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={() => toggle('italic')}><i>I</i></button>
        <button
          className={hasUnderline ? 'active' : ''}
          style={{ flex: 1 }}
          onClick={() => onChange({ textDecoration: hasUnderline ? '' : 'underline' })}
        >
          <u>U</u>
        </button>
      </div>
      <label>
        Цвет
        <input type="color" value={text.fill} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })} />
      </label>
    </>
  );

// src/presentation/components/TextToolbar.tsx
import React, { useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { TextAlign } from '@/shared/types';

export interface TextStylePatch {
  fontSize?: number;
  fontFamily?: string;
  fontStyle?: string;
  textDecoration?: string;
  fill?: string;
  align?: TextAlign;
}

interface Props {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  elementHeight: number;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  fill: string;
  align: TextAlign;
  onChange: (patch: TextStylePatch) => void;
  onCommit: () => void;
}

const FONTS = [
  'Arial', 'Times New Roman', 'Courier New',
  'Georgia', 'Verdana', 'Comic Sans MS',
];
const SIZES = [8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 24, 28, 32, 36, 48, 64, 72];
const MARGIN = 8;

export const TextToolbar: React.FC<Props> = ({
  x, y, elementHeight,
  fontSize, fontFamily, fontStyle, textDecoration, fill, align,
  onChange, onCommit,
}) => {
  const isBold = fontStyle.includes('bold');
  const isItalic = fontStyle.includes('italic');
  const isUnderline = textDecoration.includes('underline');

  const toolbarRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [size, setSize] = useState({ w: 520, h: 44 });

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const el = toolbarRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width && r.height) setSize({ w: r.width, h: r.height });
  }, [fontFamily, fontSize]);

  const vw = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerWidth : 1280;
  const vh = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerHeight : 800;

  const px = Math.max(MARGIN, Math.min(x, vw - size.w - MARGIN));

  const aboveY = y - size.h - MARGIN;
  const belowY = y + elementHeight + MARGIN;
  let py: number;
  if (aboveY >= MARGIN) py = aboveY;
  else if (belowY + size.h <= vh - MARGIN) py = belowY;
  else py = Math.max(MARGIN, vh - size.h - MARGIN);

  const toggleStyle = (flag: 'bold' | 'italic') => {
    const parts = fontStyle.split(' ').filter(Boolean);
    const idx = parts.indexOf(flag);
    if (idx >= 0) parts.splice(idx, 1);
    else parts.push(flag);
    onChange({ fontStyle: parts.join(' ') || 'normal' });
  };

  const toggleUnderline = () => {
    onChange({ textDecoration: isUnderline ? '' : 'underline' });
  };

  const keepFocus = (e: React.MouseEvent) => e.preventDefault();
  const stopBubble = (e: React.MouseEvent) => e.stopPropagation();

  return (
    <div
      ref={toolbarRef}
      className="text-toolbar"
      style={{ left: px, top: py }}
      onMouseDown={stopBubble}
    >
      <select
        value={fontFamily}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontFamily: e.target.value })}
        className="tt-select"
        title="Шрифт"
      >
        {FONTS.map((f) => (
          <option key={f} value={f}>{f}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <select
        value={Math.round(fontSize)}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontSize: +e.target.value })}
        className="tt-select tt-size"
        title="Размер"
      >
        {SIZES.map((s) => (
          <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <div className="tt-group">
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={isBold ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => toggleStyle('bold')}
          title="Жирный"
        ><b>B</b></button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={isItalic ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => toggleStyle('italic')}
          title="Курсив"
        ><i>I</i></button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={isUnderline ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={toggleUnderline}
          title="Подчёркнутый"
        ><u>U</u></button>
      </div>

      <input
        type="color"
        value={fill}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })}
        className="tt-color"
        title="Цвет"
      />

      <div className="tt-group">
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={align === 'left' ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange({ align: 'left' })}
          title="Влево"
        >◧</button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={align === 'center' ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange({ align: 'center' })}
          title="По центру"
        >◫</button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={align === 'right' ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange({ align: 'right' })}
          title="Вправо"
        >◨</button>
      </div>

      <div className="tt-group">
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          onClick={onCommit}
          title="Готово (Ctrl+Enter)"
          className="tt-done"
        >✓</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\Toolbar.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '@/shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { download } from '@/lib/download';
import { serializeSlideToSvg } from '@/lib/svg/serialize';

export const Toolbar: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);
  const data = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.data);
  // Триггер перерисовки при изменении истории:
  useStore(docStore, (s) => s.historyVersion);

  const canUndo = docStore.canUndo;
  const canRedo = docStore.canRedo;

  const activeSlide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);

  const handleExportJson = () => {
    const payload = { version: '2.0', exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), slides, data };
    download(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`, 'application/json');
  };

  const handleExportSvg = () => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    const svgString = serializeSlideToSvg(activeSlide);
    download(svgString, `slide-${activeSlide.id}.svg`, 'image/svg+xml');
  };

  return (
    <header className="toolbar">
      <h1>Редактор шаблонов</h1>
      <div className="toolbar-actions">
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={() => docStore.undo()} disabled={!canUndo}>↶ Отменить</button>
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={() => docStore.redo()} disabled={!canRedo}>↷ Повторить</button>
        <div style={{ width: 16 }} />
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={handleExportSvg}>🖼 Экспорт SVG</button>
        <button className="toolbar-btn primary" onClick={handleExportJson}>📥 Экспорт JSON</button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import type { Slide, AnchorSide, ConnectorElement, CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import {
  findNearestAnchor, computeGhostRect, cloneForConnector,
  oppositeAnchor, type Rect,
} from '@/lib/connectors';
import { generateId } from '@/shared/utils/id';

const ANCHOR_HIT_RADIUS = 30;

interface Draft {
  fromId: string;
  fromSide: AnchorSide;
  toX: number;
  toY: number;
}

export function useCanvasConnectorDraft(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  slide: Slide;
}) {
  const { docStore, slide } = params;
  const [draft, setDraftState] = useState<Draft | null>(null);
  const [ghostRect, setGhostRect] = useState<Rect | null>(null);
  const draftRef = useRef<Draft | null>(null);

  const setDraft = useCallback((d: Draft | null) => {
    draftRef.current = d;
    setDraftState(d);
    if (!d) setGhostRect(null);
  }, []);

  const startDraft = useCallback(
    (fromId: string, fromSide: AnchorSide, wx: number, wy: number) => {
      setDraft({ fromId, fromSide, toX: wx, toY: wy });
      const source = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === fromId);
      if (source) {
        const rect = computeGhostRect(source, fromSide, wx, wy);
        setGhostRect(rect);
      }
    },
    [setDraft, slide],
  );

  const update = useCallback((wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const d = draftRef.current;
    if (!d) return;

    const targetEl = slide.elements.find(
      (el) =>
        el.id !== d.fromId &&
        el.type !== 'connector' &&
        !el.locked &&
        wx >= el.x && wx <= el.x + el.width &&
        wy >= el.y && wy <= el.y + el.height,
    );

    if (!targetEl) {
      const source = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === d.fromId);
      if (source) {
        const rect = computeGhostRect(source, d.fromSide, wx, wy);
        setGhostRect(rect);
      }
    } else {
      setGhostRect(null);
    }

    setDraft({ ...d, toX: wx, toY: wy });
  }, [slide, setDraft]);

  const commit = useCallback((wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const d = draftRef.current;
    if (!d) return;

    const source = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === d.fromId);
    if (!source) {
      setDraft(null);
      return;
    }

    const nearest = slide.elements
      .filter((el) => el.id !== d.fromId && el.type !== 'connector' && !el.locked)
      .map((el) => ({ el, anchor: findNearestAnchor(el, wx, wy) }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.anchor.distance - b.anchor.distance)[0];

    docStore.commit((draftState) => {
      const s = draftState.slides.find((x) => x.id === draftState.activeId);
      if (!s) return;

      let toId = '';
      let toAnchor: AnchorSide = 'left';

      if (nearest && nearest.anchor.distance <= ANCHOR_HIT_RADIUS) {
        toId = nearest.el.id;
        toAnchor = nearest.anchor.side;
      } else if (ghostRect) {
        const newEl = cloneForConnector(ghostRect, source);
        newEl.id = generateId();
        toId = newEl.id;
        toAnchor = oppositeAnchor(d.fromSide);
        s.elements.push(newEl as CanvasElement);
      } else {
        setDraft(null);
        return;
      }

      const conn: ConnectorElement = {
        id: generateId(), type: 'connector',
        x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0, rotation: 0,
        fromId: d.fromId, fromAnchor: d.fromSide,
        toId, toAnchor,
        stroke: '#374151', strokeWidth: 2, dash: 'solid',
        isArrow: true, routing: 'orthogonal',
      };
      s.elements.push(conn);
    }, 'draft-commit');
    setDraft(null);
  }, [docStore, slide, ghostRect, setDraft]);

  const cancel = useCallback(() => setDraft(null), [setDraft]);

  return {
    startDraft, update, commit, cancel,
    current: () => draft,
    isDrafting: () => draftRef.current !== null,
    getGhostRect: () => ghostRect,
  };
}

// src/presentation/hooks/canvas/useCanvasDblClick.ts
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { findTableCellAt } from '@/domain/slide/hitTest';

export interface DblClickViewport {
  viewportLeft: number;
  viewportTop: number;
  clientX: number;
  clientY: number;
}

export function useCanvasDblClick(params: {
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
  zoom: number;
}) {
  const { modalsStore, zoom } = params;

  const handle = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, vp: DblClickViewport) => {
    const screenX = vp.viewportLeft + el.x * zoom;
    const screenY = vp.viewportTop + el.y * zoom;
    const screenW = el.width * zoom;
    const screenH = el.height * zoom;

    /* ---------- text / footer ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'text' || el.type === 'footer') {
      const isRich =
        el.type === 'footer'
          ? true
          : (el.type === 'text' ? el.richText !== false : false);

      modalsStore.openEditor({
        id: el.id,
        field: el.type === 'footer' ? 'footerText' : 'text',
        kind: el.type,
        value: el.text ?? '',
        html: 'html' in el ? el.html : undefined,
        x: screenX, y: screenY,
        width: screenW, height: screenH,
        fontSize: el.fontSize ?? 16,
        fontFamily: el.type === 'text' ? el.fontFamily : 'Arial',
        fontStyle: el.type === 'text' ? el.fontStyle : 'normal',
        textDecoration: el.type === 'text' ? el.textDecoration : '',
        fill: el.fill ?? '#000',
        align: el.type === 'text' ? el.align : 'center',
        verticalAlign: el.type === 'text' ? el.verticalAlign : 'top',
        lineHeight: el.type === 'text' ? el.lineHeight : 1.5,
        padding: el.type === 'text'
          ? el.padding
          : { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        background: el.type === 'text' ? el.background : 'transparent',
        isRich,
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- shape: inline plain-редактор ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'shape') {
      modalsStore.openEditor({
        id: el.id, field: 'shapeText', kind: 'shape',
        value: el.text ?? '',
        x: screenX, y: screenY,
        width: screenW, height: screenH,
        fontSize: el.fontSize ?? 16,
        fontFamily: el.textFontFamily ?? 'Arial',
        fontStyle: el.textFontStyle ?? 'normal',
        textDecoration: el.textDecoration ?? '',
        fill: el.textFill ?? '#000',
        align: el.textAlign ?? 'center',
        verticalAlign: el.textVerticalAlign ?? 'middle',
        lineHeight: el.textLineHeight ?? 1.3,
        padding: el.textPadding ?? { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 },
        background: 'transparent',
        isRich: false,
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- table: inline plain-редактор ячейки ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'table') {
      const worldX = (vp.clientX - vp.viewportLeft) / zoom;
      const worldY = (vp.clientY - vp.viewportTop) / zoom;
      const cell = findTableCellAt(el, worldX, worldY);
      if (!cell) return;
      const cellW = (el.width * zoom) / el.cols;
      const cellH = (el.height * zoom) / el.rows;
      modalsStore.openEditor({
        id: el.id, field: 'tableCell', kind: 'table',
        tableRow: cell.row, tableCol: cell.col,
        value: el.data[cell.row]?.[cell.col] ?? '',
        x: screenX + cell.col * cellW,
        y: screenY + cell.row * cellH,
        width: cellW, height: cellH,
        fontSize: el.fontSize,
        fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
        fill: '#111', align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
        padding: { top: 6, right: 6, bottom: 6, left: 6 },
        background: '#fff', isRich: false,
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- stamp / diagram ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'stamp') {
      modalsStore.openStampEditor(el.id);
      return;
    }
    if (el.type === 'diagram') {
      modalsStore.openDiagramEditor(el.id);
    }
  }, [modalsStore, zoom]);

  return { handle };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasDrag.ts
SIZE: 9751 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { computeSnap } from '../../../services/snap';
import { guidesStore } from '../../stores/GuidesStore';
import { dragLiveStore } from '../../stores/DragLiveStore';
import { SNAP_THRESHOLD } from '@/shared/constants';

interface DragState {
  id: string;
  /** Исходная позиция из state — компенсирует dragDistance (3px) Konva. */
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
  orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }>;
  layer: Konva.Layer | null;
  pendingX: number;
  pendingY: number;
  rafId: number | null;
  lastEvt: MouseEvent | undefined;
  /** Текущие «прилипания» с гистерезисом. */
  snappedX: number | null;
  snappedY: number | null;
  snappedXGuide: number | null;
  snappedYGuide: number | null;
}

const MIN_MOVE_PX = 1;
const HYSTERESIS_MULT = 2;

export function useCanvasDrag(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  selStore: SelectionStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
  slide: Slide;
}) {
  const { docStore, selStore, modalsStore, slide } = params;
  const state = useRef<DragState | null>(null);

  const slideRef = useRef(slide);
  slideRef.current = slide;

  const onDragStart = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, e: any) => {
    modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);

    const current = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
    const ids = current.includes(el.id) ? current : [el.id];
    if (!current.includes(el.id)) selStore.setIds([el.id]);

    const orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
    for (const ee of slideRef.current.elements) {
      if (!ids.includes(ee.id)) continue;
      if (ee.type === 'connector') continue;
      orig[ee.id] = { x: ee.x, y: ee.y };
    }

    // startX берём из state (orig), а не из e.target.x() — Konva на dragstart
    // уже сдвинут на dragDistance (3px), что даёт отставание.
    const origEl = orig[el.id];
    const startX = origEl ? origEl.x : e.target.x();
    const startY = origEl ? origEl.y : e.target.y();
    if (!isFinite(startX) || !isFinite(startY)) return;

    const layer = (e.target.getLayer?.() as Konva.Layer | null) ?? null;

    state.current = {
      id: el.id,
      startX,
      startY,
      orig,
      layer,
      pendingX: startX,
      pendingY: startY,
      rafId: null,
      lastEvt: e.evt,
      snappedX: null,
      snappedY: null,
      snappedXGuide: null,
      snappedYGuide: null,
    };
  }, [selStore, modalsStore]);

  const flush = useCallback(() => {
    const curr = state.current;
    if (!curr) return;
    curr.rafId = null;

    const rawX = curr.pendingX;
    const rawY = curr.pendingY;
    if (!isFinite(rawX) || !isFinite(rawY)) return;

    const isCtrl = !!(curr.lastEvt?.ctrlKey || curr.lastEvt?.metaKey);
    const ids = Object.keys(curr.orig);
    const isMulti = ids.length > 1;

    let finalX = rawX;
    let finalY = rawY;
    const guidesV: number[] = [];
    const guidesH: number[] = [];

    if (!isCtrl) {
      // Для группы — snap по bbox, для одиночного — по элементу
      let snapEl: { id: string; x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number } | null = null;
      if (isMulti) {
        const minX = Math.min(...ids.map(id => curr.orig[id].x));
        const minY = Math.min(...ids.map(id => curr.orig[id].y));
        const maxX = Math.max(...ids.map(id => {
          const el = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === id);
          return curr.orig[id].x + (el?.width || 0);
        }));
        const maxY = Math.max(...ids.map(id => {
          const el = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === id);
          return curr.orig[id].y + (el?.height || 0);
        }));
        snapEl = { id: 'group', x: minX, y: minY, width: maxX - minX, height: maxY - minY };
      } else {
        const activeEl = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === curr.id);
        if (activeEl) snapEl = activeEl;
      }

      if (snapEl) {
        const snap = computeSnap(snapEl, rawX, rawY, slideRef.current, ids);

        // X с гистерезисом: применяем snap если в зоне; если был snapped —
        // держим пока не выйдем за threshold×2 (без гистерезиса — дрожание).
        if (snap.x !== rawX) {
          finalX = snap.x;
          curr.snappedX = snap.x;
          const nearGuides = snap.v;
          curr.snappedXGuide = nearGuides.length ? nearGuides[0] : null;
        } else if (curr.snappedX !== null) {
          if (Math.abs(rawX - curr.snappedX) < SNAP_THRESHOLD * HYSTERESIS_MULT) {
            finalX = curr.snappedX;
          } else {
            curr.snappedX = null;
            curr.snappedXGuide = null;
          }
        }

        // Y аналогично
        if (snap.y !== rawY) {
          finalY = snap.y;
          curr.snappedY = snap.y;
          curr.snappedYGuide = snap.h.length ? snap.h[0] : null;
        } else if (curr.snappedY !== null) {
          if (Math.abs(rawY - curr.snappedY) < SNAP_THRESHOLD * HYSTERESIS_MULT) {
            finalY = curr.snappedY;
          } else {
            curr.snappedY = null;
            curr.snappedYGuide = null;
          }
        }

        // Guides: все кандидаты в зоне threshold («три линии»)
        for (const g of snap.v) {
          if (!guidesV.includes(g)) guidesV.push(g);
        }
        for (const g of snap.h) {
          if (!guidesH.includes(g)) guidesH.push(g);
        }
      }
    } else {
      // Ctrl/Cmd — snap off, гистерезис сбрасываем
      curr.snappedX = null;
      curr.snappedY = null;
      curr.snappedXGuide = null;
      curr.snappedYGuide = null;
    }

    guidesStore.set(guidesV, guidesH);

    const dx = finalX - curr.startX;
    const dy = finalY - curr.startY;

    // Двигаем Konva-ноды напрямую (нет React-rerender, нет дрожания)
    if (curr.layer) {
      for (const [id, o] of Object.entries(curr.orig)) {
        const node = curr.layer.findOne('#' + id);
        if (node) {
          node.x(o.x + dx);
          node.y(o.y + dy);
        }
      }
      curr.layer.batchDraw();
    }

    // Live-позиции для коннекторов и bbox-рамки группы — тоже snapped.
    const live: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
    for (const [id, o] of Object.entries(curr.orig)) {
      live[id] = { x: o.x + dx, y: o.y + dy };
    }
    dragLiveStore.set(live);
  }, []);

  const onDragMove = useCallback((e: any) => {
    const curr = state.current;
    if (!curr) return;
    curr.lastEvt = e.evt;
    curr.pendingX = e.target.x();
    curr.pendingY = e.target.y();
    if (curr.rafId !== null) return;
    curr.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(flush);
  }, [flush]);

  const onDragEnd = useCallback((e: any) => {
    const s = state.current;
    if (!s) return;
    if (s.rafId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(s.rafId);
    state.current = null;
    guidesStore.clear();
    dragLiveStore.clear();

    const rawX = e.target.x();
    const rawY = e.target.y();
    if (!isFinite(rawX) || !isFinite(rawY)) return;

    const isCtrl = !!(e.evt?.ctrlKey || e.evt?.metaKey);
    const ids = Object.keys(s.orig);
    const isMulti = ids.length > 1;

    // На dragEnd — финальный snap (страховка для быстрого drag, когда
    // живой snap не сработал из-за гистерезиса).
    let endX = rawX;
    let endY = rawY;

    if (!isCtrl) {
      let snapEl: { id: string; x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number } | null = null;
      if (isMulti) {
        const minX = Math.min(...ids.map(id => s.orig[id].x));
        const minY = Math.min(...ids.map(id => s.orig[id].y));
        const maxX = Math.max(...ids.map(id => {
          const el = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === id);
          return s.orig[id].x + (el?.width || 0);
        }));
        const maxY = Math.max(...ids.map(id => {
          const el = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === id);
          return s.orig[id].y + (el?.height || 0);
        }));
        snapEl = { id: 'group', x: minX, y: minY, width: maxX - minX, height: maxY - minY };
      } else {
        const activeEl = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === s.id);
        if (activeEl) snapEl = activeEl;
      }

      if (snapEl) {
        const snap = computeSnap(snapEl, rawX, rawY, slideRef.current, ids);
        endX = snap.x;
        endY = snap.y;
      }
    }

    const dx = endX - s.startX;
    const dy = endY - s.startY;

    if (Math.abs(dx) < MIN_MOVE_PX && Math.abs(dy) < MIN_MOVE_PX) return;

    const orig = s.orig;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const sl = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!sl) return;
      for (const ee of sl.elements) {
        if (ee.type === 'connector') continue;
        const o = orig[ee.id];
        if (!o) continue;
        ee.x = o.x + dx;
        ee.y = o.y + dy;
      }
    }, 'move-group');
  }, [docStore]);

  return { onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd };
}

// src/presentation/hooks/canvas/useCanvasDrop.ts — патч (только новая ветка)
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { RefObject } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import {
  makeArrayTable, makePlaceholder,
  newText, newSimpleText, newFooter, newTable, newLine, newShape,
} from '@/lib/factories';
import { resolveSample } from '@/lib/resolveSample';
import { newStampFromTemplate } from '@/lib/stamps/factories';
import { getAllStamps } from '@/lib/stamps/library';

export function useCanvasDrop(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  stageRef: RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
  zoom: number;
}) {
  const { docStore, stageRef, zoom } = params;

  const onDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
  }, []);

  const onDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return;
    const raw = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/json');
    if (!raw) return;
    let payload: any;
    try { payload = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { return; }

    const rect = stage.container().getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) / zoom;
    const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) / zoom;

    /* ---- array ---- */
    if (payload.kind === 'array') {
      const tbl = makeArrayTable(x, y, payload);
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
        if (s) s.elements.push(tbl);
      }, 'drop-array');
      return;
    }

    /* ---- field ---- */
    if (payload.kind === 'field') {
      const data = docStore.getState().data;
      const sample = resolveSample(data, payload.path) ?? payload.sample ?? '';
      const ph = makePlaceholder(x, y, payload.path, payload.fieldType, sample);
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
        if (s) s.elements.push(ph);
      }, 'drop-field');
      return;
    }

    /* ---- stamp-template ---- */
    if (payload.kind === 'stamp-template') {
      const tpl = getAllStamps().find((t) => t.id === payload.templateId);
      if (!tpl) return;
      const stamp = newStampFromTemplate(tpl, x, y);
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
        if (s) s.elements.push(stamp);
      }, 'drop-stamp');
      return;
    }

    /* ---- new-element (Фаза 1) ---- */
    if (payload.kind === 'new-element') {
      const cx = Math.max(0, x - 100);
      const cy = Math.max(0, y - 60);
      let el: any = null;
      switch (payload.elementType) {
        case 'text': el = newText(cx, cy); break;
        case 'simple-text': el = newSimpleText(cx, cy); break;
        case 'footer': {
          const state = docStore.getState();
          const active = state.slides.find((s) => s.id === state.activeId);
          if (!active) return;
          const f = newFooter(active);
          const minY = active.padding.top;
          const maxY = active.height - active.padding.bottom - f.height;
          f.y = Math.min(maxY, Math.max(minY, y));
          el = f;
          break;
        }
        case 'table': el = newTable(cx, cy); break;
        case 'line': el = newLine(cx, cy); break;
        case 'line-arrow': {
          const l = newLine(cx, cy); l.isArrow = true; el = l; break;
        }
        case 'rect': el = newShape('rect', cx, cy); break;
        case 'roundRect': el = newShape('roundRect', cx, cy); break;
        case 'circle': el = newShape('circle', cx, cy); break;
        case 'triangle': el = newShape('triangle', cx, cy); break;
        case 'star': el = newShape('star', cx, cy); break;
        default: return;
      }
      if (el) {
        docStore.commit((draft) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
          if (s) s.elements.push(el);
        }, 'drop-new-element');
      }
    }
  }, [docStore, stageRef, zoom]);

  return { onDragOver, onDrop };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasFillMode.ts
