import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { Slide, FillElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { generateId } from '@/shared/utils/id';
import { hitShapeEl } from '@/lib/geometry';
import { rasterizeFloodFill } from '@/lib/canvas/floodFill';

interface Params {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
  slide: Slide;
  stageRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
}

export function useCanvasFillMode({ docStore, modalsStore, slide, stageRef }: Params) {
  const applyFill = useCallback((wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const state = modalsStore.getState();
    const color = state.fillColor;

    const hit = slide.elements.slice().reverse().find((el) => hitShapeEl(el, wx, wy));

    if (hit && hit.type === 'fill') {
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        const t = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === hit.id);
        if (t && t.type === 'fill') t.fill = color;
      }, `fill-${hit.id}`);
      return;
    }

    if (hit && hit.type === 'shape') {
      const stage = stageRef.current;
      if (stage) {
        // Растеризуем область с учетом всех пересечений
        const result = rasterizeFloodFill(
          stage, 'elements-layer',
          wx, wy,
          slide.width, slide.height,
          color,
        );
        if (result) {
          docStore.commit((draft) => {
            const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
            const t = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === hit.id);
            if (t && t.type === 'shape') {
              // Сохраняем координаты относительно фигуры (0..1)
              t.rasterFill = {
                src: result.dataUrl,
                relX: (result.x - t.x) / t.width,
                relY: (result.y - t.y) / t.height,
                relW: result.width / t.width,
                relH: result.height / t.height,
              };
              t.fill = 'transparent'; // Сбрасываем обычную заливку
            }
          }, `fill-raster-${hit.id}`);
          return;
        }
      }

      // Fallback
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        const t = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === hit.id);
        if (t && t.type === 'shape') {
          t.fill = color;
          t.rasterFill = undefined;
        }
      }, `fill-shape-${hit.id}`);
      return;
    }

    modalsStore.setFillPoints([...state.fillPoints, wx, wy]);
  }, [docStore, modalsStore, slide, stageRef]);

  const closePolygon = useCallback(() => {
    const state = modalsStore.getState();
    const pts = state.fillPoints;
    if (pts.length < 6) return;

    const xs: number[] = [];
    const ys: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) { xs.push(pts[i]); ys.push(pts[i + 1]); }
    const minX = Math.min(...xs);
    const minY = Math.min(...ys);
    const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
    const maxY = Math.max(...ys);

    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      const el: FillElement = {
        id: generateId(), type: 'fill',
        x: minX, y: minY,
        width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
        height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
        rotation: 0,
        points: pts.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY)),
        fill: state.fillColor, stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, opacity: 1,
      };
      s.elements.push(el);
    });
    modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
  }, [docStore, modalsStore]);

  return {
    applyFill, closePolygon,
    points: () => modalsStore.getState().fillPoints,
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/canvas/useCanvasHoverAnchor.ts

   REG-B3: пишет в hoverStore, не в React state.
   Canvas больше не перерендеривается на hover.
   ============================================================ */

import { useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '@/shared/types';
import { findElementWithPadding } from '@/lib/connectors';
import { hoverStore } from '../../stores/HoverStore';

export function useCanvasHoverAnchor(params: { slide: Slide }) {
  const { slide } = params;
  const slideRef = useRef(slide);
  slideRef.current = slide;

  const update = useCallback((_e: any, wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const el = findElementWithPadding(slideRef.current.elements, wx, wy, 20);
    hoverStore.set(el ? el.id : null);
  }, []);

  return { update };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasRubberBand.ts

import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { getBBox } from '@/lib/geometry';

interface Box { x1: number; y1: number; x2: number; y2: number }

export function useCanvasRubberBand(params: { selStore: SelectionStore }) {
  const { selStore } = params;
  const [box, setBox] = useState<Box | null>(null);
  const active = useRef<Box | null>(null);

  const begin = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    active.current = { x1: x, y1: y, x2: x, y2: y };
    setBox({ ...active.current });
  }, []);

  const update = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!active.current) return;
    const b = active.current;
    active.current = {
      x1: b.x1,
      y1: b.y1,
      x2: x,
      y2: y,
    };
    setBox({ ...active.current });
  }, []);

  const finish = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    const b = active.current;
    active.current = null;
    setBox(null);
    if (!b) return;

    const minX = Math.min(b.x1, b.x2);
    const maxX = Math.max(b.x1, b.x2);
    const minY = Math.min(b.y1, b.y2);
    const maxY = Math.max(b.y1, b.y2);

    // Клик без движения — не трогаем selection (clear уже сделан в onStageDown)
    if (maxX - minX < 4 && maxY - minY < 4) return;

    if (!Array.isArray(elements) || !elements.length) return;

    const ids: string[] = [];
    for (const el of elements) {
      if (el.type === 'connector' || el.type === 'fill' || el.locked) continue;

      // Учитываем поворот: используем реальный bbox, а не x/y/width/height.
      const bb = getBBox(el);
      if (bb.x + bb.w < minX) continue;
      if (bb.x > maxX) continue;
      if (bb.y + bb.h < minY) continue;
      if (bb.y > maxY) continue;
      ids.push(el.id);
    }

    selStore.setIds(ids);
  }, [selStore]);

  return {
    begin, update, finish,
    isActive: () => active.current !== null,
    ref: () => box,
  };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasViewport.ts
SIZE: 7726 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';

interface PendingScroll {
  mx: number;
  my: number;
  oldScrollLeft: number;
  oldScrollTop: number;
  oldZoom: number;
}

interface Args {
  /** Scroll-РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂ (overflow: auto) РІРѕРєСЂСѓРі Stage. */
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
  zoom: number;
  onZoomChange: (z: number | ((prev: number) => number)) => void;
  stageRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
  minZoom?: number;
  maxZoom?: number;
}

/** F-CV-07: С€Р°Рі 0.05 (РЅРµ РјСѓР»СЊС‚РёРїР»РёРєР°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ Г—1.1). */
const STEP = 0.05;

export function useCanvasViewport({
  wrapRef,
  zoom,
  onZoomChange,
  stageRef: _stageRef,
  minZoom = 0.2,
  maxZoom = 2,
}: Args) {
  const [isPanning, setIsPanning] = useState(false);
  const panStart = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0, scrollLeft: 0, scrollTop: 0 });

  /** РђРєС‚СѓР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ zoom РєР°Рє ref вЂ” С‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ rAF-flush, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРµС‚СЊ РѕС‚ Р·Р°РјС‹РєР°РЅРёСЏ. */
  const zoomRef = useRef(zoom);
  zoomRef.current = zoom;

  /** Scroll, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ РЅР° СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРј layout-effect РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЃРјРµРЅС‹ zoom. */
  const pendingScrollRef = useRef<PendingScroll | null>(null);
  /** РќР°РєРѕРїР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ deltaY (throttle вЂ” 1 setZoom РЅР° РєР°РґСЂ). */
  const pendingDeltaRef = useRef(0);
  /** РџРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂР° РІ clientX/Y (РїРѕСЂС‚ viewport). */
  const pendingMouseRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const rafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  /* ================================================================
     1. РџСЂРёРјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ scroll вЂ” Р”Рћ paint, СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРЅРѕ СЃ РЅРѕРІС‹Рј scaleX Stage.
        Р­С‚Рѕ СѓСЃС‚СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ РІРёР·СѓР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ В«РїСЂС‹Р¶РѕРєВ» РїСЂРё Р·СѓРјРµ.
     ================================================================ */
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const pending = pendingScrollRef.current;
    if (!pending) return;
    pendingScrollRef.current = null;

    const wrap = wrapRef.current;
    if (!wrap) return;

    /* Force reflow: Сѓ Stage РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ scaleX, РЅРѕ scrollWidth РјРѕРі РµС‰С‘ РЅРµ РїРµСЂРµСЃС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊСЃСЏ. */
    void wrap.scrollWidth;
    void wrap.clientWidth;

    const ratio = zoomRef.current / pending.oldZoom;
    const maxX = Math.max(0, wrap.scrollWidth - wrap.clientWidth);
    const maxY = Math.max(0, wrap.scrollHeight - wrap.clientHeight);
    if (maxX === 0 && maxY === 0) return;

    const contentX = pending.oldScrollLeft + pending.mx;
    const contentY = pending.oldScrollTop + pending.my;

    let newScrollLeft = contentX * ratio - pending.mx;
    let newScrollTop = contentY * ratio - pending.my;

    /* Clamp вЂ” РёРЅР°С‡Рµ scroll В«РІС‹РїСЂС‹РіРёРІР°РµС‚В» Р·Р° max Рё Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµС‚
       СЃРІРѕС‘ РѕРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ, С‡С‚Рѕ РґР°С‘С‚ СЃСѓР±РїРёРєСЃРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РґСЂРµР№С„ РїСЂРё РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРЅС‹С… Р·СѓРјР°С…. */
    newScrollLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(newScrollLeft, maxX));
    newScrollTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(newScrollTop, maxY));

    const fx = Math.round(newScrollLeft);
    const fy = Math.round(newScrollTop);

    if (wrap.scrollLeft !== fx) wrap.scrollLeft = fx;
    if (wrap.scrollTop !== fy) wrap.scrollTop = fy;
  }, [zoom, wrapRef]);

  /* ================================================================
     2. Wheel-handler: РЅР°РєР°РїР»РёРІР°РµРј delta, flush С‡РµСЂРµР· rAF.
        setZoom вЂ” РјР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј 1 СЂР°Р· Р·Р° РєР°РґСЂ (throttle).
     ================================================================ */
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = wrapRef.current;
    if (!el) return;

    const flush = () => {
      rafRef.current = null;
      const delta = pendingDeltaRef.current;
      if (delta === 0) return;
      pendingDeltaRef.current = 0;

      const oldZoom = zoomRef.current;
      const direction = delta < 0 ? 1 : -1;

      /* Р‘С‹СЃС‚СЂР°СЏ РїСЂРѕРєСЂСѓС‚РєР°: 1..4 С€Р°РіР° Р·Р° РєР°РґСЂ. Р‘РµР· clamp РїРѕ steps
         РїРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј В«РїСЂС‹Р¶РєРёВ» РЅР° Р±РѕР»СЊС€РѕРј deltaY. */
      const steps = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, Math.round(Math.abs(delta) / 100)));
      const raw = oldZoom + direction * STEP * steps;
      const next = Math.max(minZoom, Math.min(maxZoom, +raw.toFixed(3)));
      if (next === oldZoom) return;

      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();

      pendingScrollRef.current = {
        mx: pendingMouseRef.current.x - rect.left,
        my: pendingMouseRef.current.y - rect.top,
        oldScrollLeft: el.scrollLeft,
        oldScrollTop: el.scrollTop,
        oldZoom,
      };

      onZoomChange(next);
    };

    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
      if (!e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();

      pendingDeltaRef.current += e.deltaY;
      pendingMouseRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };

      if (rafRef.current === null) {
        rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(flush);
      }
    };

    /* Safari: gesturestart/change Р±РµР· Ctrl. */
    const onGesture = (e: Event) => e.preventDefault();

    el.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false, capture: true });
    el.addEventListener('gesturestart', onGesture as any, { passive: false });
    el.addEventListener('gesturechange', onGesture as any, { passive: false });

    return () => {
      if (rafRef.current !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
      rafRef.current = null;
      el.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { capture: true } as any);
      el.removeEventListener('gesturestart', onGesture as any);
      el.removeEventListener('gesturechange', onGesture as any);
    };
  }, [wrapRef, onZoomChange, minZoom, maxZoom]);

  /* ================================================================
     3. Pan (Space + drag) вЂ” Р±РµР· РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№.
     ================================================================ */
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (e.code === 'Space' && !e.repeat && target.tagName !== 'INPUT' && target.tagName !== 'TEXTAREA') {
        setIsPanning(true);
      }
    };
    const onKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.code === 'Space') setIsPanning(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
    };
  }, []);

  const handleMouseDown = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent | any) => {
    if (!isPanning) return;
    const container = wrapRef.current;
    if (!container) return;

    panStart.current = {
      x: e.clientX,
      y: e.clientY,
      scrollLeft: container.scrollLeft,
      scrollTop: container.scrollTop,
    };

    const handleMouseMove = (moveEvent: MouseEvent) => {
      const dx = moveEvent.clientX - panStart.current.x;
      const dy = moveEvent.clientY - panStart.current.y;
      container.scrollLeft = panStart.current.scrollLeft - dx;
      container.scrollTop = panStart.current.scrollTop - dy;
    };

    const handleMouseUp = () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
    };

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
  }, [isPanning, wrapRef]);

  return { isPanning, handleMouseDown };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\editor\useClipboardActions.ts
SIZE: 2077 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { generateId } from '@/shared/utils/id';

export function useClipboardActions(
  docStore: DocumentStore,
  selStore: SelectionStore,
  modalsStore: ModalsStore,
) {
  const copy = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    const copyable = elements.filter((e) => e.type !== 'connector');
    if (!copyable.length) return;
    modalsStore.copy(copyable);
  }, [modalsStore]);

  const cut = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    const ids = elements.map((e) => e.id);
    if (!ids.length) return;
    copy(elements);
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => {
        if (ids.includes(el.id)) return false;
        if (el.type === 'connector' && (ids.includes(el.fromId) || ids.includes(el.toId))) return false;
        return true;
      });
    }, 'cut');
    selStore.clear();
  }, [docStore, selStore, copy]);

  const paste = useCallback(() => {
    const buf = modalsStore.getClipboard();
    if (!buf.length) return;
    const newIds: string[] = [];
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      for (const el of buf) {
        const clone = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
        clone.id = generateId();
        clone.x += 20;
        clone.y += 20;
        s.elements.push(clone);
        newIds.push(clone.id);
      }
    }, 'paste');
    selStore.setIds(newIds);
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);

  const duplicate = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    copy(elements);
    paste();
  }, [copy, paste]);

  return { copy, cut, paste, duplicate };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import type { DiagramElement } from '@/shared/types';

export function useDiagramCommit(docStore: DocumentStore) {
  return useCallback((elementId: string, patch: Partial<DiagramElement>) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((e) => e.id === elementId);
      if (!el || el.type !== 'diagram') return;
      if (patch.svg !== undefined) el.svg = patch.svg;
      if (patch.xml !== undefined) el.xml = patch.xml;
    }, `diagram-${elementId}`);
  }, [docStore]);
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import type { EditingState } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';

export function useEditingCommit(docStore: DocumentStore) {
  return useCallback((editing: EditingState, html: string | undefined, text: string) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const slide = draft.slides.find((s) => s.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = slide?.elements.find((e) => e.id === editing.id);
      if (!el) return;

      const safeText = text ?? '';

      switch (editing.field) {
        case 'text': {
          if (el.type !== 'text') return;
          // Проверяем, есть ли в HTML что-то, кроме пустых параграфов (например, таблица)
          const hasRichContent = html && (html.includes('<table') || html.includes('<img'));
          const isEmpty = !safeText.trim() && !hasRichContent;
          
          el.text = isEmpty ? 'Введите текст' : safeText;
          el.html = isEmpty ? undefined : html;
          return;
        }
        case 'footerText':
          if (el.type !== 'footer') return;
          el.text = safeText;
          if (html !== undefined) el.html = html || undefined;
          return;
        case 'shapeText':
          if (el.type !== 'shape') return;
          el.text = safeText;
          return;
        case 'tableCell': {
          if (el.type !== 'table') return;
          const r = editing.tableRow ?? 0;
          const c = editing.tableCol ?? 0;
          const rows = el.data.map((row) => [...row]);
          if (rows[r] && c < rows[r].length) rows[r][c] = safeText;
          el.data = rows;
          return;
        }
        default:
          return;
      }
    }, `commit-${editing.id}`);
  }, [docStore]);
}

// src/presentation/hooks/editor/useFileIO.ts
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore, useModalsStore,
} from '../../providers/EditorProvider';
import { migrateDocument } from '@/lib/migrations';
import {
  exportSvgServer as doExportSvgServer,
  exportPdfaServer as doExportPdfaServer,
} from '@/lib/serverExport';
import { CONFIG } from '@/shared/config';

/* ---------- helpers ---------- */

function readFileAsText(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result as string);
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
    reader.readAsText(file);
  });
}

function readFileAsDataUrl(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result as string);
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

function triggerDownload(content: Blob | string, filename: string, mime: string): void {
  const blob = typeof content === 'string'
    ? new Blob([content], { type: mime })
    : content;
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

/* ---------- hook ---------- */

export function useFileIO() {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();

  const getActiveSlide = useCallback(() => {
    const state = docStore.getState();
    return state.slides.find((s) => s.id === state.activeId) ?? null;
  }, [docStore]);

  const clearUiState = useCallback(() => {
    selStore.clear();
    modalsStore.closeEditor();
    modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);
    modalsStore.setContextMenu(null);
    modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
  }, [selStore, modalsStore]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-01: Export JSON
     ============================================================ */

  const exportJson = useCallback(() => {
    const state = docStore.getState();
    const payload = {
      version: '2.0',
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      slides: state.slides,
      data: state.data,
    };
    triggerDownload(
      JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2),
      `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`,
      'application/json',
    );
    // F-AS-06: очистка autosave при экспорте
    try { localStorage.removeItem('ticket-editor:autosave'); } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }, [docStore]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-02..09: Import JSON с миграциями
     ============================================================ */

  const importJson = useCallback(() => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = '.json,application/json';
    input.onchange = async () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;

      // F-IO-06 / REG-P5: лимит размера
      if (file.size > CONFIG.MAX_IMPORT_SIZE) {
        const mb = (CONFIG.MAX_IMPORT_SIZE / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(0);
        alert(`Файл слишком большой (макс. ${mb} MB)`);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const text = await readFileAsText(file);
        const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
        const migrated = migrateDocument(parsed);

        // activeSlideId мог быть невалидным (JSON от другой сессии)
        const validActiveId =
          migrated.slides.find((s) => s.id === migrated.activeSlideId)?.id
          ?? migrated.slides[0].id;

        docStore.commit((draft) => {
          draft.slides = migrated.slides;
          draft.activeId = validActiveId;
          draft.data = migrated.data;
        }, 'import-json');

        clearUiState();
      } catch (err) {
        alert(`Ошибка импорта: ${(err as Error).message}`);
      }
    };
    input.click();
  }, [docStore, clearUiState]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-10: SVG локально (без embed)
     ============================================================ */

  const exportSvgLocal = useCallback(async () => {
    const active = getActiveSlide();
    if (!active) return;

    const { serializeSlideToSvg } = await import('@/lib/svg/serialize');
    const svg = serializeSlideToSvg(active);
    const safeName = (active.name || 'slide').replace(/[^\w.-]+/g, '_');
    triggerDownload(svg, `${safeName}.svg`, 'image/svg+xml');
  }, [getActiveSlide]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-11: SVG через сервер
     ============================================================ */

  const exportSvgServer = useCallback(async () => {
    const active = getActiveSlide();
    if (!active) return;
    await doExportSvgServer(active);
  }, [getActiveSlide]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-12: PDF/A через сервер
     ============================================================ */

  const exportPdfaServer = useCallback(async () => {
    const active = getActiveSlide();
    if (!active) return;
    await doExportPdfaServer(active);
  }, [getActiveSlide]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IMG-05: Добавить картинку
     ============================================================ */

  const addImage = useCallback((opts: { centered?: boolean } = {}) => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = async () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;

      try {
        const src = await readFileAsDataUrl(file);
        const img = await new Promise<HTMLImageElement>((resolve, reject) => {
          const i = new Image();
          i.onload = () => resolve(i);
          i.onerror = reject;
          i.src = src;
        });

        const active = getActiveSlide();
        if (!active) return;

        const maxRatio = opts.centered ? 0.7 : 1;
        const k = Math.min(
          1,
          (active.width * maxRatio) / img.width,
          (active.height * maxRatio) / img.height,
        );
        const w = Math.max(20, Math.round(img.width * k));
        const h = Math.max(20, Math.round(img.height * k));
        const x = opts.centered ? Math.round((active.width - w) / 2) : 100;
        const y = opts.centered ? Math.round((active.height - h) / 2) : 100;

        docStore.commit((draft) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((sl) => sl.id === draft.activeId);
          if (s) {
            s.elements.push({
              id: Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10),
              type: 'image',
              x, y, width: w, height: h, rotation: 0,
              src,
            });
          }
        }, 'add-image');
      } catch (err) {
        alert(`Не удалось загрузить картинку: ${(err as Error).message}`);
      }
    };
    input.click();
  }, [docStore, getActiveSlide]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-LP-07: фон слайда
     ============================================================ */

  const setBackgroundImage = useCallback(() => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = async () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      try {
        const src = await readFileAsDataUrl(file);
        docStore.commit((draft) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((sl) => sl.id === draft.activeId);
          if (s) s.backgroundImage = src;
        }, 'set-bg');
      } catch (err) {
        alert(`Не удалось установить фон: ${(err as Error).message}`);
      }
    };
    input.click();
  }, [docStore]);

  const removeBackgroundImage = useCallback(() => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((sl) => sl.id === draft.activeId);
      if (s) s.backgroundImage = undefined;
    }, 'remove-bg');
  }, [docStore]);

  return {
    exportJson,
    importJson,
    exportSvgLocal,
    exportSvgServer,
    exportPdfaServer,
    addImage,
    setBackgroundImage,
    removeBackgroundImage,
  };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import type { StampElement } from '@/shared/types';

/**
 * Применяет patch к StampElement в активном слайде.
 * Поддерживает name / width / height / shapes / texts / images.
 */
export function useStampCommit(docStore: DocumentStore) {
  return useCallback((elementId: string, patch: Partial<StampElement>) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((e) => e.id === elementId);
      if (!el || el.type !== 'stamp') return;
      if (patch.name !== undefined) el.name = patch.name;
      if (patch.width !== undefined) el.width = patch.width;
      if (patch.height !== undefined) el.height = patch.height;
      if (patch.shapes !== undefined) el.shapes = patch.shapes.map((x) => ({ ...x }));
      if (patch.texts !== undefined) el.texts = patch.texts.map((x) => ({ ...x }));
      if (patch.images !== undefined) el.images = patch.images.map((x) => ({ ...x }));
    }, `stamp-${elementId}`);
  }, [docStore]);
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';

export function useStyleClipboard(
  docStore: DocumentStore,
  selStore: SelectionStore,
  modalsStore: ModalsStore,
) {
  const copyStyle = useCallback((el: CanvasElement) => {
    modalsStore.copyStyle(el);
  }, [modalsStore]);

  const applyStyle = useCallback(() => {
    const style = modalsStore.getStyleClipboard();
    const id = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
    if (!style || !id) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (el) Object.assign(el, style);
    }, `style-${id}`);
    modalsStore.clearStyle();
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);

  return { copyStyle, applyStyle };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/shortcuts/keyMatcher.ts

   CQ-SIZE-04: РґРµРєР»Р°СЂР°С‚РёРІРЅР°СЏ СЃРїРµС†РёС„РёРєР°С†РёСЏ РєР»Р°РІРёС€ РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ
   РІР»РѕР¶РµРЅРЅС‹С… if/else. РљР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ handler РѕРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚РѕРј KeySpec.
   ============================================================ */

export interface KeySpec {
  key?: string;
  code?: string;
  /** true вЂ” С‚СЂРµР±СѓРµС‚ Ctrl/Cmd; false вЂ” РЅРµ С‚СЂРµР±СѓРµС‚; undefined вЂ” РЅРµ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµС‚. */
  ctrl?: boolean;
  shift?: boolean;
  alt?: boolean;
}

export function matches(e: KeyboardEvent, spec: KeySpec): boolean {
  if (spec.key !== undefined && e.key !== spec.key) return false;
  if (spec.code !== undefined && e.code !== spec.code) return false;

  const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
  if (spec.ctrl !== undefined && spec.ctrl !== ctrl) return false;
  if (spec.shift !== undefined && spec.shift !== e.shiftKey) return false;
  if (spec.alt !== undefined && spec.alt !== e.altKey) return false;

  return true;
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export interface CanvasWrapSize { w: number; h: number; }

export function useStampCanvasWrap(
  ref: React.RefObject<HTMLElement>,
  fallback: CanvasWrapSize = { w: 800, h: 600 },
): CanvasWrapSize {
  const [size, setSize] = useState<CanvasWrapSize>(fallback);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const update = () => {
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      setSize({ w: r.width, h: r.height });
    };
    update();
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(update);
    ro.observe(el);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, [ref]);

  return size;
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\stamps\useStampCRUD.ts
SIZE: 2486 bytes

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { uid } from '@/lib/uid';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedType: SelectionType;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: SelectionType) => void;
  clearSelection: () => void;
}

const PASTE_OFFSET = 12;

export function useStampCRUD({
  shapes, texts, images, selectedId, selectedType, commit, select, clearSelection,
}: Args) {
  const deleteSelected = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selectedId || !selectedType) return;
    if (selectedType === 'shape') {
      commit(shapes.filter((s) => s.id !== selectedId), texts, images);
    } else if (selectedType === 'text') {
      commit(shapes, texts.filter((t) => t.id !== selectedId), images);
    } else {
      commit(shapes, texts, images.filter((im) => im.id !== selectedId));
    }
    clearSelection();
  }, [selectedId, selectedType, shapes, texts, images, commit, clearSelection]);

  const duplicateSelected = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selectedId || !selectedType) return;
    const off = PASTE_OFFSET;

    if (selectedType === 'shape') {
      const s = shapes.find((x) => x.id === selectedId);
      if (!s) return;
      const c: StampShapeItem = { ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s)), id: uid(), x: s.x + off, y: s.y + off };
      commit([...shapes, c], texts, images);
      select(c.id, 'shape');
    } else if (selectedType === 'text') {
      const t = texts.find((x) => x.id === selectedId);
      if (!t) return;
      const c: StampTextItem = { ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t)), id: uid() };
      if (c.mode === 'straight') { c.x += off; c.y += off; }
      else { c.arcCx += off; c.arcCy += off; }
      commit(shapes, [...texts, c], images);
      select(c.id, 'text');
    } else {
      const im = images.find((x) => x.id === selectedId);
      if (!im) return;
      const c: StampImageItem = { ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(im)), id: uid(), x: im.x + off, y: im.y + off };
      commit(shapes, texts, [...images, c]);
      select(c.id, 'image');
    }
  }, [selectedId, selectedType, shapes, texts, images, commit, select]);

  return { deleteSelected, duplicateSelected };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\stamps\useStampDragHandlers.ts
SIZE: 3327 bytes

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { snapPosition, snapToHostCenter, findContainingShape } from '@/lib/stamps/geometry';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  width: number;
  height: number;
  snapEnabled: boolean;
  updateShape: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => void;
  updateText: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
  updateImage: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => void;
}

function isCtrlPressed(e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<any>): boolean {
  const evt = e.evt as MouseEvent | undefined;
  return !!(evt && (evt.ctrlKey || evt.metaKey));
}

export function useStampDragHandlers({ shapes, width, height, snapEnabled, updateShape, updateText, updateImage }: Args) {
  const handleShapeDragEnd = useCallback((id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => {
    const s = shapes.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (!s || s.kind === 'fill') return;
    const node = e.target;
    const rawX = node.x() - s.width / 2;
    const rawY = node.y() - s.height / 2;
    if (isCtrlPressed(e)) {
      node.x(Math.round(rawX) + s.width / 2);
      node.y(Math.round(rawY) + s.height / 2);
      updateShape(id, { x: Math.round(rawX), y: Math.round(rawY) });
      return;
    }
    const host = findContainingShape(rawX + s.width / 2, rawY + s.height / 2, id, shapes);
    const cs = snapToHostCenter(rawX, rawY, s.width, s.height, host);
    let nx = cs.x, ny = cs.y;
    if (!cs.snappedX || !cs.snappedY) {
      const general = snapPosition(nx, ny, s.width, s.height, id, shapes, width, height, snapEnabled);
      if (!cs.snappedX) nx = general.x;
      if (!cs.snappedY) ny = general.y;
    }
    node.x(nx + s.width / 2);
    node.y(ny + s.height / 2);
    updateShape(id, { x: Math.round(nx), y: Math.round(ny) });
  }, [shapes, snapEnabled, width, height, updateShape]);

  const handleTextDragEnd = useCallback((id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, text: StampTextItem) => {
    const node = e.target;
    if (isCtrlPressed(e)) {
      node.x(Math.round(node.x())); node.y(Math.round(node.y()));
      updateText(id, { x: Math.round(node.x()), y: Math.round(node.y()) });
      return;
    }
    const snapped = snapPosition(node.x(), node.y(), text.width, text.fontSize * 1.4, id, shapes, width, height, snapEnabled);
    node.x(snapped.x); node.y(snapped.y);
    updateText(id, { x: snapped.x, y: snapped.y });
  }, [shapes, snapEnabled, width, height, updateText]);

  const handleImageDragEnd = useCallback((id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, image: StampImageItem) => {
    const node = e.target;
    if (isCtrlPressed(e)) {
      node.x(Math.round(node.x())); node.y(Math.round(node.y()));
      updateImage(id, { x: Math.round(node.x()), y: Math.round(node.y()) });
      return;
    }
    const snapped = snapPosition(node.x(), node.y(), image.width, image.height, id, shapes, width, height, snapEnabled);
    node.x(snapped.x); node.y(snapped.y);
    updateImage(id, { x: snapped.x, y: snapped.y });
  }, [shapes, snapEnabled, width, height, updateImage]);

  return { handleShapeDragEnd, handleTextDragEnd, handleImageDragEnd };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/stamps/useStampFillMode.ts
   F-STMP-E-10, F-SGEO-11. NFR-OBS-03.
   ============================================================ */

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import type {
  StampShapeItem,
  StampTextItem,
  StampImageItem,
} from '@/shared/types';
import { findShapesContaining } from '@/lib/stamps/geometry';
import { uid } from '@/lib/uid';
import { logger } from '@/shared/logger';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  width: number;
  height: number;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: 'shape' | 'text' | 'image') => void;
}

export function useStampFillMode({
  shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select,
}: Args) {
  const [fillMode, setFillMode] = useState(false);
  const [fillColor, setFillColor] = useState('#fbbf24');

  const handleCanvasClick = useCallback((px: number, py: number) => {
    if (!fillMode) return;

    const containing = findShapesContaining(px, py, shapes);
    if (containing.length === 0) {
      logger.debug('stamp.fill.no_target', { px, py });
      return;
    }

    const target = containing.reduce((a, b) =>
      a.width * a.height <= b.width * b.height ? a : b,
    );

    const localX = (px - target.x) / Math.max(1, target.width);
    const localY = (py - target.y) / Math.max(1, target.height);

    const fill: StampShapeItem = {
      id: uid(),
      kind: 'fill',
      x: 0, y: 0,
      width, height,
      rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
      stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
      fill: fillColor,
      fillTargetShapeId: target.id,
      fillAnchorLocal: { x: localX, y: localY },
      name: 'Заливка области',
    };

    logger.info('stamp.fill.created', {
      shapeId: target.id,
      kind: target.kind,
      color: fillColor,
    });

    commit([...shapes, fill], texts, images);
    select(fill.id, 'shape');
    setFillMode(false);
  }, [fillMode, fillColor, shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select]);

  return { fillMode, setFillMode, fillColor, setFillColor, handleCanvasClick };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/stamps/useStampHistory.ts
   REG-B1, REG-B4. Один setState на push (устранён двойной re-render).
   ============================================================ */

import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type {
  StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem,
} from '@/shared/types';

export interface StampSnapshot {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
}

const MAX_HISTORY = 60;
const MERGE_TIMEOUT_MS = 600;

interface HistoryEntry {
  snapshot: StampSnapshot;
  mergeKey?: string;
  timestamp: number;
}

interface HistoryState {
  snapshot: StampSnapshot;
  canUndo: boolean;
  canRedo: boolean;
}

export function useStampHistory(initial: StampSnapshot) {
  const historyRef = useRef<HistoryEntry[]>([
    { snapshot: initial, timestamp: Date.now() },
  ]);
  const indexRef = useRef(0);

  const [state, setState] = useState<HistoryState>({
    snapshot: initial,
    canUndo: false,
    canRedo: false,
  });

  const commitState = useCallback((snapshot: StampSnapshot) => {
    setState({
      snapshot,
      canUndo: indexRef.current > 0,
      canRedo: indexRef.current < historyRef.current.length - 1,
    });
  }, []);

  const push = useCallback((next: StampSnapshot, mergeKey?: string) => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const idx = indexRef.current;
    const last = historyRef.current[idx];

    // REG-B1: тот же mergeKey в пределах окна — перезаписываем верхний entry
    if (mergeKey && last?.mergeKey === mergeKey && now - last.timestamp < MERGE_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      historyRef.current[idx] = { snapshot: next, mergeKey, timestamp: now };
      commitState(next);
      return;
    }

    const trimmed = historyRef.current.slice(0, idx + 1);
    trimmed.push({ snapshot: next, mergeKey, timestamp: now });
    while (trimmed.length > MAX_HISTORY) trimmed.shift();
    historyRef.current = trimmed;
    indexRef.current = trimmed.length - 1;
    commitState(next);
  }, [commitState]);

  const replace = useCallback((next: StampSnapshot) => {
    // Замена без записи в историю (inline-редактирование).
    setState((s) => ({ ...s, snapshot: next }));
  }, []);

  const undo = useCallback(() => {
    if (indexRef.current <= 0) return;
    indexRef.current -= 1;
    commitState(historyRef.current[indexRef.current].snapshot);
  }, [commitState]);

  const redo = useCallback(() => {
    if (indexRef.current >= historyRef.current.length - 1) return;
    indexRef.current += 1;
    commitState(historyRef.current[indexRef.current].snapshot);
  }, [commitState]);

  return {
    state: state.snapshot,
    push,
    replace,
    undo,
    redo,
    canUndo: state.canUndo,
    canRedo: state.canRedo,
  };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\stamps\useStampImageCache.ts

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';

export function useStampImageCache(images: StampImageItem[]): Record<string, HTMLImageElement> {
  const [cache, setCache] = useState<Record<string, HTMLImageElement>>({});
  const cacheRef = useRef(cache);
  cacheRef.current = cache;

  const key = images.map((i) => i.src).join('|');

  useEffect(() => {
    let changed = false;
    const next = { ...cacheRef.current };
    
    for (const im of images) {
      if (im.hidden || next[im.src]) continue;
      const img = new window.Image();
      img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
      img.src = im.src;
      img.onload = () => {
        setCache((prev) => ({ ...prev, [im.src]: img }));
      };
      changed = true;
    }
    
    if (changed) setCache(next);
  }, [key, images]);

  return cache;
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { loadImage, fitImageSize } from '@/lib/imageTools';

interface Args {
  updateImage: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => void;
  stampW: number; stampH: number;
}

export function useStampImageReplace({ updateImage, stampW, stampH }: Args) {
  return useCallback((id: string) => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file'; input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0]; if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = async () => {
        const src = reader.result as string;
        try {
          const img = await loadImage(src);
          const { width: iw, height: ih } = fitImageSize(img.naturalWidth || 100, img.naturalHeight || 100, stampW, stampH, 0.5);
          updateImage(id, { src, width: iw, height: ih });
        } catch { updateImage(id, { src }); }
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };
    input.click();
  }, [updateImage, stampW, stampH]);
}

// src/presentation/hooks/stamps/useStampImageUpload.ts
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type {
  StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem,
} from '@/shared/types';
import { createImage } from '@/lib/stamps/factories';
import { loadImage, makeImageTransparent, fitImageSize } from '@/lib/imageTools';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  width: number;
  height: number;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: 'shape' | 'text' | 'image') => void;
}

export function useStampImageUpload({
  shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select,
}: Args) {
  return useCallback((transparent: boolean) => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = async () => {
        let src = reader.result as string;
        if (transparent) {
          try {
            src = await makeImageTransparent(src, { keyColor: 'auto', tolerance: 30 });
          } catch (err) {
            // chroma-key опционален: при ошибке используем исходное изображение
            console.warn('[useStampImageUpload] chroma-key failed:', err);
          }
        }
        let nw = 100;
        let nh = 100;
        try {
          const img = await loadImage(src);
          nw = img.naturalWidth || 100;
          nh = img.naturalHeight || 100;
        } catch (err) {
          // метаданные картинки недоступны — берём дефолтный размер
          console.warn('[useStampImageUpload] loadImage failed:', err);
        }
        const { width: iw, height: ih } = fitImageSize(nw, nh, width, height, 0.5);
        const im = createImage(src, iw, ih, width, height);
        commit(shapes, texts, [...images, im]);
        select(im.id, 'image');
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };
    input.click();
  }, [shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select]);
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';

type LayerKind = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  clearSelection: () => void;
}

export function useStampLayerActions({ shapes, texts, images, selectedId, commit, clearSelection }: Args) {
  const toggleHidden = useCallback((id: string, type: LayerKind) => {
    if (type === 'shape') commit(shapes.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, hidden: !s.hidden } : s)), texts, images);
    else if (type === 'text') commit(shapes, texts.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, hidden: !t.hidden } : t)), images);
    else commit(shapes, texts, images.map((im) => (im.id === id ? { ...im, hidden: !im.hidden } : im)));
  }, [shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  const toggleLocked = useCallback((id: string, type: LayerKind) => {
    if (type === 'shape') commit(shapes.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, locked: !s.locked } : s)), texts, images);
    else if (type === 'text') commit(shapes, texts.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, locked: !t.locked } : t)), images);
    else commit(shapes, texts, images.map((im) => (im.id === id ? { ...im, locked: !im.locked } : im)));
  }, [shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  const removeLayer = useCallback((id: string, type: LayerKind) => {
    if (type === 'shape') commit(shapes.filter((s) => s.id !== id), texts, images);
    else if (type === 'text') commit(shapes, texts.filter((t) => t.id !== id), images);
    else commit(shapes, texts, images.filter((im) => im.id !== id));
    if (selectedId === id) clearSelection();
  }, [shapes, texts, images, selectedId, commit, clearSelection]);

  const reorder = useCallback((id: string, type: LayerKind, dir: 1 | -1) => {
    const swap = <T extends { id: string }>(arr: T[]): T[] => {
      const i = arr.findIndex((x) => x.id === id);
      const j = i + dir;
      if (i < 0 || j < 0 || j >= arr.length) return arr;
      const copy = [...arr]; [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]]; return copy;
    };
    if (type === 'shape') commit(swap(shapes), texts, images);
    else if (type === 'text') commit(shapes, swap(texts), images);
    else commit(shapes, texts, swap(images));
  }, [shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  return { toggleHidden, toggleLocked, removeLayer, reorder };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/stamps/useStampShortcuts.ts
   F-STMP-E-17. CQ-SIZE-04: С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р° handlers.
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { matches, type KeySpec } from '../shortcuts';

export interface StampShortcutsConfig {
  editingTextId: string | null;
  selectedId: string | null;
  onCloseTextEditor: () => void;
  onClearSelection: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  onUndo: () => void;
  onRedo: () => void;
  onCloseEditor: () => void;
}

interface Handler {
  spec: KeySpec;
  handle(e: KeyboardEvent): void;
}

export function useStampShortcuts(config: StampShortcutsConfig) {
  const {
    editingTextId, selectedId, onCloseTextEditor, onClearSelection,
    onDelete, onDuplicate, onUndo, onRedo, onCloseEditor,
  } = config;

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      const isField =
        !!t &&
        (t.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
          t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
          t.tagName === 'SELECT' ||
          t.isContentEditable);

      if (isField) return;

      const handlers: Handler[] = [
        {
          spec: { key: 'Escape' },
          handle: () => {
            if (editingTextId) { onCloseTextEditor(); return; }
            if (selectedId) { onClearSelection(); return; }
            onCloseEditor();
          },
        },
        {
          spec: { key: 'Delete' },
          handle: (ev) => {
            if (!selectedId) return;
            ev.preventDefault();
            onDelete();
          },
        },
        {
          spec: { key: 'Backspace' },
          handle: (ev) => {
            if (!selectedId) return;
            ev.preventDefault();
            onDelete();
          },
        },
        {
          spec: { code: 'KeyD', ctrl: true },
          handle: (ev) => {
            if (!selectedId) return;
            ev.preventDefault();
            onDuplicate();
          },
        },
        {
          spec: { code: 'KeyZ', ctrl: true, shift: false },
          handle: (ev) => { ev.preventDefault(); onUndo(); },
        },
        {
          spec: { code: 'KeyY', ctrl: true },
          handle: (ev) => { ev.preventDefault(); onRedo(); },
        },
        {
          spec: { code: 'KeyZ', ctrl: true, shift: true },
          handle: (ev) => { ev.preventDefault(); onRedo(); },
        },
      ];

      for (const h of handlers) {
        if (matches(e, h.spec)) {
          h.handle(e);
          return;
        }
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [
    editingTextId, selectedId,
    onCloseTextEditor, onClearSelection, onDelete, onDuplicate,
    onUndo, onRedo, onCloseEditor,
  ]);
}

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';

interface Snapshot { shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[]; }

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  replace: (next: Snapshot) => void;
}

export function useStampTextEditor({ shapes, texts, images, commit, replace }: Args) {
  const [editingTextId, setEditingTextId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const openTextEditor = useCallback((id: string) => {
    if (editingTextId === id) return;
    commit(shapes, texts, images);
    setEditingTextId(id);
  }, [editingTextId, shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  const changeEditingText = useCallback((value: string) => {
    if (!editingTextId) return;
    replace({ shapes, texts: texts.map((t) => (t.id === editingTextId ? { ...t, text: value } : t)), images });
  }, [editingTextId, shapes, texts, images, replace]);

  const closeTextEditor = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editingTextId) return;
    commit(shapes, texts, images);
    setEditingTextId(null);
  }, [editingTextId, shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  return { editingTextId, openTextEditor, changeEditingText, closeTextEditor };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/stamps/useStampTextUpdate.ts
   REG-B1, REG-S1, F-SGEO-02, F-STMP-N-05.
   ============================================================ */

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type {
  StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem,
} from '@/shared/types';
import {
  MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG, DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
  type ArcDirection,
} from '@/lib/stamps/geometry';
import { autoKey } from '@/lib/stamps/history/autoKey';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  commit: (
    s: StampShapeItem[],
    t: StampTextItem[],
    i: StampImageItem[],
    mergeKey?: string,
  ) => void;
}

const clampSpan = (v: number): number =>
  Math.min(MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, Math.max(MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, v));

function resolveHost(
  t: StampTextItem,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
): StampShapeItem | null {
  if (t.arcHostId) {
    const found = shapes.find((s) => s.id === t.arcHostId);
    if (found) return found;
  }
  return shapes.find((s) => {
    if (s.hidden || s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') return false;
    return (
      Math.abs(s.x + s.width / 2 - t.arcCx) < 1 &&
      Math.abs(s.y + s.height / 2 - t.arcCy) < 1
    );
  }) || null;
}

/**
 * Пересчитывает arc-параметры изменившегося поля.
 * Возвращает patch для merge с исходным patch.
 */
function recalcArc(
  t: StampTextItem,
  merged: StampTextItem,
  host: StampShapeItem,
  patch: Partial<StampTextItem>,
): Partial<StampTextItem> {
  const hostId = host.id;
  const direction = (merged.arcDirection ?? 'cw') as ArcDirection;
  const span = clampSpan(
    merged.arcSpanDeg ??
      Math.abs(merged.arcEndDeg - merged.arcStartDeg) ??
      DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
  );

  // Переключение направления
  if ('arcDirection' in patch) {
    const endDeg =
      direction === 'cw'
        ? merged.arcStartDeg + span
        : merged.arcStartDeg - span;
    return {
      arcDirection: direction,
      arcStartDeg: merged.arcStartDeg,
      arcEndDeg: endDeg,
      arcSpanDeg: span,
      arcFlip: direction === 'ccw',
      arcHostId: hostId,
    };
  }

  // Изменение span
  if ('arcSpanDeg' in patch) {
    const endDeg =
      direction === 'cw'
        ? merged.arcStartDeg + span
        : merged.arcStartDeg - span;
    return {
      arcSpanDeg: span,
      arcStartDeg: merged.arcStartDeg,
      arcEndDeg: endDeg,
      arcFlip: direction === 'ccw',
      arcHostId: hostId,
    };
  }

  // Изменение start
  if ('arcStartDeg' in patch) {
    const endDeg =
      direction === 'cw'
        ? merged.arcStartDeg + span
        : merged.arcStartDeg - span;
    return {
      arcStartDeg: merged.arcStartDeg,
      arcEndDeg: endDeg,
      arcSpanDeg: span,
      arcFlip: direction === 'ccw',
      arcHostId: hostId,
    };
  }

  // Изменение end — пересчитываем span и direction
  if ('arcEndDeg' in patch) {
    const rawSpan = merged.arcEndDeg - merged.arcStartDeg;
    const newDirection: ArcDirection = rawSpan >= 0 ? 'cw' : 'ccw';
    return {
      arcDirection: newDirection,
      arcEndDeg: merged.arcEndDeg,
      arcSpanDeg: clampSpan(Math.abs(rawSpan) || DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG),
      arcFlip: newDirection === 'ccw',
      arcHostId: hostId,
    };
  }

  // Изменение offset / fontSize / arcOutside
  if ('arcOffset' in patch || 'fontSize' in patch || 'arcOutside' in patch) {
    const hostBaseR = Math.min(host.width, host.height) / 2;
    const arcOffset = Math.max(0, merged.arcOffset ?? DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET);
    const isOutside = !!merged.arcOutside;
    const isPerimeterHost = host.kind === 'rect' || host.kind === 'triangle';
    const newRadius = isPerimeterHost
      ? hostBaseR
      : isOutside
        ? hostBaseR + arcOffset
        : Math.max(merged.fontSize * 0.8, hostBaseR - arcOffset);
    return {
      arcRadius: newRadius,
      arcHostId: hostId,
    };
  }

  return {};
}

export function useStampTextUpdate({ shapes, texts, images, commit }: Args) {
  return useCallback((id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => {
    const key = autoKey(id, patch);
    const t = texts.find((x) => x.id === id);

    if (!t) {
      commit(
        shapes,
        texts.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch } : x)),
        images,
        key,
      );
      return;
    }

    const merged: StampTextItem = { ...t, ...patch };

    if (merged.mode === 'arc') {
      const host = resolveHost(t, shapes);
      if (host) {
        const extras = recalcArc(t, merged, host, patch);
        commit(
          shapes,
          texts.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch, ...extras } : x)),
          images,
          key,
        );
        return;
      }
    }

    commit(
      shapes,
      texts.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch } : x)),
      images,
      key,
    );
  }, [shapes, texts, images, commit]);
}

import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampShapeKind, StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { createShape, createStraightText, createArcText, type FactoryContext } from '@/lib/stamps/factories';
import type { PlacementMode } from '@/lib/stamps/geometry';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[];
  width: number; height: number; placementMode: PlacementMode;
  selectedId: string | null; selectedType: SelectionType;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: SelectionType) => void;
}

export function useStampToolbarActions({
  shapes, texts, images, width, height, placementMode, selectedId, selectedType, commit, select,
}: Args) {
  const ctx: FactoryContext = useMemo(() => ({
    shapes, selectedId, selectedType, stampW: width, stampH: height, placementMode,
  }), [shapes, selectedId, selectedType, width, height, placementMode]);

  const addShape = useCallback((kind: StampShapeKind) => {
    const s = createShape(kind, ctx);
    commit([...shapes, s], texts, images);
    select(s.id, 'shape');
  }, [ctx, shapes, texts, images, commit, select]);

  const addStraightText = useCallback(() => {
    const t = createStraightText(ctx);
    commit(shapes, [...texts, t], images);
    select(t.id, 'text');
  }, [ctx, shapes, texts, images, commit, select]);

  const addArcText = useCallback(() => {
    const t = createArcText(ctx);
    commit(shapes, [...texts, t], images);
    select(t.id, 'text');
  }, [ctx, shapes, texts, images, commit, select]);

  return { addShape, addStraightText, addArcText };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampImageItem } from '@/shared/types';
import { getShapeStrategy } from '@/lib/stamps/shapes';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[]; images: StampImageItem[];
  selectedType: SelectionType;
  updateShape: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => void;
  updateImage: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => void;
}

export function useStampTransform({ shapes, images, selectedType, updateShape, updateImage }: Args) {
  return useCallback((e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<Event>) => {
    const node = e.target; const id = node.id();
    const evt = e.evt as MouseEvent | undefined;
    const ctrl = !!(evt && (evt.ctrlKey || evt.metaKey));

    if (selectedType === 'shape') {
      const s = shapes.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (!s || s.kind === 'fill') return;
      const strategy = getShapeStrategy(s.kind);
      const result = strategy.applyTransform({
        shape: s, nodeX: node.x(), nodeY: node.y(), nodeRotation: node.rotation(),
        scaleX: node.scaleX(), scaleY: node.scaleY(), ctrl, shapes,
      });
      if (result.resetScale) { node.scaleX(1); node.scaleY(1); }
      updateShape(id, result.patch);
      return;
    }

    if (selectedType === 'image') {
      const im = images.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (!im) return;
      const nw = Math.max(8, im.width * node.scaleX());
      const nh = Math.max(8, im.height * node.scaleY());
      node.scaleX(1); node.scaleY(1);
      updateImage(id, { x: Math.round(node.x()), y: Math.round(node.y()), width: Math.round(nw), height: Math.round(nh) });
    }
  }, [shapes, images, selectedType, updateShape, updateImage]);
}

  clearSelection: () => void;
}

export function useStampUndoRedo({ history, clearSelection }: Args) {

    history.undo();
    clearSelection();

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';

const MIN_ZOOM = 0.4;
const MAX_ZOOM = 6;

export function useStampZoomPan(stageRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Stage>) {
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  const onWheel = useCallback((e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<WheelEvent>) => {
    e.evt.preventDefault();
    const dir = e.evt.deltaY < 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return;
    const oldZoom = zoom;
    const next = Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, +(oldZoom + dir * 0.08).toFixed(3)));
    if (next === oldZoom) return;
    const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
    if (!pointer) { setZoom(next); return; }
    const mx = (pointer.x - pan.x) / oldZoom;
    const my = (pointer.y - pan.y) / oldZoom;
    setPan({ x: pointer.x - mx * next, y: pointer.y - my * next });
    setZoom(next);
  }, [stageRef, zoom, pan]);

  const zoomIn = useCallback(() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, +(z + 0.1).toFixed(2))), []);
  const zoomOut = useCallback(() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, +(z - 0.1).toFixed(2))), []);
  
  const zoomFit = useCallback((wrapW: number, wrapH: number, stampW: number, stampH: number) => {
    const fitX = (wrapW - 60) / stampW;
    const fitY = (wrapH - 60) / stampH;
    setZoom(Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(fitX, fitY))));
    setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  }, []);

  return { zoom, pan, setZoom, setPan, onWheel, zoomIn, zoomOut, zoomFit, MIN_ZOOM, MAX_ZOOM };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useAutoFitZoom.ts
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect } from 'react';

interface Args {
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  slideWidth: number;
  slideHeight: number;
  setZoom: (z: number) => void;
  padding?: number;
  topReserve?: number;
  minZoom?: number;
  maxZoom?: number;
}

export function useAutoFitZoom({
  wrapRef, slideWidth, slideHeight, setZoom,
  padding = 40, topReserve = 80, minZoom = 0.2, maxZoom = 2,
}: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const wrap = wrapRef.current;
    if (!wrap || !slideWidth || !slideHeight) return;

    const fit = () => {
      const rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
      // РћС‚РЅРёРјР°РµРј РїР°РґРґРёРЅРіРё Рё СЂРµР·РµСЂРІ РїРѕРґ ZoomBar
      const availableW = Math.max(100, rect.width - padding * 2);
      const availableH = Math.max(100, rect.height - padding * 2 - topReserve);

      const zoomX = availableW / slideWidth;
      const zoomY = availableH / slideHeight;
      const next = Math.max(minZoom, Math.min(maxZoom, Math.min(zoomX, zoomY)));

      setZoom(+next.toFixed(3));
    };

    fit();

    let raf = 0;
    const onResize = () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(fit);
    };

    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
    };
  }, [wrapRef, slideWidth, slideHeight, setZoom, padding, topReserve, minZoom, maxZoom]);
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useAutoSave.ts
   F-AS-02: debounce 2000ms.

import { useEffect, useRef, type MutableRefObject } from 'react';
import type { Slide, DataRoot } from '@/shared/types';
import { logger } from '@/shared/logger';

const AUTO_SAVE_KEY = 'ticket-editor:autosave';
const AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS = 2000;
const MAX_AGE_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export interface AutoSaveData {
  slides: Slide[];
  data: DataRoot[];
  activeSlideId: string | null;
  savedAt: string;
}

interface SerializeRequest { id: number; payload: unknown; }
interface SerializeResponse { id: number; ok: boolean; text?: string; error?: string; }

/**
 * В части браузеров (Safari < 18) `requestIdleCallback` отсутствует.
 * Типизируем через пересечение — не через extends Window, чтобы
 * не конфликтовать с lib.dom.d.ts.
 */
type IdleCapableWindow = Window & {
  requestIdleCallback?: (callback: () => void) => number;
};

export function useAutoSave(
  slides: Slide[],
  data: DataRoot[],
  activeSlideId: string | null,
): void {
  const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const workerRef = useRef<Worker | null>(null);
  const reqIdRef = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const worker = new Worker(
        new URL('../../workers/serializeWorker.ts', import.meta.url),
        { type: 'module' },
      );
      workerRef.current = worker;
      return () => {
        worker.terminate();
        workerRef.current = null;
      };
    } catch (err) {
      logger.warn('autosave.worker_unavailable', { error: String(err) });
      return;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (timerRef.current) window.clearTimeout(timerRef.current);

    timerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      const saveData: AutoSaveData = {
        slides,
        data,
        activeSlideId,
        savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      void writeAutoSave(saveData, workerRef, reqIdRef);
    }, AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS);

    return () => {
      if (timerRef.current) window.clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    };
  }, [slides, data, activeSlideId]);
}

async function writeAutoSave(
  data: AutoSaveData,
  workerRef: MutableRefObject<Worker | null>,
  reqIdRef: MutableRefObject<number>,
): Promise<void> {
  const worker = workerRef.current;

  if (worker) {
    try {
      const id = ++reqIdRef.current;
      const text = await serializeViaWorker(worker, id, data);
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY, text);
      } catch (err) {
        logger.warn('autosave.write_failed', { error: String(err) });
      }
      return;
    } catch (err) {
      logger.warn('autosave.worker_failed', { error: String(err) });
    }
  }

  const doSave = (): void => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
    } catch (err) {
      logger.warn('autosave.write_failed', { error: String(err) });
    }
  };

  const win = window as IdleCapableWindow;
  if (typeof win.requestIdleCallback === 'function') {
    win.requestIdleCallback(doSave);
  } else {
    window.setTimeout(doSave, 0);
  }
}

function serializeViaWorker(
  worker: Worker,
  id: number,
  payload: unknown,
): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const handler = (e: MessageEvent<SerializeResponse>): void => {
      if (e.data.id !== id) return;
      worker.removeEventListener('message', handler);
      if (e.data.ok && typeof e.data.text === 'string') {
        resolve(e.data.text);
      } else {
        reject(new Error(e.data.error || 'serialize failed'));
      }
    };
    worker.addEventListener('message', handler);
    const request: SerializeRequest = { id, payload };
    worker.postMessage(request);
  });
}

export function loadAutoSave(): AutoSaveData | null {
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY);
    if (!saved) return null;
    const data: AutoSaveData = JSON.parse(saved);
    if (Date.now() - new Date(data.savedAt).getTime() > MAX_AGE_MS) {
      clearAutoSave();
      return null;
    }
    return data;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function clearAutoSave(): void {
  try { localStorage.removeItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY); } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useEditorShortcuts.ts

   F-H-06, F-CB-*, F-CM-*, §38.
   CQ-SIZE-04: cyclomatic complexity снижена с 48 до ~3
   через декларативную таблицу handlers.
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import type {
  DocumentStore,
  DocumentState,
} from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { generateId } from '@/shared/utils/id';
import { matches, type KeySpec } from './shortcuts';

interface Args {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  selStore: SelectionStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
}

interface Handler {
  spec: KeySpec;
  handle(e: KeyboardEvent): void;
}

const isEditable = (el: EventTarget | null): boolean => {
  const h = el as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!h) return false;
  return (
    h.isContentEditable ||
    h.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
    h.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
    h.tagName === 'SELECT'
  );
};

function cloneWithOffset(el: CanvasElement, offset = 20): CanvasElement {
  const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
  cloned.id = generateId();
  (cloned as { x?: number }).x = ((cloned as { x?: number }).x ?? 0) + offset;
  (cloned as { y?: number }).y = ((cloned as { y?: number }).y ?? 0) + offset;
  return cloned;
}

export function useEditorShortcuts({ docStore, selStore, modalsStore }: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const closeFillOrSelection = (): void => {
      const ms = modalsStore.getState();
      if (ms.editing) {
        modalsStore.closeEditor();
        return;
      }
      if (ms.fillPoints.length) {
        modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
        return;
      }
      selStore.clear();
    };

    const closeFillPolygon = (): void => {
      const ms = modalsStore.getState();
      const pts = ms.fillPoints.slice();
      if (pts.length < 6) return;

      docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        if (!s) return;
        const xs: number[] = [];
        const ys: number[] = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) {
          xs.push(pts[i]);
          ys.push(pts[i + 1]);
        }
        const minX = Math.min(...xs);
        const minY = Math.min(...ys);
        const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
        const maxY = Math.max(...ys);
        const fillEl: CanvasElement = {
          id: generateId(),
          type: 'fill',
          x: minX,
          y: minY,
          width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
          height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
          rotation: 0,
          points: pts.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY)),
          fill: ms.fillColor,
          stroke: '',
          strokeWidth: 0,
          opacity: 1,
        } as CanvasElement;
        s.elements.push(fillEl);
      });
      modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
    };

    const deleteSelected = (): void => {
      const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
      if (!ids.length) return;
      docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        if (!s) return;
        s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => !ids.includes(el.id));
      });
      selStore.clear();
    };

    const copySelection = (): void => {
      const state = docStore.getState();
      const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
      if (!slide) return;
      const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
      const els = slide.elements.filter(
        (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
      );
      if (els.length) modalsStore.copy(els);
    };

    const cutSelection = (): void => {
      const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
      if (!ids.length) return;
      copySelection();
      docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        if (!s) return;
        s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => !ids.includes(el.id));
      });
      selStore.clear();
    };

    const pasteFromClipboard = (): void => {
      const buf = modalsStore.getClipboard();
      if (!buf.length) return;
      const newIds: string[] = [];
      docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        if (!s) return;
        for (const el of buf) {
          const cloned = cloneWithOffset(el);
          s.elements.push(cloned);
          newIds.push(cloned.id);
        }
      });
      selStore.setIds(newIds);
    };

    const duplicateSelection = (): void => {
      const state = docStore.getState();
      const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
      if (!slide) return;
      const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
      const els = slide.elements.filter(
        (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
      );
      if (!els.length) return;
      const newIds: string[] = [];
      docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        if (!s) return;
        for (const el of els) {
          const cloned = cloneWithOffset(el);
          s.elements.push(cloned);
          newIds.push(cloned.id);
        }
      });
      selStore.setIds(newIds);
    };

    const copyStyleOfSelection = (): void => {
      const state = docStore.getState();
      const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
      if (!slide) return;
      const selId = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
      const el = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === selId);
      if (el) modalsStore.copyStyle(el);
    };

    const applyStyleToSelection = (): void => {
      const style = modalsStore.getStyleClipboard();
      const selId = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
      if (!style || !selId) return;
      docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === selId);
        if (el) Object.assign(el, style);
      });
      modalsStore.clearStyle();
    };

    const handlers: Handler[] = [
      { spec: { key: 'Escape' }, handle: () => closeFillOrSelection() },
      {
        spec: { key: 'Enter' },
        handle: (e) => {
          const ms = modalsStore.getState();
          if (ms.editing) return;
          if (ms.fillPoints.length >= 6) {
            e.preventDefault();
            closeFillPolygon();
          }
        },
      },
      {
        spec: { key: 'Delete' },
        handle: (e) => {
          const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
          if (!ids.length) return;
          e.preventDefault();
          deleteSelected();
        },
      },
      {
        spec: { key: 'Backspace' },
        handle: (e) => {
          const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
          if (!ids.length) return;
          e.preventDefault();
          deleteSelected();
        },
      },
      {
        spec: { code: 'KeyZ', ctrl: true, shift: false },
        handle: (e) => { e.preventDefault(); docStore.undo(); },
      },
      {
        spec: { code: 'KeyY', ctrl: true },
        handle: (e) => { e.preventDefault(); docStore.redo(); },
      },
      {
        spec: { code: 'KeyZ', ctrl: true, shift: true },
        handle: (e) => { e.preventDefault(); docStore.redo(); },
      },
      { spec: { code: 'KeyC', ctrl: true, alt: false }, handle: (e) => { e.preventDefault(); copySelection(); } },
      { spec: { code: 'KeyX', ctrl: true, alt: false }, handle: (e) => { e.preventDefault(); cutSelection(); } },
      { spec: { code: 'KeyV', ctrl: true, alt: false }, handle: (e) => { e.preventDefault(); pasteFromClipboard(); } },
      { spec: { code: 'KeyD', ctrl: true }, handle: (e) => { e.preventDefault(); duplicateSelection(); } },
      { spec: { code: 'KeyC', ctrl: true, alt: true }, handle: (e) => { e.preventDefault(); copyStyleOfSelection(); } },
      { spec: { code: 'KeyV', ctrl: true, alt: true }, handle: (e) => { e.preventDefault(); applyStyleToSelection(); } },
    ];

    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      const ms = modalsStore.getState();
      if (ms.stampEditorTarget || ms.diagramEditorTarget) return;
      if (isEditable(e.target) && !ms.editing) return;

      for (const h of handlers) {
        if (matches(e, h.spec)) {
          h.handle(e);
          return;
        }
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useFillRasterizer.ts
   F-RFILL-04, F-RFILL-12..13.

   Добавлен debounce 80ms (F-RFILL-12), чтобы при быстром drag
   в штампе не отправлять в worker кадр за кадром.
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';
import type { FillSpec } from '@/lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import {
  getFillRasterizer,
  type FillCacheEntry,
} from '@/lib/stamps/fillRasterizer';

const DEBOUNCE_MS = 80;

export interface UseFillRasterizerArgs {
  key: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
  enabled: boolean;
}

export function useFillRasterizer(args: UseFillRasterizerArgs): FillCacheEntry | null {
  const { key, width, height, shapes, fills, enabled } = args;

  const [entry, setEntry] = useState<FillCacheEntry | null>(
    () => getFillRasterizer().get(key) ?? null,
  );

  // Подписка на обновления кэша по key.
  useEffect(() => {
    const rasterizer = getFillRasterizer();
    const cached = rasterizer.get(key);
    if (cached) setEntry(cached);
    return rasterizer.subscribe((changedKey, changedEntry) => {
      if (changedKey === key) setEntry(changedEntry);
    });
  }, [key]);

  // Запрос растеризации с debounce 80ms (F-RFILL-12).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const rasterizer = getFillRasterizer();
    if (rasterizer.get(key)) return;
    if (rasterizer.isInflight(key)) return;

    const timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
      rasterizer.request({ key, width, height, shapes, fills });
    }, DEBOUNCE_MS);

    return () => window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }, [key, width, height, shapes, fills, enabled]);

  return entry;
}
