/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useFocusTrap.ts
   NFR-A11Y-03: focus trap + Esc РґР»СЏ РјРѕРґР°Р»РѕРє.
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect, type RefObject } from 'react';

const FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR =
  'a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), ' +
  'select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), ' +
  '[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';

export function useFocusTrap(
  ref: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>,
  active: boolean,
  onEscape?: () => void,
): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return;
    const root = ref.current;
    if (!root) return;

    const getFocusable = (): HTMLElement[] =>
      Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR)).filter(
        (el) => el.offsetParent !== null,
      );

    const previousActive = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    const first = getFocusable()[0];
    first?.focus();

    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && onEscape) {
        e.preventDefault();
        onEscape();
        return;
      }
      if (e.key !== 'Tab') return;

      const focusable = getFocusable();
      if (focusable.length === 0) {
        e.preventDefault();
        return;
      }

      const firstEl = focusable[0];
      const lastEl = focusable[focusable.length - 1];

      if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === firstEl) {
        e.preventDefault();
        lastEl.focus();
      } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === lastEl) {
        e.preventDefault();
        firstEl.focus();
      }
    };

    root.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => {
      root.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      previousFocusRestore(previousActive);
    };
  }, [ref, active, onEscape]);
}

function previousFocusRestore(el: HTMLElement | null): void {
  if (el && typeof el.focus === 'function') {
    try { el.focus(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }
}

import { useStore } from '@/shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';

export function useSelectedElement() {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();

  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const selectedIds = useStore(selStore, (s) => s.selectedIds);

  if (selectedIds.length !== 1) return null;
  const slide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);
  return slide?.elements.find((e) => e.id === selectedIds[0]) ?? null;
}

import React, { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
import { DocumentStore } from '@/infra/state/DocumentStore';
import { SelectionStore } from '../stores/SelectionStore';
import { ModalsStore } from '../stores/ModalsStore';

export interface EditorStores {
  document: DocumentStore;
  selection: SelectionStore;
  modals: ModalsStore;
}

const EditorContext = createContext<EditorStores | null>(null);

export const EditorProvider: React.FC<{ stores: EditorStores; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ stores, children }) => (
  <EditorContext.Provider value={stores}>{children}</EditorContext.Provider>
);

export function useStores(): EditorStores {
  const ctx = useContext(EditorContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useStores must be used within EditorProvider');
  return ctx;
}

export const useDocumentStore = () => useStores().document;
export const useSelectionStore = () => useStores().selection;

export class DragLiveStore {
  private positions: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();
  private version = 0;

  subscribe = (listener: () => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
  };

  getSnapshot = () => this.version;

  get(id: string): { x: number; y: number } | undefined {
    return this.positions[id];
  }

  set(positions: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }>) {
    this.positions = positions;
    this.version++;
    this.listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  }

  clear() {
    if (Object.keys(this.positions).length === 0) return;
    this.positions = {};
    this.version++;
    this.listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  }
}

export const dragLiveStore = new DragLiveStore();

/* ============================================================
   GuidesStore.ts
   РњРёРєСЂРѕ-СЃС‚РѕСЂ РґР»СЏ Р»РёРЅРёР№ РїСЂРёР»РёРїР°РЅРёСЏ.
   РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕСЂР° РќР• РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚ СЂРµСЂРµРЅРґРµСЂ Canvas,
   Р° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚ GuidesLayer.
   ============================================================ */

export class GuidesStore {
  private state = { v: [] as number[], h: [] as number[] };
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  subscribe = (listener: () => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
  };

  getSnapshot = () => this.state;

  set = (v: number[], h: number[]) => {
    // РџСЂРµРґРѕС‚РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј Р»РёС€РЅРёРµ РІС‹Р·РѕРІС‹, РµСЃР»Рё Р»РёРЅРёРё РЅРµ РёР·РјРµРЅРёР»РёСЃСЊ
    if (this.state.v.length === v.length && this.state.h.length === h.length) {
      let same = true;
      for (let i = 0; i < v.length; i++) if (this.state.v[i] !== v[i]) { same = false; break; }
      if (same) for (let i = 0; i < h.length; i++) if (this.state.h[i] !== h[i]) { same = false; break; }
      if (same) return;
    }
    this.state = { v, h };
    this.listeners.forEach(l => l());
  };

  clear = () => this.set([], []);
}

export const guidesStore = new GuidesStore();

/* ============================================================
   presentation/stores/HoverStore.ts

   REG-B3, NFR-PERF-01: hover РЅРµ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ С‚СЂРёРіРіРµСЂРёС‚СЊ СЂРµСЂРµРЅРґРµСЂ Canvas.

export class HoverStore {
  private hoveredId: string | null = null;
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
  };

  getSnapshot = (): string | null => this.hoveredId;

  set(id: string | null): void {
    if (this.hoveredId === id) return;
    this.hoveredId = id;
    this.listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  }

  clear(): void { this.set(null); }
}

export const hoverStore = new HoverStore();

// src/presentation/stores/ModalsStore.ts
import { SimpleStore } from '@/shared/store/store';
import type {
  CanvasElement,
  TextAlign,
  TextVerticalAlign,
  Padding,
} from '@/shared/types';

export type EditingKind =
  | 'text'
  | 'shape'
  | 'footer'
  | 'stamp'
  | 'table'
  | 'placeholder';

export type EditingField =
  | 'text'
  | 'footerText'
  | 'shapeText'
  | 'tableCell'
  | 'topText'
  | 'centerText'
  | 'bottomText';

export interface EditingState {
  id: string;
  field: EditingField;
  kind: EditingKind;
  zone?: 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom';
  tableRow?: number;
  tableCol?: number;
  value: string;
  html?: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  fill: string;
  align: TextAlign;
  verticalAlign: TextVerticalAlign;
  lineHeight: number;
  padding: Padding;
  background: string;
  isRich: boolean;
}

export interface ContextMenuState {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  elementId: string | null;
}

export interface ModalsState {
  editing: EditingState | null;
  floatingPos: { x: number; y: number } | null;
  contextMenu: ContextMenuState | null;
  stampEditorTarget: string | null;
  diagramEditorTarget: string | null;
  fillMode: boolean;
  fillColor: string;
  fillPoints: number[];
  showGrid: boolean;
  showMargins: boolean;
}

const INITIAL: ModalsState = {
  editing: null,
  floatingPos: null,
  contextMenu: null,
  stampEditorTarget: null,
  diagramEditorTarget: null,
  fillMode: false,
  fillColor: '#fbbf24',
  fillPoints: [],
  showGrid: true,
  showMargins: true,
};

/**
 * UI-состояние модалок и transient-режимов.
 * ARCH-STATE-02/06: единый store + точечные селекторы.
 */
export class ModalsStore extends SimpleStore<ModalsState> {
  private clipboard: CanvasElement[] = [];
  private styleClipboard: Partial<CanvasElement> | null = null;

  constructor() {
    super(INITIAL);
  }

  /* ---------- editing ---------- */

  openEditor(state: EditingState) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      editing: state,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
    }));
  }

  setEditing(state: EditingState | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, editing: state }));
  }

  closeEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, editing: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- floating ---------- */

  setFloatingPos(p: { x: number; y: number } | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, floatingPos: p }));
  }

  /* ---------- context menu ---------- */

  setContextMenu(m: ContextMenuState | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, contextMenu: m }));
  }

  /* ---------- stamp editor ---------- */

  openStampEditor(id: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      stampEditorTarget: id,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
      editing: null,
    }));
  }

  closeStampEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, stampEditorTarget: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- diagram editor ---------- */

  openDiagramEditor(id: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      diagramEditorTarget: id,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
      editing: null,
    }));
  }

  closeDiagramEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, diagramEditorTarget: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- fill mode ---------- */

  setFillMode(v: boolean) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      fillMode: v,
      fillPoints: v ? prev.fillPoints : [],
    }));
  }

  setFillColor(c: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, fillColor: c }));
  }

  setFillPoints(pts: number[]) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, fillPoints: pts }));
  }

  /* ---------- view ---------- */

  setShowGrid(v: boolean) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, showGrid: v }));
  }

  setShowMargins(v: boolean) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, showMargins: v }));
  }

  /* ---------- clipboard ---------- */

  copy(elements: CanvasElement[]) {
    this.clipboard = elements.map(
      (el) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement,
    );
  }

  getClipboard(): CanvasElement[] {
    return this.clipboard;
  }

  copyStyle(element: CanvasElement) {
    const { id: _id, x: _x, y: _y, rotation: _r, type: _t, ...rest } = element as Partial<CanvasElement> & {
      id?: string; x?: number; y?: number; rotation?: number; type?: string;
    };
    this.styleClipboard = rest as Partial<CanvasElement>;
  }

  getStyleClipboard(): Partial<CanvasElement> | null {
    return this.styleClipboard;
  }

  clearStyle() {
    this.styleClipboard = null;
  }
}

import { SimpleStore } from '@/shared/store/store';

export interface SelectionState {
  selectedIds: string[];
}

export class SelectionStore extends SimpleStore<SelectionState> {
  constructor() { super({ selectedIds: [] }); }

  select(id: string, multi = false) {
    this.setState((s) => ({
      selectedIds: multi
        ? (s.selectedIds.includes(id)
            ? s.selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== id)
            : [...s.selectedIds, id])
        : [id],
    }));
  }

  toggle(id: string) {
    this.setState((s) => ({
      selectedIds: s.selectedIds.includes(id)
        ? s.selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== id)
        : [...s.selectedIds, id],
    }));
  }

  setIds(ids: string[]) { this.setState({ selectedIds: ids }); }
  clear() { this.setState({ selectedIds: [] }); }
}

FILE: src\services\snap.ts
SIZE: 3497 bytes

import type { Slide, CanvasElement } from '@/shared/types';
import { GRID_SIZE, SNAP_THRESHOLD } from '@/shared/constants';

export interface SnapResult {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  v: number[];
  h: number[];
}

const gridRound = (v: number): number => Math.round(v / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;

export function computeSnap(
  el: CanvasElement | { id: string; x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number },
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide,
  excludeIds: string[] = []
): SnapResult {
  const v: number[] = [];
  const h: number[] = [];
  const bw = el.width;
  const bh = el.height;
  const xs: { v: number; d: number; guide: number }[] = [];
  const ys: { v: number; d: number; guide: number }[] = [];

  // 1. Сетка
  const gx = gridRound(x);
  const gy = gridRound(y);
  xs.push({ v: gx, d: Math.abs(x - gx), guide: gx });
  ys.push({ v: gy, d: Math.abs(y - gy), guide: gy });

  // 2. Слайд: края + центр
  const cx = x + bw / 2;
  const cy = y + bh / 2;
  const stageW = slide.width;
  const stageH = slide.height;

  xs.push({ v: stageW / 2 - bw / 2, d: Math.abs(cx - stageW / 2), guide: stageW / 2 });
  xs.push({ v: 0, d: Math.abs(x), guide: 0 });
  xs.push({ v: stageW - bw, d: Math.abs(x + bw - stageW), guide: stageW });

  // 3. Padding
  const pad = slide.padding;
  xs.push({ v: pad.left, d: Math.abs(x - pad.left), guide: pad.left });
  xs.push({ v: stageW - pad.right - bw, d: Math.abs(x + bw - (stageW - pad.right)), guide: stageW - pad.right });

  ys.push({ v: pad.top, d: Math.abs(y - pad.top), guide: pad.top });
  ys.push({ v: stageH - pad.bottom - bh, d: Math.abs(y + bh - (stageH - pad.bottom)), guide: stageH - pad.bottom });
  ys.push({ v: stageH / 2 - bh / 2, d: Math.abs(cy - stageH / 2), guide: stageH / 2 });
  ys.push({ v: 0, d: Math.abs(y), guide: 0 });
  ys.push({ v: stageH - bh, d: Math.abs(y + bh - stageH), guide: stageH });

  // 4. Другие элементы: L / R / C — три линии на фигуру
  for (const other of slide.elements) {
    if (excludeIds.includes(other.id) || (el as any).id === other.id) continue;
    if (other.locked) continue;

    const oL = other.x;
    const oR = other.x + other.width;
    const oC = other.x + other.width / 2;
    const oT = other.y;
    const oB = other.y + other.height;
    const oM = other.y + other.height / 2;
    const l = x;
    const r = x + bw;
    const c = x + bw / 2;
    const t = y;
    const b = y + bh;
    const m = y + bh / 2;

    xs.push({ v: oL, d: Math.abs(l - oL), guide: oL });
    xs.push({ v: oR - bw, d: Math.abs(r - oR), guide: oR });
    xs.push({ v: oC - bw / 2, d: Math.abs(c - oC), guide: oC });
    ys.push({ v: oT, d: Math.abs(t - oT), guide: oT });
    ys.push({ v: oB - bh, d: Math.abs(b - oB), guide: oB });
    ys.push({ v: oM - bh / 2, d: Math.abs(m - oM), guide: oM });
  }

  // Snap применяем к ЛУЧШЕМУ кандидату
  const bestX = xs.reduce((a, b) => (b.d < a.d ? b : a), xs[0]);
  const bestY = ys.reduce((a, b) => (b.d < a.d ? b : a), ys[0]);

  if (bestX && bestX.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD) x = bestX.v;
  if (bestY && bestY.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD) y = bestY.v;

  // Guides: ВСЕ кандидаты в зоне threshold (это и есть «три линии»)
  for (const xc of xs) {
    if (xc.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD && !v.includes(xc.guide)) v.push(xc.guide);
  }
  for (const yc of ys) {
    if (yc.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD && !h.includes(yc.guide)) h.push(yc.guide);
  }

  return { x, y, v, h };
}

/* ============================================================
   shared/logger.ts
   NFR-OBS-03: structured logs {level, event, payload, ts}.
   NFR-OBS-02: РІ prod РѕС€РёР±РєРё РёРґСѓС‚ РІ Sentry (РµСЃР»Рё РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂ Р·Р°РґР°РЅ).
   ============================================================ */

import { getSentry } from './sentry';

export type LogLevel = 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error';

export interface LogEntry {
  level: LogLevel;
  event: string;
  payload?: Record<string, unknown>;
  ts: string;
}

const IS_DEV = import.meta.env.DEV;

function serializeError(error: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    return { name: error.name, message: error.message, stack: error.stack };
  }
  return { message: String(error) };
}

function write(level: LogLevel, event: string, payload?: Record<string, unknown>): void {
  const entry: LogEntry = {
    level,
    event,
    payload,
    ts: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  if (!IS_DEV && (level === 'debug' || level === 'info')) return;

  const prefix = `[${entry.ts}] [${level}] ${event}`;

  /* eslint-disable no-console */
  switch (level) {
    case 'error':
      console.error(prefix, entry);
      if (!IS_DEV) getSentry()?.captureMessage(event, { ...payload, level });
      break;
    case 'warn':
      console.warn(prefix, entry);
      break;
    case 'info':
      console.info(prefix, entry);
      break;
    case 'debug':
      console.debug(prefix, entry);
      break;
  }
  /* eslint-enable no-console */
}

export const logger = {
  debug(event: string, payload?: Record<string, unknown>): void {
    write('debug', event, payload);
  },

  info(event: string, payload?: Record<string, unknown>): void {
    write('info', event, payload);
  },

  warn(event: string, payload?: Record<string, unknown>): void {
    write('warn', event, payload);
  },

  error(event: string, error: unknown, payload?: Record<string, unknown>): void {
    const errorPayload = { ...payload, error: serializeError(error) };
    write('error', event, errorPayload);
    if (!IS_DEV) getSentry()?.captureException(error, { event, ...errorPayload });
  },
};

/* === Limits === */
export const SCHEMA_LIMITS = {
  MAX_IMPORT_SIZE: 20 * 1024 * 1024,
  MAX_SLIDES: 500,
  MAX_ELEMENTS_PER_SLIDE: 2000,
  MAX_ARRAY_SAMPLE_COUNT: 100,
} as const;

/* === Common === */
const PaddingSchema = z.object({
  top: z.number(),
  right: z.number(),
  bottom: z.number(),
  left: z.number(),
});

const DocFormatSchema = z.enum(['a4', 'a5', 'a6', 'letter', 'ticket', 'card', 'custom']);
const OrientationSchema = z.enum(['portrait', 'landscape']);
const TextAlignSchema = z.enum(['left', 'center', 'right', 'justify']);
const TextVerticalAlignSchema = z.enum(['top', 'middle', 'bottom']);
const DashTypeSchema = z.enum(['solid', 'dashed', 'dotted', 'dashdot', 'longdash']);
const DataFieldTypeSchema = z.enum(['string', 'number', 'date', 'boolean', 'image', 'object', 'array']);
const AnchorSideSchema = z.enum(['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left']);
const ConnectorRoutingSchema = z.enum(['straight', 'orthogonal']);

/* === Data === */
export const DataFieldSchema: z.ZodType<unknown> = z.lazy(() =>
  z.object({
    id: z.string(),
    name: z.string(),
    type: DataFieldTypeSchema,
    sample: z.string().default(''),
    children: z.array(DataFieldSchema).optional(),
    sampleCount: z.number().int().positive().max(SCHEMA_LIMITS.MAX_ARRAY_SAMPLE_COUNT).optional(),
  }).passthrough(),
);

export const DataRootSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  fields: z.array(DataFieldSchema).default([]),
}).passthrough();

/* === Base element === */
const BaseElementFields = {
  id: z.string(),
  x: z.number(),
  y: z.number(),
  width: z.number().nonnegative(),
  height: z.number().nonnegative(),
  rotation: z.number().default(0),
  locked: z.boolean().optional(),
};

/* === Elements === */
export const TextElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('text'),
  text: z.string(),
  html: z.string().optional(),
  richText: z.boolean().optional(),
  fontSize: z.number().positive(),
  fontFamily: z.string(),
  fill: z.string(),
  fontStyle: z.string().default('normal'),
  textDecoration: z.string().default(''),
  align: TextAlignSchema.default('left'),
  verticalAlign: TextVerticalAlignSchema.default('top'),
  lineHeight: z.number().positive().default(1.5),
  padding: PaddingSchema.default({ top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 }),
  background: z.string().default('transparent'),
}).passthrough();

export const ImageElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('image'),
  src: z.string(),
}).passthrough();

export const ShapeElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('shape'),
  shape: z.enum(['rect', 'roundRect', 'circle', 'triangle', 'star', 'arrow']),
  fill: z.string(),
  stroke: z.string(),
  strokeWidth: z.number().nonnegative(),
  text: z.string().default(''),
  fontSize: z.number().positive().default(16),
  textFill: z.string().default('#111'),
  radius: z.number().nonnegative().optional(),
  textAlign: TextAlignSchema.optional(),
  textVerticalAlign: TextVerticalAlignSchema.optional(),
  textPadding: PaddingSchema.optional(),
  textFontFamily: z.string().optional(),
  textLineHeight: z.number().positive().optional(),
  textFontStyle: z.string().optional(),
  textDecoration: z.string().optional(),
}).passthrough();

export const LineElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('line'),
  points: z.array(z.number()).min(4),
  stroke: z.string(),
  strokeWidth: z.number().nonnegative(),
  dash: DashTypeSchema.default('solid'),
  isArrow: z.boolean().default(false),
}).passthrough();

export const TableElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('table'),
  rows: z.number().int().positive(),
  cols: z.number().int().positive(),
  data: z.array(z.array(z.string())),
  borderColor: z.string(),
  headerBg: z.string(),
  fontSize: z.number().positive(),
}).passthrough();

export const FooterElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('footer'),
  text: z.string(),
  html: z.string().optional(),
  fill: z.string(),
  bgColor: z.string(),
  fontSize: z.number().positive(),
}).passthrough();

export const PlaceholderElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('placeholder'),
  fieldPath: z.string(),
  fieldType: DataFieldTypeSchema,
  sample: z.string().default(''),
  label: z.string().default(''),
  inline: z.boolean().optional(),
  fontSize: z.number().positive(),
  fontFamily: z.string(),
  fill: z.string(),
  fontStyle: z.string().default('normal'),
  textDecoration: z.string().default(''),
  align: TextAlignSchema.default('left'),
  verticalAlign: TextVerticalAlignSchema.default('top'),
  lineHeight: z.number().positive().default(1.2),
  padding: PaddingSchema.default({ top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 }),
  background: z.string().default('transparent'),
  imageMode: z.enum(['fit', 'fill']).optional(),
  html: z.string().optional(),
}).passthrough();

export const FillElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('fill'),
  points: z.array(z.number()).min(6),
  fill: z.string(),
  stroke: z.string().default(''),
  strokeWidth: z.number().nonnegative().default(0),
  opacity: z.number().min(0).max(1).default(1),
}).passthrough();

export const DiagramElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('diagram'),
  svg: z.string(),
  xml: z.string(),
}).passthrough();

export const ConnectorElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('connector'),
  fromId: z.string(),
  fromAnchor: AnchorSideSchema,
  toId: z.string(),
  toAnchor: AnchorSideSchema,
  stroke: z.string(),
  strokeWidth: z.number().nonnegative(),
  dash: DashTypeSchema.default('solid'),
  isArrow: z.boolean().default(true),
  routing: ConnectorRoutingSchema.default('orthogonal'),
  label: z.string().optional(),
  labelFontSize: z.number().positive().optional(),
  labelFill: z.string().optional(),
  labelOffsetX: z.number().optional(),
  labelOffsetY: z.number().optional(),
}).passthrough();

/* Stamp: passthrough, С‚.Рє. legacy Рё new СЃРёР»СЊРЅРѕ СЂР°Р·Р»РёС‡Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ. */
export const StampElementSchema = z.object({
  ...BaseElementFields,
  type: z.literal('stamp'),
}).passthrough();

export const CanvasElementSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('type', [
  TextElementSchema,
  ImageElementSchema,
  ShapeElementSchema,
  LineElementSchema,
  TableElementSchema,
  FooterElementSchema,
  PlaceholderElementSchema,
  FillElementSchema,
  DiagramElementSchema,
  ConnectorElementSchema,
  StampElementSchema,
]);

/* === Slide === */
export const SlideSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  format: DocFormatSchema.default('a4'),
  orientation: OrientationSchema.default('portrait'),
  width: z.number().positive(),
  height: z.number().positive(),
  background: z.string().default('#ffffff'),
  backgroundImage: z.string().optional(),
  padding: PaddingSchema.default({ top: 20, right: 20, bottom: 20, left: 20 }),
  elements: z.array(CanvasElementSchema).max(SCHEMA_LIMITS.MAX_ELEMENTS_PER_SLIDE).default([]),
}).passthrough();

/* === Document (v2.0) === */
export const DocumentSchema = z.object({
  version: z.string().regex(/^\d+\.\d+$/, 'version must be "N.N"').default('2.0'),
  exportedAt: z.string().optional(),
  slides: z.array(SlideSchema).min(1).max(SCHEMA_LIMITS.MAX_SLIDES),
  data: z.array(DataRootSchema).default([]),
  activeSlideId: z.string().optional(),
}).passthrough();

/* === Public types === */
export type ValidatedDocument = z.infer<typeof DocumentSchema>;
export type ValidatedSlide = z.infer<typeof SlideSchema>;
export type ValidatedCanvasElement = z.infer<typeof CanvasElementSchema>;
export type ValidatedDataRoot = z.infer<typeof DataRootSchema>;

/* === Result helper === */
export type ValidationResult<T> =
  | { ok: true; value: T }
  | { ok: false; issues: z.ZodIssue[] };

export function validateDocument(raw: unknown): ValidationResult<ValidatedDocument> {
  const result = DocumentSchema.safeParse(raw);
  return result.success
    ? { ok: true, value: result.data }
    : { ok: false, issues: result.error.issues };
}

/* ============================================================
   shared/sentry.ts
   NFR-OBS-02: prod-only Sentry. SDK РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЃРЅР°СЂСѓР¶Рё

export interface SentryContext {
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

export interface SentryLike {
  captureException(error: unknown, context?: SentryContext): void;
  captureMessage(message: string, context?: SentryContext): void;
}

let provider: SentryLike | null = null;

export function setSentryProvider(p: SentryLike | null): void {
  provider = p;
}

export function getSentry(): SentryLike | null {
  return provider;
}

FILE: src\shared\store\store.ts
SIZE: 952 bytes

import { useSyncExternalStore, useCallback } from 'react';

export type Listener = () => void;

export class SimpleStore<T> {
  private state: T;
  private listeners = new Set<Listener>();

  constructor(initialState: T) {
    this.state = initialState;
  }

  getState = (): T => this.state;

  setState = (nextState: T | ((prev: T) => T)): void => {
    this.state =
      typeof nextState === 'function'
        ? (nextState as (prev: T) => T)(this.state)
        : nextState;
    this.listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  };

  subscribe = (listener: Listener): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

export function useStore<T, S>(store: SimpleStore<T>, selector: (state: T) => S): S {
  const getSnapshot = useCallback(() => selector(store.getState()), [store, selector]);
  return useSyncExternalStore(store.subscribe, getSnapshot, getSnapshot);
}

/* ============================================================
   shared/utils/formats.ts

   Р§РёСЃС‚Р°СЏ С„СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ: СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ СЃР»Р°Р№РґР° РїРѕ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Сѓ + РѕСЂРёРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёРё.
   Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ Р¶РёР»Р° РІ domain/slide/operations.ts, РЅРѕ lib РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°Р»
   РµС‘ в†’ lib в†’ domain РЅР°СЂСѓС€РµРЅРёРµ ARC-LAYER.
   ============================================================ */

import { FORMATS } from '../constants';
import type { DocFormat, Orientation } from '../types';

export interface FormatSize {
  readonly w: number;
  readonly h: number;
}

export function getFormatSize(
  format: DocFormat,
  orientation: Orientation,
  customSize?: FormatSize,
): FormatSize {
  const base =
    format === 'custom' && customSize
      ? customSize
      : FORMATS[format]?.portrait ?? FORMATS.a4.portrait;

  return orientation === 'portrait'
    ? { w: base.w, h: base.h }
    : { w: base.h, h: base.w };
}

FILE: src\shared\utils\id.ts

/**
 * ID generation utilities
 */

let counter = 0;

/**
 * Generate unique ID
 */
export function generateId(): string {
  const timestamp = Date.now().toString(36);
  const random = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8);
  const count = (counter++).toString(36);
  return `${timestamp}-${random}-${count}`;
}

/**
 * Generate ID with prefix
 */
export function generateIdWithPrefix(prefix: string): string {
  return `${prefix}-${generateId()}`;
}

/**
 * Check if string is valid ID
 */
export function isValidId(id: string): boolean {
  return typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0;
}

import type { Slide, DataRoot, CanvasElement, DataField } from '@/shared/types';
import { uid, makeText, makeShape, makePh } from '../helpers';

/* ---------- Карточка товара ---------- */
function card(x: number, y: number, idx: number): CanvasElement[] {
  const CARD_W = 238;
  const CARD_H = 290;
  const RED = '#E30611';
  const DARK = '#111111';
  const MUTED = '#777777';
  const GRAY_BG = '#F5F5F5';
  const p = `items[${idx}]`;

  return [
    makeShape({
      x, y, width: CARD_W, height: CARD_H,
      fill: GRAY_BG, stroke: '#E5E5E5', strokeWidth: 1, radius: 10,
    }),
    makeShape({
      x: x + 10, y: y + 10, width: CARD_W - 20, height: 130,
      fill: '#FFFFFF', stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0, radius: 6,
    }),
    makePh({
      x: x + 20, y: y + 20, width: CARD_W - 40, height: 110,
      fieldPath: `${p}.image`, fieldType: 'image',
      sample: 'https://picsum.photos/seed/p' + idx + '/200/120',
    }),
    makeShape({
      x: x + 10, y: y + 10, width: 56, height: 26,
      fill: RED, stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0, radius: 6,
    }),
    makePh({
      x: x + 10, y: y + 15, width: 56, height: 20,
      fieldPath: `${p}.discount`, fieldType: 'string', sample: '-25%',
      fontSize: 13, fontStyle: 'bold', align: 'center', fill: '#FFFFFF',
    }),
    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 152, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 40,
      fieldPath: `${p}.name`, fieldType: 'string',
      sample: 'Колоко пастеризованное 3,2%',
      fontSize: 13, fontStyle: 'bold', lineHeight: 1.25, fill: DARK,
    }),
    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 200, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 32,
      fieldPath: `${p}.price`, fieldType: 'string', sample: '99 ₽',
      fontSize: 24, fontStyle: 'bold', fill: RED,
    }),
    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 240, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 20,
      fieldPath: `${p}.oldPrice`, fieldType: 'string', sample: '129 ₽',
      fontSize: 14, fill: MUTED, textDecoration: 'line-through',
    }),
    makePh({
      x: x + 14, y: y + 262, width: CARD_W - 28, height: 18,
      fieldPath: `${p}.unit`, fieldType: 'string', sample: 'за 1 л.',
      fontSize: 10, fill: MUTED,
    }),
  ];
}

      { id: uid(), name: 'image',    type: 'image',  sample: 'https://picsum.photos/seed/p0/200/120' },
    ],
  };

  return [

      id: uid(), name: 'catalog',

function yellowStripe(x: number, y: number, w: number, rotation: number) {
  return makeShape({
    x, y, width: w, height: 46,
    fill: '#FFDD00', stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0,
    rotation, radius: 0,
  });
}

// src/templates/helpers.ts
import type {
  TextElement, ShapeElement, PlaceholderElement,
  TableElement, LineElement, FooterElement, Padding,
} from '@/shared/types';
import { FORMATS } from '@/shared/constants';

export const uid = (): string => Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);

export function makeText(o: Partial<TextElement> = {}): TextElement {
  const base: TextElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'text', x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, height: 30, rotation: 0,
    text: '', richText: true, fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fill: '#111',
    fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
    align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
    padding: { top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 },
    background: 'transparent',
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeShape(o: Partial<ShapeElement> = {}): ShapeElement {
  const base: ShapeElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'shape', shape: 'rect',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 100, rotation: 0,
    fill: 'transparent', stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0,
    text: '', fontSize: 16, textFill: '#111', radius: 0,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makePh(o: Partial<PlaceholderElement> = {}): PlaceholderElement {
  const base: PlaceholderElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'placeholder',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, height: 30, rotation: 0,
    fieldPath: '', fieldType: 'string', sample: '', label: '',
    inline: true,
    fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fill: '#111',
    fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
    align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
    padding: { top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 },
    background: 'transparent',
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeTable(o: Partial<TableElement> = {}): TableElement {
  const base: TableElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'table',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 400, height: 120, rotation: 0,
    rows: 3, cols: 3,
    data: [['A', 'B', 'C'], ['', '', ''], ['', '', '']],
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1', headerBg: '#e2e8f0', fontSize: 13,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeLine(o: Partial<LineElement> = {}): LineElement {
  const base: LineElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'line',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 300, height: 20, rotation: 0,
    points: [0, 10, 300, 10],
    stroke: '#1a1a1a', strokeWidth: 1, dash: 'solid', isArrow: false,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeFooter(o: Partial<FooterElement> = {}): FooterElement {
  const base: FooterElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'footer',
    x: 0, y: 1000, width: FORMATS.a4.portrait.w, height: 40, rotation: 0,
    text: 'ООО «Компания» · +7 (000) 000-00-00',
    fill: '#ffffff', bgColor: '#1e293b', fontSize: 12,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeFooterInPrintArea(
  slideW: number, slideH: number,
  padding: Padding,
  o: Partial<FooterElement> = {},
): FooterElement {
  const height = o.height ?? 40;
  const { x: _x, y: _y, width: _w, ...rest } = o;
  return {
    id: uid(), type: 'footer',
    x: padding.left,
    y: slideH - padding.bottom - height,
    width: Math.max(0, slideW - padding.left - padding.right),
    height,
    rotation: 0,
    text: 'ООО «Компания» · www.example.com',
    fill: '#ffffff', bgColor: '#1e293b', fontSize: 12,
    ...rest,
  };
}

FILE: src\workers\fillWorker.ts
SIZE: 1036 bytes

/// <reference lib="webworker" />
/* ============================================================
   workers/fillWorker.ts
   Тонкая обёртка над rasterizeCompositeFills.
   ============================================================ */

import { rasterizeCompositeFills } from '@/lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import type { FillWorkerRequest, FillWorkerResponse } from './protocol';

const ctx = self as unknown as DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope;

ctx.onmessage = async (e: MessageEvent<FillWorkerRequest>) => {
  const { seq, width, height, shapes, fills } = e.data;

  let response: FillWorkerResponse;

  try {
    const result = await rasterizeCompositeFills({ width, height, shapes, fills });
    response = result.ok
      ? { seq, ok: true, blob: result.value }
      : { seq, ok: false, reason: result.reason };
  } catch (err) {
    response = { seq, ok: false, reason: 'all-fills-rejected' };
    console.error('[fillWorker] unhandled exception', err);
  }

  ctx.postMessage(response);
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\workers\protocol.ts
SIZE: 623 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/* ============================================================
   workers/protocol.ts
   Типы сообщений main thread <-> fill worker.
   ============================================================ */

import type { FillSpec, FillError } from '@/lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '@/shared/types';

export interface FillWorkerRequest {
  seq: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
}

export type FillWorkerResponse =
  | { seq: number; ok: true; blob: Blob }
  | { seq: number; ok: false; reason: FillError };

FILE: src\workers\serializeWorker.ts
SIZE: 1024 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// <reference lib="webworker" />
/* ============================================================
   workers/serializeWorker.ts

   NFR-PERF-10 / REG-B8: JSON.stringify РІРЅРµ main thread.
   РџСЂРёРЅРёРјР°РµС‚ { id, payload }, РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ { id, ok, text }.
   ============================================================ */

interface SerializeRequest {
  id: number;
  payload: unknown;
}

interface SerializeResponse {
  id: number;
  ok: boolean;
  text?: string;
  error?: string;
}

const ctx = self as unknown as DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope;

ctx.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<SerializeRequest>) => {
  const { id, payload } = e.data;
  try {
    const text = JSON.stringify(payload);
    const response: SerializeResponse = { id, ok: true, text };
    ctx.postMessage(response);
  } catch (err) {
    const response: SerializeResponse = {
      id,
      ok: false,
      error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
    };
    ctx.postMessage(response);
  }
};

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  createSlide, cloneSlide, changeFormat, changeOrientation, moveElementLayer,
} from '../../../src/domain/slide/operations';
import type { CanvasElement, Slide } from '../../../src/shared/types';

function makeFakeElement(overrides: Partial<CanvasElement> = {}): CanvasElement {
  return {
    id: 'el-1',
    type: 'shape',
    shape: 'rect',
    x: 10, y: 20, width: 100, height: 50, rotation: 0,
    fill: '#fff', stroke: '#000', strokeWidth: 1,
    text: '', fontSize: 14, textFill: '#000',
    ...overrides,
  } as CanvasElement;
}

describe('domain/slide/operations', () => {
  it('createSlide РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Р№ СЃР»Р°Р№Рґ СЃ СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рј id', () => {
    const a = createSlide(1);
    const b = createSlide(2);
    expect(a.id).toBeTruthy();
    expect(a.id).not.toBe(b.id);
    expect(a.elements).toEqual([]);
    expect(a.width).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(a.height).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('cloneSlide РєРѕРїРёСЂСѓРµС‚ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚С‹ СЃ РЅРѕРІС‹РјРё id', () => {
    const src: Slide = {
      ...createSlide(1),
      elements: [makeFakeElement({ id: 'a' }), makeFakeElement({ id: 'b' })],
    };
    const copy = cloneSlide(src);
    expect(copy.id).not.toBe(src.id);
    expect(copy.elements.length).toBe(2);
    expect(copy.elements[0].id).not.toBe('a');
    expect(copy.elements[1].id).not.toBe('b');
    expect(copy.elements[0].id).not.toBe(copy.elements[1].id);
  });

  it('changeFormat РїРµСЂРµСЃС‡РёС‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂС‹ A4 в†’ A5', () => {
    const slide = createSlide(1);
    const changed = changeFormat(slide, 'a5');
    expect(changed.format).toBe('a5');
    expect(changed.width).toBeLessThan(slide.width);
    expect(changed.height).toBeLessThan(slide.height);
  });

  it('changeOrientation swap С€РёСЂРёРЅС‹ Рё РІС‹СЃРѕС‚С‹', () => {
    const slide = createSlide(1);
    const flipped = changeOrientation(slide, 'landscape');
    expect(flipped.width).toBe(slide.height);
    expect(flipped.height).toBe(slide.width);
  });

  it('moveElementLayer РјРµРЅСЏРµС‚ z-order', () => {
    const slide: Slide = {
      ...createSlide(1),
      elements: [
        makeFakeElement({ id: 'a' }),
        makeFakeElement({ id: 'b' }),
        makeFakeElement({ id: 'c' }),
      ],
    };
    const moved = moveElementLayer(slide, 'b', -1);
    expect(moved.elements.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(['b', 'a', 'c']);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  parsePoints, serializePoints, findNearestSegment,
  insertPoint, removePoint, ensureMinimalPoints, getPointsBBox,
} from '../../src/lib/polyline';

describe('lib/polyline', () => {
  it('parsePoints в†’ []', () => {
    expect(parsePoints(undefined)).toEqual([]);
    expect(parsePoints([])).toEqual([]);
    expect(parsePoints([1, 2])).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('parse/serialize roundtrip', () => {
    const flat = [0, 0, 10, 10, 20, 5];
    const pts = parsePoints(flat);
    expect(pts).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(serializePoints(pts)).toEqual(flat);
  });

  it('insertPoint РІСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµС‚ РјРµР¶РґСѓ С‚РѕС‡РєР°РјРё', () => {
    const pts = parsePoints([0, 0, 10, 0, 20, 0]);
    const next = insertPoint(pts, 5, 1, 0.01);
    expect(next).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(next[1].x).toBeCloseTo(5);
    expect(next[1].y).toBeCloseTo(0);
  });

  it('removePoint РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»СЏРµС‚ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёРµ РґРІРµ С‚РѕС‡РєРё', () => {
    const pts = parsePoints([0, 0, 10, 0]);
    expect(removePoint(pts, 0)).toEqual(pts);
    const big = parsePoints([0, 0, 10, 0, 20, 0]);
    expect(removePoint(big, 1)).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('findNearestSegment РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ Р±Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€РёР№ СЃРµРіРјРµРЅС‚', () => {
    const pts = parsePoints([0, 0, 10, 0, 20, 0]);
    const r = findNearestSegment(pts, 8, 3);
    expect(r.segmentIndex).toBe(0);
    expect(r.distance).toBeCloseTo(3, 1);
  });

  it('ensureMinimalPoints РїРѕРґСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµС‚ РґРµС„РѕР»С‚', () => {
    expect(ensureMinimalPoints(undefined, 100, 40)).toEqual([0, 20, 100, 20]);
    expect(ensureMinimalPoints([0, 0, 10, 10], 100, 40)).toEqual([0, 0, 10, 10]);
  });

  it('getPointsBBox СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚ СЂР°РјРєСѓ', () => {
    const bbox = getPointsBBox(parsePoints([0, 0, 10, 5, 20, 3]));
    expect(bbox.width).toBe(20);
    expect(bbox.height).toBe(5);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

describe('lib/stamps/history/autoKey', () => {
  it('РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ undefined РґР»СЏ РґРёСЃРєСЂРµС‚РЅС‹С… РїРѕР»РµР№', () => {

    expect(autoKey('id1', { text: 'abc' })).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РєР»СЋС‡ РґР»СЏ РЅРµРїСЂРµСЂС‹РІРЅС‹С…', () => {
    expect(autoKey('id1', { arcSpanDeg: 200 })).toBe('id1:arcSpanDeg');
  });

    expect(autoKey('id1', { arcSpanDeg: 200, x: 10 })).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂСѓРµС‚ РїРѕР»СЏ', () => {

      .toBe('id1:arcOffset,arcSpanDeg');
  });

  it('РїСѓСЃС‚РѕР№ РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚ в†’ undefined', () => {
    expect(autoKey('id1', {})).toBeUndefined();
  });

/* РњРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ setup РґР»СЏ jsdom: РЅРµ С‚СЏРЅРµРј СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ Konva/DOM-Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё. */
import { vi } from 'vitest';

// jsdom РЅРµ СЂРµР°Р»РёР·СѓРµС‚ ResizeObserver вЂ” Р·Р°РіР»СѓС€РёРј.
class ResizeObserverStub {
  observe(): void {}
  unobserve(): void {}
  disconnect(): void {}
}

if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && !('ResizeObserver' in window)) {
  (window as unknown as { ResizeObserver: typeof ResizeObserverStub }).ResizeObserver =
    ResizeObserverStub;
}

// localStorage РІ jsdom РёРЅРѕРіРґР° РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ вЂ” Р·Р°РіР»СѓС€РёРј.
if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && !window.localStorage) {
  const store = new Map<string, string>();
  const stub = {
    getItem: (k: string) => store.get(k) ?? null,
    setItem: (k: string, v: string) => { store.set(k, v); },
    removeItem: (k: string) => { store.delete(k); },
    clear: () => store.clear(),
    key: () => null,
    length: 0,
  };
  Object.defineProperty(window, 'localStorage', { value: stub });
}

// Р“Р»СѓС€РёРј console.warn РІ С‚РµСЃС‚Р°С…, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ Р·Р°СЃРѕСЂСЏС‚СЊ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚.
vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => {});
